if genv._DEATH_NOTE_BOOT then pcall(function() genv._DEATH_NOTE_BOOT:Destroy() end);genv._DEATH_NOTE_BOOT=nil end

    pcall(function()
        genv._DEATH_NOTE_INSTANCE:Destroy()
    end)
    genv._DEATH_NOTE_LOADED = false
end

local function safeGetService(serviceName)
    local ok, service = pcall(function() return game:GetService(serviceName) end)
    if ok and service then return service end
    return game:FindService(serviceName)
end

local Players           = safeGetService("Players")
local TweenService      = safeGetService("TweenService")
local RunService        = safeGetService("RunService")
local UserInputService  = safeGetService("UserInputService")
local CoreGui           = safeGetService("CoreGui")
local HttpService       = safeGetService("HttpService")
local MarketplaceService= safeGetService("MarketplaceService")
local SoundService      = safeGetService("SoundService")
local TeleportService   = safeGetService("TeleportService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
if not LocalPlayer then
    local startTime = tick()
    while not LocalPlayer and (tick() - startTime < 10) do
        task.wait(0.1)
        LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    end
end
if not LocalPlayer then LocalPlayer = Players:GetPlayers()[1] end

-- Environment & System Helpers
local RedEnv = {}
do
    local function detectExecutor()
        if identifyexecutor then
            local ok, name = pcall(identifyexecutor)
            if ok and name then return tostring(name) end
        end
        if getexecutorname then
            local ok, name = pcall(getexecutorname)
            if ok and name then return tostring(name) end
        end
        if delta or Delta or DELTA_LOADED then return "Delta Android" end
        if codex or Codex then return "Codex Mobile" end
        if arceus or Arceus or ARCEUS_LOADED then return "Arceus X" end
        if fluxus or Fluxus then return "Fluxus" end
        if hydrogen or Hydrogen then return "Hydrogen" end
        if solara or SOLARA_LOADED then return "Solara" end
        if wave or WAVE_LOADED then return "Wave" end
        return "Delta Mobile"
    end
    RedEnv.ExecutorName = detectExecutor()

    RedEnv.SetClipboard = function(text)
        local setclip = setclipboard or toclipboard or set_clipboard or (Clipboard and Clipboard.set)
        if setclip then
            local s = pcall(setclip, tostring(text))
            if s then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    RedEnv.GetSafeParent = function()
        if gethui then
            local ok, hui = pcall(gethui)
            if ok and hui then return hui end
        end
        if CoreGui then
            local ok, test = pcall(function()
                local f = Instance.new("Folder")
                f.Parent = CoreGui
                f:Destroy()
                return CoreGui
            end)
            if ok and test then return CoreGui end
        end
        if LocalPlayer then
            return LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5) or LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        end
        return CoreGui
    end
end

-- Audio Engine
local RedAudio = { Enabled = true, MasterVolume = 0.7 }
do
    local soundHolder = Instance.new("Folder")
    soundHolder.Name = "DeathNote_Audio"
    pcall(function() soundHolder.Parent = SoundService end)

    RedAudio.Presets = {
        Click = { Id = "rbxassetid://6895079853", Vol = 0.65, Pitch = 1.05 },
        Hover = { Id = "rbxassetid://6895079737", Vol = 0.35, Pitch = 1.2 },
        TabSwitch = { Id = "rbxassetid://9069609268", Vol = 0.6, Pitch = 1.0 },
        ToggleOn = { Id = "rbxassetid://6895079853", Vol = 0.7, Pitch = 1.15 },
        ToggleOff = { Id = "rbxassetid://6895079737", Vol = 0.6, Pitch = 0.95 },
        CopySuccess = { Id = "rbxassetid://6422098522", Vol = 0.8, Pitch = 1.05 },
        Notification = { Id = "rbxassetid://6895079634", Vol = 0.75, Pitch = 1.0 },
        ThemeChange = { Id = "rbxassetid://9069608974", Vol = 0.65, Pitch = 1.1 },
        WindowOpen = { Id = "rbxassetid://9069609268", Vol = 0.65, Pitch = 1.05 },
        WindowClose = { Id = "rbxassetid://6895079737", Vol = 0.55, Pitch = 0.9 }
    }

    function RedAudio.Play(name)
        if not RedAudio.Enabled then return end
        local p = RedAudio.Presets[name]
        if not p then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            local s = Instance.new("Sound")
            s.SoundId = p.Id
            s.Volume = p.Vol * RedAudio.MasterVolume
            s.Pitch = p.Pitch or 1.0
            s.Parent = soundHolder
            s:Play()
            s.Ended:Connect(function() s:Destroy() end)
            task.delay(2.5, function() if s and s.Parent then s:Destroy() end end)
        end)
    end
end

function RedTheme.Get(key)
    local cur = RedTheme.Palettes[RedTheme.CurrentThemeName] or RedTheme.Palettes["Crimson Eclipse"]
    return cur[key] or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
end

function RedTheme.Bind(instance, prop, key)
    if not instance then return end
    table.insert(RedTheme.Bound, { Instance = instance, Prop = prop, Key = key })
    pcall(function() instance[prop] = RedTheme.Get(key) end)
end

function RedTheme.SetTheme(name)
    if not RedTheme.Palettes[name] then return end
    RedTheme.CurrentThemeName = name
    local pal = RedTheme.Palettes[name]
    RedAudio.Play("ThemeChange")
    for i = #RedTheme.Bound, 1, -1 do
        local b = RedTheme.Bound[i]
        if b.Instance and b.Instance.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                TweenService:Create(b.Instance, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    [b.Prop] = pal[b.Key]
                }):Play()
            end)
        else
            table.remove(RedTheme.Bound, i)
        end
    end
end

-- Animations Helper
local RedTween = {}
function RedTween.Play(inst, duration, goals, style, dir)
    local t = TweenService:Create(inst, TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.25, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), goals)
    t:Play()
    return t
end
function RedTween.Spring(inst, duration, goals)
    return RedTween.Play(inst, duration or 0.45, goals, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
end

-- Unified Touch & Mouse Draggable
local function makeDraggable(handle, targetFrame)
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
    handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = targetFrame.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
            end)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            RedTween.Play(targetFrame, 0.1, {
                Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        end
    end)
end

-- Live FPS Calculator
local frames = 0
local lastFpsTick = tick()
RedTelemetry.FPSConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    frames = frames + 1
    local now = tick()
    if now - lastFpsTick >= 0.5 then
        RedTelemetry.CurrentFPS = math.floor(frames / (now - lastFpsTick))
        frames = 0
        lastFpsTick = now
    end
end)

-- Live Ping
task.spawn(function()
    while not RedTelemetry.Closed and task.wait(1.5) do
        local ping = 50
        pcall(function()
            if LocalPlayer then ping = math.floor(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 1000) end
        end)
        RedTelemetry.CurrentPing = math.clamp(ping, 1, 999)
    end
end)

-- Time & Greeting Formatter
function RedTelemetry.UpdateTime()
    local d = os.date("*t")
    local h, m, s = d.hour, d.min, d.sec
    local ampm = "AM"
    if h >= 12 then ampm = "PM" if h > 12 then h = h - 12 end elseif h == 0 then h = 12 end
    RedTelemetry.LocalTimeFormatted = string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d %s", h, m, s, ampm)
    local raw = d.hour
    if raw >= 4 and raw < 12 then RedTelemetry.Greeting = "Good morning"
    elseif raw >= 12 and raw < 17 then RedTelemetry.Greeting = "Good afternoon"
    elseif raw >= 17 and raw < 22 then RedTelemetry.Greeting = "Good evening"
    else RedTelemetry.Greeting = "Good night" end
end

-- User-supplied outfit valuation semantics, integrated into the existing GUI.
-- Clothing + GetAccessories(), session cache, PriceInRobux first, limited fallback 1000.
-- Failed/missing values contribute zero: this is a script estimate, NOT exact wealth.
RedTelemetry.AvatarService = (function()
    local api = { closed=false }
    local assetValueCache, assetFlags = {}, {}
    api.UPDATE_INTERVAL, api.LIMITED_BASE_VALUE = 5, 1000
    function api.bounded(seconds, work, valid)
        if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) then return false,"cancelled" end
        local complete, abandoned, result = false, false, nil
        local worker = task.spawn(function()
            local values = table.pack(pcall(work))
            if not abandoned then result=values;complete=true end
        end)
        local deadline=os.clock()+math.max(0.05,seconds)
        while not complete do
            if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) or os.clock()>=deadline then
                abandoned=true
                if type(worker)=="thread" and type(task.cancel)=="function" then pcall(task.cancel,worker) end
                return false,(api.closed or (valid and not valid())) and "cancelled" or "timeout"
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
        return table.unpack(result,1,result.n)
    end
    function api.getAssetValue(assetId,valid,deadline)
        if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) then return 0,{cancelled=true} end
        if assetValueCache[assetId]~=nil then return assetValueCache[assetId],assetFlags[assetId] end
        local ok,productInfo=api.bounded(math.min(6,math.max(0.05,(deadline or os.clock()+6)-os.clock())),function()
            return MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(assetId,Enum.InfoType.Asset)
        end,valid)
        if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) then return 0,{cancelled=true} end
        local value,flags=0,{}
        if ok and type(productInfo)=="table" then
            if productInfo.PriceInRobux~=nil and productInfo.PriceInRobux~=false then
                value=tonumber(productInfo.PriceInRobux) or 0
            elseif productInfo.IsLimited or productInfo.IsLimitedUnique then
                value=api.LIMITED_BASE_VALUE;flags.synthetic=true
            else flags.missing=true end
        else flags.failed=true end
        if value~=value or value<0 or value==math.huge then value=0;flags.failed=true end
        assetValueCache[assetId],assetFlags[assetId]=value,flags
        return value,flags
    end
    function api.price(id,valid,deadline)
        local value,flags=api.getAssetValue(id,valid,deadline)
        if flags.cancelled then return nil,"cancelled" end
        return {price=value,known=true,synthetic=flags.synthetic,apiFailure=flags.failed}
    end
    function api.value(player,valid,seconds)
        if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) then return nil end
        local result={total=0,known=0,unknown=0,failures=0,synthetic=0,items={},source="USER SCRIPT ESTIMATE",terminal=true,comparable=true,at=os.clock()}
        local character=player.Character
        local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then result.reason="Character/humanoid unavailable: source logic returns 0";return result end
        local deadline=os.clock()+(seconds or 25)
        local success,description=api.bounded(math.min(5,seconds or 25),function() return humanoid:GetAppliedDescription() end,valid)
        if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) then return nil end
        if not success or not description then result.reason="Description unavailable: source logic returns 0";return result end
        local function add(id)
            if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) then return end
            table.insert(result.items,id)
            local value,flags
            if os.clock()>=deadline then value,flags=0,{failed=true}
            else value,flags=api.getAssetValue(id,valid,deadline) end
            result.total=result.total+value
            result.known=result.known+1
            if flags.synthetic then result.synthetic=result.synthetic+1 end
            if flags.failed or flags.missing then result.failures=result.failures+1 end
        end
        -- Exactly the supplied value inputs: no head/body assets or profile fallback.
        for _,name in ipairs({"Shirt","Pants","GraphicTShirt"}) do
            local id=description[name]
            if id and id>0 then add(id) end
        end
        local accSuccess,accessories=pcall(function() return description:GetAccessories() end)
        if accSuccess and accessories then
            for _,accessory in ipairs(accessories) do if accessory.AssetId then add(accessory.AssetId) end end
        end
        pcall(function() description:Destroy() end)
        if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) then return nil end
        result.unknown=result.failures
        return result
    end
    function api.preview(userId,valid)
        local fallback="rbxthumb://type=Avatar&id="..tostring(userId).."&w=420&h=420"
        local ok,image,ready=api.bounded(5,function()
            return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(userId,Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarThumbnail,Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420)
        end,valid)
        if ok and type(image)=="string" and image~="" and ready~=false then return image end
        return fallback
    end
    function api.close() api.closed=true end
    return api
end)()

RedTelemetry.ExactAvatarValue,RedTelemetry.EstimatedAvatarValue=0,0 -- legacy aliases; display is an estimate
RedTelemetry.AvatarItemsCount,RedTelemetry.AvatarUnknownCount=0,0
RedTelemetry.AvatarValuationStatus="User-supplied outfit estimate"
function RedTelemetry.AvatarService.startOwn()
    if RedTelemetry.AvatarService.ownStarted then return end
    RedTelemetry.AvatarService.ownStarted=true
    task.spawn(function()
        while not RedTelemetry.AvatarService.closed do
            local result=RedTelemetry.AvatarService.value(LocalPlayer,function() return not RedTelemetry.AvatarService.closed end,25)
            if result and not RedTelemetry.AvatarService.closed then
                RedTelemetry.ExactAvatarValue,RedTelemetry.EstimatedAvatarValue=result.total,result.total
                RedTelemetry.AvatarItemsCount,RedTelemetry.AvatarUnknownCount=result.known,result.failures
                RedTelemetry.AvatarValuationStatus="Script estimate / "..result.synthetic.." limited fallbacks / "..result.failures.." zero-value lookups"
            end
            task.wait(RedTelemetry.AvatarService.UPDATE_INTERVAL)
        end
    end)
end

-- One shared account-age source: Home, About and Chat Age cannot disagree on self.
-- LocalPlayer uses Roblox AccountAge; other accounts use verified creation + server UTC.
RedTelemetry.AgeService = (function()
    local api={closed=false}
    local creationCache={}
    local function validNumber(value) return type(value)=="number" and value==value and value>=0 and value<math.huge end
    function api.serverNow()
        local ok,now=pcall(function() return game:GetService("Workspace"):GetServerTimeNow() end)
        if ok and validNumber(now) and now>946684800 then return now end
        return nil,"Server UTC clock unavailable; age was not guessed"
    end
    local function creation(player,valid)
        local id=player.UserId
        if creationCache[id] then return creationCache[id] end
        local ok,profile=RedTelemetry.AvatarService.bounded(8,function()
            if id<=0 then error("No public user ID") end
            local raw=game:HttpGet("https://users.roblox.com/v1/users/"..tostring(id))
            local data=HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
            if type(data)~="table" or tonumber(data.id)~=id or type(data.created)~="string" then error("User ID / creation date mismatch") end
            local date=DateTime.fromIsoDate(data.created)
            local epoch=date and ((date.UnixTimestampMillis and date.UnixTimestampMillis/1000) or date.UnixTimestamp)
            if not validNumber(epoch) or epoch<1104537600 then error("Invalid creation timestamp") end
            return {userId=id,epoch=epoch,iso=data.created,created=data.created:sub(1,10)}
        end,function() return not api.closed and (not valid or valid()) end)
        if ok and not api.closed then creationCache[id]=profile;return profile end
        return nil,tostring(profile)
    end
    function api.current(player,record)
        if record and record.userId~=player.UserId then record=nil end
        local days,source
        if player==LocalPlayer then
            local ok,value=pcall(function() return player.AccountAge end)
            if ok and validNumber(value) and value==math.floor(value) then days,source=value,"ROBLOX ACCOUNTAGE" end
        end
        if days==nil then
            if not record then return nil,"Verified creation date unavailable" end
            local now,reason=api.serverNow()
            if not now then return nil,reason end
            if record.epoch>now then return nil,"Creation date is ahead of server UTC; not guessed" end
            days=math.floor((now-record.epoch)/86400)
            source="ELAPSED FULL DAYS / SERVER UTC"
        end
        return {userId=player.UserId,days=days,created=record and record.created or "Unavailable",iso=record and record.iso,epoch=record and record.epoch,source=source}
    end
    function api.get(player,valid)
        if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) then return nil,"cancelled" end
        local record,reason=creation(player,valid)
        if api.closed or (valid and not valid()) then return nil,"cancelled" end
        local result,errorReason=api.current(player,record)
        return result,errorReason or reason
    end
    function api.refreshLocal(fetch)
        local record=creationCache[LocalPlayer.UserId]
        local result
        if fetch then result=api.get(LocalPlayer) else result=api.current(LocalPlayer,record) end
        if result then
            RedTelemetry.AccountAgeDays=result.days
            RedTelemetry.AccountAgeText=tostring(result.days).." Days"
            RedTelemetry.AccountCreationDate=result.created
            RedTelemetry.AccountAgeSource=result.source
        else
            RedTelemetry.AccountAgeDays=nil
            RedTelemetry.AccountAgeText="Unavailable"
            RedTelemetry.AccountCreationDate="Unavailable"
            RedTelemetry.AccountAgeSource="NO GUESS"
        end
        return result
    end
    local ageConnection
    function api.start()
        if api.started then return end
        api.started=true
        pcall(function() ageConnection=LocalPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AccountAge"):Connect(function() if not api.closed then api.refreshLocal(false) end end) end)
        task.spawn(function()
            while not api.closed do api.refreshLocal(false);task.wait(30) end
        end)
    end
    function api.close() api.closed=true;if ageConnection then ageConnection:Disconnect() end end
    return api
end)()

-- AccountAge is available locally; network assets never gate the GUI.
RedTelemetry.AgeService.refreshLocal(false)

-- Position & Velocity Realtime Tracker
RedTelemetry.PositionConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    pcall(function()
        local char = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
        if root then
            RedTelemetry.CharPosition = root.Position
            RedTelemetry.CharVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity or root.Velocity or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
    end)
end)

-- Realtime Social & Online Friends Scanner
function RedTelemetry.ScanServer()
    pcall(function()
        local plrs = Players:GetPlayers()
        RedTelemetry.ServerPlayerCount = #plrs
        RedTelemetry.ServerMaxPlayers = Players.MaxPlayers or 20
        local fCount, friends = 0, {}
        for _, p in ipairs(plrs) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                local s, isF = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(p.UserId) end)
                if s and isF then
                    fCount = fCount + 1
                    table.insert(friends, { Name = p.DisplayName, User = p.Name, Id = p.UserId })
                end
            end
        end
        RedTelemetry.FriendsInServer = fCount
        RedTelemetry.FriendList = friends
    end)
end

-- Online friends: preserve the raw presence data for the Friends carousel.
RedTelemetry.FriendsError = nil
function RedTelemetry.ScanOnlineFriendsPresences()
    if not LocalPlayer or RedTelemetry.FriendsScanning then return end
    RedTelemetry.FriendsScanning = true
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        local success, data = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetFriendsOnlineAsync(200) end)
        if success then return data end
        return LocalPlayer:GetFriendsOnline(200)
    end)
    if ok and type(result) == "table" then
        RedTelemetry.FriendsPresences = result
        RedTelemetry.TotalFriendsCount = #result
        RedTelemetry.FriendsError = nil
    else
        RedTelemetry.FriendsError = tostring(result or "Could not fetch online friends")
    end
    RedTelemetry.FriendsScanning = false
end

RedTelemetry.UpdateTime()
task.spawn(RedTelemetry.ScanServer)
task.spawn(function()
    while not RedTelemetry.Closed and task.wait(1) do RedTelemetry.UpdateTime() end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while not RedTelemetry.Closed and task.wait(5) do RedTelemetry.ScanServer() end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while not RedTelemetry.Closed and task.wait(10) do RedTelemetry.ScanOnlineFriendsPresences() end
end)
task.spawn(RedTelemetry.ScanOnlineFriendsPresences)


-- UI Building Blocks
local RedUI = {}

function RedUI.Corner(inst, radius)
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 16)
    c.Parent = inst
    return c
end

function RedUI.Stroke(inst, color, thickness)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    s.Color = color or RedTheme.Get("CardStroke")
    s.Thickness = thickness or 1.2
    s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    s.Parent = inst
    return s
end

function RedUI.Label(parent, text, size, pos, font, sizePt, color, alignX)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.Text = text or ""
    l.Size = size or UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    l.Position = pos or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    l.Font = font or Enum.Font.GothamBold
    l.TextSize = sizePt or 12
    l.TextColor3 = color or RedTheme.Get("TextPrimary")
    l.TextXAlignment = alignX or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.BorderSizePixel = 0
    l.Parent = parent
    return l
end

function RedUI.Button(parent, text, size, pos, bg, textCol, radius, onClick)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Text = text or ""
    b.Size = size or UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 30)
    b.Position = pos or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = bg or RedTheme.Get("Accent")
    b.TextColor3 = textCol or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 11
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.ClipsDescendants = true
    b.Parent = parent
    RedUI.Corner(b, radius or 12)

    local orig = b.BackgroundColor3
    b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        RedAudio.Play("Hover")
        RedTween.Play(b, 0.15, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(math.clamp(orig.R*255+25, 0, 255), math.clamp(orig.G*255+25, 0, 255), math.clamp(orig.B*255+25, 0, 255)) })
    end)
    b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() RedTween.Play(b, 0.2, { BackgroundColor3 = orig }) end)
    b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        RedAudio.Play("Click")
        RedTween.Play(b, 0.08, { Size = UDim2.new(size.X.Scale, size.X.Offset - 2, size.Y.Scale, size.Y.Offset - 2) })
    end)
    b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() RedTween.Play(b, 0.1, { Size = size }) end)
    if onClick then b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() onClick(b) end) end
    return b
end

function RedUI.Card(parent, size, pos)
    local c = Instance.new("Frame")
    c.Size = size or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 80)
    c.Position = pos or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    c.BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("CardBase")
    c.BorderSizePixel = 0
    c.Parent = parent
    RedUI.Corner(c, 16)
    local s = RedUI.Stroke(c, RedTheme.Get("CardStroke"), 1.2)
    RedTheme.Bind(c, "BackgroundColor3", "CardBase")
    RedTheme.Bind(s, "Color", "CardStroke")

    c.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        RedTween.Play(c, 0.2, { BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("CardHover") })
        RedTween.Play(s, 0.2, { Color = RedTheme.Get("CardStrokeActive") })
    end)
    c.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        RedTween.Play(c, 0.25, { BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("CardBase") })
        RedTween.Play(s, 0.25, { Color = RedTheme.Get("CardStroke") })
    end)
    return c, s
end

-- Toast System
local ToastContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
ToastContainer.Name = "Toasts"
ToastContainer.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 1, -20)
ToastContainer.Position = UDim2.new(1, -270, 0, 10)
ToastContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
ToastContainer.Parent = MainGui

local toastLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
toastLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
toastLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
toastLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)

local function ShowToast(title, msg, toastType)
    local color = (toastType == "Success" and RedTheme.Get("StatusGreen")) or (toastType == "Warning" and RedTheme.Get("StatusYellow")) or RedTheme.Get("Accent")
    RedAudio.Play(toastType == "Success" and "CopySuccess" or "Notification")
    local t = Instance.new("Frame")
    t.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 54)
    t.BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("CardBase")
    t.BorderSizePixel = 0
    t.Parent = ToastContainer
    RedUI.Corner(t, 12)
    RedUI.Stroke(t, color, 1.2)

    local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, 0)
    bar.BackgroundColor3 = color
    bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bar.Parent = t

    RedUI.Label(t, title, UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 18), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 6), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 11, RedTheme.Get("TextPrimary"))
    RedUI.Label(t, msg, UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 18), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 26), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 9, RedTheme.Get("TextSecondary"))

    t.Position = UDim2.new(1, 50, 0, 0)
    RedTween.Spring(t, 0.35, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) })
    task.delay(3, function()
        if t and t.Parent then
            local fade = RedTween.Play(t, 0.25, { Position = UDim2.new(1, 50, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
            fade.Completed:Connect(function() t:Destroy() end)
        end
    end)
end

local function setWindowState(open)
    isWindowOpen = open
    if isWindowOpen then
        MainWindow.Visible = true
        RedAudio.Play("WindowOpen")
        pillStatusDot.BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("StatusGreen")
        RedTween.Spring(MainWindow, 0.4, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 620, 0, 350),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -310, 0.5, -175),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0
        })
        ShowToast("DEATH NOTE", "Interface Expanded", "Info")
    else
        RedAudio.Play("WindowClose")
        pillStatusDot.BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("StatusYellow")
        local t = RedTween.Play(MainWindow, 0.25, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 620, 0, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -310, 0.5, -175),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1
        }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        t.Completed:Connect(function()
            if not isWindowOpen then
                MainWindow.Visible = false
            end
        end)
        ShowToast("Minimized", "Tap Floating Pill on left to reopen", "Info")
    end
end

local function toggleWindow()
    setWindowState(not isWindowOpen)
end

pillArrowBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleWindow)
pillTouchOverlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleWindow)

makeDot(Color3.fromRGB(0, 230, 118), function()
    MainWindow.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -310, 0.5, -175)
    ShowToast("Centered", "Reset window coordinates to center", "Info")
end)
makeDot(Color3.fromRGB(255, 179, 0), function()
    setWindowState(false)
end)
makeDot(Color3.fromRGB(255, 45, 85), function()
    MainGui:Destroy()
    genv._DEATH_NOTE_LOADED = false
end)

-- Content Pages Viewport
local ContentFrame = Instance.new("Frame")

local Pages, TabBtns = {}, {}
RedUI.DockButtons = {}
local currentTab = "Dashboard"

-- Quick is a bottom-level workspace, not a Home navigation tab.
RedUI.QuickNavTitle = RedUI.Label(TopBar, "QUICK  /  Movement controls",
    UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 0),
    Enum.Font.GothamBold, 12, RedTheme.Get("TextPrimary"))
RedUI.QuickNavTitle.Visible = false
RedTheme.Bind(RedUI.QuickNavTitle, "TextColor3", "TextPrimary")

function RedUI.SelectPage(name)
    if not Pages[name] then return end
    currentTab = name
    local isQuick = name == "Quick"
    local isVisual = name == "Visuals"
    local isAnimations = name == "Animations"
    local isWorkspace = isQuick or isVisual or isAnimations
    TabsBar.Visible = not isWorkspace
    RedUI.QuickNavTitle.Visible = isWorkspace
    RedUI.QuickNavTitle.Text = isAnimations and "ANIMATIONS / R15 pack studio" or isVisual and "VISUALS / Shader studio" or "QUICK / Movement controls"
    for n, button in pairs(TabBtns) do
        local selected = n == name
        button.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1
        button.BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("Accent")
        button.TextColor3 = selected and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or RedTheme.Get("TextSecondary")
        Pages[n].Visible = selected
    end
    for destination, button in pairs(RedUI.DockButtons) do
        local selected = destination == name or destination == "Dashboard" and not isWorkspace
        button.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0 or 1
        button.BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("Accent")
        button.TextColor3 = selected and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or RedTheme.Get("TextSecondary")
    end
end

local function AddTab(name, dockOnly)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Text = name
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, (name == "Update Logs" and 88 or 78), 0, 26)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = (name == currentTab and RedTheme.Get("Accent") or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = (name == currentTab and 0 or 1)
    btn.TextColor3 = (name == currentTab and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or RedTheme.Get("TextSecondary"))
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    btn.TextSize = 10
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.Parent = TabsBar
    btn.Visible = not dockOnly
    RedUI.Corner(btn, 13)

    local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = RedTheme.Get("Accent")
    scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    scroll.Visible = (name == currentTab)
    scroll.Parent = ContentFrame

    local list = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    list.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    list.Parent = scroll

    Pages[name] = scroll
    TabBtns[name] = btn

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if currentTab == name then return end
        RedAudio.Play("TabSwitch")
        RedUI.SelectPage(name)
    end)

    return scroll
end

local PageDashboard  = AddTab("Dashboard")
local PageActivity   = AddTab("Activity")
local PageFriends    = AddTab("Friends")
local PageUpdates    = AddTab("Update Logs")
local PageShortcuts  = AddTab("Shortcuts")

-- ============================================================================
-- QUICK WORKSPACE V13 - PERFORMANCE / SHIFT LOCK / HDR / WEAPON / F9 / RESET
-- Bottom-dock destination, separate from every Home workspace page.
-- Two compact feature cards per row, with independent expandable controls.
-- ============================================================================
do
(function()
    local page = AddTab("Quick", true)
    page.Name = "QuickWorkspace"
    page.ScrollBarThickness = 3

    local connections = {}
    local animations = {}
    local featureCards = {}
    local states = {}
    local alive = true
    local humanoid = nil
    local drag = nil
    local wasScrolling = true
    local updateMovementHud

    local colors = {
        White = Color3.fromRGB(241, 244, 255),
        Muted = Color3.fromRGB(158, 165, 187),
        Pink = Color3.fromRGB(255, 71, 130),
        Cyan = Color3.fromRGB(61, 220, 255),
        Track = Color3.fromRGB(47, 48, 67),
        Field = Color3.fromRGB(25, 26, 40),
        Off = Color3.fromRGB(52, 52, 70)
    }

    local function connect(signal, callback)
        local connection = signal:Connect(callback)
        table.insert(connections, connection)
        return connection
    end

    local function newFrame(parent, name, size, position, color)
        local result = Instance.new("Frame")
        result.Name = name
        result.Size = size
        result.Position = position or UDim2.new()
        result.BackgroundColor3 = color or colors.Field
        result.BorderSizePixel = 0
        result.Parent = parent
        return result
    end

    local function label(parent, content, size, position, fontSize, color, bold)
        local result = RedUI.Label(
            parent,
            content,
            size,
            position,
            bold and Enum.Font.GothamBold or Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
            fontSize or 10,
            color or colors.White
        )
        result.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        return result
    end

    local function stopDragging()
        if not drag then return end
        drag = nil
        if page.Parent then page.ScrollingEnabled = wasScrolling end
    end

    local function cancelTween(tween)
        if tween then tween:Cancel() end
    end

    local function finiteNumber(value)
        local number = tonumber(value)
        if not number or number ~= number then return nil end
        if number == math.huge or number == -math.huge then return nil end
        return number
    end

    local function currentHumanoid()
        if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then return humanoid end
        return nil
    end

    -- Controller units: 1 stud is treated as 0.28 metres for the metric display.
    local QuickPlus
    local world = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local byKey, rows = {}, {}
    local METRES_PER_STUD = 0.28
    local ACCELERATION_PER_SECOND = 20
    local verticalHeld = { up = false, down = false }
    local movementGroup = { "FlyR6", "SwimR6", "FlyR15", "SwimR15", "VehicleFly", "SkyWalk" }

    local function notify(message)
        pcall(function() ShowToast("QUICK", message, "Info") end)
    end
    local function getRoot(target)
        local character = target and target.Parent
        return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    local function feetOffset(target, root)
        local offset = target.HipHeight + root.Size.Y * 0.5
        if target.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then offset = offset + 2 end
        return offset
    end
    local function disconnectList(list)
        if not list then return end
        for _, connection in ipairs(list) do connection:Disconnect() end
    end
    local function keyDown(code)
        if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return false end
        return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(code)
    end
    local function verticalInput()
        local up = verticalHeld.up or keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E)
        local down = verticalHeld.down or keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) or keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q)
        return (up and 1 or 0) - (down and 1 or 0)
    end

    local function flightDirection(target)
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return Vector3.zero end
        local look, right = camera.CFrame.LookVector, camera.CFrame.RightVector
        local flatLook = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if flatLook.Magnitude < 0.01 then flatLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
        flatLook = flatLook.Unit
        local flatRight = Vector3.new(right.X, 0, right.Z)
        if flatRight.Magnitude < 0.01 then flatRight = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
        flatRight = flatRight.Unit
        local move = target.MoveDirection
        local forward = move:Dot(flatLook)
        local sideways = move:Dot(flatRight)
        -- WASD fallback also works when a custom character controller stops MoveDirection.
        if keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then forward = 1 end
        if keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then forward = -1 end
        if keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then sideways = 1 end
        if keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then sideways = -1 end
        local direction = look * forward + right * sideways + Vector3.new(0, verticalInput(), 0)
        if direction.Magnitude > 1 then direction = direction.Unit end
        return direction
    end

    local function clearController(state)
        local controller = state.controller
        if not controller then return end
        state.controller = nil
        for _, item in ipairs(controller.instances) do pcall(function() item:Destroy() end) end
        for _, joint in ipairs(controller.joints or {}) do
            if joint.motor.Parent then joint.motor.Transform = joint.original end
        end
        local target = controller.humanoid
        if target and target.Parent and controller.flags then
            target.PlatformStand = controller.flags.PlatformStand
            target.AutoRotate = controller.flags.AutoRotate
            if target.Health > 0 and not target.Sit and not controller.flags.PlatformStand then
                target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
        end
        if controller.root and controller.root.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                controller.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                controller.root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        end
    end

    -- Shared collision ownership prevents Noclip and Underground from restoring
    -- each other's temporary collision settings in the wrong order.
    local collisionOwners = {}
    local function acquireNoclip(token, character)
        if not character then return end
        for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local record = collisionOwners[part]
                if not record then
                    record = { original = part.CanCollide, owners = {} }
                    collisionOwners[part] = record
                end
                record.owners[token] = true
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end
    local function releaseNoclip(token)
        for part, record in pairs(collisionOwners) do
            record.owners[token] = nil
            if next(record.owners) == nil then
                if part.Parent then part.CanCollide = record.original end
                collisionOwners[part] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    local function uprightRotation(root)
        local look = root.CFrame.LookVector
        local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude < 0.01 then flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
        return CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, flat.Unit, Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
    end
    local function captureJoints(character)
        local joints = {}
        for _, item in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if item:IsA("Motor6D") then
                table.insert(joints, { motor = item, original = item.Transform, current = item.Transform })
            end
        end
        return joints
    end
    local function restoreJoints(joints)
        for _, entry in ipairs(joints or {}) do
            if entry.motor.Parent then entry.motor.Transform = entry.original end
        end
    end
    local function makeMotion(root, name)
        local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
        attachment.Name = name .. "Attachment"
        attachment.Parent = root
        local velocity = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
        velocity.Name = name .. "Velocity"
        velocity.Attachment0 = attachment
        velocity.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
        velocity.VelocityConstraintMode = Enum.VelocityConstraintMode.Vector
        velocity.ForceLimitsEnabled = false
        velocity.MaxForce = 100000000
        velocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
        velocity.Parent = root
        local orientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
        orientation.Name = name .. "Orientation"
        orientation.Attachment0 = attachment
        orientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
        orientation.MaxTorque = 100000000
        orientation.MaxAngularVelocity = 25
        orientation.Responsiveness = 35
        orientation.RigidityEnabled = false
        orientation.CFrame = root.CFrame.Rotation
        orientation.Parent = root
        return { attachment = attachment, velocity = velocity, orientation = orientation,
            instances = { velocity, orientation, attachment } }
    end
    local function destroyMotion(motion)
        if not motion then return end
        for _, item in ipairs(motion.instances) do item:Destroy() end
    end
    local function walkingGait(rig, speed, delta, bend)
        local moving = math.clamp(speed / 5, 0, 1)
        rig.gaitPhase = (rig.gaitPhase or 0) + delta * math.clamp(3 + speed * 0.4, 3, 15)
        local wave = math.sin(rig.gaitPhase)
        local breath = math.sin(os.clock() * 2.5)
        bend = bend or 0
        for _, entry in ipairs(rig.joints) do
            local name = entry.motor.Name:gsub(" ", "")
            local side = name:find("Right") and 1 or -1
            local swing = wave * side * moving
            local pose = CFrame.new()
            if name == "RightHip" or name == "LeftHip" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(swing * 32 + bend * 28), 0, 0)
            elseif name == "RightShoulder" or name == "LeftShoulder" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-swing * 28 - bend * 20), 0,
                    math.rad(side * (3 + bend * 24 + breath)))
            elseif name == "RightKnee" or name == "LeftKnee" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(math.max(0, -swing) * 38 + bend * 38), 0, 0)
            elseif name == "RightElbow" or name == "LeftElbow" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(7 + math.max(0, swing) * 16), 0, 0)
            elseif name == "RightAnkle" or name == "LeftAnkle" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-swing * 10), 0, 0)
            elseif name == "Waist" or name == "RootJoint" or name == "Root" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(bend * 12), math.rad(wave * moving * 3), 0)
            elseif name == "Neck" then pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-bend * 10 + breath), 0, 0) end
            if rig.humanoid and rig.humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
                local basis = entry.motor.C0.Rotation
                pose = basis:Inverse() * pose * basis
            end
            entry.current = entry.current:Lerp(pose, 1 - math.exp(-13 * math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1)))
            if entry.motor.Parent then entry.motor.Transform = entry.current end
        end
    end
    local function characterRay(target, origin, direction)
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { target.Parent }
        params.IgnoreWater = true
        return world:Raycast(origin, direction, params)
    end
    local function findWall(target)
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not root then return nil end
        local move = target.MoveDirection
        local forward = move.Magnitude > 0.05 and move.Unit or root.CFrame.LookVector
        forward = Vector3.new(forward.X, 0, forward.Z)
        if forward.Magnitude < 0.01 then forward = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
        forward = forward.Unit
        local right = Vector3.new(-forward.Z, 0, forward.X)
        local best, nearest
        for _, direction in ipairs({ forward, right, right * -1, forward * -1,
            (forward + right).Unit, (forward - right).Unit,
            (forward * -1 + right).Unit, (forward * -1 - right).Unit }) do
            local hit = characterRay(target, root.Position, direction * 4)
            if hit and math.abs(hit.Normal.Y) < 0.35 then
                local distance = (hit.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if not nearest or distance < nearest then best, nearest = hit, distance end
            end
        end
        return best
    end
    local function clearSurfaceMode(state)
        local rig = state.surfaceRig
        if not rig then return end
        state.surfaceRig = nil
        destroyMotion(rig.motion)
        restoreJoints(rig.joints)
        if rig.root.Parent then
            local position = rig.root.Position
            if state.key == "HeadWalk" and rig.floorY and rig.humanoid.Health > 0 then
                position = Vector3.new(position.X, rig.floorY + feetOffset(rig.humanoid, rig.root) + 0.1, position.Z)
            end
            if rig.humanoid.Health > 0 then rig.root.CFrame = CFrame.new(position) * uprightRotation(rig.root) end
            rig.root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            rig.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        releaseNoclip(rig)
        if rig.humanoid.Parent then
            rig.humanoid.PlatformStand = rig.platformStand
            rig.humanoid.AutoRotate = rig.autoRotate
            if rig.humanoid.Health > 0 and not rig.platformStand then
                rig.humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
        end
        state.statusMessage = nil
    end
    local function ensureSurfaceMode(state, target)
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not root or root.Anchored or target.SeatPart then return end
        if state.surfaceRig and state.surfaceRig.root ~= root then clearSurfaceMode(state) end
        if state.surfaceRig then return end
        local hit
        if state.key == "WallWalk" then
            hit = findWall(target)
            if not hit then state.statusMessage = "APPROACH A WALL"; return end
        else
            hit = characterRay(target, root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Vector3.new(0, -128, 0))
            if not hit or hit.Normal.Y < 0.5 then state.statusMessage = "FIND A FLOOR"; return end
        end
        local head = target.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local headDistance = head and math.abs((head.Position - root.Position):Dot(root.CFrame.UpVector)) + head.Size.Y * 0.5 or 2
        local rig = { root = root, humanoid = target, motion = makeMotion(root, "Quick" .. state.key),
            joints = captureJoints(target.Parent), platformStand = target.PlatformStand,
            autoRotate = target.AutoRotate, normal = hit.Normal, lastHit = os.clock(),
            headDistance = math.max(1.2, headDistance), floorY = hit.Position.Y }
        state.surfaceRig = rig
        acquireNoclip(rig, target.Parent)
        target.PlatformStand = true
        target.AutoRotate = false
        target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
        state.statusMessage = nil
    end
    local function planarInput(target)
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        local look = camera and camera.CFrame.LookVector or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        local forward = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if forward.Magnitude < 0.01 then forward = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
        forward = forward.Unit
        local right = Vector3.new(-forward.Z, 0, forward.X)
        local f, s = target.MoveDirection:Dot(forward), target.MoveDirection:Dot(right)
        if keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then f = 1 end
        if keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then f = -1 end
        if keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then s = -1 end
        if keyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then s = 1 end
        return f, s, forward, right
    end
    local function stepSurfaceMode(state, target, delta)
        if not state or not state.enabled then return end
        ensureSurfaceMode(state, target)
        local rig = state.surfaceRig
        if not rig then return end
        local root, hit = rig.root, nil
        local desired, look, up, normalVelocity
        local f, s, cameraForward, cameraRight = planarInput(target)
        if state.key == "WallWalk" then
            hit = characterRay(target, root.Position, rig.normal * -(feetOffset(target, root) + 4))
            if not hit or math.abs(hit.Normal.Y) >= 0.35 then hit = findWall(target) end
            if not hit then
                if os.clock() - rig.lastHit > 0.2 then clearSurfaceMode(state) end
                return
            end
            rig.lastHit, rig.normal = os.clock(), hit.Normal
            local wallUp = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) - rig.normal * rig.normal.Y
            wallUp = wallUp.Unit
            local wallRight = wallUp:Cross(rig.normal).Unit
            desired = wallUp * f + wallRight * s
            look = desired.Magnitude > 0.05 and desired.Unit or wallUp
            up = rig.normal
            local distance = (root.Position - hit.Position):Dot(rig.normal)
            normalVelocity = rig.normal * math.clamp((feetOffset(target, root) - distance) * 16, -45, 45)
        else
            hit = characterRay(target, root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Vector3.new(0, -128, 0))
            if not hit or hit.Normal.Y < 0.5 then clearSurfaceMode(state); return end
            rig.floorY = hit.Position.Y
            desired = cameraForward * f + cameraRight * s
            look = desired.Magnitude > 0.05 and desired.Unit or cameraForward
            up = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0)
            normalVelocity = Vector3.new(0,
                math.clamp((hit.Position.Y + rig.headDistance - root.Position.Y) * 16, -55, 55), 0)
        end
        if desired.Magnitude > 1 then desired = desired.Unit end
        acquireNoclip(rig, target.Parent)
        local speed = target.WalkSpeed
        rig.motion.velocity.VectorVelocity = desired * speed + normalVelocity
        local goal = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, look, up)
        rig.motion.orientation.CFrame = rig.motion.orientation.CFrame:Lerp(goal, 1 - math.exp(-10 * math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1)))
        walkingGait(rig, desired.Magnitude * speed, delta)
    end
    local function performWallHop()
        local state = byKey.WallHop
        local target = currentHumanoid()
        if not alive or not state or not state.enabled or not target or target.Health <= 0 or target.SeatPart then return false end
        if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return false end
        if (byKey.GroundSink and byKey.GroundSink.ground) or (byKey.HeadWalk and byKey.HeadWalk.enabled) then return false end
        for _, key in ipairs(movementGroup) do
            if byKey[key] and byKey[key].enabled and byKey[key].kind then return false end
        end
        local now = os.clock()
        if state.lastHop and now - state.lastHop < 0.14 then return false end
        local hit = findWall(target)
        if not hit then return false end
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not root then return false end
        state.lastHop = now
        if byKey.WallWalk and byKey.WallWalk.enabled then byKey.WallWalk.setEnabled(false, true) end
        local power = target.UseJumpPower and target.JumpPower or math.sqrt(math.max(0, 2 * world.Gravity * target.JumpHeight))
        local tangent = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity - hit.Normal * root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Dot(hit.Normal)
        tangent = Vector3.new(tangent.X, 0, tangent.Z) * 0.35
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = tangent + hit.Normal * 26 + Vector3.new(0, math.max(50, power), 0)
        target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
        return true
    end

    -- Underground movement uses a free X/Z velocity controller, not a permanently
    -- anchored character. A surface-level camera subject preserves normal orbit/zoom.
    local function prepareGround(target)
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not root or root.Anchored or target.SeatPart then return nil end
        local hit = characterRay(target, root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Vector3.new(0, -1000, 0))
        if not hit or hit.Normal.Y < 0.35 then return nil end
        local surfaceY = hit.Position.Y + feetOffset(target, root) + 0.1
        local surface = CFrame.new(root.Position.X, surfaceY, root.Position.Z) * uprightRotation(root)
        return { surface = surface, surfaceY = surfaceY, destination = surface + Vector3.new(0, -8, 0) }
    end
    local function updateGroundCamera(ground)
        if not ground.proxy or not ground.proxy.Parent or not ground.root.Parent then return end
        local root = ground.root
        ground.proxy.Position = Vector3.new(root.Position.X, ground.surfaceY + 1.5, root.Position.Z)
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        if not ground.cameras[camera] then
            ground.cameras[camera] = { subject = camera.CameraSubject, cameraType = camera.CameraType, cframe = camera.CFrame }
        end
        camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        camera.CameraSubject = ground.proxy
    end
    local function cancelGroundTween(ground)
        if ground.tweenConnection then ground.tweenConnection:Disconnect(); ground.tweenConnection = nil end
        if ground.tween then ground.tween:Cancel(); ground.tween = nil end
    end
    local function finishGround(state, returnToSurface)
        local ground = state.ground
        if not ground then return end
        state.ground = nil
        cancelGroundTween(ground)
        destroyMotion(ground.motion)
        local root, target = ground.root, ground.humanoid
        if root.Parent then
            root.Anchored = true
            if returnToSurface and target.Health > 0 then
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position.X, ground.surfaceY, root.Position.Z) * uprightRotation(root)
            end
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.Anchored = ground.anchored
        end
        restoreJoints(ground.joints)
        releaseNoclip(ground)
        if target.Parent then
            target.PlatformStand = ground.platformStand
            target.AutoRotate = ground.autoRotate
            if target.Health > 0 and not ground.platformStand then target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end
        end
        for camera, saved in pairs(ground.cameras) do
            pcall(function()
                local subject = saved.subject
                camera.CameraSubject = subject and subject.Parent and subject or target
                camera.CameraType = saved.cameraType
                if saved.cameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then camera.CFrame = saved.cframe end
            end)
        end
        if ground.proxy then ground.proxy:Destroy() end
        state.pendingGround = nil
        state.statusMessage = nil
    end
    local function beginGroundReturn(state)
        local ground = state.ground
        if not ground or ground.phase == "exiting" then return end
        if not ground.root.Parent or ground.humanoid.Health <= 0 then finishGround(state, false); return end
        cancelGroundTween(ground)
        ground.phase, ground.animStarted = "exiting", os.clock()
        state.statusMessage = "RETURNING TO SURFACE"
        ground.root.Anchored = true
        ground.motion.velocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
        ground.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        local destination = CFrame.new(ground.root.Position.X, ground.surfaceY, ground.root.Position.Z) * uprightRotation(ground.root)
        ground.tween = TweenService:Create(ground.root,
            TweenInfo.new(0.85, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), { CFrame = destination })
        ground.tweenConnection = ground.tween.Completed:Connect(function(playback)
            if state.ground == ground and ground.phase == "exiting" and playback == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
                finishGround(state, true)
            end
        end)
        ground.tween:Play()
    end
    local function applyGround(state, target)
        if state.ground then return end
        local root = getRoot(target)
        local destination = state.pendingGround or prepareGround(target)
        if not root or not destination then state.statusMessage = "NO GROUND BELOW"; return end
        local ground = { root = root, humanoid = target, surfaceY = destination.surfaceY,
            surface = destination.surface, anchored = root.Anchored, platformStand = target.PlatformStand,
            autoRotate = target.AutoRotate, cameras = {}, joints = captureJoints(target.Parent),
            phase = "entering", animStarted = os.clock(), motion = makeMotion(root, "QuickUnderground") }
        state.ground = ground
        state.pendingGround = nil
        acquireNoclip(ground, target.Parent)
        local proxy = Instance.new("Part")
        proxy.Name = "QuickUndergroundSurfaceCamera"
        proxy.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        proxy.Anchored = true
        proxy.CanCollide = false
        proxy.CanTouch = false
        proxy.CanQuery = false
        proxy.Transparency = 1
        proxy.CastShadow = false
        proxy.Parent = world
        ground.proxy = proxy
        updateGroundCamera(ground)
        target.PlatformStand = true
        target.AutoRotate = false
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.Anchored = true
        state.statusMessage = "ENTERING GROUND"
        ground.tween = TweenService:Create(root,
            TweenInfo.new(0.85, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), { CFrame = destination.destination })
        ground.tweenConnection = ground.tween.Completed:Connect(function(playback)
            if state.ground == ground and state.enabled and ground.phase == "entering" and playback == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
                ground.phase = "walking"
                root.Anchored = false
                target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
                state.statusMessage = "UNDERGROUND / MOVE"
            end
        end)
        ground.tween:Play()
    end
    local function stepGround(state, delta, time)
        local ground = state and state.ground
        if not ground or not ground.root.Parent then return end
        acquireNoclip(ground, ground.humanoid.Parent)
        local root, target = ground.root, ground.humanoid
        local bend, speed = 0, 0
        if ground.phase == "walking" and state.enabled then
            local hit = characterRay(target,
                Vector3.new(root.Position.X, ground.surfaceY + 12, root.Position.Z), Vector3.new(0, -128, 0))
            if hit and hit.Normal.Y > 0.35 then
                local desiredY = hit.Position.Y + feetOffset(target, root) + 0.1
                ground.surfaceY = ground.surfaceY + (desiredY - ground.surfaceY) * (1 - math.exp(-8 * math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1)))
            end
            local f, s, forward, right = planarInput(target)
            local move = forward * f + right * s
            if move.Magnitude > 1 then move = move.Unit end
            speed = move.Magnitude * target.WalkSpeed
            local correction = math.clamp((ground.surfaceY - 8 - root.Position.Y) * 16, -65, 65)
            ground.motion.velocity.VectorVelocity = move * target.WalkSpeed + Vector3.new(0, correction, 0)
            local facing = move.Magnitude > 0.05 and move.Unit or forward
            ground.motion.orientation.CFrame = ground.motion.orientation.CFrame:Lerp(
                CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, facing, Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)), 1 - math.exp(-10 * math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1)))
        else
            local progress = math.clamp((time - ground.animStarted) / 0.85, 0, 1)
            bend = math.sin(progress * math.pi)
        end
        walkingGait(ground, speed, delta, bend)
        updateGroundCamera(ground)
    end

    -- Extra movement modes own their instances and restore every property they change.
    local performTeleport

    local function safeVoidY(value)
        local killY = finiteNumber(world.FallenPartsDestroyHeight) or -500
        return math.max(value, killY + 25)
    end

    local function restoreExtra(state, characterOnly, animateGround)
        if QuickPlus and QuickPlus.restore(state, characterOnly) then return true end
        if state.key == "ClickTP" then
            disconnectList(state.toolConnections)
            state.toolConnections = nil
            if state.tool then state.tool:Destroy(); state.tool = nil end
            state.toolMouse = nil
            state.statusMessage = nil
            if QuickPlus then QuickPlus.releaseTool(state) end
            return true
        elseif state.key == "SelfFling" or state.key == "Spin" then
            local spin = state.spin
            state.spin = nil
            if spin then
                spin.orientation:Destroy()
                spin.attachment:Destroy()
                if spin.root.Parent then
                    spin.root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    if spin.humanoid.Health > 0 then spin.root.CFrame = CFrame.new(spin.root.Position) * uprightRotation(spin.root) end
                end
                if spin.humanoid.Parent then
                    spin.humanoid.AutoRotate = spin.autoRotate
                    spin.humanoid.PlatformStand = spin.platformStand
                end
            end
            return true
        elseif state.key == "AntiFling" then
            for part, original in pairs(state.colliders or {}) do
                if part.Parent then part.CanCollide = original end
            end
            state.colliders = nil
            state.safeCFrame = nil
            return true
        elseif state.key == "AntiVoid" then
            if state.voidPlatform then state.voidPlatform:Destroy(); state.voidPlatform = nil end
            return true
        elseif state.key == "GroundSink" then
            if animateGround and not characterOnly and alive and state.ground then beginGroundReturn(state)
            else finishGround(state, true) end
            state.pendingGround = nil
            return true
        elseif state.key == "Noclip" then
            releaseNoclip(state)
            return true
        elseif state.key == "WallWalk" or state.key == "HeadWalk" then
            clearSurfaceMode(state)
            return true
        elseif state.key == "WallHop" then
            state.lastHop = nil
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    local function ensureTpTool(state)
        if state.tool and state.tool.Parent then return end
        disconnectList(state.toolConnections)
        if state.tool then state.tool:Destroy() end
        local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if not backpack then state.statusMessage = "WAITING FOR BACKPACK"; return end
        if QuickPlus then QuickPlus.acquireTool(state) end
        local tool = Instance.new("Tool")
        tool.Name = "TP Tool"
        tool.ToolTip = "Equip, then click or tap a world surface to teleport instantly."
        tool.RequiresHandle = false
        tool.CanBeDropped = false
        tool.Enabled = true
        state.tool = tool
        state.statusMessage = "EQUIP TP TOOL"
        state.toolConnections = {
            tool.Equipped:Connect(function(mouse)
                state.toolMouse = mouse or LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
                state.statusMessage = "TOOL EQUIPPED"
                if state.repaint then state.repaint() end
            end),
            tool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
                state.toolMouse = nil
                state.statusMessage = "EQUIP TP TOOL"
                if state.repaint then state.repaint() end
            end),
            tool.Activated:Connect(function()
                if not alive or not state.enabled or tool.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then return end
                local mouse = state.toolMouse or LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
                if not mouse or not mouse.Target then return end
                -- Do not teleport through this UI when a menu/button covers the pointer.
                if mouse.X and mouse.Y then
                    for _, frame in ipairs({ MainWindow, FloatingPill, MainGui:FindFirstChild("QuickVerticalControls") }) do
                        if frame.Visible then
                            local pos, size = frame.AbsolutePosition, frame.AbsoluteSize
                            if mouse.X >= pos.X and mouse.X <= pos.X + size.X and
                               mouse.Y >= pos.Y and mouse.Y <= pos.Y + size.Y then return end
                        end
                    end
                end
                local params = RaycastParams.new()
                params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character }
                local ray = mouse.UnitRay
                local hit = ray and world:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 50000, params)
                if not hit then
                    hit = { Position = mouse.Hit.Position, Normal = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) }
                end
                performTeleport(hit)
            end)
        }
        tool.Parent = backpack
    end

    -- Upright self-spin: no AngularVelocity actuator, no PlatformStand, no forced
    -- falling state. Humanoid translation and the normal Animate script remain active.
    local function applySpin(state, target, delta)
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not root or target.SeatPart then
            if state.spin then restoreExtra(state) end
            return
        end
        if state.spin and state.spin.root ~= root then restoreExtra(state) end
        if not state.spin then
            local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
            attachment.Name = "QuickUprightSpinAttachment"
            attachment.Parent = root
            local orientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
            orientation.Name = "QuickUprightSpin"
            orientation.Attachment0 = attachment
            orientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
            orientation.MaxTorque = 100000000
            orientation.Responsiveness = 55
            orientation.RigidityEnabled = false
            orientation.CFrame = uprightRotation(root)
            orientation.Parent = root
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            local previousPlatformStand = target.PlatformStand
            local currentState = target:GetState()
            if previousPlatformStand or currentState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or
               currentState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or currentState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then
                target.PlatformStand = false
                target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
            state.spin = { root = root, humanoid = target, autoRotate = target.AutoRotate,
                platformStand = previousPlatformStand,
                attachment = attachment, orientation = orientation, rotation = uprightRotation(root), angle = 0 }
        end
        local spin = state.spin
        if state.value <= 0 then
            spin.orientation.Enabled = false
            target.AutoRotate = spin.autoRotate
            spin.rotation, spin.angle = uprightRotation(root), 0
            return
        end
        spin.orientation.Enabled = true
        target.AutoRotate = false
        spin.angle = (spin.angle + math.rad(state.value) * math.clamp(delta or 0, 0, 0.1)) % (2 * math.pi)
        spin.orientation.MaxAngularVelocity = math.rad(state.value) * 1.2 + 2
        spin.orientation.CFrame = spin.rotation * CFrame.Angles(0, spin.angle, 0)
    end

    local function scanAntiFling(state, target)
        state.colliders = state.colliders or {}
        local root = getRoot(target)
        local nearby = {}
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local character = player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character
            local otherRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root and otherRoot and (otherRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < 60 then
                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        nearby[part] = true
                        if state.colliders[part] == nil then state.colliders[part] = part.CanCollide end
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for part, original in pairs(state.colliders) do
            if not nearby[part] then
                if part.Parent then part.CanCollide = original end
                state.colliders[part] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function stepAntiFling(state, target, delta)
        if not state or not state.enabled then return end
        if byKey.GroundSink and byKey.GroundSink.ground then return end
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not root or root.Anchored or target.SeatPart then return end
        state.scanElapsed = (state.scanElapsed or 1) + delta
        if state.scanElapsed >= 0.15 then state.scanElapsed = 0; scanAntiFling(state, target) end
        local limit = math.max(200, target.WalkSpeed * 2 + 100,
            (target.UseJumpPower and target.JumpPower or 50) * 2 + 100)
        for _, key in ipairs(movementGroup) do
            local mode = byKey[key]
            if mode and mode.enabled and mode.value then limit = math.max(limit, mode.value * 2 + 100) end
        end
        local linear, angular = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity, root.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        if linear.Magnitude > limit or angular.Magnitude > 50 then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            if state.safeCFrame then root.CFrame = state.safeCFrame end
        else
            state.safeCFrame = root.CFrame
        end
    end

    local function stepAntiVoid(state, target, delta)
        if not state or not state.enabled or (byKey.GroundSink and byKey.GroundSink.ground) then return end
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not root or root.Anchored or target.SeatPart then return end
        state.value = safeVoidY(state.value)
        if not state.voidPlatform or not state.voidPlatform.Parent then
            local platform = Instance.new("Part")
            platform.Name = "QuickAntiVoidFloor"
            platform.Size = Vector3.new(128, 1, 128)
            platform.Anchored = true
            platform.Transparency = 1
            platform.CanCollide = true
            platform.CanTouch = false
            platform.CanQuery = false
            platform.CastShadow = false
            platform.Parent = world
            state.voidPlatform = platform
        end
        local floorY = state.value - feetOffset(target, root)
        state.voidPlatform.Position = Vector3.new(root.Position.X, floorY - 0.5, root.Position.Z)
        local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local dt = math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.25)
        local projectedY = root.Position.Y + velocity.Y * dt - 0.5 * world.Gravity * dt * dt
        if root.Position.Y < state.value or (velocity.Y < 0 and projectedY <= state.value) then
            root.CFrame = root.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, state.value - root.Position.Y, 0)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, math.max(0, velocity.Y), velocity.Z)
            if byKey.AntiFling then byKey.AntiFling.safeCFrame = root.CFrame end
        end
    end

    local function restoreFeature(state, characterOnly, animateGround)
        if state.key == "Gravity" then
            if not characterOnly and state.originalGravity ~= nil then
                pcall(function() world.Gravity = state.originalGravity end)
                state.originalGravity = nil
            end
            return
        end
        if restoreExtra(state, characterOnly, animateGround) then return end
        clearController(state)
        disconnectList(state.sitConnections)
        state.sitConnections = nil
        if state.platform then state.platform:Destroy(); state.platform = nil end
        state.floorY, state.accumulatedSpeed = nil, nil
        local target, original = state.originalHumanoid, state.original
        state.originalHumanoid, state.original = nil, nil
        state.statusMessage = nil
        if not target or not target.Parent or not original then return end
        pcall(function()
            if state.key == "WalkSpeed" or state.key == "AcceleratingWalk" then
                target.WalkSpeed = original.WalkSpeed
            elseif state.key == "JumpPower" then
                target.JumpPower = original.JumpPower
                target.UseJumpPower = original.UseJumpPower
            elseif state.key == "AntiSit" then
                target:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, original.SeatedEnabled)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function enableAntiSit(state, target)
        if state.originalHumanoid == target and state.sitConnections then return end
        restoreFeature(state)
        state.originalHumanoid = target
        state.original = { SeatedEnabled = target:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated) }
        local recovering = false
        local function rejectSeat()
            if recovering or not alive or not state.enabled or not target.Parent or target.Health <= 0 then return end
            recovering = true
            target:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
            if target.Sit or target.SeatPart then
                target.Sit = false
                target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
            recovering = false
        end
        state.sitConnections = {
            target:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Sit"):Connect(rejectSeat),
            target.Seated:Connect(rejectSeat),
            target.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
                if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated then rejectSeat() end
            end)
        }
        rejectSeat()
    end

    local function createPlatform(state, target)
        if state.platform and state.platform.Parent then return end
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not root then return end
        state.floorY = root.Position.Y - feetOffset(target, root)
        local platform = Instance.new("Part")
        platform.Name = "DeathNote_Quick_" .. state.key
        platform.Size = Vector3.new(48, 1, 48)
        platform.Anchored = true
        platform.Transparency = 1
        platform.CanCollide = true
        platform.CanTouch = false
        platform.CanQuery = false
        platform.CastShadow = false
        platform.Position = Vector3.new(root.Position.X, state.floorY - 0.5, root.Position.Z)
        platform.Parent = world
        state.platform = platform
    end

    local function createController(state, target)
        if state.rig and target.RigType ~= state.rig then
            clearController(state)
            state.statusMessage = "REQUIRES " .. (state.rig == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 and "R6" or "R15")
            return
        end
        local vehicle = state.kind == "vehicle"
        local seat = vehicle and target.SeatPart or nil
        local root = vehicle and seat and (seat.AssemblyRootPart or seat) or (not vehicle and getRoot(target))
        if not root or not root.Parent or not root:IsA("BasePart") then
            clearController(state)
            state.statusMessage = vehicle and "SIT IN A VEHICLE" or "WAITING FOR ROOT"
            return
        end
        if root.Anchored then
            clearController(state)
            state.statusMessage = "ASSEMBLY IS ANCHORED"
            return
        end
        local old = state.controller
        if old and old.root == root and old.humanoid == target and old.seat == seat then
            state.statusMessage = nil
            return
        end
        clearController(state)
        local controller = { root = root, humanoid = target, seat = seat, instances = {}, joints = {},
            started = os.clock(), cruise = 0, smoothedVelocity = Vector3.zero }
        state.controller = controller
        local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
        attachment.Name = "QuickMovementAttachment"
        attachment.Parent = root
        table.insert(controller.instances, attachment)
        local velocity = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
        velocity.Name = "QuickMovementVelocity"
        velocity.Attachment0 = attachment
        velocity.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
        velocity.VelocityConstraintMode = Enum.VelocityConstraintMode.Vector
        velocity.MaxForce = 100000000
        velocity.ForceLimitsEnabled = false
        velocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
        velocity.Parent = root
        table.insert(controller.instances, velocity)
        local orientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
        orientation.Name = "QuickMovementOrientation"
        orientation.Attachment0 = attachment
        orientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
        orientation.MaxTorque = 100000000
        orientation.Responsiveness = 25
        orientation.RigidityEnabled = false
        orientation.CFrame = root.CFrame.Rotation
        orientation.Parent = root
        table.insert(controller.instances, orientation)
        controller.velocity, controller.orientation = velocity, orientation
        if not vehicle then
            controller.flags = { PlatformStand = target.PlatformStand, AutoRotate = target.AutoRotate }
            target.AutoRotate = false
            target.PlatformStand = state.kind == "fly"
            target:ChangeState(state.kind == "swim" and Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming or Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
            for _, item in ipairs(target.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
                if item:IsA("Motor6D") then
                    table.insert(controller.joints, { motor = item, original = item.Transform })
                end
            end
        end
        state.statusMessage = nil
    end

    -- Continuous pose animation, not a fixed statue pose. No third-party asset IDs.
    local function proceduralPose(state, time, delta)
        local controller = state.controller
        if not controller then return end
        local age = time - controller.started
        local cruise = controller.cruise or 0
        local swim = state.kind == "swim"
        local superman = state.key == "FlyR15" and state.style == "Superman"
        local ironman = state.key == "FlyR15" and state.style == "Iron Man"
        local breath = math.sin(age * 2.4)
        local wave = math.sin(age * (swim and (4 + cruise * 3) or 4.2))
