        local turn = controller.turn or 0
        local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-11 * math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1))
        for _, entry in ipairs(controller.joints) do
            local name = entry.motor.Name:gsub(" ", "")
            local side = name:find("Right") and 1 or -1
            local pose = CFrame.new()
            if name == "RightShoulder" or name == "LeftShoulder" then
                if swim then
                    pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(135 + wave * side * (22 + cruise * 28)),
                        math.rad(side * breath * 4), math.rad(side * 14))
                elseif superman then
                    local reach = side == 1 and (155 + cruise * 20) or (28 + cruise * 130)
                    pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(reach + breath * 2.5 + wave * side * 1.5),
                        math.rad(turn * 7), math.rad(side * (-7 + breath * 1.2)))
                elseif ironman then
                    pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-12 - cruise * 25 + breath * 4),
                        math.rad(side * (7 + breath * 2)), math.rad(side * (18 - cruise * 8)))
                else
                    pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(28 + wave * side * 7), 0,
                        math.rad(side * (40 + breath * 4)))
                end
            elseif name == "RightElbow" or name == "LeftElbow" then
                local bend = swim and (25 + math.sin(age * 5 + side) * 20) or
                    (superman and (side == 1 and 5 or 20 + (1 - cruise) * 25)) or
                    (ironman and (20 - cruise * 10 + breath * 3)) or 8
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(bend), 0, 0)
            elseif name == "RightWrist" or name == "LeftWrist" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(ironman and (-28 + breath * 3) or -7),
                    0, math.rad(ironman and side * 10 or 0))
            elseif name == "RightHip" or name == "LeftHip" then
                local kick = swim and wave * side * (12 + cruise * 18) or
                    ((ironman and 8 or -5) * (1 - cruise) + wave * side * 2.5)
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(kick), math.rad(turn * 3), math.rad(side * (3 + breath)))
            elseif name == "RightKnee" or name == "LeftKnee" then
                local kick = swim and math.max(0, wave * side) * 35 or
                    (1 - cruise) * (side == 1 and 8 or 20) + breath * side * 3
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(kick), 0, 0)
            elseif name == "RightAnkle" or name == "LeftAnkle" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-8 + cruise * 12 + breath * 2), 0, 0)
            elseif name == "Neck" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad((swim or superman) and (-10 - cruise * 12 + breath * 2) or breath * 2),
                    math.rad(turn * -6), 0)
            elseif name == "Waist" then
                pose = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-cruise * 5 + breath * 1.8),
                    math.rad(turn * 5), math.rad(swim and wave * 3 or breath))
            elseif name == "RootJoint" or name == "Root" then
                pose = CFrame.new(0, breath * 0.055 * (1 - cruise), 0) *
                    CFrame.Angles(math.rad(breath * 1.2), 0, math.rad(turn * 2))
            end
            entry.current = (entry.current or entry.original):Lerp(pose, alpha)
            if entry.motor.Parent then entry.motor.Transform = entry.current end
        end
    end

    local function applyFeature(state)
        if not alive or not state.enabled then return end
        if QuickPlus and QuickPlus.apply(state, currentHumanoid()) then return end
        if state.key == "Gravity" then
            if state.originalGravity == nil then state.originalGravity = world.Gravity end
            world.Gravity = state.value / METRES_PER_STUD
            return
        end
        if state.key == "ClickTP" then ensureTpTool(state); return end
        local target = currentHumanoid()
        if not target or target.Health <= 0 then return end
        if state.key == "UnlimitedJump" or state.key == "WallHop" then return end
        if state.key == "Noclip" then acquireNoclip(state, target.Parent); return end
        if state.key == "WallWalk" or state.key == "HeadWalk" then ensureSurfaceMode(state, target); return end
        if state.key == "SelfFling" or state.key == "Spin" then applySpin(state, target); return end
        if state.key == "GroundSink" then applyGround(state, target); return end
        if state.key == "AntiVoid" then stepAntiVoid(state, target, 0); return end
        if state.key == "AntiFling" then
            if not state.safeCFrame then local root = getRoot(target); state.safeCFrame = root and root.CFrame end
            scanAntiFling(state, target)
            return
        end
        if state.key == "AntiSit" then enableAntiSit(state, target); return end
        if state.kind then createController(state, target); return end
        if state.key == "SkyWalk" then createPlatform(state, target); return end
        if state.originalHumanoid ~= target then
            restoreFeature(state)
            state.originalHumanoid = target
            if state.key == "WalkSpeed" or state.key == "AcceleratingWalk" then
                state.original = { WalkSpeed = target.WalkSpeed }
            else
                state.original = { JumpPower = target.JumpPower, UseJumpPower = target.UseJumpPower }
            end
        end
        if state.key == "WalkSpeed" then
            target.WalkSpeed = state.value
        elseif state.key == "JumpPower" then
            target.UseJumpPower = true
            target.JumpPower = state.value
        elseif state.key == "AcceleratingWalk" then
            if state.accumulatedSpeed == nil then state.accumulatedSpeed = target.WalkSpeed end
            target.WalkSpeed = state.accumulatedSpeed
        end
    end

    local function stepPlatform(state, target, delta)
        if not state or not state.enabled then return end
        applyFeature(state)
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if state.key == "AntiFall" and QuickPlus then QuickPlus.syncFall(state, target, delta); return end
        if not root or not state.platform or not state.floorY then return end
        if state.key == "SkyWalk" then
            local changeY = verticalInput() * 20 * math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.25)
            state.floorY = state.floorY + changeY
            -- Translate both player and support, including when Gravity is zero.
            if changeY ~= 0 then root.CFrame = root.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, changeY, 0) end
        end
        state.platform.Position = Vector3.new(root.Position.X, state.floorY - 0.5, root.Position.Z)
        local minimumY = state.floorY + feetOffset(target, root)
        if root.Position.Y < minimumY - 0.35 then
            local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            root.CFrame = root.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, minimumY - root.Position.Y, 0)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, math.max(0, velocity.Y), velocity.Z)
        end
    end

    local function stepController(state, target, time, delta)
        if not state or not state.enabled then return end
        applyFeature(state)
        local controller = state.controller
        if not controller then return end
        local direction = flightDirection(target)
        if state.kind == "vehicle" and controller.seat and direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then
            local seat = controller.seat
            if seat:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
                direction = seat.CFrame.LookVector * seat.ThrottleFloat + seat.CFrame.RightVector * seat.SteerFloat
                if direction.Magnitude > 1 then direction = direction.Unit end
            end
        end
        local dt = math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.1)
        local cruiseAlpha = 1 - math.exp(-6 * dt)
        controller.cruise = controller.cruise + (direction.Magnitude - controller.cruise) * cruiseAlpha
        controller.smoothedVelocity = controller.smoothedVelocity:Lerp(direction * state.value, 1 - math.exp(-9 * dt))
        controller.velocity.VectorVelocity = controller.smoothedVelocity
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        local facing = camera and camera.CFrame.LookVector or controller.root.CFrame.LookVector
        local flat = Vector3.new(facing.X, 0, facing.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude < 0.01 then flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
        flat = flat.Unit
        local right = Vector3.new(-flat.Z, 0, flat.X)
        local turn = controller.lastFacing and controller.lastFacing:Dot(right) / math.max(dt, 0.008) * 0.2 or 0
        controller.turn = math.clamp(turn + direction:Dot(right) * 0.5, -1, 1)
        controller.lastFacing = flat
        local cruise, tilt = controller.cruise, 0
        if state.kind == "swim" then tilt = -65 - cruise * 17
        elseif state.key == "FlyR15" then
            tilt = state.style == "Superman" and (-18 - cruise * 54) or (-5 - cruise * 28)
        elseif state.kind == "fly" then tilt = -8 - cruise * 40 end
        local takeoff = 1 - math.exp(-(time - controller.started) * 4)
        local bank = state.kind == "vehicle" and 0 or controller.turn * -14
        local goal = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, flat) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(tilt * takeoff), 0, math.rad(bank))
        controller.orientation.CFrame = controller.orientation.CFrame:Lerp(goal, 1 - math.exp(-8 * dt))
        if state.kind == "swim" then target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming) end
        if state.kind ~= "vehicle" then proceduralPose(state, time, delta) end
    end

    local function performUnlimitedJump()
        local state = byKey.UnlimitedJump
        if byKey.GroundSink and byKey.GroundSink.ground then return end
        if (byKey.HeadWalk and byKey.HeadWalk.enabled) or (byKey.WallWalk and byKey.WallWalk.enabled) then return end
        if not alive or not state or not state.enabled or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        local target = currentHumanoid()
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not target or target.Health <= 0 or not root then return end
        for _, key in ipairs(movementGroup) do
            if byKey[key] and byKey[key].enabled and byKey[key].kind then return end
        end
        local power = target.UseJumpPower and target.JumpPower or
            math.sqrt(math.max(0, 2 * world.Gravity * target.JumpHeight))
        local boost = math.max(50, power) * 1.25
        target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
        local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, math.max(velocity.Y, boost), velocity.Z)
    end

    local function stepMovement(delta)
        if not alive then return end
        local target = currentHumanoid()
        if not target or target.Health <= 0 then return end
        if QuickPlus and QuickPlus.stepPre(delta) then return end
        local accelerating = byKey.AcceleratingWalk
        if accelerating and accelerating.enabled then
            applyFeature(accelerating)
            if target.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                local nextValue = (accelerating.accumulatedSpeed or target.WalkSpeed) +
                    ACCELERATION_PER_SECOND * math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.25)
                if finiteNumber(nextValue) then
                    accelerating.accumulatedSpeed = nextValue
                    target.WalkSpeed = nextValue
                end
            end
        end
        local antiSit = byKey.AntiSit
        if antiSit and antiSit.enabled then
            applyFeature(antiSit)
            target:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
            if target.Sit then target.Sit = false; target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end
        end
        stepPlatform(byKey.AntiFall, target, delta)
        stepPlatform(byKey.SkyWalk, target, delta)
        local time = os.clock()
        if byKey.ClickTP and byKey.ClickTP.enabled then ensureTpTool(byKey.ClickTP) end
        if byKey.SelfFling and byKey.SelfFling.enabled then applySpin(byKey.SelfFling, target, delta) end
        if byKey.Spin and byKey.Spin.enabled then applySpin(byKey.Spin, target, delta) end
        -- Underground is activated by its native Tool, not by arming its toggle.
        stepGround(byKey.GroundSink, delta, time)
        if byKey.Noclip and byKey.Noclip.enabled then acquireNoclip(byKey.Noclip, target.Parent) end
        stepSurfaceMode(byKey.WallWalk, target, delta)
        stepSurfaceMode(byKey.HeadWalk, target, delta)
        stepAntiFling(byKey.AntiFling, target, delta)
        stepAntiVoid(byKey.AntiVoid, target, delta)
        for _, key in ipairs(movementGroup) do
            local state = byKey[key]
            if state and state.kind then stepController(state, target, time, delta) end
        end
        if QuickPlus then QuickPlus.stepPost() end
    end

    -- An actual Tool invokes this only when equipped and deliberately activated.
    -- No Tween or incremental movement is used for teleportation.
    performTeleport = function(hit)
        local state = byKey.ClickTP
        if not alive or not state or not state.enabled or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        local target = currentHumanoid()
        local root = getRoot(target)
        if not target or target.Health <= 0 or not root or not hit or not hit.Position then return end
        if target.SeatPart then notify("Leave the vehicle before using TP Tool."); return end
        if QuickPlus then QuickPlus.cancelExclusive() end
        for _, key in ipairs({ "SelfFling", "Spin", "WallWalk", "HeadWalk" }) do
            local other = byKey[key]
            if other and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false, true) end
        end
        if byKey.GroundSink and byKey.GroundSink.ground then finishGround(byKey.GroundSink, true) end
        local destination = hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, feetOffset(target, root) + 0.2, 0)
        if hit.Normal and hit.Normal.Y < 0.4 then destination = destination + hit.Normal * 3 end
        local character = target.Parent
        local desiredRoot = CFrame.new(destination) * root.CFrame.Rotation
        local modelOffset = root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(character:GetPivot())
        character:PivotTo(desiredRoot * modelOffset)
        target:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
        target:MoveTo(destination)
        for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end
        for _, key in ipairs(movementGroup) do
            local mode = byKey[key]
            if mode and mode.controller then
                mode.controller.smoothedVelocity = Vector3.zero
                mode.controller.velocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end
        for _, key in ipairs({ "AntiFall", "SkyWalk" }) do
            local support = byKey[key]
            if support and support.enabled then
                if key=="AntiFall" and QuickPlus then QuickPlus.rebaseFall(support,target)
                else restoreFeature(support);applyFeature(support) end
            end
        end
        if byKey.AntiFling then byKey.AntiFling.safeCFrame = root.CFrame end
    end






    -- Native tools / local effects / requested third-party Dex viewing wrapper.
    -- No hidden chat remotes, key injection, or claimed server-only visibility.
    QuickPlus = (function()
        local api = {}
        local Social, Session, Extra
        local toolOwners, originalHotbar = {}, nil
        local keyboard = { W = false, A = false, S = false, D = false }
        local keyPointers, usingVirtual = {}, false
        local windows = {}
        local repeatJob, repeatGeneration = nil, 0
        local MIN_CHAT_INTERVAL, MAX_CHAT_DURATION = 3, 120
        local pulse = 0
        local stopReplay, startReplay, resetHistory, stepReplay

        function api.acquireTool(state)
            if toolOwners[state] then return end
            if next(toolOwners) == nil then
                pcall(function()
                    local starter = game:GetService("StarterGui")
                    originalHotbar = starter:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack)
                    starter:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack, true)
                end)
            end
            toolOwners[state] = true
        end
        function api.releaseTool(state)
            toolOwners[state] = nil
            if next(toolOwners) == nil and originalHotbar ~= nil then
                pcall(function() game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack, originalHotbar) end)
                originalHotbar = nil
            end
        end
        local function button(parent, title, size, position, callback, accent)
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Text, b.Size, b.Position = title, size, position
            b.BackgroundColor3 = accent or Color3.fromRGB(48, 43, 70)
            b.TextColor3, b.Font, b.TextSize = colors.White, Enum.Font.GothamBold, 11
            b.AutoButtonColor, b.BorderSizePixel = false, 0
            b.Parent = parent
            RedUI.Corner(b, 7)
            if callback then b.Activated:Connect(callback) end
            return b
        end
        local function inputBox(parent, text, size, position, multiline)
            local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
            box.Text, box.Size, box.Position = text, size, position
            box.ClearTextOnFocus, box.MultiLine = false, multiline or false
            box.BackgroundColor3, box.TextColor3 = colors.Field, colors.White
            box.Font, box.TextSize = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 12
            box.BorderSizePixel, box.TextWrapped = 0, multiline or false
            box.Parent = parent
            RedUI.Corner(box, 7)
            RedUI.Stroke(box, colors.Track, 1)
            return box
        end
        local function dragPanel(handle, panel)
            local pointer, start, original
            connect(handle.InputBegan, function(event)
                if event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    pointer, start, original = event, event.Position, panel.Position
                end
            end)
            connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(event)
                if not pointer or not panel.Visible then return end
                local touch = pointer.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and event == pointer
                local mouse = pointer.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                if touch or mouse then
                    local delta = event.Position - start
                    panel.Position = UDim2.new(original.X.Scale, original.X.Offset + delta.X, original.Y.Scale, original.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
                end
            end)
            connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(event)
                if event == pointer then pointer = nil end
            end)
        end
        local function panel(name, title, width, height, close)
            local root = newFrame(MainGui, name, UDim2.new(0, width, 0, height),
                UDim2.new(0.5, -width / 2, 0.5, -height / 2), Color3.fromRGB(19, 20, 33))
            root.ZIndex, root.Visible = 70, false
            root.AnchorPoint, root.Position = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            RedUI.Corner(root, 12)
            RedUI.Stroke(root, colors.Cyan, 1.4)
            local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
            local camera = world.CurrentCamera
            local view = camera and camera.ViewportSize
            scale.Scale = view and math.clamp(math.min(1, (view.X - 24) / width, (view.Y - 24) / height), 0.3, 1) or 1
            scale.Parent = root
            local top = newFrame(root, "TitleBar", UDim2.new(1, -42, 0, 32), UDim2.new(), Color3.fromRGB(30, 29, 48))
            label(top, title, UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0), 11, colors.Cyan, true)
            button(root, "X", UDim2.new(0, 27, 0, 25), UDim2.new(1, -33, 0, 4), close)
            dragPanel(top, root)
            windows[name] = root
            return root
        end
        local function clearKeys()
            keyboard.W, keyboard.A, keyboard.S, keyboard.D = false, false, false, false
            keyPointers = {}
            if usingVirtual then
                local target = currentHumanoid()
                if target then target:Move(Vector3.zero, true) end
                usingVirtual = false
            end
        end
        local function destroyTool(state)
            disconnectList(state.toolConnections)
            state.toolConnections = nil
            if state.tool then state.tool:Destroy(); state.tool = nil end
            api.releaseTool(state)
        end
        local function ensureTool(state, name, equipped, activated, unequipped)
            if state.tool and state.tool.Parent then return end
            destroyTool(state)
            local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            if not backpack then state.statusMessage = "WAITING FOR BACKPACK"; return end
            api.acquireTool(state)
            local tool = Instance.new("Tool")
            tool.Name, tool.ToolTip = name, name .. " - equip to use"
            tool.RequiresHandle, tool.CanBeDropped, tool.Enabled = false, false, true
            state.tool, state.toolConnections = tool, {}
            local function add(signal, callback) table.insert(state.toolConnections, signal:Connect(callback)) end
            if equipped then add(tool.Equipped, equipped) end
            if activated then add(tool.Activated, function()
                if alive and state.enabled and tool.Parent == LocalPlayer.Character then activated() end
            end) end
            if unequipped then add(tool.Unequipped, unequipped) end
            tool.Parent = backpack
            state.statusMessage = "EQUIP " .. string.upper(name)
        end
        local function releaseFreeze(state)
            local frozen = state and state.frozen
            if not frozen then return end
            state.frozen = nil
            if frozen.root.Parent then
                frozen.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                frozen.root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                frozen.root.Anchored = frozen.anchored
            end
            state.statusMessage = "ACTIVATE TO FREEZE"
            if state.tool then state.tool.Name = "Freeze Me" end
        end
        local function stopMotions()
            for _, key in ipairs(movementGroup) do
                local state = byKey[key]
                if state and state.enabled then state.setEnabled(false, true) end
            end
            for _, key in ipairs({ "WallWalk", "HeadWalk", "SelfFling", "Spin", "AntiFall" }) do
                local state = byKey[key]
                if state and state.enabled then state.setEnabled(false, true) end
            end
            if byKey.GroundSink and byKey.GroundSink.ground then finishGround(byKey.GroundSink, true) end
            releaseFreeze(byKey.FreezeTool)
        end
        local function toggleUnderground(state)
            if state.ground then
                beginGroundReturn(state)
            else
                api.cancelExclusive()
                stopMotions()
                local target = currentHumanoid()
                state.pendingGround = target and prepareGround(target)
                if not state.pendingGround then
                    state.statusMessage = "STAND OVER A FLOOR / LEAVE SEAT"
                    notify("Underground: stand over a collidable floor and leave your seat first.")
                    return
                end
                applyGround(state, target)
            end
            if state.tool then state.tool.Name = state.ground and "Return Surface" or "Underground" end
            if state.repaint then state.repaint() end
        end
        local function toggleFreeze(state)
            if state.frozen then releaseFreeze(state); return end
            if byKey.Flashback and byKey.Flashback.replay then stopReplay(byKey.Flashback) end
            stopMotions()
            local target = currentHumanoid()
            local root = getRoot(target)
            if not target or target.Health <= 0 or not root or target.SeatPart then notify("Leave the seat and use a living character."); return end
            state.frozen = { root = root, anchored = root.Anchored }
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity, root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero
            root.Anchored = true
            state.statusMessage = "FROZEN / ACTIVATE TO RELEASE"
            if state.tool then state.tool.Name = "Unfreeze Me" end
        end
        local function stopRepeat(reason)
            repeatGeneration = repeatGeneration + 1
            repeatJob = nil
            local state = byKey.SpamTool
            if state and state.chatStatus then state.chatStatus.Text = reason or "Stopped. No more messages will be scheduled." end
        end
        local function startRepeat(state)
            local message = state.messageInput.Text
            local count = utf8 and utf8.len(message) or #message
            if not message:match("%S") or not count or count > 200 then
                state.chatStatus.Text = "Enter 1-200 characters."; return
            end
            local interval, duration = finiteNumber(state.intervalInput.Text), finiteNumber(state.durationInput.Text)
            if not interval or not duration then state.chatStatus.Text = "Enter valid interval and duration numbers."; return end
            interval, duration = math.clamp(interval, MIN_CHAT_INTERVAL, 60), math.clamp(duration, 1, MAX_CHAT_DURATION)
            state.intervalInput.Text, state.durationInput.Text = tostring(interval), tostring(duration)
            local ok, channel = pcall(function()
                local channels = game:GetService("TextChatService"):FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
                return channels and channels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
            end)
            if not ok or not channel then state.chatStatus.Text = "Public RBXGeneral chat is unavailable. Nothing sent."; return end
            stopRepeat()
            repeatJob = { channel = channel, message = message, interval = interval, duration = duration,
                started = os.clock(), nextSend = os.clock(), generation = repeatGeneration, inFlight = false, sent = 0 }
            state.chatStatus.Text = "Running in PUBLIC GAME CHAT. Press STOP to cancel."
        end
        local function showChat(state)
            if not state.chatPanel then
                local root = panel("QuickChatRepeat", "TEXT REPEAT / PUBLIC GAME CHAT", 308, 252, function()
                    stopRepeat(); if state.chatPanel then state.chatPanel.Visible = false end
                end)
                state.chatPanel = root
                state.messageInput = inputBox(root, "", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 66), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 42), true)
                state.messageInput.PlaceholderText = "Message (max 200 characters)"
                label(root, "INTERVAL (3-60s)", UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 16), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 117), 9, colors.Muted, true)
                label(root, "DURATION (1-120s)", UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 16), UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 117), 9, colors.Muted, true)
                state.intervalInput = inputBox(root, "5", UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 26), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 137))
                state.durationInput = inputBox(root, "20", UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 26), UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 137))
                button(root, "START", UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 172), function() startRepeat(state) end)
                button(root, "STOP", UDim2.new(0.5, -15, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 172), function() stopRepeat() end, colors.Pink)
                state.chatStatus = label(root, "Filters and game rate limits apply. No bypass or retry loop.",
                    UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 38), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 207), 10, colors.Muted)
                state.chatStatus.TextWrapped = true
            end
            state.chatPanel.Visible = true
            state.statusMessage = "PUBLIC CHAT PANEL OPEN"
        end
        local function showKeyboard(state)
            if not state.keyboardPanel then
                local root = newFrame(MainGui, "QuickWASD", UDim2.new(0, 136, 0, 112),
                    UDim2.new(1, -150, 0.56, 0), Color3.fromRGB(20, 21, 36))
                root.ZIndex = 80
                RedUI.Corner(root, 10); RedUI.Stroke(root, colors.Cyan, 1.3)
                label(root, "MOVE KEYS", UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 18), UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 3), 9, colors.Cyan, true)
                button(root, "X", UDim2.new(0, 19, 0, 18), UDim2.new(1, -23, 0, 3), function() root.Visible = false; clearKeys() end)
                for _, definition in ipairs({ {"W",51,27}, {"A",11,65}, {"S",51,65}, {"D",91,65} }) do
                    local key = definition[1]
                    local b = button(root, key, UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 33), UDim2.new(0, definition[2], 0, definition[3]))
                    b.InputBegan:Connect(function(event)
                        if event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                            keyboard[key], keyPointers[key] = true, event
                        end
                    end)
                end
                state.keyboardPanel = root
            end
            state.keyboardPanel.Visible = true
            state.statusMessage = "RIGHT-SIDE W A S D OPEN"
        end
        connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(event)
            for key, pointer in pairs(keyPointers) do
                if event == pointer or (event.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and pointer.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
                    keyboard[key] = false; keyPointers[key] = nil
                end
            end
        end)
        connect(UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased, clearKeys)
        local function stepKeyboard()
            local state = byKey.KeyboardTool
            local active = alive and state and state.enabled and state.keyboardPanel and state.keyboardPanel.Visible
            local target = currentHumanoid()
            if not active or not target or target.Health <= 0 or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then clearKeys(); return end
            local move = Vector3.new((keyboard.D and 1 or 0) - (keyboard.A and 1 or 0), 0,
                (keyboard.S and 1 or 0) - (keyboard.W and 1 or 0))
            if move.Magnitude > 0 then
                if move.Magnitude > 1 then move = move.Unit end
                target:Move(move, true); usingVirtual = true
            elseif usingVirtual then target:Move(Vector3.zero, true); usingVirtual = false end
        end
        local keyboardBinding = "DeathNote_Quick_WASD"
        local bound = pcall(function()
            RunService:BindToRenderStep(keyboardBinding, (Enum.RenderPriority.Input.Value or 100) + 1, stepKeyboard)
        end)
        if not bound then connect(RunService.RenderStepped, stepKeyboard) end
        local espRecords, espConnections, espGeneration = {}, {}, 0
        local playerColours, nextColour = {}, 0
        local history = { frames = {}, head = 0, count = 0, capacity = 1302, last = -math.huge, character = nil }
        local function destroyEspRecord(player)
            local record = espRecords[player]
            if not record then return end
            if record.highlight then record.highlight:Destroy() end
            if record.billboard then record.billboard:Destroy() end
            espRecords[player] = nil
        end
        local function buildEsp(player, character, generation)
            if player == LocalPlayer then return end
            task.spawn(function()
                local target = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 8)
                local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character:WaitForChild("Head", 8) or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local state = byKey.ESP
                if not alive or generation ~= espGeneration or not state or not state.enabled or not target or not head or player.Character ~= character then return end
                destroyEspRecord(player)
                if not playerColours[player.UserId] then
                    nextColour = nextColour + 1
                    playerColours[player.UserId] = Color3.fromHSV((nextColour * 0.61803398875) % 1, 0.78, 1)
                end
                local colour = playerColours[player.UserId]
                local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                highlight.Name, highlight.Adornee = "QuickColourESP", character
                highlight.FillColor, highlight.OutlineColor = colour, colour
                highlight.FillTransparency, highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.8, 0
                highlight.DepthMode, highlight.Parent = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop, character
                local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                billboard.Name, billboard.Adornee = "QuickPlayerInfo", head
                billboard.AlwaysOnTop, billboard.MaxDistance = true, 0
                billboard.Size, billboard.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = UDim2.new(0, 210, 0, 58), Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                billboard.Parent = character
                local name = label(billboard, player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")",
                    UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 23), UDim2.new(), 13, colour, true)
                name.TextXAlignment, name.TextStrokeTransparency = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, 0.25
                local info = label(billboard, "", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 23), 11, colors.White, true)
                info.TextXAlignment, info.TextStrokeTransparency = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, 0.25
                local bar = newFrame(billboard, "HealthTrack", UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 0, 4), UDim2.new(0.125, 0, 0, 48), colors.Track)
                RedUI.Corner(bar, 3)
                local fill = newFrame(bar, "Health", UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.new(), colour)
                RedUI.Corner(fill, 3)
                espRecords[player] = { character = character, humanoid = target, highlight = highlight, billboard = billboard, info = info, fill = fill }
            end)
        end
        local function enableEsp()
            if #espConnections > 0 then return end
            espGeneration = espGeneration + 1
            local generation = espGeneration
            local function watch(player)
                if player == LocalPlayer then return end
                table.insert(espConnections, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character) buildEsp(player, character, generation) end))
                if player.Character then buildEsp(player, player.Character, generation) end
            end
            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do watch(player) end
            table.insert(espConnections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(watch))
            table.insert(espConnections, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(destroyEspRecord))
        end
        local function disableEsp()
            espGeneration = espGeneration + 1
            disconnectList(espConnections); espConnections = {}
            for player in pairs(espRecords) do destroyEspRecord(player) end
        end
        local function updateEsp()
            local localRoot = getRoot(currentHumanoid())
            for player, record in pairs(espRecords) do
                local root = record.character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if player.Character ~= record.character or not record.humanoid.Parent then destroyEspRecord(player)
                else
                    local distance = localRoot and root and math.floor((root.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude) or 0
                    local hp, maximum = math.max(0, record.humanoid.Health), math.max(1, record.humanoid.MaxHealth)
                    record.info.Text = string.format("HP %.0f/%.0f | %d studs", hp, maximum, distance)
                    record.fill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(hp / maximum, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
                end
            end
        end
        resetHistory = function()
            history.frames, history.head, history.count, history.last = {}, 0, 0, -math.huge
            history.character = nil
        end
        local function recordHistory()
            local state = byKey.Flashback
            if state and state.replay then return end
            local target = currentHumanoid()
            local root = getRoot(target)
            if not target or target.Health <= 0 or not root then return end
            if history.character ~= target.Parent then resetHistory(); history.character = target.Parent end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - history.last < 0.049 then return end
            history.last = now
            local joints = {}
            for _, item in ipairs(target.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
                if item:IsA("Motor6D") then joints[item] = item.Transform end
            end
            local previous = history.frames[history.head]
            local frame = { t = now, cframe = root.CFrame, joints = joints,
                cut = previous and (root.Position - previous.cframe.Position).Magnitude > 120 or false }
            history.head = history.head % history.capacity + 1
            history.frames[history.head] = frame
            history.count = math.min(history.count + 1, history.capacity)
        end
        local function historySnapshot()
            local result = {}
            for i = 1, history.count do
                local index = (history.head - history.count + i - 1) % history.capacity + 1
                table.insert(result, history.frames[index])
            end
            return result
        end
        local function availableHistory()
            if history.count < 2 then return 0 end
            local oldest = history.frames[(history.head - history.count) % history.capacity + 1]
            return math.min(60, history.frames[history.head].t - oldest.t)
        end
        stopReplay = function(state)
            local replay = state and state.replay
            if not replay then return end
            state.replay = nil
            if replay.root.Parent then
                replay.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity, replay.root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero
                replay.root.Anchored = replay.anchored
            end
            restoreJoints(replay.joints)
            releaseNoclip(replay)
            if replay.humanoid.Parent then
                replay.humanoid.PlatformStand, replay.humanoid.AutoRotate = replay.platformStand, replay.autoRotate
                if replay.humanoid.Health > 0 and not replay.platformStand then replay.humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end
            end
            for _, item in ipairs(replay.effects) do item:Destroy() end
            if replay.camera then pcall(function() replay.camera.FieldOfView = replay.fieldOfView end) end
            state.statusMessage = "REWIND STOPPED / RECORDING"
            resetHistory()
        end
        startReplay = function(state)
            if not state.enabled or state.replay then return end
            local target = currentHumanoid()
            local root = getRoot(target)
            if not target or target.Health <= 0 or not root or target.SeatPart then notify("Leave the seat before rewinding."); return end
            local available = availableHistory()
            if state.value <= 0 or available < 0.15 then state.statusMessage = "RECORD MORE HISTORY FIRST"; return end
            stopMotions()
            target, root = currentHumanoid(), getRoot(currentHumanoid())
            local frames = historySnapshot()
            local replay = { root = root, humanoid = target, frames = frames, index = #frames,
                cursor = frames[#frames].t, goal = frames[#frames].t - math.min(state.value, available),
                anchored = root.Anchored, platformStand = target.PlatformStand, autoRotate = target.AutoRotate,
                joints = captureJoints(target.Parent), effects = {} }
            state.replay = replay
            acquireNoclip(replay, target.Parent)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity, root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero
            root.Anchored, target.PlatformStand, target.AutoRotate = true, true, false
            local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
            attachment.Name, attachment.Position, attachment.Parent = "QuickRewindFlame", Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 1.5), root
            local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
            emitter.Name, emitter.Texture = "RewindFlame", "rbxasset://textures/particles/fire_main.dds"
            emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 50), Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 90))
            emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1.3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0) })
            emitter.Lifetime, emitter.Speed, emitter.Rate = NumberRange.new(0.2, 0.5), NumberRange.new(4, 9), 70
            emitter.EmissionDirection, emitter.LightEmission = Enum.NormalId.Back, 0.9
            emitter.Parent = attachment
            table.insert(replay.effects, emitter); table.insert(replay.effects, attachment)
            local left, right = Instance.new("Attachment"), Instance.new("Attachment")
            left.Position, right.Position = Vector3.new(-0.55, 0.2, 1.2), Vector3.new(0.55, 0.2, 1.2)
            left.Parent, right.Parent = root, root
            local trail = Instance.new("Trail")
            trail.Name, trail.Attachment0, trail.Attachment1 = "QuickRewindTrail", left, right
            trail.Lifetime, trail.MinLength, trail.FaceCamera, trail.LightEmission = 0.5, 0.05, true, 1
            trail.Color = emitter.Color
            trail.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
            trail.Parent = root
            table.insert(replay.effects, trail); table.insert(replay.effects, left); table.insert(replay.effects, right)
            local tint = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            tint.Name, tint.TintColor, tint.Saturation = "QuickRewindTint", Color3.fromRGB(180, 215, 255), -0.2
            tint.Parent = game:GetService("Lighting")
            table.insert(replay.effects, tint)
            replay.camera = world.CurrentCamera
            if replay.camera then
                replay.fieldOfView = replay.camera.FieldOfView
                replay.camera.FieldOfView = math.clamp(replay.fieldOfView - 6, 5, 120)
            end
            state.statusMessage = string.format("REWINDING / %.1fX", state.replaySpeed)
        end
        stepReplay = function(state, delta)
            local replay = state.replay
            if not replay then return end
            if not replay.root.Parent or replay.humanoid.Health <= 0 then stopReplay(state); return end
            replay.cursor = math.max(replay.goal, replay.cursor - math.clamp(delta, 0, 0.25) * math.clamp(state.replaySpeed or 1, 0.5, 5))
            while replay.index > 1 and replay.frames[replay.index - 1].t > replay.cursor do replay.index = replay.index - 1 end
            local high = replay.frames[replay.index]
            local low = replay.frames[math.max(1, replay.index - 1)]
            local ratio = high.t > low.t and math.clamp((replay.cursor - low.t) / (high.t - low.t), 0, 1) or 0
            if high.cut then ratio = replay.cursor < high.t and 0 or 1 end
            replay.root.CFrame = low.cframe:Lerp(high.cframe, ratio)
            for motor, transform in pairs(low.joints) do
                if motor.Parent then motor.Transform = transform:Lerp(high.joints[motor] or transform, ratio) end
            end
            acquireNoclip(replay, replay.humanoid.Parent)
            state.statusMessage = string.format("REWIND %.1fS / %.1fX", replay.cursor - replay.goal, state.replaySpeed)
            if replay.cursor <= replay.goal then stopReplay(state) end
        end
        local function ownsPhysics()
            if byKey.Flashback and byKey.Flashback.replay then return true end
            if byKey.FreezeTool and byKey.FreezeTool.frozen then return true end
            if byKey.GroundSink and byKey.GroundSink.ground then return true end
            for _, key in ipairs(movementGroup) do if byKey[key] and byKey[key].controller then return true end end
            return (byKey.WallWalk and byKey.WallWalk.surfaceRig) or (byKey.HeadWalk and byKey.HeadWalk.surfaceRig)
        end
        local function restoreAntiSlap(state)
            local guard = state.slapGuard
            if not guard then return end
            state.slapGuard = nil
            disconnectList(guard.connections)
            if guard.humanoid.Parent then
                for kind, enabled in pairs(guard.states) do guard.humanoid:SetStateEnabled(kind, enabled) end
            end
        end
        local function enableAntiSlap(state, target)
            if not target then return end
            if state.slapGuard and state.slapGuard.humanoid == target then return end
            restoreAntiSlap(state)
            local root = getRoot(target)
            local guard = { humanoid = target, states = {}, connections = {}, previous = root and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero }
            state.slapGuard = guard
            for _, kind in ipairs({ Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll }) do
                guard.states[kind] = target:GetStateEnabled(kind)
                target:SetStateEnabled(kind, false)
            end
            local recovering = false
            local function recover()
                if recovering or not alive or not state.enabled or target.Health <= 0 or ownsPhysics() then return end
                recovering = true
                if target.PlatformStand then target.PlatformStand = false end
                local kind = target:GetState()
                if kind == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or kind == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or kind == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then
                    target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                end
                recovering = false
            end
            table.insert(guard.connections, target.StateChanged:Connect(recover))
            table.insert(guard.connections, target:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PlatformStand"):Connect(recover))
            recover()
        end
        local function stepAntiSlap(state, target)
            if not state or not state.enabled or not target then return end
            enableAntiSlap(state, target)
            if ownsPhysics() or target.SeatPart then return end
            local root = getRoot(target)
            if not root or root.Anchored then return end
            local guard, velocity = state.slapGuard, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            local desired = target.MoveDirection * target.WalkSpeed
            local mismatch = (Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z) - desired).Magnitude
            local sudden = (velocity - guard.previous).Magnitude > math.max(40, target.WalkSpeed * 0.8)
            local recentJump = os.clock() - (state.lastJump or -math.huge) < 0.7
            local y = velocity.Y
            if not recentJump and sudden and y > 70 then y = 0 end
            if sudden and mismatch > math.max(38, target.WalkSpeed * 1.4) then
                velocity = Vector3.new(desired.X, y, desired.Z)
            elseif y ~= velocity.Y then velocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, y, velocity.Z) end
            local spinning = (byKey.SelfFling and byKey.SelfFling.enabled and byKey.SelfFling.value > 0) or
                (byKey.Spin and byKey.Spin.enabled and byKey.Spin.value > 0)
            if not spinning and root.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > 35 then root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = velocity
            guard.previous = velocity
            if target.PlatformStand then target.PlatformStand = false; target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end
        end
        -- Requested FusionWTF release. This remains a third-party, full Dex package,
        -- not the previous read-only viewer. Isolation is not a security sandbox.
        -- Automatic bytecode-upload and save/export fallbacks are disabled here.
        local DEX_URL = "https://github.com/FusionWTF/Dex-Explorer/releases/download/Beta/out.lua"
        local dexCache = genv._DEATH_NOTE_FUSION_DEX
        if type(dexCache) ~= "table" or dexCache.url ~= DEX_URL then
            dexCache = { url = DEX_URL, guis = {}, visible = false, loading = false }
            genv._DEATH_NOTE_FUSION_DEX = dexCache
        end
        local function syncDexVisibility()
            for gui in pairs(dexCache.guis) do pcall(function() gui.Enabled = dexCache.visible end) end
        end
        local function setDexVisible(value)
            dexCache.visible = value and alive
            syncDexVisibility()
        end
        local function showDex(state)
            setDexVisible(true)
            if dexCache.bridge then
                state.statusMessage = "FUSION DEX / OPEN"
                local apps = dexCache.bridge.Apps
                for _, name in ipairs({ "Explorer", "Properties" }) do
                    local window = apps and apps[name] and apps[name].Window
                    if window and window.Closed then pcall(function() window:Show() end) end
                end
                return
            end
            if dexCache.loading then state.statusMessage = "DEX IS STILL LOADING"; return end
            if type(loadstring) ~= "function" or type(setfenv) ~= "function" then
                state.statusMessage = "DEX NEEDS LOADSTRING + SETFENV"
                notify("Fusion Dex requires an executor supporting loadstring and setfenv.")
                return
            end
            dexCache.loading, dexCache.error = true, nil
            state.statusMessage = "DOWNLOADING FUSION DEX"
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    local source = game:HttpGet(DEX_URL)
                    if type(source) ~= "string" or #source < 1000 or #source > 2000000 or not source:find("DeX Explorer", 1, true) then
                        error("Unexpected Dex release response")
                    end
                    local suffix = [[
local __control = __QUICK_DEX_CONTROL
local __secure = Main.SecureGui
Main.SecureGui = function(gui)
    __control.guis[gui] = true
    local result = __secure(gui)
    pcall(function() gui.Enabled = __control.visible end)
    return result
end
Main.ShowGui = Main.SecureGui
Main.Init()
return {Main = Main, Apps = Apps}
]]
                    -- Replace only the final entry-point; embedded module code is retained.
                    local replacementCount
                    source, replacementCount = source:gsub("Main%.Init%(%)%s*\r?\n%s*%-%-for i,v in pairs%(Main%.MissingEnv%) do print%(i,v%) end%s*$", function() return suffix end, 1)
                    if replacementCount ~= 1 then error("Dex release entry-point changed; wrapper refused to guess") end
                    local compiled, compileError = loadstring(source, "FusionWTF_Dex_Beta")
                    if not compiled then error(tostring(compileError)) end
                    local base = getfenv and getfenv() or genv
                    local environment = setmetatable({ __QUICK_DEX_CONTROL = dexCache }, { __index = base })
                    environment._G = environment
                    environment.getgenv = function() return environment end
                    environment.saveinstance = function() error("Save/export is disabled by the Quick viewing wrapper") end
                    environment.decompile = function() return "-- External/built-in decompilation is disabled in this viewing wrapper." end
                    environment.writefile, environment.appendfile, environment.makefolder = false, false, false
                    environment.delfile, environment.delfolder, environment.loadfile = false, false, false
                    local originalRequest = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request)
                    local function readRequest(options)
                        if type(options) ~= "table" or string.upper(options.Method or "GET") ~= "GET" or options.Body then
                            error("Dex wrapper blocks outbound POST/body requests")
                        end
                        if not originalRequest then error("Request API unavailable") end
                        return originalRequest(options)
                    end
                    environment.request, environment.http_request = readRequest, readRequest
                    environment.http = setmetatable({ request = readRequest }, { __index = http or {} })
                    environment.syn = setmetatable({ request = readRequest }, { __index = syn or {} })
                    environment.fluxus = setmetatable({ request = readRequest }, { __index = fluxus or {} })
                    setfenv(compiled, environment)
                    return compiled()
                end)
                dexCache.loading = false
                if ok and type(result) == "table" and result.Main then
                    dexCache.bridge = result
                    -- A new hub may have acquired this cached load after the old UI closed.
                    -- Do not apply the old UI's `alive` flag to its new owner's visibility.
                    syncDexVisibility()
                    if alive then state.statusMessage = state.enabled and "FUSION DEX / OPEN" or "DEX HIDDEN" end
                else
                    dexCache.error = tostring(result)
                    setDexVisible(false)
                    if alive then
                        state.statusMessage = "DEX LOAD FAILED / CHECK EXECUTOR"
                        notify("Fusion Dex failed to load: " .. tostring(result):sub(1, 140))
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        local thumbnailCache = {}
        local function inServer(player)
            for _, current in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if current == player then return true end end
            return false
        end
        local function pickPlayer(state, player)
            if not state.enabled then return end
            state.selectedPlayer = player
            state.playerStatus.Text = player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")"
            if state.onPickPlayer then state.onPickPlayer(player) end
            if state.key ~= "ChatAgeTool" then return end
            state.ageGeneration = (state.ageGeneration or 0) + 1
            local generation = state.ageGeneration
            state.ageResult = nil
            state.playerStatus.Text = player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")\nLoading public account creation date..."
            task.spawn(function()
                local function valid() return alive and state.enabled and state.ageGeneration==generation and state.selectedPlayer==player and inServer(player) end
                local result,reason=RedTelemetry.AgeService.get(player,valid)
                if not valid() then return end
                if result then
                    state.ageResult=result
                    state.playerStatus.Text=player.DisplayName.." (@"..player.Name..")\nACCOUNT AGE: "..result.days.." days | Joined: "..result.created.." UTC"
                    state.playerNotice.Text=result.source..". Account age, not real-life age. Game filters apply."
                else
                    state.playerStatus.Text=player.DisplayName.." (@"..player.Name..")\nAccount age unavailable - verified lookup failed."
                    state.playerNotice.Text=tostring(reason or "No age guessed. Retry later."):sub(1,115)
                end
            end)
        end
        local function refreshAgeDisplay()
            local state=byKey.ChatAgeTool
            if not state or not state.enabled or not state.ageResult or not state.selectedPlayer or not state.playersPanel or not state.playersPanel.Visible then return end
            local result=RedTelemetry.AgeService.current(state.selectedPlayer,state.ageResult)
            if not result then state.ageResult=nil;state.playerStatus.Text="Server time / verified age unavailable";return end
            state.ageResult=result
            state.playerStatus.Text=state.selectedPlayer.DisplayName.." (@"..state.selectedPlayer.Name..")\nACCOUNT AGE: "..result.days.." days | Joined: "..result.created.." UTC"
        end
        local function refreshPlayerRows(state)
            if not state.playerList then return end
            state.playerListGeneration = (state.playerListGeneration or 0) + 1
            local generation = state.playerListGeneration
            for _, child in ipairs(state.playerList:GetChildren()) do if child:IsA("GuiObject") then child:Destroy() end end
            local players = Players:GetPlayers()
            table.sort(players, state.sortPlayers or function(a, b) return a.Name:lower() < b.Name:lower() end)
            local query = state.searchInput and state.searchInput.Text:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"):gsub("^@", "") or ""
            for index, player in ipairs(players) do
                local matches = query == "" or player.Name:lower():find(query, 1, true) or player.DisplayName:lower():find(query, 1, true) or tostring(player.UserId):find(query, 1, true)
                if matches then
                local row = button(state.playerList, "", UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, state.describePlayerRow and 78 or 60), UDim2.new(), function() pickPlayer(state, player) end)
                row.Name, row.LayoutOrder = "PlayerChoice_" .. player.UserId, index
                local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                image.Name, image.Size, image.Position = "Avatar", UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 46), UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7)
                image.BackgroundTransparency, image.Image = 1, "rbxasset://textures/ui/GuiImagePlaceholder.png"
                image.Parent = row
                RedUI.Corner(image, 9)
                label(row, player.DisplayName .. (player == LocalPlayer and " (You)" or ""), UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 23),
                    UDim2.new(0, 61, 0, 8), 12, colors.White, true)
                label(row, "@" .. player.Name, UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 20), UDim2.new(0, 61, 0, 32), 11, colors.Cyan)
                if state.describePlayerRow then
                    label(row, state.describePlayerRow(player), UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 19), UDim2.new(0, 61, 0, 54), 10, colors.Muted, true)
                end
                task.spawn(function()
                    if not alive or not state.enabled or state.playerListGeneration ~= generation or not row.Parent then return end
                    local ok, thumbnail = pcall(function()
                        if thumbnailCache[player.UserId] then return thumbnailCache[player.UserId] end
                        local imageUrl = Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(player.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
                        if type(imageUrl) == "string" and imageUrl ~= "" then thumbnailCache[player.UserId] = imageUrl end
                        return imageUrl
                    end)
                    if ok and type(thumbnail) == "string" and alive and state.enabled and state.playerListGeneration == generation and row.Parent then image.Image = thumbnail end
                end)
                end
            end
            if state.selectedPlayer and not inServer(state.selectedPlayer) then
                state.selectedPlayer, state.ageResult = nil, nil
                state.playerStatus.Text = "Selected player left the server. Choose another player."
            end
        end
        local function gotoSelected(state)
            if not alive or not state.enabled or not state.playersPanel or not state.playersPanel.Visible then return end
            local selected = state.selectedPlayer
            local target = currentHumanoid()
            local root = getRoot(target)
            local otherCharacter = selected and selected.Character
            local otherRoot = otherCharacter and otherCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local otherHumanoid = otherCharacter and otherCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not selected or not inServer(selected) or not otherRoot or not otherHumanoid or otherHumanoid.Health <= 0 or not target or target.Health <= 0 or not root then
                state.playerNotice.Text = "Select a player whose character is available on this client."; return
            end
            if target.SeatPart then state.playerNotice.Text = "Leave your seat before using Goto."; return end
            local distance = finiteNumber(state.gotoDistance.Text)
            if not distance then state.playerNotice.Text = "Enter a valid offset distance."; return end
            distance = math.clamp(distance, 0, 1000)
            state.gotoDistance.Text = tostring(distance)
            api.cancelExclusive()
            stopMotions()
            target, root = currentHumanoid(), getRoot(currentHumanoid())
            local look = otherRoot.CFrame.LookVector
            local forward = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
            if forward.Magnitude < 0.01 then forward = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
            forward = forward.Unit
            local right = Vector3.new(-forward.Z, 0, forward.X)
            local directions = { Behind = forward * -1, Front = forward, Left = right * -1, Right = right }
            local destination = otherRoot.Position + (directions[state.gotoSide or "Behind"] or directions.Behind) * distance
            local params = RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType, params.FilterDescendantsInstances = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, { target.Parent, selected.Character }
            local hit = world:Raycast(destination + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), Vector3.new(0, -40, 0), params)
            if hit and hit.Normal.Y > 0.35 then destination = Vector3.new(destination.X, hit.Position.Y + feetOffset(target, root) + 0.2, destination.Z) end
            local desired = CFrame.new(destination) * uprightRotation(root)
            local offset = root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(target.Parent:GetPivot())
            target.Parent:PivotTo(desired * offset)
            target:Move(Vector3.zero, false); target:MoveTo(destination)
            for _, part in ipairs(target.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero; part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
            end
            if byKey.AntiFling then byKey.AntiFling.safeCFrame = root.CFrame end
            state.playerNotice.Text = "Moved " .. distance .. " studs " .. string.lower(state.gotoSide or "Behind") .. " @" .. selected.Name .. "."
        end
        local function sendAccountAge(state)
            if not alive or not state.enabled or not state.playersPanel or not state.playersPanel.Visible then return end
            local player, result = state.selectedPlayer, state.ageResult
            if not player or not result or result.userId ~= player.UserId or not inServer(player) then state.playerNotice.Text = "Select a player and wait for a verified account-age result."; return end
            result=RedTelemetry.AgeService.current(player,result)
            if not result then state.playerNotice.Text="Verified age/server clock unavailable; nothing sent.";return end
            state.ageResult=result
            if state.ageSending or os.clock() - (state.lastAgeSend or -math.huge) < 5 then state.playerNotice.Text = "Wait 5 seconds between manual chat sends."; return end
            state.lastAgeSend, state.ageSending = os.clock(), true
            local ticket = {}
            state.ageSendTicket = ticket
            local message = "@" .. player.Name .. "'s Roblox account age is " .. result.days .. " days" .. (result.created~="Unavailable" and (" (joined "..result.created.." UTC).") or ".")
            task.spawn(function()
                if not alive or not state.enabled or state.ageSendTicket ~= ticket or state.selectedPlayer ~= player or not inServer(player) or not state.playersPanel.Visible then
                    if state.ageSendTicket == ticket then state.ageSending, state.ageSendTicket = false, nil end
                    return
                end
                -- Recompute at actual submission too: a queued task may cross a UTC day boundary.
                local current=RedTelemetry.AgeService.current(player,state.ageResult)
                if not current then state.ageSending,state.ageSendTicket=false,nil;state.playerNotice.Text="Verified age unavailable; nothing sent.";return end
                state.ageResult=current
                message="@"..player.Name.."'s Roblox account age is "..current.days.." days"..(current.created~="Unavailable" and (" (joined "..current.created.." UTC).") or ".")
                -- Once SendAsync has been submitted, Roblox cannot retract it on OFF.
                local ok, response = pcall(function()
                    local channels = game:GetService("TextChatService"):FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
                    local channel = channels and channels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
                    if not channel then error("Public game chat unavailable") end
                    return channel:SendAsync(message)
                end)
                if state.ageSendTicket ~= ticket then return end
                state.ageSending, state.ageSendTicket = false, nil
                if not alive or not state.enabled then return end
                local accepted = ok and (not response or response.Status == Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success)
                state.playerNotice.Text = accepted and "Sent normally. Roblox filtering may still replace text with tags." or "Game chat rejected/limited the message. No bypass or retry."
            end)
        end
        local function addPlayerSearch(state, root, y)
            state.searchInput = inputBox(root, "", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 26), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, y))
            state.searchInput.Name = "SearchPlayer"
            state.searchInput.PlaceholderText = "Search Player / @username / display name / user ID"
            connect(state.searchInput:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function()
                if state.enabled and state.playersPanel and state.playersPanel.Visible then refreshPlayerRows(state) end
            end)
        end
        local function showPlayersTool(state)
            local ageMode = state.key == "ChatAgeTool"
            if not state.playersPanel then
                local root = panel(ageMode and "QuickChatAge" or "QuickGotoPlayers", ageMode and "CHAT AGE / ROBLOX ACCOUNT AGE" or "GOTO PLAYERS / LIVE SERVER", 382, 430, function()
                    if state.playersPanel then state.playersPanel.Visible = false end
                end)
                state.playersPanel = root
                state.playerStatus = label(root, "Select a player below.", UDim2.new(1, -106, 0, 45), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 40), 11, colors.White, true)
                state.playerStatus.TextWrapped = true
                button(root, "REFRESH", UDim2.new(0, 77, 0, 25), UDim2.new(1, -87, 0, 42), function() refreshPlayerRows(state) end)
                if ageMode then
                    label(root, "Account age is NOT the person's real-life age.", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 19), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 91), 10, colors.Muted)
                    button(root, "SEND SELECTED ACCOUNT AGE TO CHAT", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 29), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 122), function() sendAccountAge(state) end)
                else
                    label(root, "OFFSET DISTANCE (0-1000 STUDS)", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 17), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 87), 9, colors.Muted, true)
                    state.gotoDistance = inputBox(root, "5", UDim2.new(0, 63, 0, 26), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 108))
                    state.gotoSide = "Behind"
                    for index, side in ipairs({ "Behind", "Front", "Left", "Right" }) do
                        button(root, string.upper(side), UDim2.new(0, 66, 0, 26), UDim2.new(0, 80 + (index - 1) * 72, 0, 108), function()
                            state.gotoSide = side
                            state.playerNotice.Text = "Direction: " .. side .. ". Select a player, then GO TO SELECTED."
                        end)
                    end
                    button(root, "GO TO SELECTED", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 29), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 145), function() gotoSelected(state) end)
                end
                addPlayerSearch(state, root, 184)
                local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                list.Name, list.Size, list.Position = "ServerPlayers", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 158), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 218)
                list.BackgroundColor3, list.BorderSizePixel = colors.Field, 0
                list.ScrollBarThickness, list.AutomaticCanvasSize = 3, Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                list.CanvasSize, list.Parent = UDim2.new(), root
                local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                layout.SortOrder, layout.Padding, layout.Parent = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, UDim.new(0, 5), list
                state.playerList = list
                state.playerNotice = label(root, "Select a player. Actions require a separate button press.", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 40), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 382), 10, colors.Muted)
                state.playerNotice.TextWrapped = true
            end
            state.playersPanel.Visible = true
            state.statusMessage = ageMode and "ACCOUNT AGE PANEL OPEN" or "GOTO PANEL OPEN"
            refreshPlayerRows(state)
        end
        local function refreshVisiblePlayerTools()
            for _, key in ipairs({ "GotoTool", "ChatAgeTool", "HandlekillAuto" }) do
                local state = byKey[key]
                if state and state.enabled and state.playersPanel and state.playersPanel.Visible then refreshPlayerRows(state) end
            end
        end
        connect(Players.PlayerAdded, refreshVisiblePlayerTools)
        connect(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(player)
            for _, key in ipairs({ "GotoTool", "ChatAgeTool", "HandlekillAuto" }) do
                local state = byKey[key]
                if state and state.selectedPlayer == player then
                    state.selectedPlayer, state.ageResult = nil, nil
                    state.ageGeneration = (state.ageGeneration or 0) + 1
                    if state.playerStatus then state.playerStatus.Text = "Player left. Select another player." end
                end
            end
            task.defer(refreshVisiblePlayerTools)
        end)
        -- Local-only social tools. No player destruction, kick/ban remote, chat bypass,
        -- inventory wealth estimate, or claim of complete legacy-VC/custom-UI control.
        Social = (function()
            local social = {}
            local slots, ownerEntries, hiddenRecords, audioInputs = {}, {}, {}, {}
            local blocked, allowed, grace, messageTimes, seenMessages, seenOrder = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}
            local appearanceVersions, appearanceConnections = {}, {}
            local chatRows, chatPanel, chatList, chatClosed = {}, nil, nil, false
            local socialPulse, rankPulse, scannedAudio = 0, 0, false
            local chatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
            local function wanted(player)
                if not player or player == LocalPlayer then return false end
                return (byKey.BeAlone and byKey.BeAlone.enabled) or
                    (byKey.DeleteBadPeople and byKey.DeleteBadPeople.enabled and blocked[player.UserId] ~= nil)
            end
            function social.isHidden(player) return wanted(player) end
            local function releaseEntry(entry, owner)
                if not entry.owners[owner] then return end
                entry.owners[owner] = nil
                if ownerEntries[owner] then
                    ownerEntries[owner][entry] = nil
                    if not next(ownerEntries[owner]) then ownerEntries[owner] = nil end
                end
                if next(entry.owners) then return end
                if entry.connection then entry.connection:Disconnect() end
                local ok, value = pcall(entry.read)
                if ok and value == entry.forced then pcall(entry.write, entry.original) end
                if slots[entry.object] then
                    slots[entry.object][entry.key] = nil
                    if not next(slots[entry.object]) then slots[entry.object] = nil end
                end
            end
            local function releaseOwner(owner)
                for entry in pairs(ownerEntries[owner] or {}) do releaseEntry(entry, owner) end
                ownerEntries[owner] = nil
            end
            local function lease(owner, object, key, forced, read, write)
                slots[object] = slots[object] or {}
                local entry = slots[object][key]
                if not entry then
                    local custom = read ~= nil
                    read = read or function() return object[key] end
                    write = write or function(value) object[key] = value end
                    local ok, original = pcall(read)
                    if not ok or original == nil then return nil end
                    entry = { object=object, key=key, original=original, forced=forced, owners={}, read=read, write=write, custom=custom }
                    if not pcall(write, forced) then return nil end
                    slots[object][key] = entry
                    pcall(function()
                        entry.connection = object:GetPropertyChangedSignal(key):Connect(function()
                            if not next(entry.owners) then return end
                            local readable, current = pcall(read)
                            if readable and current ~= entry.forced then
                                entry.original = current
                                pcall(write, entry.forced)
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                if not entry.owners[owner] then
                    entry.owners[owner] = true
                    ownerEntries[owner] = ownerEntries[owner] or {}
                    ownerEntries[owner][entry] = true
                end
                return entry
            end
            local function bindAudio(input)
                local record = audioInputs[input]
                if not record then return end
                local ok, player = pcall(function() return input.Player end)
                local owner = ok and hiddenRecords[player] or nil
                if record.owner and record.owner ~= owner then
                    local entry = slots[input] and slots[input].Muted
                    if entry then releaseEntry(entry, record.owner) end
                end
                record.owner = owner
                if owner then lease(owner, input, "Muted", true) end
            end
            local function watchAudio(input)
                if audioInputs[input] then bindAudio(input); return end
                audioInputs[input] = {}
                pcall(function()
                    audioInputs[input].connection = input:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Player"):Connect(function() bindAudio(input) end)
                end)
                bindAudio(input)
            end
            local function inspectHidden(record, object)
                if object:IsA("BasePart") then lease(record, object, "LocalTransparencyModifier", 1)
                elseif object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then lease(record, object, "Transparency", 1)
                elseif object:IsA("Sound") then lease(record, object, "Volume", 0)
                elseif object:IsA("AudioDeviceInput") then watchAudio(object)
                elseif object:IsA("Humanoid") then
                    lease(record, object, "DisplayDistanceType", Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None)
                elseif object:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or object:IsA("Trail") or object:IsA("Beam") or object:IsA("Highlight") or
                    object:IsA("Fire") or object:IsA("Smoke") or object:IsA("Sparkles") or object:IsA("BillboardGui") or object:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                    lease(record, object, "Enabled", false)
                end
            end
            local function releaseHidden(player)
                local record = hiddenRecords[player]
                if not record then return end
                hiddenRecords[player] = nil
                disconnectList(record.connections)
                releaseOwner(record)
                for input in pairs(audioInputs) do bindAudio(input) end
            end
            local function releaseObject(owner, object)
                local properties = slots[object]
                if not properties then return end
                for _, entry in pairs(properties) do releaseEntry(entry, owner) end
            end
            local function syncHidden()
                for player, record in pairs(hiddenRecords) do
                    if not wanted(player) or not inServer(player) or record.character ~= player.Character then releaseHidden(player) end
                end
                for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if wanted(player) and not hiddenRecords[player] then
                        local record = { character=player.Character, connections={} }
                        hiddenRecords[player] = record
                        local function inspect(object) if hiddenRecords[player] == record then inspectHidden(record, object) end end
                        for _, object in ipairs(player:GetDescendants()) do inspect(object) end
                        table.insert(record.connections, player.DescendantAdded:Connect(inspect))
                        table.insert(record.connections, player.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(object) releaseObject(record, object) end))
                        if record.character then
                            for _, object in ipairs(record.character:GetDescendants()) do inspect(object) end
                            table.insert(record.connections, record.character.DescendantAdded:Connect(inspect))
                            table.insert(record.connections, record.character.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(object) releaseObject(record, object) end))
                        end
                    end
                end
                if not scannedAudio and ((byKey.BeAlone and byKey.BeAlone.enabled) or (byKey.DeleteBadPeople and byKey.DeleteBadPeople.enabled)) then
                    scannedAudio = true
                    for _, object in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do if object:IsA("AudioDeviceInput") then watchAudio(object) end end
                end
                for input in pairs(audioInputs) do
                    if input.Parent then bindAudio(input)
                    else
                        if audioInputs[input].connection then audioInputs[input].connection:Disconnect() end
                        if audioInputs[input].owner then releaseObject(audioInputs[input].owner,input) end
