                        audioInputs[input] = nil
                    end
                end
            end
            connect(game.DescendantAdded, function(object) if object:IsA("AudioDeviceInput") then watchAudio(object) end end)
            local function chatSetting(owner, name)
                local configuration = chatService:FindFirstChild(name)
                if configuration then lease(owner, configuration, "Enabled", false) end
            end
            local function coreSetting(owner, kind)
                local starter = game:GetService("StarterGui")
                lease(owner, starter, "CoreGui_" .. tostring(kind), false,
                    function() return starter:GetCoreGuiEnabled(kind) end,
                    function(value) starter:SetCoreGuiEnabled(kind, value) end)
            end
            local function applyQuietSettings(state)
                chatSetting(state, "ChatWindowConfiguration")
                chatSetting(state, "BubbleChatConfiguration")
                if state.key == "BeAlone" then
                    chatSetting(state, "ChatInputBarConfiguration")
                    coreSetting(state, Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
                    coreSetting(state, Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList)
                end
            end
            local function renderLocalChat()
                local state = byKey.DeleteBadPeople
                if not state or not state.enabled then if chatPanel then chatPanel.Visible = false end; return end
                if not chatPanel then
                    chatPanel = panel("QuickFilteredChat", "LOCAL CHAT / SPAM FILTER", 350, 204, function() chatClosed=true; if chatPanel then chatPanel.Visible=false end end)
                    chatPanel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 184, 1, -118)
                    chatList = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                    chatList.Name, chatList.Size, chatList.Position = "FilteredMessages", UDim2.new(1,-16,1,-42), UDim2.new(0,8,0,36)
                    chatList.AutomaticCanvasSize, chatList.CanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, UDim2.new()
                    chatList.ScrollBarThickness, chatList.BackgroundTransparency, chatList.BorderSizePixel = 3, 1, 0
                    chatList.Parent = chatPanel
                    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                    layout.SortOrder, layout.Padding, layout.Parent = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, UDim.new(0,4), chatList
                end
                for _, child in ipairs(chatList:GetChildren()) do if child:IsA("GuiObject") then child:Destroy() end end
                for index, row in ipairs(chatRows) do
                    local player
                    for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if candidate.UserId == row.userId then player = candidate; break end end
                    if not (player and wanted(player)) and not blocked[row.userId] then
                        local text = label(chatList, row.text, UDim2.new(1,-6,0,34), UDim2.new(), 11, colors.White)
                        text.Name, text.LayoutOrder = "FilteredMessage_" .. index, index
                        text.RichText, text.TextWrapped, text.TextTruncate = false, true, Enum.TextTruncate.None
                    end
                end
                chatPanel.Visible = not chatClosed and not (byKey.BeAlone and byKey.BeAlone.enabled)
            end
            local function flagPlayer(player, reason)
                if player == LocalPlayer then return end
                blocked[player.UserId] = reason
                for index=#chatRows,1,-1 do if chatRows[index].userId == player.UserId then table.remove(chatRows,index) end end
                syncHidden(); renderLocalChat()
                local state = byKey.DeleteBadPeople
                if state and state.playersPanel and state.playersPanel.Visible then refreshPlayerRows(state) end
            end
            local function incoming(message)
                local state = byKey.DeleteBadPeople
                if not alive or not state or not state.enabled then return end
                if message.Status and message.Status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success then return end
                local source = message.TextSource
                local userId = source and source.UserId or 0
                local id = message.MessageId
                if id and id ~= "" then
                    local key = tostring(userId) .. ":" .. id
                    if seenMessages[key] then return end
                    seenMessages[key] = true; table.insert(seenOrder,key)
                    if #seenOrder > 256 then seenMessages[table.remove(seenOrder,1)] = nil end
                end
                local player
                for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if candidate.UserId == userId then player=candidate; break end end
                local now = os.clock()
                if player and player ~= LocalPlayer and not allowed[userId] and not blocked[userId] and now >= (grace[userId] or 0) then
                    local times = messageTimes[userId] or {}; messageTimes[userId] = times
                    while times[1] and now-times[1] > 2 do table.remove(times,1) end
                    table.insert(times,now)
                    if #times >= 3 then flagPlayer(player,"3 messages within 2 seconds") end
                end
                if (player and wanted(player)) or blocked[userId] then return end
                local prefix = player and (player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. "): ") or "[System] "
                table.insert(chatRows,{ userId=userId, text=prefix .. tostring(message.Text or ""):sub(1,500) })
                while #chatRows > 60 do table.remove(chatRows,1) end
                renderLocalChat()
            end
            local chatHookReady = pcall(function() connect(chatService.MessageReceived, incoming) end)

            -- Anti Fall follows actual nearby walkable surfaces, not every falling Y.
            -- A normal jump does not raise its reference to an unsupported jump apex.
            function social.syncFall(state, target, delta)
                local root = getRoot(target)
                if not root then return end
                local offset = feetOffset(target,root)
                local footY = root.Position.Y-offset
                local params = RaycastParams.new()
                local ignored = {}
                if state.platform then table.insert(ignored,state.platform) end
                for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if player.Character then table.insert(ignored,player.Character) end end
                if byKey.AntiVoid and byKey.AntiVoid.voidPlatform then table.insert(ignored,byKey.AntiVoid.voidPlatform) end
                params.FilterType, params.FilterDescendantsInstances, params.RespectCanCollide = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, ignored, true
                local hit = world:Raycast(root.Position + Vector3.new(0,0.25,0), Vector3.new(0,-offset-1.1,0), params)
                local supported = hit and hit.Normal.Y >= 0.5 and math.abs(footY-hit.Position.Y) <= 0.85
                if target.SeatPart or root.Anchored then state.floorY=footY;if state.platform then state.platform.CanCollide=false end;return end
                if supported then state.floorY = hit.Position.Y end
                if state.floorY == nil then state.floorY = footY end
                if not state.antiFallActive or not state.platform then
                    state.statusMessage="TOOL READY / PROTECTION OFF / HEIGHT SYNC"
                    return
                end
                local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local dt = math.clamp(delta,0,0.25)
                local projected = footY + velocity.Y*dt - 0.5*world.Gravity*dt*dt
                local catching = not supported and velocity.Y <= 0 and (projected < state.floorY-0.08 or footY < state.floorY-0.08)
                state.platform.Size = Vector3.new(5,0.35,5)
                state.platform.Position = Vector3.new(root.Position.X,state.floorY-0.175,root.Position.Z)
                state.platform.CanCollide = not supported and velocity.Y <= 0 and footY <= state.floorY+0.25
                if catching then
                    root.CFrame = root.CFrame + Vector3.new(0,state.floorY+offset+0.1-root.Position.Y,0)
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X,0,velocity.Z)
                    if byKey.AntiFling then byKey.AntiFling.safeCFrame = root.CFrame end
                end
                state.statusMessage = string.format("SYNC Y %.1f / %s",state.floorY,supported and "FLOOR" or "PROTECT")
            end

            local function rankedPlayers(state)
                local players=Players:GetPlayers();table.sort(players,state.sortPlayers);return players
            end
            local function scanAvatars(state)
                if state.scanning then state.rankRescan=true;return end
                state.scanGeneration=(state.scanGeneration or 0)+1
                local generation=state.scanGeneration
                local players=Players:GetPlayers()
                local scanDeadline=os.clock()+150
                state.scanning,state.rankRescan=true,false
                state.priceResults=state.priceResults or {}
                for _,player in ipairs(players) do
                    if not state.priceResults[player.UserId] then
                        state.priceResults[player.UserId]={terminal=true,total=0,known=0,unknown=0,synthetic=0,failures=0,comparable=true,reason="First scan queued (source default 0)"}
                    end
                end
                state.rankDirty=true
                local nextIndex,remaining,completed=1,2,0
                local function valid() return alive and state.enabled and state.scanGeneration==generation end
                local function finish()
                    remaining=remaining-1
                    if remaining~=0 or not valid() then return end
                    for _,player in ipairs(players) do
                        local result=state.priceResults[player.UserId]
                        if result and result.loading then
                            state.priceResults[player.UserId]={terminal=true,total=0,known=0,unknown=0,reason="Scan timeout: source default 0 / SYNC NOW",comparable=true}
                        end
                    end
                    state.scanning=false;state.nextRankSync=os.clock()+RedTelemetry.AvatarService.UPDATE_INTERVAL
                    state.statusMessage="OUTFIT ESTIMATE / REFRESH 5S"
                    state.rankDirty=true
                end
                for _=1,2 do
                    task.spawn(function()
                        while valid() and os.clock()<scanDeadline do
                            local index=nextIndex;nextIndex=nextIndex+1
                            local player=players[index]
                            if not player then break end
                            state.statusMessage="SYNC "..(completed+1).."/"..#players.." / BOUNDED LOOKUP"
                            local version=appearanceVersions[player.UserId] or 0
                            local ok,result=pcall(function()
                                return RedTelemetry.AvatarService.value(player,valid,math.min(25,math.max(0.05,scanDeadline-os.clock())))
                            end)
                            if not valid() then break end
                            if not ok or not result then result={terminal=true,total=0,known=0,unknown=0,comparable=true,failures=1,reason="Lookup failed: source default 0 / SYNC NOW retries"} end
                            if inServer(player) and version==(appearanceVersions[player.UserId] or 0) then state.priceResults[player.UserId]=result
                            elseif inServer(player) then
                                state.priceResults[player.UserId]={terminal=true,total=0,known=0,unknown=0,comparable=true,reason="Outfit changed / resync queued"}
                                state.rankRescan=true
                            end
                            completed=completed+1;state.rankDirty=true
                        end
                        finish()
                    end)
                end
            end
            local function rosterList(state,root,y,height)
                addPlayerSearch(state,root,y)
                local list=Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                list.Name,list.Size,list.Position="ServerPlayers",UDim2.new(1,-20,0,height),UDim2.new(0,10,0,y+34)
                list.BackgroundColor3,list.BorderSizePixel,list.ScrollBarThickness=colors.Field,0,3
                list.AutomaticCanvasSize,list.CanvasSize,list.Parent=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,UDim2.new(),root
                local layout=Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                layout.SortOrder,layout.Padding,layout.Parent=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,UDim.new(0,5),list
                state.playerList=list
            end
            local function describePrice(state,player)
                local result=state.priceResults and state.priceResults[player.UserId]
                if not result then return "0 R$ / first scan queued" end
                return string.format("%.0f R$ / SCRIPT ESTIMATE / %d fallback(s), %d zero lookup(s)",result.total or 0,result.synthetic or 0,result.failures or 0)..(result.reason and (" / "..result.reason) or "")
            end
            local function prepareRank(state)
                if state.sortPlayers then return end
                state.priceResults=state.priceResults or {}
                state.sortPlayers=function(a,b)
                    local x,y=state.priceResults[a.UserId],state.priceResults[b.UserId]
                    local av,bv=x and x.total or 0,y and y.total or 0
                    if av~=bv then return av>bv end
                    local an,bn=a.Name:lower(),b.Name:lower()
                    if an~=bn then return an<bn end
                    return a.UserId<b.UserId
                end
                state.describePlayerRow=function(player)
                    local position=1
                    local ordered=Players:GetPlayers();table.sort(ordered,state.sortPlayers)
                    for index,candidate in ipairs(ordered) do if candidate==player then position=index;break end end
                    return "#"..position.." / "..describePrice(state,player)
                end
            end
            local function updateOutfitPreview(state,player)
                local version=appearanceVersions[player.UserId] or 0
                if state.previewUserId==player.UserId and state.previewVersion==version then return end
                state.previewUserId,state.previewVersion=player.UserId,version
                state.previewGeneration=(state.previewGeneration or 0)+1
                local generation=state.previewGeneration
                state.outfitImage.Image="rbxthumb://type=Avatar&id="..player.UserId.."&w=420&h=420"
                state.outfitCaption.Text="PROFILE AVATAR PREVIEW"
                task.spawn(function()
                    local function valid() return alive and state.enabled and state.previewGeneration==generation end
                    local image=RedTelemetry.AvatarService.preview(player.UserId,valid)
                    if valid() then state.outfitImage.Image=image end
                end)
            end
            local function showRank(state)
                prepareRank(state)
                if not state.playersPanel then
                    local root=panel("QuickAvatarPrices","AVATAR / OUTFIT VALUE LEADERBOARD",460,570,function() if state.playersPanel then state.playersPanel.Visible=false end end)
                    state.playersPanel=root
                    state.playerStatus=label(root,"Select a player. Supplied outfit-value logic.\nRefresh: 5 seconds; limited fallback: 1000 R$.",UDim2.new(1,-175,0,46),UDim2.new(0,10,0,39),11,colors.White,true)
                    state.playerStatus.TextWrapped=true
                    button(root,"SYNC NOW",UDim2.new(0,106,0,28),UDim2.new(0,10,0,91),function() scanAvatars(state) end)
                    button(root,"TOP VALUE",UDim2.new(0,111,0,28),UDim2.new(0,124,0,91),function()
                        local top=rankedPlayers(state)[1]
                        if top and state.priceResults[top.UserId] and state.priceResults[top.UserId].comparable then pickPlayer(state,top)
                        else state.playerNotice.Text="No completed outfit-value result yet." end
                    end)
                    label(root,"Shirt + Pants + GraphicTShirt + GetAccessories(). Item values cached, failed lookups = 0. Preview is the profile avatar.",UDim2.new(1,-175,0,52),UDim2.new(0,10,0,124),10,colors.Muted).TextWrapped=true
                    local image=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                    image.Name,image.Size,image.Position="SelectedOutfit",UDim2.new(0,135,0,133),UDim2.new(1,-145,0,39)
                    image.BackgroundColor3,image.BorderSizePixel,image.Image=colors.Field,0,""
                    image.Parent=root;RedUI.Corner(image,12)
                    state.outfitImage=image
                    state.outfitCaption=label(root,"PROFILE AVATAR",UDim2.new(0,135,0,18),UDim2.new(1,-145,0,175),8,colors.Muted,true)
                    state.gotoDistance=inputBox(root,"5",UDim2.new(0,55,0,28),UDim2.new(0,10,0,184));state.gotoSide="Behind"
                    button(root,"TP SELECTED",UDim2.new(0,160,0,28),UDim2.new(0,73,0,184),function() gotoSelected(state) end)
                    label(root,"Offset in studs (behind). Provided logic: PriceInRobux first; limited fallback 1000.",UDim2.new(1,-20,0,20),UDim2.new(0,10,0,216),9,colors.Muted)
                    rosterList(state,root,240,237)
                    state.playerNotice=label(root,"Uses your supplied valuation logic. Limited fallback 1000 and failed lookup 0 are estimates, not exact market value or account wealth.",UDim2.new(1,-20,0,45),UDim2.new(0,10,0,518),10,colors.Muted)
                    state.playerNotice.TextWrapped=true
                    state.onPickPlayer=function(player)
                        state.playerStatus.Text=player.DisplayName.." (@"..player.Name..")\n"..describePrice(state,player)
                        updateOutfitPreview(state,player)
                    end
                end
                state.playersPanel.Visible=true;refreshPlayerRows(state)
                if not state.selectedPlayer then pickPlayer(state,LocalPlayer) end
                if not state.scanning and (not state.nextRankSync or os.clock()>=state.nextRankSync) then scanAvatars(state) end
            end
            local function showManager(state)
                if not state.playersPanel then
                    local root=panel("QuickSpamManager","DELETE BAD PEOPLE / LOCAL MANAGER",430,510,function() if state.playersPanel then state.playersPanel.Visible=false end end)
                    state.playersPanel=root
                    state.playerStatus=label(root,"Select a player. Detection: 3 messages / 2 seconds.\nThis does not kick, delete or punish anyone on the server.",UDim2.new(1,-20,0,45),UDim2.new(0,10,0,39),11,colors.White,true)
                    state.playerStatus.TextWrapped=true
                    local function selectedAction(action)
                        local player=state.selectedPlayer
                        if not player or player==LocalPlayer or not inServer(player) then state.playerNotice.Text="Select another player who is still in this server.";return end
                        local id=player.UserId
                        if action=="hide" then allowed[id]=nil;flagPlayer(player,"Manually hidden")
                        elseif action=="restore" then blocked[id]=nil;messageTimes[id]=nil;grace[id]=os.clock()+5
                        elseif action=="allow" then
                            allowed[id]=not allowed[id];blocked[id]=nil;messageTimes[id]=nil;grace[id]=os.clock()+5
                        end
                        syncHidden();renderLocalChat();refreshPlayerRows(state)
                        state.playerNotice.Text="Updated @"..player.Name..". Restore has 5s grace. Be Alone, if ON, still hides everyone."
                    end
                    for index,entry in ipairs({{"HIDE","hide"},{"RESTORE","restore"},{"ALLOWLIST","allow"}}) do
                        button(root,entry[1],UDim2.new(1/3,-14,0,28),UDim2.new((index-1)/3,10,0,95),function() selectedAction(entry[2]) end)
                    end
                    button(root,"RESET HIDDEN",UDim2.new(0.5,-15,0,27),UDim2.new(0,10,0,132),function()
                        blocked,messageTimes,grace={},{},{}
                        syncHidden();renderLocalChat();refreshPlayerRows(state)
                    end)
                    button(root,"SHOW FILTERED CHAT",UDim2.new(0.5,-15,0,27),UDim2.new(0.5,5,0,132),function() chatClosed=false;renderLocalChat() end)
                    label(root,"Audio API VC only; legacy VC/custom chat may need Roblox mute controls.",UDim2.new(1,-20,0,24),UDim2.new(0,10,0,162),10,colors.Muted).TextWrapped=true
                    rosterList(state,root,192,224)
                    state.describePlayerRow=function(player)
                        if player==LocalPlayer then return "YOU / never auto-hidden" end
                        if blocked[player.UserId] then return "HIDDEN / "..blocked[player.UserId] end
                        return allowed[player.UserId] and "ALLOWLISTED / tap ALLOWLIST to remove" or "NORMAL / monitoring received messages"
                    end
                    state.playerNotice=label(root,"Filtered local chat replaces the default window/bubbles while ON. Old native history returns on OFF.",UDim2.new(1,-20,0,48),UDim2.new(0,10,0,456),10,colors.Muted)
                    state.playerNotice.TextWrapped=true
                    state.onPickPlayer=function(player) state.playerStatus.Text=player.DisplayName.." (@"..player.Name..")\n"..state.describePlayerRow(player) end
                end
                state.playersPanel.Visible=true;refreshPlayerRows(state)
            end
            local function appearanceChanged(player)
                appearanceVersions[player.UserId]=(appearanceVersions[player.UserId] or 0)+1
                local state=byKey.AvatarRankTool
                if state and state.enabled then
                    if state.priceResults then state.priceResults[player.UserId]=nil end
                    state.rankRescan,state.rankDirty=true,true
                end
            end
            local function watchPlayer(player)
                if appearanceConnections[player] then return end
                local list={player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                    syncHidden()
                    task.delay(1,function() if alive and inServer(player) then appearanceChanged(player) end end)
                end)}
                pcall(function() table.insert(list,player.CharacterAppearanceLoaded:Connect(function() appearanceChanged(player) end)) end)
                appearanceConnections[player]=list
            end
            local function refreshSocialRosters()
                for _,key in ipairs({"AvatarRankTool","DeleteBadPeople"}) do
                    local state=byKey[key]
                    if state and state.enabled and state.playersPanel and state.playersPanel.Visible then refreshPlayerRows(state) end
                end
            end
            for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do watchPlayer(player) end
            connect(Players.PlayerAdded,function(player) watchPlayer(player);appearanceChanged(player);syncHidden();refreshSocialRosters() end)
            connect(Players.PlayerRemoving,function(player)
                releaseHidden(player)
                disconnectList(appearanceConnections[player]);appearanceConnections[player]=nil
                blocked[player.UserId],messageTimes[player.UserId],grace[player.UserId]=nil,nil,nil
                local state=byKey.AvatarRankTool
                if state then
                    if state.priceResults then state.priceResults[player.UserId]=nil end
                    if state.selectedPlayer==player then
                        state.selectedPlayer=nil
                        if state.playerStatus then state.playerStatus.Text="Selected player left the server." end
                    end
                    state.rankDirty=true
                end
                local manager=byKey.DeleteBadPeople
                if manager and manager.selectedPlayer==player then
                    manager.selectedPlayer=nil
                    if manager.playerStatus then manager.playerStatus.Text="Selected player left the server." end
                end
                task.defer(refreshSocialRosters)
            end)
            function social.apply(state)
                if state.key=="BeAlone" then
                    applyQuietSettings(state);syncHidden();renderLocalChat()
                    state.statusMessage="LOCAL HIDE / CHAT OFF / VC PARTIAL"
                    return true
                elseif state.key=="DeleteBadPeople" then
                    applyQuietSettings(state)
                    syncHidden();renderLocalChat()
                    ensureTool(state,"Spam Manager",function() showManager(state) end,function() showManager(state) end,function() if state.playersPanel then state.playersPanel.Visible=false end end)
                    state.statusMessage=chatHookReady and "3 MSG / 2S / LOCAL FILTER" or "MODERN CHAT EVENT UNAVAILABLE"
                    return true
                elseif state.key=="AvatarRankTool" then
                    ensureTool(state,"Avatar Prices",function() showRank(state) end,function() showRank(state) end,function() if state.playersPanel then state.playersPanel.Visible=false end end)
                    prepareRank(state)
                    if not state.nextRankSync and not state.scanning then scanAvatars(state) end
                    return true
                end
                return false
            end
            function social.restore(state,characterOnly)
                if state.key=="BeAlone" then
                    if not characterOnly then releaseOwner(state);syncHidden();renderLocalChat() end
                    return true
                elseif state.key=="DeleteBadPeople" or state.key=="AvatarRankTool" then
                    destroyTool(state)
                    if state.playersPanel then state.playersPanel.Visible=false end
                    state.playerListGeneration=(state.playerListGeneration or 0)+1
                    state.previewGeneration=(state.previewGeneration or 0)+1
                    state.previewUserId=nil
                    state.selectedPlayer=nil
                    if not characterOnly then
                        if state.key=="DeleteBadPeople" then
                            blocked,messageTimes,grace,seenMessages,seenOrder,chatRows={},{},{},{},{},{}
                            releaseOwner(state);syncHidden();renderLocalChat()
                        else
                            state.scanGeneration=(state.scanGeneration or 0)+1
                            state.scanning,state.nextRankSync,state.rankRescan=false,nil,false
                            state.priceResults={}
                        end
                    end
                    return true
                end
                return false
            end
            function social.heartbeat(delta)
                socialPulse,rankPulse=socialPulse+delta,rankPulse+delta
                if socialPulse>=0.35 then
                    socialPulse=0
                    syncHidden()
                    for _,properties in pairs(slots) do
                        for _,entry in pairs(properties) do
                            if entry.custom then
                                local ok,current=pcall(entry.read)
                                if ok and current~=entry.forced then entry.original=current;pcall(entry.write,entry.forced) end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                local state=byKey.AvatarRankTool
                if state and state.enabled then
                    if not state.scanning and (state.rankRescan or not state.nextRankSync or os.clock()>=state.nextRankSync) then scanAvatars(state) end
                    if rankPulse>=0.5 and state.rankDirty and state.playersPanel and state.playersPanel.Visible then
                        state.rankDirty=false;refreshPlayerRows(state)
                        if state.selectedPlayer and state.onPickPlayer then state.onPickPlayer(state.selectedPlayer) end
                    end
                end
                if rankPulse>=0.5 then rankPulse=0 end
            end
            function social.reset()
                blocked,allowed,messageTimes,grace,seenMessages,seenOrder,chatRows={},{},{},{},{},{},{}
                syncHidden();renderLocalChat()
            end
            function social.shutdown()
                for player in pairs(hiddenRecords) do releaseHidden(player) end
                for owner in pairs(ownerEntries) do releaseOwner(owner) end
                for _,record in pairs(audioInputs) do if record.connection then record.connection:Disconnect() end end
                for _,list in pairs(appearanceConnections) do disconnectList(list) end
                audioInputs,appearanceConnections={},{ }
                if chatPanel then chatPanel:Destroy();chatPanel=nil end
            end
            return social
        end)()
        -- Public-server actions are explicit one-shot commands, not toggles.
        -- Local Anti Kick only intercepts supported client :Kick() namecalls.
        -- Anti Ban Guard is failure/retry backoff, NEVER a server/platform ban bypass.
        Session = (function()
            local session={}
            local teleport=game:GetService("TeleportService")
            local httpService=game:GetService("HttpService")
            local generation,busy,pending,lastAction=0,false,nil,-math.huge
            local guardTripped=false
            local history=genv._DEATH_NOTE_HOP_HISTORY
            if type(history)~="table" then history={};genv._DEATH_NOTE_HOP_HISTORY=history end
            local placeKey=tostring(game.PlaceId)
            history[placeKey]=history[placeKey] or {}
            if type(game.JobId)=="string" and game.JobId~="" then history[placeKey][game.JobId]=os.time() end
            local kick=genv._DEATH_NOTE_LOCAL_KICK_GUARD
            if type(kick)~="table" then kick={active=false,blocked=0};genv._DEATH_NOTE_LOCAL_KICK_GUARD=kick end
            local function status(state,message)
                if not alive then return end
                state.statusMessage=message
                if state.repaint then state.repaint() end
            end
            local function kickEnable(state)
                if kick.installed then kick.player=LocalPlayer;kick.active=true;status(state,"LOCAL KICK ONLY / "..kick.blocked.." BLOCKED");return end
                if type(hookmetamethod)~="function" or type(getnamecallmethod)~="function" or type(getrawmetatable)~="function" then
                    kick.active=false;status(state,"UNSUPPORTED / NO PROTECTION")
                    notify("Anti Kick needs supported executor hooks. Server kicks cannot be blocked.");return
                end
                local ok,err=pcall(function()
                    local raw=getrawmetatable(game)
                    local original=raw and raw.__namecall
                    if type(original)~="function" then error("Original namecall unavailable") end
                    local getMethod=getnamecallmethod
                    local wrapper=function(target,...)
                        if kick.active and target==kick.player and string.lower(tostring(getMethod()))=="kick" then
                            kick.blocked=kick.blocked+1;return nil
                        end
                        return original(target,...)
                    end
                    if type(newcclosure)=="function" then wrapper=newcclosure(wrapper) end
                    local previous=hookmetamethod(game,"__namecall",wrapper)
                    if type(previous)=="function" then original=previous end
                    kick.installed,kick.player,kick.active=true,LocalPlayer,true
                end)
                if ok then status(state,"LOCAL NAMECALL KICK GUARD")
                else kick.active=false;status(state,"HOOK FAILED / NO PROTECTION");notify(tostring(err):sub(1,120)) end
            end
            function session.canTeleport()
                if not alive then return false,"Interface closed" end
                if byKey.AntiBanGuard and byKey.AntiBanGuard.enabled and guardTripped then
                    return false,"Retry guard stopped joins after a teleport failure. Turn Anti Ban Guard OFF to retry; it cannot remove bans."
                end
                if pending then return false,"A server join is already pending" end
                return true
            end
            local function clearActionBusy()
                for _,state in ipairs(states) do
                    if state.command and state.busy then state.busy=false;if state.repaint then state.repaint() end end
                end
            end
            local function tripFailure(message)
                generation=generation+1
                clearActionBusy()
                if byKey.AntiBanGuard and byKey.AntiBanGuard.enabled then
                    guardTripped=true
                    status(byKey.AntiBanGuard,"RETRY STOPPED / NOT AN UNBAN")
                end
                if pending then status(pending.state,"JOIN FAILED / "..tostring(message):sub(1,70)) end
                pending=nil;busy=false
            end
            pcall(function()
                connect(teleport.TeleportInitFailed,function(player,result,errorMessage)
                    if player==LocalPlayer then tripFailure(errorMessage or tostring(result)) end
                end)
            end)
            pcall(function()
                connect(LocalPlayer.OnTeleport,function(phase)
                    if phase==Enum.TeleportState.Failed then tripFailure("Teleport failed") end
                end)
            end)
            local function validServer(row)
                if type(row)~="table" or type(row.id)~="string" or row.id=="" or #row.id>128 or row.id==game.JobId then return false end
                local playing,maxPlayers=finiteNumber(row.playing),finiteNumber(row.maxPlayers)
                if not playing or not maxPlayers or playing<0 or maxPlayers<=0 or playing>=maxPlayers then return false end
                row.playing,row.maxPlayers=playing,maxPlayers
                return true
            end
            local function serverCandidates(mode,state,token)
                local cursor,rows,seen=nil,{},{}
                local order=mode=="ServerMost" and "Desc" or "Asc"
                local maxPages=(mode=="ServerLatency" or mode=="ServerHop") and 5 or 3
                local function valid() return alive and generation==token end
                for pageNumber=1,maxPages do
                    if not valid() then return nil,"cancelled" end
                    status(state,"READING PUBLIC SERVERS / PAGE "..pageNumber)
                    local url="https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"..placeKey.."/servers/Public?sortOrder="..order.."&limit=100&excludeFullGames=true"
                    if cursor then url=url.."&cursor="..httpService:UrlEncode(cursor) end
                    local ok,response=RedTelemetry.AvatarService.bounded(8,function()
                        local body=game:HttpGet(url)
                        if type(body)~="string" or #body>2000000 then error("Invalid server-list response") end
                        local decoded=httpService:JSONDecode(body)
                        if type(decoded)~="table" or type(decoded.data)~="table" then error("Public server list unavailable") end
                        return decoded
                    end,valid)
                    if not ok then return nil,"Server list "..tostring(response) end
                    for _,row in ipairs(response.data) do
                        if validServer(row) and not seen[row.id] then seen[row.id]=true;table.insert(rows,row) end
                    end
                    if (mode=="ServerMost" or mode=="ServerLeast") and #rows>0 then break end
                    local nextCursor=response.nextPageCursor
                    if type(nextCursor)~="string" or nextCursor=="" or nextCursor==cursor then break end
                    cursor=nextCursor;task.wait(0.25)
                end
                return rows
            end
            local function chooseServer(mode,rows)
                if #rows==0 then return nil,"No different, non-full public server found" end
                if mode=="ServerMost" then table.sort(rows,function(a,b) return a.playing>b.playing end);return rows[1]
                elseif mode=="ServerLeast" then table.sort(rows,function(a,b) return a.playing<b.playing end);return rows[1]
                elseif mode=="ServerLatency" then
                    local best
                    for _,row in ipairs(rows) do
                        local ping=finiteNumber(row.ping)
                        if ping and ping>0 and ping<60000 and (not best or ping<best.ping) then best={id=row.id,playing=row.playing,maxPlayers=row.maxPlayers,ping=ping} end
                    end
                    return best,best and nil or "No valid reported ping; personal latency cannot be measured before joining"
                end
                local unvisited={}
                for _,row in ipairs(rows) do if not history[placeKey][row.id] then table.insert(unvisited,row) end end
                if #unvisited==0 then history[placeKey]={[game.JobId]=os.time()};unvisited=rows end
                return unvisited[math.random(1,#unvisited)]
            end
            local function submit(state,server,token)
                if not alive or generation~=token then return end
                local allowed,message=session.canTeleport()
                if not allowed then status(state,message);return end
                local request={state=state,id=server.id,token=token,created=os.clock()}
                pending=request
                local ok,err=pcall(function() teleport:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId,server.id,LocalPlayer) end)
                if not ok then tripFailure(err);return end
                if pending~=request then return end
                history[placeKey][server.id]=os.time()
                local count=0;for _ in pairs(history[placeKey]) do count=count+1 end
                if count>500 then history[placeKey]={[game.JobId]=os.time(),[server.id]=os.time()} end
                local text=state.key=="ServerLatency" and ("JOIN REQUESTED / REPORTED "..tostring(server.ping).."MS") or ("JOIN REQUESTED / "..server.playing.." PLAYERS")
                status(state,text)
                task.delay(20,function()
                    if alive and pending==request and os.clock()-request.created>=19.9 then tripFailure("Join not completed after 20 seconds") end
                end)
            end
            function session.run(state)
                if not alive then return end
                if state.key=="ResetCharacter" then
                    if pending then status(state,"WAIT FOR PENDING SERVER JOIN");return end
                    generation=generation+1;busy=false;clearActionBusy()
                    local target=currentHumanoid()
                    if not target or target.Health<=0 then status(state,"NO LIVING CHARACTER");return end
                    api.cancelExclusive();stopMotions()
                    for _,feature in ipairs(states) do if feature.enabled then restoreFeature(feature,true) end end
                    target.Sit=false;target.Health=0
                    pcall(function() target:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) end)
                    status(state,"RESET REQUESTED / GAME RESPAWN")
                    return
                end
                local allowed,message=session.canTeleport()
                if not allowed then status(state,message);return end
                if busy or os.clock()-lastAction<1.5 then status(state,"WAIT / ONE SERVER ACTION AT A TIME");return end
                if not finiteNumber(game.PlaceId) or game.PlaceId<=0 then status(state,"INVALID PLACE ID");return end
                generation=generation+1
                local token=generation
                busy,state.busy,lastAction=true,true,os.clock()
                status(state,"FINDING A PUBLIC SERVER")
                task.spawn(function()
                    local ok,rows,failure=pcall(serverCandidates,state.key,state,token)
                    if not alive or generation~=token then return end
                    busy,state.busy=false,false
                    if not ok or not rows then status(state,tostring(failure or rows):sub(1,100));return end
                    local chosen,reason=chooseServer(state.key,rows)
                    if not chosen then status(state,reason);return end
                    submit(state,chosen,token)
                    if state.repaint then state.repaint() end
                end)
            end
            function session.apply(state)
                if state.key=="AntiKick" then kickEnable(state);return true end
                if state.key=="AntiBanGuard" then
                    if not state.guardArmed then guardTripped=false;state.guardArmed=true end
                    status(state,guardTripped and "RETRY STOPPED / NOT AN UNBAN" or "RETRY GUARD / NO BAN BYPASS")
                    return true
                end
                if state.command then return true end
                return false
            end
            function session.restore(state,characterOnly)
                if state.key=="AntiKick" then if not characterOnly then kick.active=false end;return true end
                if state.key=="AntiBanGuard" then if not characterOnly then guardTripped=false;state.guardArmed=false end;return true end
                if state.command then return true end
                return false
            end
            function session.heartbeat()
                if byKey.AntiKick and byKey.AntiKick.enabled and kick.active then byKey.AntiKick.statusMessage="LOCAL ONLY / "..kick.blocked.." BLOCKED" end
            end
            function session.shutdown()
                generation=generation+1;busy=false;pending=nil;kick.active=false
            end
            RedTelemetry.SessionGuard=session
            return session
        end)()
        -- Reversible local graphics, mobile/PC shift lock, normal weapon use and native F9.
        -- No server damage remotes, forced touches, asset deletion, or startup gate.
        Extra = (function()
            local e={}
            local lighting=game:GetService("Lighting")
            local starter=game:GetService("StarterGui")
            local modes={}
            -- Original graphics state must survive Lua GC until OFF/reset cleanup.
            -- Weak instance keys can lose restoration records while the boost is active.
            -- Entries are released normally after the final graphics owner turns OFF.
            local ledger={}
            local owned=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
            local priority={FPSBooster=1,LagDestroyer=2,BestGraphics=3}
            local quality
            pcall(function() quality=UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings") end)
            local shift={locked=true}
            local consolePrior,consoleOwned=nil,false
            local resetPanel
            local function read(object,key)
                local ok,value=pcall(function() return object[key] end)
                if ok then return value end
            end
            local function write(object,key,value) return pcall(function() object[key]=value end) end
            local function force(mode,object,key,value)
                if not object then return end
                local current=read(object,key)
                if current==nil then return end
                local entries=ledger[object]
                if not entries then entries={};ledger[object]=entries end
                local record=entries[key]
                if not record then record={original=current,owners={}};entries[key]=record
                elseif current~=record.forced then record.original=current end
                record.owners[mode]=value
                local selected,rank=nil,-1
                for owner,v in pairs(record.owners) do if priority[owner]>rank then selected,rank=v,priority[owner] end end
                if write(object,key,selected) then record.forced=selected end
            end
            local function release(mode)
                for object,entries in pairs(ledger) do
                    for key,record in pairs(entries) do
                        if record.owners[mode]~=nil then
                            local current=read(object,key)
                            if current~=nil and current~=record.forced then record.original=current end
                            record.owners[mode]=nil
                            local selected,rank=nil,-1
                            for owner,v in pairs(record.owners) do if priority[owner]>rank then selected,rank=v,priority[owner] end end
                            if rank<0 then
                                if current==record.forced then write(object,key,record.original) end
                                entries[key]=nil
                            elseif write(object,key,selected) then record.forced=selected end
                        end
                    end
                    if next(entries)==nil then ledger[object]=nil end
                end
            end
            local function protected(node)
                local cursor=node
                while cursor and cursor~=world and cursor~=lighting do
                    if cursor:IsA("Tool") then return true end
                    if cursor:IsA("Model") and cursor:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return true end
                    cursor=cursor.Parent
                end
                return false
            end
            local function atmosphere(mode,node)
                if mode=="BestGraphics" then
                    force(mode,node,"Density",0.25);force(mode,node,"Haze",0.7);force(mode,node,"Glare",0.1)
                    force(mode,node,"Color",Color3.fromRGB(199,212,235));force(mode,node,"Decay",Color3.fromRGB(110,125,145))
                else force(mode,node,"Density",0);force(mode,node,"Haze",0);force(mode,node,"Glare",0) end
            end
            local function process(mode,node)
                if owned[node] then return end
                if node:IsA("PostEffect") then force(mode,node,"Enabled",false) end
                if node:IsA("Atmosphere") then atmosphere(mode,node) end
                if mode=="BestGraphics" then return end
                if node:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or node:IsA("Trail") or node:IsA("Beam") or node:IsA("Smoke") or node:IsA("Fire") or node:IsA("Sparkles") then force(mode,node,"Enabled",false) end
                if node:IsA("BasePart") and not node:IsA("Terrain") then
                    force(mode,node,"CastShadow",false)
                    if mode=="LagDestroyer" then force(mode,node,"Material",Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic);force(mode,node,"Reflectance",0) end
                end
                if mode=="LagDestroyer" then
                    if node:IsA("Decal") or node:IsA("Texture") then force(mode,node,"Transparency",1) end
                    if node:IsA("Light") then force(mode,node,"Enabled",false) end
                end
            end
            local function enqueue(mode,node)
                local data=modes[mode]
                if data and node and not data.seen[node] then
                    data.seen[node]=true;data.tail=data.tail+1;data.queue[data.tail]=node
                end
            end
            local function createEffect(data,class,props)
                local object=Instance.new(class)
                owned[object]=true;table.insert(data.objects,object)
                object.Name="DEATH_NOTE_HDR_"..class
                for key,value in pairs(props) do write(object,key,value) end
                object.Parent=lighting
                return object
            end
            local function begin(mode)
                if modes[mode] then return end
                local data={queue={},head=1,tail=0,seen=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"}),objects={}}
                modes[mode]=data
                if mode=="BestGraphics" then
                    local properties={Brightness=2,ClockTime=14.5,ExposureCompensation=0.15,GlobalShadows=true,ShadowSoftness=0.25,
                        Ambient=Color3.fromRGB(80,86,100),OutdoorAmbient=Color3.fromRGB(125,133,146),EnvironmentDiffuseScale=0.6,EnvironmentSpecularScale=1}
                    for key,value in pairs(properties) do force(mode,lighting,key,value) end
                    for _,node in ipairs(lighting:GetDescendants()) do process(mode,node) end
                    createEffect(data,"BloomEffect",{Intensity=0.12,Size=24,Threshold=1.25})
                    createEffect(data,"ColorCorrectionEffect",{Contrast=0.12,Saturation=0.08,Brightness=0.015,TintColor=Color3.fromRGB(245,247,255)})
                    createEffect(data,"SunRaysEffect",{Intensity=0.035,Spread=0.8})
                    if not lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere") then
                        createEffect(data,"Atmosphere",{Density=0.25,Haze=0.7,Glare=0.1,Color=Color3.fromRGB(199,212,235),Decay=Color3.fromRGB(110,125,145)})
                    end
                    if quality then force(mode,quality,"SavedQualityLevel",Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel10) end
                else
                    force(mode,lighting,"GlobalShadows",false)
                    if quality then force(mode,quality,"SavedQualityLevel",Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel1) end
                    enqueue(mode,world);enqueue(mode,lighting)
                    if mode=="LagDestroyer" then
                        local terrain=world:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
                        for key,value in pairs({WaterWaveSize=0,WaterWaveSpeed=0,WaterReflectance=0}) do force(mode,terrain,key,value) end
                    end
                end
            end
            local function finish(mode)
                local data=modes[mode];modes[mode]=nil
                release(mode)
                if data then for _,object in ipairs(data.objects) do owned[object]=nil;object:Destroy() end end
            end
            local function queueNew(node)
                for mode in pairs(modes) do
                    if mode~="BestGraphics" or node:IsDescendantOf(lighting) then enqueue(mode,node) end
                end
            end
            connect(world.DescendantAdded,queueNew)
            connect(lighting.DescendantAdded,queueNew)
            local function processQueues()
                local deadline=os.clock()+0.0025
                local count=0
                for mode,data in pairs(modes) do
                    local batch=0
                    while data.head<=data.tail and count<60 and batch<25 and os.clock()<=deadline do
                        local node=data.queue[data.head];data.queue[data.head]=nil;data.head=data.head+1;count=count+1;batch=batch+1
                        if node and node.Parent and not protected(node) then
                            process(mode,node)
                            for _,child in ipairs(node:GetChildren()) do enqueue(mode,child) end
                        end
                    end
                    if data.head>data.tail then data.queue={};data.head,data.tail=1,0 end
                    local state=byKey[mode]
                    if state then state.statusMessage=(mode=="BestGraphics" and "HDR STYLE / LOCAL LIGHTING") or (data.tail>=data.head and "APPLYING / BATCHED LOCAL CHANGES" or "LOCAL BOOST / NO PING GUARANTEE") end
                end
            end

            local function restoreShiftHumanoid()
                local saved=shift.humanoid
                if saved then
                    if read(saved.object,"AutoRotate")==false then write(saved.object,"AutoRotate",saved.rotate) end
                    if read(saved.object,"CameraOffset")==saved.forcedOffset then write(saved.object,"CameraOffset",saved.offset) end
                    shift.humanoid=nil
                end
            end
            function e.pauseShift()
                restoreShiftHumanoid()
                if shift.mouse then
                    if read(UserInputService,"MouseBehavior")==Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter then write(UserInputService,"MouseBehavior",shift.mouse.behavior) end
                    if read(UserInputService,"MouseIconEnabled")==false then write(UserInputService,"MouseIconEnabled",shift.mouse.icon) end
                    shift.mouse=nil
                end
                if shift.crosshair then shift.crosshair.Visible=false end
            end
            local function shiftBlocked(target,root,camera)
                if not target or target.Health<=0 or not root or root.Anchored or target.SeatPart or not camera or camera.CameraType==Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then return true end
                if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return true end
                local menu=false;pcall(function() menu=game:GetService("GuiService").MenuIsOpen end)
                if menu then return true end
                if byKey.FreezeTool and byKey.FreezeTool.frozen then return true end
                if byKey.Flashback and byKey.Flashback.replay then return true end
                if byKey.GroundSink and byKey.GroundSink.ground then return true end
                for _,key in ipairs({"SelfFling","Spin","WallWalk","HeadWalk","FlyR6","FlyR15","SwimR6","SwimR15","VehicleFly"}) do
                    if byKey[key] and byKey[key].enabled then return true end
                end
                return false
            end
            local function renderShift()
                local state=byKey.ShiftLock
                if not alive or not state or not state.enabled or not shift.locked then e.pauseShift();return end
                local target=currentHumanoid();local root=getRoot(target);local camera=world.CurrentCamera
                if shiftBlocked(target,root,camera) then e.pauseShift();state.statusMessage="LOCK PAUSED / CHARACTER OR CAMERA";return end
                if shift.humanoid and shift.humanoid.object~=target then restoreShiftHumanoid() end
                if not shift.humanoid then
                    local offset=read(target,"CameraOffset") or Vector3.zero
                    shift.humanoid={object=target,offset=offset,rotate=target.AutoRotate,forcedOffset=offset+Vector3.new(1.75,0,0)}
                end
                if not shift.mouse then shift.mouse={behavior=UserInputService.MouseBehavior,icon=UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled} end
                write(target,"CameraOffset",shift.humanoid.forcedOffset);write(target,"AutoRotate",false)
                write(UserInputService,"MouseBehavior",Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter);write(UserInputService,"MouseIconEnabled",false)
                local look=camera.CFrame.LookVector;local flat=Vector3.new(look.X,0,look.Z)
                if flat.Magnitude>0.01 then root.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(root.Position,root.Position+flat.Unit) end
                shift.crosshair.Visible=true;state.statusMessage="LOCKED / SHIFT OR TAP TO RELEASE"
            end
            local function toggleLock()
                local state=byKey.ShiftLock
                if not state or not state.enabled then return end
                shift.locked=not shift.locked
                if shift.button then shift.button.Text=shift.locked and "LOCK ON" or "LOCK OFF" end
                if not shift.locked then e.pauseShift();state.statusMessage="UNLOCKED / SHIFT OR TAP" end
            end
            connect(UserInputService.InputBegan,function(input,processed)
                if not processed and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() and (input.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift or input.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.RightShift) then toggleLock() end
            end)
            local function beginShift()
                if shift.started then return end
                shift.started,shift.locked=true,true
                if not shift.button then
                    shift.button=button(MainGui,"LOCK ON",UDim2.new(0,82,0,36),UDim2.new(1,-104,0.58,0),toggleLock,colors.Pink)
                    shift.button.Name,shift.button.ZIndex="QuickShiftLockButton",65
                    shift.crosshair=label(MainGui,"+",UDim2.new(0,24,0,24),UDim2.new(0.5,-12,0.5,-12),20,colors.White,true)
                    shift.crosshair.Name,shift.crosshair.ZIndex="QuickShiftCrosshair",64
                    shift.crosshair.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                end
                shift.button.Text,shift.button.Visible,shift.crosshair.Visible="LOCK ON",true,false
                local ok=pcall(function() RunService:BindToRenderStep("DEATH_NOTE_SHIFT_LOCK",Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value+1,renderShift) end)
                if not ok then shift.connection=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(renderShift) end
            end
            local function endShift()
                e.pauseShift();shift.started=false;shift.locked=true
                pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("DEATH_NOTE_SHIFT_LOCK") end)
                if shift.connection then shift.connection:Disconnect();shift.connection=nil end
                if shift.button then shift.button.Visible=false end
            end

            local function stopAttack(state,reason)
                state.attackGeneration=(state.attackGeneration or 0)+1
                state.attackActive,state.attackBusy=false,false
                if state.activatedTool then pcall(function() state.activatedTool:Deactivate() end);state.activatedTool=nil end
                if state.playerNotice then state.playerNotice.Text=reason or "Stopped. Select a player, hold your weapon, then press START." end
                state.statusMessage="STOPPED / MANUAL START REQUIRED"
            end
            local function showAttack(state)
                if not state.playersPanel then
                    local root=panel("QuickHandlekill","HANDLEKILL AUTO / SELECTED PLAYER",440,510,function()
                        stopAttack(state,"Stopped: panel closed.");state.playersPanel.Visible=false
                    end)
                    state.playersPanel=root
                    state.playerStatus=label(root,"Select a player. Hold your game's weapon and face the target.\nOnly normal Tool activation; damage is controlled by the game.",UDim2.new(1,-20,0,44),UDim2.new(0,10,0,38),10,colors.White,true)
                    state.playerStatus.TextWrapped=true
                    state.attackRange=inputBox(root,"6",UDim2.new(0,45,0,27),UDim2.new(0,10,0,101))
                    state.attackInterval=inputBox(root,"0.35",UDim2.new(0,48,0,27),UDim2.new(0,62,0,101))
                    label(root,"studs / seconds",UDim2.new(0,110,0,18),UDim2.new(0,10,0,82),9,colors.Muted)
                    button(root,"START",UDim2.new(0,91,0,27),UDim2.new(0,121,0,101),function()
                        if not state.enabled or not state.selectedPlayer or state.selectedPlayer==LocalPlayer or not inServer(state.selectedPlayer) then state.playerNotice.Text="Select another player first.";return end
                        local range,interval=finiteNumber(state.attackRange.Text),finiteNumber(state.attackInterval.Text)
                        if not range or not interval then state.playerNotice.Text="Enter valid range and interval numbers.";return end
                        state.attackDistance,state.attackEvery=math.clamp(range,1,15),math.clamp(interval,0.2,3)
                        state.attackRange.Text,state.attackInterval.Text=tostring(state.attackDistance),tostring(state.attackEvery)
                        stopAttack(state);state.attackActive=true;state.nextAttack=0
                        state.attackTargetCharacter=state.selectedPlayer.Character
                        state.attackLocalCharacter=LocalPlayer.Character
                        state.playerNotice.Text="Running only while selected target is nearby, in front and visible. Weapon damage/range are game-controlled."
                    end,colors.Cyan)
                    button(root,"STOP",UDim2.new(0,91,0,27),UDim2.new(0,223,0,101),function() stopAttack(state) end,colors.Pink)
                    addPlayerSearch(state,root,141)
                    local list=Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                    list.Name,list.Size,list.Position="ServerPlayers",UDim2.new(1,-20,0,248),UDim2.new(0,10,0,175)
                    list.BackgroundColor3,list.BorderSizePixel,list.ScrollBarThickness=colors.Field,0,3
                    list.AutomaticCanvasSize,list.CanvasSize,list.Parent=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,UDim2.new(),root
                    local layout=Instance.new("UIListLayout");layout.Padding,layout.SortOrder,layout.Parent=UDim.new(0,5),Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,list
                    state.playerList=list
                    state.playerNotice=label(root,"Damage cannot be auto-verified. Requires an equipped Tool with a real Handle. No forced kill, reach extension or auto-teleport.",UDim2.new(1,-20,0,66),UDim2.new(0,10,0,432),10,colors.Muted)
                    state.playerNotice.TextWrapped=true
                    state.onPickPlayer=function(player)
                        stopAttack(state,"Target changed. Press START to use the equipped weapon normally.")
                        if player==LocalPlayer then state.selectedPlayer=nil;state.playerStatus.Text="Choose another player, not yourself.";return end
                        state.playerStatus.Text=player.DisplayName.." (@"..player.Name..")\nFace target / hold damage Tool / press START"
                    end
                end
                state.playersPanel.Visible=true;refreshPlayerRows(state)
            end
            local function attackCheck(state)
                local player=state.selectedPlayer
                if not player or player==LocalPlayer or not inServer(player) then return nil,"Target left/unselected",true end
                local target=currentHumanoid();local root=getRoot(target)
                if LocalPlayer.Character~=state.attackLocalCharacter or player.Character~=state.attackTargetCharacter then return nil,"Character changed",true end
                local other=player.Character;local otherHum=other and other:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local otherRoot=other and other:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not target or target.Health<=0 then return nil,"Your character is unavailable",true end
                if not otherHum or otherHum.Health<=0 or not otherRoot then return nil,"Target dead/unavailable",true end
                local character=target.Parent
                local tool
                for _,candidate in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                    if candidate:IsA("Tool") and candidate.Enabled~=false then
                        local handle=candidate:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                        if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then tool=candidate;break end
                    end
                end
                if not tool then return nil,"Hold an enabled weapon Tool with Handle" end
                if not root then return nil,"Character root unavailable",true end
                local delta=otherRoot.Position-root.Position
                if delta.Magnitude>(state.attackDistance or 6) then return nil,"Selected target out of range" end
                if delta.Magnitude>0.1 and root.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(delta.Unit)<0.15 then return nil,"Face the selected target" end
                local params=RaycastParams.new();params.FilterType,params.FilterDescendantsInstances=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude,{character}
                local hit=delta.Magnitude>0.1 and world:Raycast(root.Position,delta,params) or nil
                if hit and hit.Instance and not hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(other) then return nil,"Target blocked by an obstacle" end
                return tool
            end
            local function attackTick()
                local state=byKey.HandlekillAuto
                if not state or not state.enabled or not state.attackActive or state.attackBusy or os.clock()<(state.nextAttack or 0) then return end
                local tool,reason,stop=attackCheck(state)
                if not tool then
                    if state.activatedTool then pcall(function() state.activatedTool:Deactivate() end);state.activatedTool=nil end
                    if stop then stopAttack(state,reason..". Press START again.") else state.statusMessage=reason end
                    state.nextAttack=os.clock()+0.2;return
                end
                state.nextAttack=os.clock()+(state.attackEvery or 0.35);state.attackBusy=true
                local generation=state.attackGeneration
                task.spawn(function()
                    if not alive or not state.enabled or not state.attackActive or state.attackGeneration~=generation then return end
                    local current=attackCheck(state)
                    if current~=tool then state.attackBusy=false;return end
                    state.activatedTool=tool
                    local ok=pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
                    if state.attackGeneration~=generation then return end
                    state.attackBusy=false
                    if not ok then stopAttack(state,"Tool activation unavailable; nothing forced.");return end
                    state.statusMessage="NORMAL TOOL USE / GAME DAMAGE RULES"
                    task.delay(0.08,function()
                        if state.activatedTool==tool and state.attackGeneration==generation then pcall(function() tool:Deactivate() end);state.activatedTool=nil end
                    end)
                end)
            end
            function e.beforeEnable(state)
                if state.key=="BestGraphics" then
                    for _,key in ipairs({"FPSBooster","LagDestroyer"}) do local other=byKey[key];if other and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false,true) end end
                elseif state.key=="FPSBooster" or state.key=="LagDestroyer" then
                    local other=byKey.BestGraphics;if other and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false,true) end
                end
            end
            function e.apply(state)
                if priority[state.key] then begin(state.key);return true end
                if state.key=="ShiftLock" then beginShift();return true end
                if state.key=="HandlekillAuto" then
                    if not state.extraStarted then state.extraStarted=true;showAttack(state) end
                    return true
                end
                return false
            end
            function e.restore(state,characterOnly)
                if priority[state.key] then if not characterOnly then finish(state.key) end;return true end
                if state.key=="ShiftLock" then if characterOnly then e.pauseShift() else endShift() end;return true end
                if state.key=="HandlekillAuto" then
                    stopAttack(state,characterOnly and "Character changed. Press START again." or "OFF / no more activations.")
                    if not characterOnly then state.extraStarted=false;state.selectedPlayer=nil;if state.playersPanel then state.playersPanel.Visible=false end end
                    return true
                end
                return false
            end
            local function restoreConsole()
                if consoleOwned and consolePrior~=nil then pcall(function() starter:SetCore("DevConsoleVisible",consolePrior) end) end
                consoleOwned,consolePrior=false,nil
            end
            function e.run(state)
                if state.key=="DeveloperConsole" then
                    if not consoleOwned then local ok,value=pcall(function() return starter:GetCore("DevConsoleVisible") end);if ok and type(value)=="boolean" then consolePrior=value end end
                    local ok=pcall(function() starter:SetCore("DevConsoleVisible",true) end)
                    if ok then consoleOwned=true end
                    state.statusMessage=ok and "NATIVE F9 / AVAILABLE TABS ONLY" or "CONSOLE NOT READY / PRESS AGAIN"
                    if state.repaint then state.repaint() end
                    return true
                elseif state.key=="ResetAllQuick" then
                    if not resetPanel then
                        resetPanel=panel("QuickResetConfirm","RESET ALL QUICK / CONFIRM",378,230,function() resetPanel.Visible=false end)
                        local text=label(resetPanel,"Turn OFF all QUICK features and restore their local changes/settings?\n\nGUI stays open. Character is NOT reset. Submitted teleports/chat and external Dex edits cannot be undone.",UDim2.new(1,-24,0,120),UDim2.new(0,12,0,42),11,colors.White)
                        text.TextWrapped=true
                        local cancel=button(resetPanel,"CANCEL",UDim2.new(0.5,-18,0,32),UDim2.new(0,12,1,-45),function() resetPanel.Visible=false end)
                        cancel.Name="CancelQuickReset"
                        local confirm=button(resetPanel,"YES, RESET",UDim2.new(0.5,-18,0,32),UDim2.new(0.5,6,1,-45),function()
                            resetPanel.Visible=false;api.resetAll()
                        end,colors.Pink)
                        confirm.Name="ConfirmQuickReset"
                    end
                    resetPanel.Visible=true
                    return true
                end
                return false
            end
            function e.heartbeat() processQueues();attackTick() end
            function e.reset()
                for _,mode in ipairs({"FPSBooster","LagDestroyer","BestGraphics"}) do finish(mode) end
                endShift();restoreConsole()
            end
            function e.shutdown() e.reset() end
            return e
        end)()

        RedTelemetry.StopQuickGraphics=function()
            for _,key in ipairs({"FPSBooster","LagDestroyer","BestGraphics"}) do
                local state=byKey[key]
                if state and state.enabled then state.setEnabled(false,true) end
            end
        end
        function api.beforeEnable(state, exclusive)
            if (state.key=="FPSBooster" or state.key=="LagDestroyer" or state.key=="BestGraphics") and RedTelemetry.Visuals then RedTelemetry.Visuals.disable() end
            Extra.beforeEnable(state)
            if exclusive then Extra.pauseShift() end
            if not exclusive then return end
            releaseFreeze(byKey.FreezeTool)
            if byKey.Flashback and byKey.Flashback.replay then stopReplay(byKey.Flashback) end
        end
        function api.cancelExclusive()
            Extra.pauseShift()
            releaseFreeze(byKey.FreezeTool)
            if byKey.Flashback and byKey.Flashback.replay then stopReplay(byKey.Flashback) end
        end
        function api.toggleReplay(state)
            if state.replay then stopReplay(state) else startReplay(state) end
            if state.repaint then state.repaint() end
        end
        function api.noteJump()
            if byKey.AntiSlapped then byKey.AntiSlapped.lastJump = os.clock() end
        end
        -- Anti Fall toggle grants a Tool. Only deliberate Tool activation protects.
        local function stopAntiFall(state,clearHeight)
            state.antiFallActive=false
            if state.platform then state.platform.CanCollide=false;state.platform:Destroy();state.platform=nil end
            if clearHeight then state.floorY=nil end
            if state.tool then state.tool.Name="Anti Fall [OFF]" end
            state.statusMessage="TOOL READY / PROTECTION OFF / TAP TO START"
        end
        function api.rebaseFall(state,target)
            if state.platform then state.platform.CanCollide=false;state.platform:Destroy();state.platform=nil end
            state.floorY=nil
            Social.syncFall(state,target,0)
            if state.antiFallActive then
                local height=state.floorY
                createPlatform(state,target)
                state.floorY=height
                Social.syncFall(state,target,0)
            end
        end
        local function toggleAntiFall(state)
            if not alive or not state.enabled then return end
            if state.antiFallActive then stopAntiFall(state,false);return end
            local target=currentHumanoid();local root=getRoot(target)
            if not target or target.Health<=0 or not root then notify("Use Anti Fall with a living character.");return end
            if target.SeatPart or root.Anchored then notify("Leave your seat/unfreeze before Anti Fall.");return end
            api.cancelExclusive()
            for _,key in ipairs(movementGroup) do local other=byKey[key];if other and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false,true) end end
            for _,key in ipairs({"SelfFling","Spin","WallWalk","HeadWalk"}) do local other=byKey[key];if other and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false,true) end end
            if byKey.GroundSink and byKey.GroundSink.ground then finishGround(byKey.GroundSink,true) end
            Social.syncFall(state,target,0)
            local height=state.floorY
            state.antiFallActive=true
            createPlatform(state,target)
            state.floorY=height
            Social.syncFall(state,target,0)
            if state.tool then state.tool.Name="Anti Fall [ON]" end
        end
        function api.syncFall(state, target, delta) Social.syncFall(state, target, delta) end
        function api.runCommand(state) if not Extra.run(state) then Session.run(state) end end
        function api.apply(state, target)
            if state.key=="AntiFall" then
                if not state.tool or not state.tool.Parent then
                    stopAntiFall(state,false)
                    ensureTool(state,"Anti Fall [OFF]",nil,function() toggleAntiFall(state) end)
                    if state.tool then
                        state.tool.ToolTip="Tap/click to turn Anti Fall protection ON/OFF. Unequipping keeps the selected state."
                        table.insert(state.toolConnections,state.tool.Destroying:Connect(function() stopAntiFall(state,false) end))
                    end
                end
                if state.antiFallActive and target and target.Health>0 then
                    local height=state.floorY
                    createPlatform(state,target)
                    if height~=nil then state.floorY=height end
                end
                return true
            end
            if Extra.apply(state) then return true end
            if Session.apply(state) then return true end
            if Social.apply(state) then return true end
            if state.key == "ESP" then enableEsp(); return true end
            if state.key == "Explorer" then
                showDex(state)
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "GroundSink" then
                ensureTool(state, "Underground", nil, function() toggleUnderground(state) end)
                if state.tool then state.tool.Name = state.ground and "Return Surface" or "Underground" end
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "GotoTool" or state.key == "ChatAgeTool" then
                local function show() showPlayersTool(state) end
                local function hide() if state.playersPanel then state.playersPanel.Visible = false end end
                ensureTool(state, state.key == "GotoTool" and "Goto Players" or "Account Age", show, show, hide)
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "FreezeTool" then
                ensureTool(state, "Freeze Me", nil, function() toggleFreeze(state) end)
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "SpamTool" then
                ensureTool(state, "Text Repeat", function() showChat(state) end, function() showChat(state) end,
                    function() stopRepeat("Stopped: Text Repeat tool unequipped."); if state.chatPanel then state.chatPanel.Visible = false end end)
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "KeyboardTool" then
                ensureTool(state, "Move Keys", function() showKeyboard(state) end, function() showKeyboard(state) end,
                    function() clearKeys(); if state.keyboardPanel then state.keyboardPanel.Visible = false end end)
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "Flashback" then return true end
            if state.key == "AntiSlapped" then enableAntiSlap(state, target); return true end
            return false
        end
        function api.restore(state, characterOnly)
            if state.key=="AntiFall" then
                stopAntiFall(state,true)
                destroyTool(state)
                return true
            end
            if Extra.restore(state,characterOnly) then return true end
            if Session.restore(state, characterOnly) then return true end
            if Social.restore(state, characterOnly) then return true end
            if state.key == "ESP" then if not characterOnly then disableEsp() end; return true end
            if state.key == "Explorer" then
                if not characterOnly then setDexVisible(false) end
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "GroundSink" then destroyTool(state); return false end
            if state.key == "GotoTool" or state.key == "ChatAgeTool" then
                destroyTool(state)
                state.ageGeneration = (state.ageGeneration or 0) + 1
                state.ageResult, state.selectedPlayer = nil, nil
                state.ageSendTicket, state.ageSending = nil, false
                if state.playerStatus then state.playerStatus.Text = "Select a player below." end
                state.playerListGeneration = (state.playerListGeneration or 0) + 1
                if state.playersPanel then state.playersPanel.Visible = false end
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "FreezeTool" then releaseFreeze(state); destroyTool(state); return true end
            if state.key == "SpamTool" then
                stopRepeat("Stopped."); destroyTool(state)
                if state.chatPanel then state.chatPanel.Visible = false end
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "KeyboardTool" then
                clearKeys(); destroyTool(state)
                if state.keyboardPanel then state.keyboardPanel.Visible = false end
                return true
            end
            if state.key == "Flashback" then if state.replay then stopReplay(state) end; return true end
            if state.key == "AntiSlapped" then restoreAntiSlap(state); return true end
            return false
        end
        function api.stepPre(delta)
            local state = byKey.Flashback
            if state and state.replay then stepReplay(state, delta); return true end
            local frozen = byKey.FreezeTool and byKey.FreezeTool.frozen
            if frozen then
                if frozen.root.Parent then frozen.root.Anchored = true; frozen.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
                return true
            end
