            return false
        end
        function api.stepPost()
            local target = currentHumanoid()
            if target then stepAntiSlap(byKey.AntiSlapped, target) end
        end
        function api.heartbeat(delta)
            Extra.heartbeat(delta)
            Session.heartbeat()
            Social.heartbeat(delta)
            recordHistory()
            local job = repeatJob
            if job then
                local state = byKey.SpamTool
                if not state or not state.enabled or not state.tool or state.tool.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character or not state.chatPanel or not state.chatPanel.Visible then
                    stopRepeat("Stopped: tool/panel is no longer active.")
                elseif os.clock() - job.started >= job.duration then
                    stopRepeat("Finished. Sent " .. job.sent .. " message(s).")
                elseif not job.inFlight and os.clock() >= job.nextSend then
                    job.inFlight, job.nextSend = true, os.clock() + job.interval
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local ok, result = pcall(function() return job.channel:SendAsync(job.message) end)
                        if not alive or repeatJob ~= job or job.generation ~= repeatGeneration then return end
                        job.inFlight = false
                        local accepted = ok and (not result or result.Status == Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success)
                        if not accepted then stopRepeat("Chat rejected/limited this send. Stopped; no retry or bypass."); return end
                        job.sent = job.sent + 1
                        state.chatStatus.Text = string.format("Sent %d | %.0fs remaining | STOP cancels", job.sent, math.max(0, job.duration - (os.clock() - job.started)))
                    end)
                end
            end
            pulse = pulse + delta
            if pulse >= 0.12 then
                pulse = 0
                refreshAgeDisplay()
                if byKey.ESP and byKey.ESP.enabled then updateEsp() end
                if byKey.Explorer and byKey.Explorer.enabled then
                    if dexCache.bridge then byKey.Explorer.statusMessage = "FUSION DEX / OPEN"
                    elseif dexCache.error then byKey.Explorer.statusMessage = "DEX LOAD FAILED / RETOGGLE" end
                end
                local flash = byKey.Flashback
                if flash and not flash.replay then flash.statusMessage = string.format("HISTORY %.1fS / %.1fX", availableHistory(), flash.replaySpeed) end
                local target = currentHumanoid()
                if target and target.Health > 0 then
                    for _, key in ipairs({ "FreezeTool", "SpamTool", "KeyboardTool", "GroundSink", "GotoTool", "ChatAgeTool", "AvatarRankTool", "DeleteBadPeople", "AntiFall" }) do
                        if byKey[key] and byKey[key].enabled and (not byKey[key].tool or not byKey[key].tool.Parent or key == "GroundSink") then api.apply(byKey[key], target) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        function api.characterReset()
            resetHistory()
            clearKeys()
        end
        -- Reset QUICK, never close its GUI or reset/kill the character.
        function api.resetAll()
            if not alive or api.resetting then return end
            api.resetting=true
            Session.shutdown() -- invalidate queued server requests, not the whole interface
            stopRepeat("Stopped: QUICK reset.");clearKeys()
            releaseFreeze(byKey.FreezeTool)
            if byKey.Flashback and byKey.Flashback.replay then stopReplay(byKey.Flashback) end
            for _,state in ipairs(states) do
                if state.enabled then state.setEnabled(false,true) else restoreFeature(state,false) end
            end
            Extra.reset();Social.reset();resetHistory()
            for _,window in pairs(windows) do window.Visible=false end
            for _,state in ipairs(states) do
                state.busy,state.edited=false,false
                state.statusMessage=nil
                state.replaySpeed=1
                state.style=state.defaultStyle
                state.gotoSide="Behind"
                if state.defaultValue~=nil and state.setValue then state.setValue(state.defaultValue,false) end
                for key,value in pairs({messageInput="",intervalInput="5",durationInput="20",gotoDistance="5",searchInput="",attackRange="6",attackInterval="0.35"}) do
                    if state[key] then state[key].Text=value end
                end
                state.selectedPlayer,state.ageResult=nil,nil
                if state.repaint then state.repaint() end
            end
            if drag then wasScrolling=true;page.ScrollingEnabled=true;drag=nil end
            verticalHeld.up,verticalHeld.down=false,false
            for _,box in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do if box:IsA("TextBox") and box:IsFocused() then box:ReleaseFocus() end end
            if updateMovementHud then updateMovementHud() end
            api.resetting=false
            local state=byKey.ResetAllQuick
            if state then state.statusMessage="RESET COMPLETE / GUI KEPT OPEN";state.repaint() end
            notify("All QUICK features OFF and settings reset. GUI stays open; character unchanged.")
        end
        function api.shutdown()
            Extra.shutdown()
            Session.shutdown()
            Social.shutdown()
            RedTelemetry.Closed=true
            if RedTelemetry.FPSConnection then RedTelemetry.FPSConnection:Disconnect() end
            if RedTelemetry.PositionConnection then RedTelemetry.PositionConnection:Disconnect() end
            RedTelemetry.AvatarService.close()
            RedTelemetry.AgeService.close()
            stopRepeat("Stopped: interface closed.")
            clearKeys(); disableEsp(); setDexVisible(false)
            pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(keyboardBinding) end)
            for _, root in pairs(windows) do root:Destroy() end
            if byKey.KeyboardTool and byKey.KeyboardTool.keyboardPanel then byKey.KeyboardTool.keyboardPanel:Destroy() end
            for owner in pairs(toolOwners) do api.releaseTool(owner) end
            resetHistory()
        end
        return api
    end)()
    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5)
    padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8)
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5)
    padding.Parent = page
    local list = page:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
    list.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    list.Padding = UDim.new(0, 9)
    local header = newFrame(page, "QuickHeader", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38))
    header.LayoutOrder = 1
    header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    local heading = label(header, "QUICK", UDim2.new(0, 110, 0, 23),
        UDim2.new(), 18, colors.White, true)
    heading.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    local headingColor = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    headingColor.Color = ColorSequence.new(colors.Pink, colors.Cyan)
    headingColor.Parent = heading
    label(header, "49 CONTROLS  /  42 TOGGLES + 7 ACTIONS", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 13),
        UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 25), 10, colors.Muted, true)
    local footnote = newFrame(page, "QuickHint", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30))
    footnote.LayoutOrder = 100
    footnote.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label(footnote, "FLASHBACK: SELECT SECONDS, THEN REWIND. TOOLS APPEAR IN THE HOTBAR.",
        UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 13), UDim2.new(), 9, colors.Muted, true)
    label(footnote, "Fly: WASD/joystick + SPACE/E to rise, CTRL/Q to descend. Touch: UP/DOWN.",
        UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 13), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16), 9, colors.Muted)
    -- Each pair has its own row height, so opening Gravity does not enlarge row one.
    local function getRow(index)
        local number = math.ceil(index / 2)
        if rows[number] then return rows[number] end
        local frame = newFrame(page, "QuickRow" .. number, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 76))
        frame.LayoutOrder = number + 1
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        local grid = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
        grid.CellSize = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 0, 76)
        grid.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
        grid.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        grid.FillDirectionMaxCells = 2
        grid.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        grid.Parent = frame
        local row = { frame = frame, grid = grid, states = {}, tween = nil }
        rows[number] = row
        return row
    end

    local function resizeRow(row)
        local height = 76
        for _, state in ipairs(row.states) do
            if state.enabled and state.hasSlider then
                height = math.max(height, state.expandedHeight + 4)
            end
        end
        row.grid.CellSize = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 0, height)
        cancelTween(row.tween)
        row.tween = RedTween.Play(row.frame, 0.23, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height) })
    end

    local function createFeature(config)
        local state = {
            key = config.key,
            command = config.command == true,
            value = config.default,
            defaultValue = config.default,
            defaultStyle = config.styles and config.styles[1] or "Superman",
            minimum = config.minimum,
            maximum = config.maximum,
            hasSlider = config.minimum ~= nil,
            expandedHeight = config.replaySpeed and 252 or ((config.styles or config.action) and 208 or 172),
            replaySpeed = 1,
            kind = config.kind,
            rig = config.rig,
            style = "Superman",
            enabled = false,
            edited = false
        }
        table.insert(states, state)
        byKey[state.key] = state
        local row = getRow(#states)
        table.insert(row.states, state)

        local cell = newFrame(row.frame, config.key .. "Cell", UDim2.new())
        cell.LayoutOrder = #states
        cell.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        local card = newFrame(cell, config.key .. "Card", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 72),
            UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2), Color3.fromRGB(19, 19, 32))
        card.ClipsDescendants = true
        card.ZIndex = 2
        RedUI.Corner(card, 12)

        local wash = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        wash.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(39, 27, 53), Color3.fromRGB(17, 34, 45))
        wash.Rotation = 20
        wash.Parent = card
        local border = RedUI.Stroke(card, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1.7)
        border.Transparency = 0.02
        local edge = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        -- Identical lighting treatment on Walk Speed, Jump Power and all other cards.
        edge.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, colors.Pink),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, config.accent),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.68, colors.Cyan),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, colors.Pink)
        })
        edge.Parent = border
        local spin = TweenService:Create(edge,
            TweenInfo.new(6, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1),
            { Rotation = 360 })
        table.insert(animations, spin)

        -- A broad transparent stroke supplies a soft neon halo outside each box.
        local halo = newFrame(cell, "NeonHalo", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 72), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2))
        halo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        halo.ZIndex = 1
        RedUI.Corner(halo, 12)
        local haloStroke = RedUI.Stroke(halo, config.accent, 5)
        haloStroke.Transparency = 0.83

        local light = newFrame(card, "TopLight", UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 2),
            UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
        local lightGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        lightGradient.Color = edge.Color
        lightGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        lightGradient.Parent = light

        local marker = newFrame(card, "AccentMarker", UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 32),
            UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 18), config.accent)
        RedUI.Corner(marker, 2)
        local title = label(card, string.upper(config.title), UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 23),
            UDim2.new(0, 19, 0, 13), 14, colors.White, true)
        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        if config.command then title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 23) end
        local status = label(card, "OFF", UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 15),
            UDim2.new(0, 19, 0, 39), 10, colors.Muted, true)

        local toggle = Instance.new("TextButton")
        toggle.Name = config.key .. "Toggle"
        toggle.Text = ""
        toggle.AutoButtonColor = false
        toggle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 23)
        toggle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 24)
        toggle.BackgroundColor3 = colors.Off
        toggle.BorderSizePixel = 0
        toggle.Parent = card
        RedUI.Corner(toggle, 12)
        if config.command then
            toggle.Name = config.key .. "Action"
            toggle.Text, toggle.Font, toggle.TextSize = config.buttonLabel or "GO", Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 11
            toggle.TextColor3, toggle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12,14,22), config.accent
            toggle.Size, toggle.Position = UDim2.new(0,57,0,27), UDim2.new(1,-66,0,22)
        end
        state.button = toggle
        local knob = newFrame(toggle, "Knob", UDim2.new(0, 17, 0, 17),
            UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3), colors.White)
        knob.Visible = not config.command
        RedUI.Corner(knob, 9)

        local controls, entry, fill, thumb, track, slider, entryBorder
        if state.hasSlider then
            controls = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
            controls.Name = config.key .. "Controls"
            controls.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, config.replaySpeed and 179 or ((config.styles or config.action) and 127 or 91))
            controls.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 73)
            controls.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            controls.GroupTransparency = 1
            controls.Visible = false
            controls.Parent = card
            newFrame(controls, "FeatureBoundary", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
                UDim2.new(), Color3.fromRGB(70, 66, 95))
            label(controls, "SET VALUE", UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 24),
                UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 9), 10, colors.Muted, true)
            entry = Instance.new("TextBox")
            entry.Name = config.key .. "Value"
            entry.Size = UDim2.new(0, 63, 0, 26)
            entry.Position = UDim2.new(1, -63, 0, 8)
            entry.BackgroundColor3 = colors.Field
            entry.BorderSizePixel = 0
            entry.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            entry.TextSize = 13
            entry.TextColor3 = config.accent
            entry.Text = tostring(state.value)
            entry.PlaceholderText = tostring(config.minimum) .. "-" .. tostring(config.maximum)
            entry.ClearTextOnFocus = false
            entry.Parent = controls
            RedUI.Corner(entry, 7)
            entryBorder = RedUI.Stroke(entry, colors.Track, 1)
            slider = Instance.new("TextButton")
            slider.Name = config.key .. "Slider"
            slider.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 30)
            slider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 38)
            slider.Text = ""
            slider.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            slider.AutoButtonColor = false
            slider.Active = true
            slider.Parent = controls
            track = newFrame(slider, "Track", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 5),
                UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -2), colors.Track)
            RedUI.Corner(track, 3)
            fill = newFrame(track, "Fill", UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), UDim2.new(), colors.White)
            RedUI.Corner(fill, 3)
            local fillColor = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            fillColor.Color = ColorSequence.new(colors.Pink, colors.Cyan)
            fillColor.Parent = fill
            thumb = newFrame(track, "Thumb", UDim2.new(0, 13, 0, 13),
                UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), colors.White)
            thumb.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            RedUI.Corner(thumb, 7)
            RedUI.Stroke(thumb, config.accent, 1.6)
            label(controls, tostring(config.minimum), UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 14),
                UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 72), 10, colors.Muted, true)
            local maximum = label(controls, tostring(config.maximum), UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 14),
                UDim2.new(1, -35, 0, 72), 10, colors.Muted, true)
            maximum.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
            local unit = label(controls, config.unit, UDim2.new(1, -74, 0, 14),
                UDim2.new(0, 37, 0, 72), 8, colors.Muted, true)
            unit.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        end

        local styleButtons = {}
        if config.styles and controls then
            for index, style in ipairs(config.styles) do
                local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
                button.Name = "Style" .. index
                button.Text = string.upper(style)
                button.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 27)
                button.Position = UDim2.new((index - 1) * 0.5, index == 1 and 0 or 4, 0, 94)
                button.BackgroundColor3 = colors.Field
                button.TextColor3 = colors.White
                button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                button.TextSize = 10
                button.AutoButtonColor = false
                button.BorderSizePixel = 0
                button.Parent = controls
                RedUI.Corner(button, 7)
                RedUI.Stroke(button, config.accent, 1)
                styleButtons[style] = button
                connect(button.Activated, function() state.setStyle(style) end)
            end
        end

        local actionButton
        local speedButtons = {}
        if config.replaySpeed and controls then
            label(controls, "REPLAY SPEED / MAX 5X", UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 92), 9, colors.Muted, true)
            for index, multiplier in ipairs({ 0.5, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5 }) do
                local speedButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
                speedButton.Name, speedButton.Text = "ReplaySpeed" .. tostring(multiplier), tostring(multiplier) .. "X"
                speedButton.Size = UDim2.new(1/6, -3, 0, 23)
                speedButton.Position = UDim2.new((index - 1)/6, 0, 0, 110)
                speedButton.Font, speedButton.TextSize = Enum.Font.GothamBold, 10
                speedButton.BorderSizePixel, speedButton.AutoButtonColor, speedButton.Parent = 0, false, controls
                RedUI.Corner(speedButton, 6)
                speedButtons[multiplier] = speedButton
                connect(speedButton.Activated, function() if state.enabled then state.setReplaySpeed(multiplier) end end)
            end
        end
        if config.action and controls then
            actionButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
            actionButton.Name, actionButton.Text = "RewindAction", "REWIND NOW"
            actionButton.Size, actionButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 27), UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, config.replaySpeed and 140 or 94)
            actionButton.BackgroundColor3, actionButton.TextColor3 = config.accent, Color3.fromRGB(12, 14, 22)
            actionButton.Font, actionButton.TextSize = Enum.Font.GothamBold, 11
            actionButton.BorderSizePixel, actionButton.AutoButtonColor, actionButton.Parent = 0, false, controls
            RedUI.Corner(actionButton, 7)
            connect(actionButton.Activated, function() if state.enabled then QuickPlus.toggleReplay(state) end end)
        end
        local tweens = {}
        local transition = 0
        local function stopTweens()
            for _, tween in pairs(tweens) do cancelTween(tween) end
            tweens = {}
        end
        local function repaint()
            if state.hasSlider then
                local ratio = (state.value - state.minimum) / (state.maximum - state.minimum)
                fill.Size = UDim2.new(ratio, 0, 1, 0)
                thumb.Position = UDim2.new(ratio, 0, 0.5, 0)
                if not entry:IsFocused() then entry.Text = tostring(state.value) end
            end
            for style, button in pairs(styleButtons) do
                button.BackgroundColor3 = state.style == style and config.accent or colors.Field
                button.TextColor3 = state.style == style and Color3.fromRGB(12, 14, 22) or colors.White
            end
            for multiplier, speedButton in pairs(speedButtons) do
                local selected = math.abs(state.replaySpeed - multiplier) < 0.001
                speedButton.BackgroundColor3 = selected and config.accent or colors.Field
                speedButton.TextColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(12,14,22) or colors.White
            end
            if actionButton then actionButton.Text = state.replay and "STOP REWIND" or "REWIND NOW" end
            if config.command then
                toggle.Text = state.busy and "..." or (config.buttonLabel or "GO")
                status.Text = state.statusMessage or ("READY / " .. config.hint)
            elseif not state.enabled then
                status.Text = "OFF  /  " .. config.hint
            elseif state.statusMessage then
                status.Text = "ON / " .. state.statusMessage
            elseif state.key == "FlyR15" then
                status.Text = string.upper(state.style) .. " / " .. tostring(state.value)
            elseif state.key == "AcceleratingWalk" then
                status.Text = "ON  /  " .. string.format("%.0f", state.accumulatedSpeed or 0) .. " SPEED"
            elseif (state.key == "AntiFall" or state.key == "SkyWalk") and state.floorY then
                status.Text = "ON  /  HEIGHT " .. string.format("%.1f", state.floorY)
            elseif state.hasSlider then
                status.Text = "ON  /  " .. tostring(state.value)
            else
                status.Text = "ON  /  " .. config.activeHint
            end
            status.TextColor3 = (state.enabled or config.command) and config.accent or colors.Muted
        end
        state.repaint = repaint
        state.setReplaySpeed = function(value)
            local number = finiteNumber(value)
            if not number then return false end
            state.replaySpeed = math.clamp(number, 0.5, 5)
            repaint(); return true
        end
        state.setStyle = function(style)
            if not config.styles or not styleButtons[style] then return false end
            state.style = style
            repaint()
            return true
        end

        local function setValue(value, userEdited)
            if not state.hasSlider then return false end
            local number = finiteNumber(value)
            if not number then repaint(); return false end
            if state.key == "AntiVoid" then number = safeVoidY(number) end
            local precision = config.precision or 1
            state.value = math.clamp(math.floor(number * precision + 0.5) / precision,
                state.minimum, state.maximum)
            if userEdited then state.edited = true end
            repaint()
            applyFeature(state)
            return true
        end
        state.setValue = setValue

        local function setEnabled(value, immediate)
            if config.command then return false end
            if not alive or state.enabled == value then return end
            local exclusive = state.kind or state.key == "SelfFling" or state.key == "Spin" or
                state.key == "SkyWalk" or state.key == "WallWalk" or state.key == "HeadWalk"
            if value and QuickPlus then QuickPlus.beforeEnable(state, exclusive) end
            local underground = byKey.GroundSink
            if value and exclusive and underground and underground.ground then
                finishGround(underground, true)
            end
            if value and (state.key == "HeadWalk" or state.key == "WallWalk") then
                local target = currentHumanoid()
                if target and target.SeatPart then notify("Leave the vehicle first."); return false end
            end
            if value and (state.key == "SelfFling" or state.key == "Spin") then
                local target = currentHumanoid()
                if target and target.SeatPart then notify("Leave the vehicle before Self Fling."); return false end
            end
            if value and state.rig then
                local target = currentHumanoid()
                if target and target.RigType ~= state.rig then
                    notify("This mode requires " .. (state.rig == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 and "R6" or "R15") .. ".")
                    return false
                end
            end
            -- Locomotion constraints are exclusive, so two controllers cannot fight.
            if value and exclusive then
                for _, key in ipairs(movementGroup) do
                    local other = byKey[key]
                    if other and other ~= state and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false, true) end
                end
                for _, key in ipairs({ "SelfFling", "Spin", "WallWalk", "HeadWalk" }) do
                    local other = byKey[key]
                    if other and other ~= state and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false, true) end
                end
                local support = byKey.AntiFall
                if support and support ~= state and support.enabled then support.setEnabled(false, true) end
            end
            if value and (state.key == "AntiFling" or state.key == "SelfFling") then
                local other = byKey[state.key == "AntiFling" and "SelfFling" or "AntiFling"]
                if other and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false, true) end
            end
            if value and (state.key == "AntiSit" or state.key == "VehicleFly") then
                local key = state.key == "AntiSit" and "VehicleFly" or "AntiSit"
                local other = byKey[key]
                if other and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false, true) end
            end
            -- Fixed speed and accelerating speed cannot compete for WalkSpeed.
            if value and config.conflict then
                local other = byKey[config.conflict]
                if other and other.enabled then other.setEnabled(false, true) end
            end
            state.enabled = value
            transition = transition + 1
            local token = transition
            if drag and drag.state == state then stopDragging() end
            if value then
                if controls then controls.Visible = true end
                -- The agreed Gravity preset is applied on each OFF -> ON transition.
                if state.key == "Gravity" then state.value = 9.8 end
                applyFeature(state)
            else
                if entry and entry:IsFocused() then entry:ReleaseFocus() end
                restoreFeature(state, false, state.key == "GroundSink" and not immediate)
            end
            repaint()
            RedAudio.Play(value and "ToggleOn" or "ToggleOff")
            stopTweens()
            local height = value and state.hasSlider and state.expandedHeight or 72
            tweens.card = RedTween.Play(card, 0.23, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height) })
            tweens.halo = RedTween.Play(halo, 0.23, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height) })
            if controls then
                tweens.fade = RedTween.Play(controls, 0.16, { GroupTransparency = value and 0 or 1 })
            end
            tweens.knob = RedTween.Play(knob, 0.18, {
                Position = UDim2.new(0, value and 20 or 3, 0, 3)
            })
            tweens.toggle = RedTween.Play(toggle, 0.18, {
                BackgroundColor3 = value and config.accent or colors.Off
            })
            haloStroke.Transparency = value and 0.65 or 0.83
            resizeRow(row)
            if updateMovementHud then updateMovementHud() end
            if not value and controls then
                task.delay(0.24, function()
                    if alive and token == transition and not state.enabled then controls.Visible = false end
                end)
            end
        end
        state.setEnabled = setEnabled
        connect(toggle.Activated, function()
            if config.command then QuickPlus.runCommand(state) else setEnabled(not state.enabled) end
        end)

        if state.hasSlider then
            local function slide(x)
                if not state.enabled or track.AbsoluteSize.X <= 0 then return end
                local ratio = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / track.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
                setValue(state.minimum + ratio * (state.maximum - state.minimum), true)
            end
            connect(entry.FocusLost, function()
                setValue(entry.Text, true)
                entryBorder.Color = colors.Track
            end)
            connect(entry.Focused, function() entryBorder.Color = config.accent end)
            connect(slider.InputBegan, function(input)
                if not state.enabled or not page.Visible then return end
                local kind = input.UserInputType
                if kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
                stopDragging()
                wasScrolling = page.ScrollingEnabled
                page.ScrollingEnabled = false
                drag = { state = state, input = input, slide = slide }
                slide(input.Position.X)
            end)
        end
        table.insert(featureCards, { animation = spin, cancel = stopTweens })
        repaint()
    end
    -- Row 1: core speed and jump controls.
    createFeature({
        key = "WalkSpeed", title = "Walk Speed",
        minimum = 1, maximum = 500, default = 16,
        accent = colors.Pink, unit = "STUDS / SEC",
        hint = "1 - 500", conflict = "AcceleratingWalk"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "JumpPower", title = "Jump Power",
        minimum = 0, maximum = 500, default = 50,
        accent = colors.Cyan, unit = "JUMP POWER", hint = "0 - 500"
    })
    -- Row 2: continuously increasing walk speed and instant air jumps.
    createFeature({
        key = "AcceleratingWalk", title = "Accelerating Walk",
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 180, 67),
        hint = "+20 SPEED / SEC", activeHint = "KEEP MOVING", conflict = "WalkSpeed"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "UnlimitedJump", title = "Unlimited Jump",
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(187, 117, 255),
        hint = "INSTANT / 1.25X", activeHint = "INSTANT AIR JUMP"
    })
    -- Row 3: metric gravity and a fixed-height safety surface.
    createFeature({
        key = "Gravity", title = "Gravity",
        minimum = 0, maximum = 200, default = 9.8, precision = 10,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(88, 246, 167), unit = "METRES / SEC^2", hint = "9.8 M/S^2"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "AntiFall", title = "Anti Fall Tool",
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 119, 178),
        hint = "SPAWN TOOL / TAP ON-OFF", activeHint = "EQUIP ANTI FALL TOOL"
    })
    -- Row 4: proactive sitting prevention and R6 flight.
    createFeature({
        key = "AntiSit", title = "Anti Sit",
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 88),
        hint = "BLOCK SEATED STATE", activeHint = "SEATED STATE BLOCKED"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "FlyR6", title = "Fly R6", kind = "fly", rig = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6,
        minimum = 1, maximum = 300, default = 35,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(80, 207, 255), unit = "FLY SPEED", hint = "R6 / 1 - 300"
    })
    -- Row 5: R6 simulated swimming and R15 flight with exclusive poses.
    createFeature({
        key = "SwimR6", title = "Swim R6", kind = "swim", rig = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6,
        minimum = 1, maximum = 300, default = 20,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(63, 238, 218), unit = "SWIM SPEED", hint = "R6 / SWIM POSE"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "FlyR15", title = "Fly R15", kind = "fly", rig = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15,
        minimum = 1, maximum = 300, default = 35,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 138, 86), unit = "FLY SPEED", hint = "SUPERMAN / IRON MAN",
        styles = { "Superman", "Iron Man" }
    })
    -- Row 6: R15 simulated swimming and an adjustable-height walking surface.
    createFeature({
        key = "SwimR15", title = "Swim R15", kind = "swim", rig = Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15,
        minimum = 1, maximum = 300, default = 20,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(102, 181, 255), unit = "SWIM SPEED", hint = "R15 / SWIM POSE"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "SkyWalk", title = "Sky Walk",
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(209, 154, 255),
        hint = "UP / DOWN TO CLIMB", activeHint = "AIR FLOOR ACTIVE"
    })
    -- Row 7: local vehicle assembly flight and deliberate click-to-teleport.
    createFeature({
        key = "VehicleFly", title = "Vehicle Fly", kind = "vehicle",
        minimum = 1, maximum = 300, default = 40,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 191, 65), unit = "VEHICLE FLY SPEED", hint = "SIT FIRST / 1 - 300"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "ClickTP", title = "Click To TP",
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(251, 121, 211),
        hint = "SPAWN A TP TOOL", activeHint = "EQUIP TP TOOL"
    })

    -- Row 8: self-only spin and local anti-fling stabilisation.
    createFeature({
        key = "SelfFling", title = "Fling (Self)",
        minimum = 0, maximum = 10000, default = 100,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 90), unit = "TURN DEG / SEC", hint = "UPRIGHT / 0 - 10000"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "AntiFling", title = "Anti Fling",
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(84, 233, 186),
        hint = "LOCAL STABILISER", activeHint = "COLLISION / SPEED GUARD"
    })
    -- Row 9: configurable void height and an eight-stud sinking animation.
    createFeature({
        key = "AntiVoid", title = "Anti Void",
        minimum = -2000, maximum = 1000, default = -400,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 181, 91), unit = "WORLD Y / STUDS", hint = "STOP AT Y = -400"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "GroundSink", title = "Underground Tool",
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(170, 141, 255),
        hint = "TOOL / SINK + SURFACE", activeHint = "EQUIP UNDERGROUND TOOL"
    })

    -- Row 10: owned collision toggling and manual wall-jump assistance.
    createFeature({
        key = "Noclip", title = "Noclip", accent = Color3.fromRGB(119, 211, 255),
        hint = "PASS THROUGH WALLS", activeHint = "COLLISIONS DISABLED"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "WallHop", title = "Real Wall Hop", accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 202, 104),
        hint = "JUMP NEAR A WALL", activeHint = "PRESS JUMP TO BOUNCE"
    })
    -- Row 11: attached wall locomotion and inverted headstand gait.
    createFeature({
        key = "WallWalk", title = "Walk On Wall", accent = Color3.fromRGB(151, 139, 255),
        hint = "APPROACH A WALL", activeHint = "WALL WALKING"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "HeadWalk", title = "Headstand Walk", accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 133, 178),
        hint = "HEAD DOWN / FEET UP", activeHint = "INVERTED WALK + GAIT"
    })

    -- Row 12: colourful player information and the specifically requested external Dex package.
    createFeature({
        key = "ESP", title = "Colourful ESP", accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 139, 211),
        hint = "PLAYER / HP / DISTANCE", activeHint = "UNIQUE PLAYER COLOURS"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "Explorer", title = "Fusion Dex", accent = Color3.fromRGB(112, 199, 255),
        hint = "EXTERNAL DEX / VIEWING USE", activeHint = "FUSION DEX OPEN"
    })
    -- Row 13: explicit freeze activation and recorded reverse-time replay.
    createFeature({
        key = "FreezeTool", title = "Freeze Tool", accent = Color3.fromRGB(143, 223, 255),
        hint = "FREEZE / UNFREEZE ME", activeHint = "USE FREEZE ME TOOL"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "Flashback", title = "Flashback", minimum = 0, maximum = 60, default = 10,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 171, 92), unit = "SECONDS TO REWIND", hint = "0-60 SEC / 0.5X-5X", action = true, replaySpeed = true
    })
    -- Row 14: bounded, user-started public chat repetition and a separate upright spin.
    createFeature({
        key = "SpamTool", title = "Text Repeat Tool", accent = Color3.fromRGB(190, 145, 255),
        hint = "TEXT / INTERVAL / STOP", activeHint = "EQUIP TEXT REPEAT"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "Spin", title = "Spin", minimum = 0, maximum = 1000, default = 90,
        accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 213, 105), unit = "TURN DEG / SEC", hint = "UPRIGHT / 0 - 1000"
    })
    -- Row 15: right-side movement buttons and best-effort knockback resistance.
    createFeature({
        key = "KeyboardTool", title = "Keyboard Tool", accent = Color3.fromRGB(112, 234, 192),
        hint = "RIGHT-SIDE W A S D", activeHint = "EQUIP MOVE KEYS"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "AntiSlapped", title = "Anti Slapped", accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 138, 151),
        hint = "LOCAL KNOCKBACK GUARD", activeHint = "WALK / MOMENTUM GUARD"
    })

    -- Row 16: native player-roster tools, explicit teleport and explicit account-age chat.
    createFeature({
        key = "GotoTool", title = "Goto Tool", accent = Color3.fromRGB(105, 245, 185),
        hint = "PLAYERS / PHOTO / OFFSET", activeHint = "EQUIP GOTO PLAYERS"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "ChatAgeTool", title = "Chat Age Tool", accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 105),
        hint = "ACCOUNT AGE / PUBLIC CHAT", activeHint = "EQUIP ACCOUNT AGE TOOL"
    })

    -- Rows 17-18: live worn-asset subtotals and reversible local social filtering.
    createFeature({
        key = "AvatarRankTool", title = "Avatar Price Rank", accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 211, 96),
        hint = "SUPPLIED LOGIC / 5 SEC REFRESH", activeHint = "EQUIP AVATAR PRICES"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "BeAlone", title = "Be Alone", accent = Color3.fromRGB(151, 178, 255),
        hint = "LOCAL QUIET / NO TOOL", activeHint = "OTHERS HIDDEN LOCALLY"
    })
    createFeature({
        key = "DeleteBadPeople", title = "Delete Bad People", accent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 125, 148),
        hint = "LOCAL FILTER / 3 MSG IN 2S", activeHint = "EQUIP SPAM MANAGER"
    })

    -- Rows 18-21: limited client guards + explicit server/reset action buttons.
    createFeature({
        key="AntiKick", title="Anti Kick", accent=Color3.fromRGB(120,230,255),
        hint="LOCAL KICK ONLY / LIMITED", activeHint="SUPPORTED LOCAL CALLS ONLY"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="AntiBanGuard", title="Anti Ban Guard", accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,191,115),
        hint="RETRY GUARD / NOT UNBAN", activeHint="NO SERVER BAN BYPASS"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="ServerMost", title="Most Players", command=true, buttonLabel="JOIN",
        accent=Color3.fromRGB(139,243,159), hint="PUBLIC / MOST NON-FULL"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="ServerLeast", title="Lowest Players", command=true, buttonLabel="JOIN",
        accent=Color3.fromRGB(149,177,255), hint="PUBLIC / LOWEST COUNT"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="ServerLatency", title="Best Latency", command=true, buttonLabel="JOIN",
        accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,222,106), hint="LOWEST REPORTED PING"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="ServerHop", title="Server Hop", command=true, buttonLabel="HOP",
        accent=Color3.fromRGB(230,150,255), hint="DIFFERENT PUBLIC SERVER"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="ResetCharacter", title="Reset Character", command=true, buttonLabel="RESET",
        accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,133,154), hint="CHARACTER RESET / RESPAWN"
    })

    -- New controls follow the existing 42; RESET is deliberately last.
    createFeature({
        key="FPSBooster",title="FPS Booster",accent=Color3.fromRGB(115,241,169),
        hint="LOCAL EFFECTS / REVERSIBLE",activeHint="LOW EFFECTS / SHADOWS OFF"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="LagDestroyer",title="Lag Destroyer",accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,198,103),
        hint="EXTRA LOW DETAIL / LOCAL",activeHint="BATCHED / NOT SERVER PING"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="ShiftLock",title="Shift Lock",accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,131,198),
        hint="PC SHIFT / MOBILE BUTTON",activeHint="SHIFT OR TAP LOCK BUTTON"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="BestGraphics",title="Best Graphics HDR",accent=Color3.fromRGB(143,187,255),
        hint="HDR STYLE / LOCAL LIGHTING",activeHint="HIGH DETAIL / DEVICE LIMITED"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="HandlekillAuto",title="Handlekill Auto",accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,129,112),
        hint="SELECT / EQUIPPED TOOL ONLY",activeHint="NORMAL TOOL / GAME RULES"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="DeveloperConsole",title="F9 Console",command=true,buttonLabel="OPEN",accent=Color3.fromRGB(143,225,245),
        hint="NATIVE / AVAILABLE TABS"
    })
    createFeature({
        key="ResetAllQuick",title="Reset All Quick",command=true,buttonLabel="RESET",accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,121,155),
        hint="CONFIRM / KEEP GUI OPEN"
    })

    -- Touch-friendly vertical controls stay accessible after the main UI is minimised.
    local moveHud = newFrame(MainGui, "QuickVerticalControls", UDim2.new(0, 112, 0, 99),
        UDim2.new(1, -126, 1, -156), Color3.fromRGB(18, 20, 34))
    moveHud.ZIndex = 50
    moveHud.Visible = false
    RedUI.Corner(moveHud, 12)
    RedUI.Stroke(moveHud, colors.Cyan, 1.4)
    label(moveHud, "MOVE VERTICALLY", UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 19),
        UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 5), 9, colors.Cyan, true).TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    local heldPointers = {}
    local function verticalButton(title, x, direction)
        local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
        button.Text = title
        button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        button.TextSize = 11
        button.TextColor3 = colors.White
        button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 36)
        button.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 29)
        button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(46, 43, 70)
        button.BorderSizePixel = 0
        button.Parent = moveHud
        RedUI.Corner(button, 8)
        connect(button.InputBegan, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                verticalHeld[direction] = true
                heldPointers[direction] = input
            end
        end)
    end
    verticalButton("UP", 7, "up")
    verticalButton("DOWN", 60, "down")
    label(moveHud, "SPACE/E  |  CTRL/Q", UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 20),
        UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 72), 8, colors.Muted, true).TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    updateMovementHud = function()
        local active = false
        for _, key in ipairs(movementGroup) do
            if byKey[key] and byKey[key].enabled then active = true end
        end
        local target = currentHumanoid()
        moveHud.Visible = alive and active and target ~= nil and target.Health > 0
        if not moveHud.Visible then
            verticalHeld.up, verticalHeld.down = false, false
            heldPointers = {}
        end
    end

    local characterConnections = {}
    local function attachCharacter(character)
        local target = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
        if not alive or not target or LocalPlayer.Character ~= character then return end
        disconnectList(characterConnections)
        characterConnections = {}
        for _, state in ipairs(states) do restoreFeature(state, true) end
        humanoid = target
        if QuickPlus then QuickPlus.characterReset() end
        for _, state in ipairs(states) do
            if state.enabled and state.rig and state.rig ~= target.RigType then
                state.setEnabled(false)
            end
            if not state.enabled and not state.edited and
               (state.key == "WalkSpeed" or state.key == "JumpPower") then
                state.setValue(state.key == "WalkSpeed" and target.WalkSpeed or target.JumpPower, false)
            end
            applyFeature(state)
            state.repaint()
        end
        table.insert(characterConnections, target.Died:Connect(function()
            if humanoid ~= target then return end
            for _, state in ipairs(states) do restoreFeature(state, true) end
            updateMovementHud()
        end))
        updateMovementHud()
    end
    connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(character) task.spawn(attachCharacter, character) end)
    connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving, function(character)
        if humanoid and humanoid:IsDescendantOf(character) then
            disconnectList(characterConnections)
            characterConnections = {}
            for _, state in ipairs(states) do restoreFeature(state, true) end
            humanoid = nil
            if QuickPlus then QuickPlus.characterReset() end
            updateMovementHud()
        end
    end)
    if LocalPlayer.Character then task.spawn(attachCharacter, LocalPlayer.Character) end

    connect(UserInputService.JumpRequest, function()
        if QuickPlus then QuickPlus.noteJump() end
        if not performWallHop() then performUnlimitedJump() end
    end)
    connect(RunService.PreSimulation, stepMovement)
    local uiElapsed = 0
    connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(delta)
        if QuickPlus then QuickPlus.heartbeat(delta) end
        uiElapsed = uiElapsed + delta
        if uiElapsed < 0.15 then return end
        uiElapsed = 0
        updateMovementHud()
        if not page.Visible or not MainWindow.Visible then return end
        for _, state in ipairs(states) do
            if state.enabled then state.repaint() end
        end
    end)

    -- Tool.Activated owns teleport input; no global world-click listener remains.
    connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
        if not drag or not page.Visible or not MainWindow.Visible then return end
        local pointer = drag.input
        if (pointer.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == pointer) or
           (pointer.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
            drag.slide(input.Position.X)
        end
    end)
    connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
        for direction, pointer in pairs(heldPointers) do
            if input == pointer then verticalHeld[direction] = false; heldPointers[direction] = nil end
        end
        if not drag then return end
        if input == drag.input or
           (drag.input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
            stopDragging()
        end
    end)
    connect(UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased, function()
        stopDragging()
        verticalHeld.up, verticalHeld.down = false, false
        heldPointers = {}
    end)

    local function updateAnimationVisibility()
        stopDragging()
        local visible = page.Visible and MainWindow.Visible
        for _, feature in ipairs(featureCards) do
            if visible then feature.animation:Play() else feature.animation:Pause() end
        end
        if not visible then
            for _, descendant in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("TextBox") and descendant:IsFocused() then descendant:ReleaseFocus() end
            end
        end
    end
    connect(page:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), updateAnimationVisibility)
    connect(MainWindow:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), updateAnimationVisibility)
    updateAnimationVisibility()

    connect(MainGui.Destroying, function()
        alive = false
        stopDragging()
        disconnectList(characterConnections)
        verticalHeld.up, verticalHeld.down = false, false
        for _, state in ipairs(states) do state.enabled = false; restoreFeature(state) end
        if QuickPlus then QuickPlus.shutdown() end
        for _, row in ipairs(rows) do cancelTween(row.tween) end
        for _, feature in ipairs(featureCards) do feature.cancel() end
        for _, animation in ipairs(animations) do animation:Cancel() end
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
    end)
end)()
end

local VisualSchema = {
    groups = {
        "Time & Sun",
        "Lighting",
        "Color Grade",
        "Bloom",
        "Atmosphere",
        "Clouds & Fog",
        "Depth of Field",
        "Blur & Motion",
        "Sun & Lens",
        "Skybox",
        "Water",
        "Reflections & GI",
        "Quality",
        "Profiles",
        "Diagnostics",
    },
    fields = {
    },
    byId = {
    },
}
do
    local function add(group, target, prop, label, kind, value, min, max, step, help, optional)
        if kind == "boolean" and type(min) == "string" and help == nil then
            help = min
            min = nil
        end
        local d = {
            id = target .. "." .. prop,
            group = group,
            target = target,
            prop = prop,
            label = label,
            kind = kind,
            default = value,
            min = min,
            max = max,
            step = step or 0.01,
            help = help,
            optional = optional,
        }
        table.insert(VisualSchema.fields, d)
        ;
        VisualSchema.byId[d.id] = d
    end
    local c = Color3.fromRGB
    add(
        "Time & Sun",

-- Audited local shader controller. Preset data only; no pshade UI/loaders/checks/feedback.
RedTelemetry.Visuals = (function()
    local api = {
        enabled = false,
        closed = false,
        name = "GAME ORIGINAL",
        revision = 0,
        errors = {
        },
    }
    local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local world = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local terrain = world:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    local quality
    ;
    pcall(function()
        quality = UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings")
    end)
    local classes = {
        Color = "ColorCorrectionEffect",
        Bloom = "BloomEffect",
        DOF = "DepthOfFieldEffect",
        Blur = "BlurEffect",
        SunRays = "SunRaysEffect",
        Motion = "BlurEffect",
        Sky = "Sky",
        Atmosphere = "Atmosphere",
        Clouds = "Clouds",
    }
    local effects, owned, records, connections, values, initial, slots = {
    }, {
    }, {
    }, {
    }, {
    }, {
    }, {
    }
    local reflectQueue, reflectSeen, reflectHead = {
    }, {
    }, 1
    local giFolder, giLights, giIndex = nil, {
    }, 0
    local flareRoot, flareImages = nil, {
    }
    local lastLook, filteredBlur, pulse, giPulse, flarePulse = nil, 0, 0, 0, 0
    local function copy(t)
        local r = {
        }
        ;
        for k, v in pairs(t) do
            r[k] = v
        end
        ;
        return r
    end
    local function finite(x)
        return type(x) == "number" and x == x and math.abs(x) < math.huge
    end
    local function read(object, key)
        if not object then
            return nil
        end
        ;
        local ok, v = pcall(function()
            return object[key]
        end)
        ;
        if ok then
            return v
        end
    end
    local function put(object, key, value)
        if not object then
            return false
        end
        ;
        return pcall(function()
            object[key] = value
        end)
    end
    local function existing(target)
        if target == "Lighting" then
            return lighting
        elseif target == "Terrain" then
            return terrain
        elseif target == "Quality" then
            return quality
        end
        local class = classes[target]
        if class then
            local parent = lighting
            ;
            if target == "Clouds" then
                parent = terrain
            end
            ;
            return parent and parent:FindFirstChildOfClass(class)
        end
    end
    local function rawValue(d)
        local v = read(existing(d.target), d.prop)
        if d.target == "Quality" then
            return v and v.Value or nil
        end
        return v
    end
    local listeners, watchTargets = {
    }, {
    }
    local nextListener = 0
    local bindObjects, unbindObjects
    local applying = false
    local atmosphereParking
    local baseValues
    local resetState
    local driftPending = false
    local driftPulse = 0
    api.overrideCount = 0
    api.contextRevision = 0
    api.baseName = "GAME ORIGINAL"
    api.baseId = nil
    api.skyName = "Game Original"
    api.dirty = {
    }
    api.dirtyCount = 0
    local function replaceValues(source)
        for key in pairs(values) do
            values[key] = nil
        end
        for key, value in pairs(source) do
            values[key] = value
        end
        api.values = values
    end
    local function same(a, b)
        if a == b then
            return true
        end
        if type(a) == "number" and type(b) == "number" then
            return math.abs(a - b) < 1e-05
        end
        local kind = typeof(a)
        if kind == "Color3" and typeof(b) == "Color3" then
            return math.abs(a.R - b.R) < 1e-05 and math.abs(a.G - b.G) < 1e-05 and math.abs(a.B - b.B) < 1e-05
        end
        if kind == "Vector3" and typeof(b) == "Vector3" then
            return (a - b).Magnitude < 1e-05
        end
        return false
    end
    local function updateDirty()
        api.dirty = {
        }
        ;
        api.dirtyCount = 0
        for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
            if d.kind ~= "readonly" and baseValues and not same(values[d.id], baseValues[d.id]) then
                api.dirty[d.id] = true
                ;
                api.dirtyCount = api.dirtyCount + 1
            end
        end
        api.name = api.baseName .. (api.dirtyCount > 0 and " / CUSTOM" or "")
    end
    local function publish(reason, changed)
        api.revision = api.revision + 1
        local batch = {
        }
        for id, fn in pairs(listeners) do
            batch[#batch + 1] = {
                id,
                fn,
            }
        end
        for _, entry in ipairs(batch) do
            if listeners[entry[1]] == entry[2] then
                local ok, err = pcall(entry[2], reason, changed or {
                }, api.revision)
                if not ok then
                    api.lastBindingError = tostring(err):sub(1, 180)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    function api.subscribe(fn)
        if type(fn) ~= "function" or api.closed then
            return {
                Disconnect = function()

                end,
            }
        end
        nextListener = nextListener + 1
        local id = nextListener
        ;
        listeners[id] = fn
        local connection = {
            Connected = true,
        }
        function connection:Disconnect()
            if not self.Connected then
                return 
            end
            self.Connected = false
            ;
            listeners[id] = nil
        end
        return connection
    end
    local function newContext(name, id)
        api.baseName = name
        ;
        api.baseId = id
        baseValues = copy(values)
        api.contextRevision = api.contextRevision + 1
        updateDirty()
    end
    local function dependency(id)
        local d = VisualSchema.byId[id]
        if not d then
            return nil
        end
        if d.prop ~= "Enabled" and (d.target == "Color" or d.target == "Bloom" or d.target == "DOF" or d.target == "Blur" or d.target == "SunRays" or d.target == "Clouds") then
            return d.target .. ".Enabled"
        end
        if id == "Lighting.FogStart" or id == "Lighting.FogEnd" or id == "Lighting.FogColor" then
            return "FX.FogEnabled"
        end
        if id == "Quality.SavedQualityLevel" then
            return "FX.ManageQuality"
        end
        local gates = {
            ["Lighting.ShadowSoftness"] = "Lighting.GlobalShadows",
            ["Sky.SunAngularSize"] = "Sky.CelestialBodiesShown",
            ["Sky.MoonAngularSize"] = "Sky.CelestialBodiesShown",
            ["Sky.SunTextureId"] = "Sky.CelestialBodiesShown",
            ["Sky.MoonTextureId"] = "Sky.CelestialBodiesShown",
            ["FX.Reflectance"] = "FX.ReflectEnabled",
            ["FX.CycleMinutes"] = "FX.AutoCycle",
            ["FX.MotionStrength"] = "FX.MotionEnabled",
            ["FX.MotionCap"] = "FX.MotionEnabled",
            ["FX.FlareIntensity"] = "FX.FlareEnabled",
            ["FX.FlareSize"] = "FX.FlareEnabled",
            ["FX.GICount"] = "FX.GIEnabled",
            ["FX.GIBrightness"] = "FX.GIEnabled",
            ["FX.GIRange"] = "FX.GIEnabled",
            ["FX.GIRadius"] = "FX.GIEnabled",
        }
        return gates[id]
    end
    function api.dependency(id)
        return dependency(id)
    end
    local skyProbe
    if not existing("Sky") then
        pcall(function()
            skyProbe = Instance.new("Sky")
        end)
    end
    local optionalSupport = {
    }
    for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
        local value = rawValue(d)
        if value == nil and d.target == "Sky" and skyProbe then
            value = read(skyProbe, d.prop)
        end
        if d.optional then
            optionalSupport[d.id] = value ~= nil
        end
        if d.kind == "readonly" then
            value = value ~= nil and tostring(value) or "UNAVAILABLE / ENGINE CONTROLLED"
        elseif value == nil then
            value = d.default
        end
        values[d.id] = value
        ;
        initial[d.id] = value
    end
    if skyProbe then
        skyProbe:Destroy()
        ;
        skyProbe = nil
    end
    api.values, api.initial, api.schema = values, initial, VisualSchema
    baseValues = copy(values)
    local function force(object, key, value)
        if not object then
            return false
        end
        local current = read(object, key)
        if current == nil then
            return false
        end
        if owned[object] then
            if same(current, value) then
                return true
            end
            ;
            return put(object, key, value)
        end
        local r = records[object]
        ;
        if not r then
            r = {
            }
            ;
            records[object] = r
        end
        if not r[key] then
            r[key] = {
                original = current,
            }
        end
        if same(current, value) then
            r[key].forced = current
            ;
            return true
        end
        local ok = put(object, key, value)
        if ok then
            r[key].forced = read(object, key)
        end
        return ok
    end
    local function restore(filter)
        for object, props in pairs(records) do
            for key, r in pairs(props) do
                if not filter or filter(object, key) then
                    local current = read(object, key)
                    if current == r.forced then
                        put(object, key, r.original)
                    end
                    props[key] = nil
                end
            end
            if not next(props) then
                records[object] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    local function create(class, parent)
        local ok, obj = pcall(function()
            return Instance.new(class)
        end)
        if not ok then
            return nil
        end
        owned[obj] = true
        ;
        obj.Name = "DEATH_NOTE_VISUALS_" .. class
        ;
        obj.Parent = parent
        return obj
    end
    local function ensureEffects()
        for target, class in pairs(classes) do
            if not effects[target] or not effects[target].Parent then
                local old = effects[target]
                if old and owned[old] then
                    owned[old] = nil
                    ;
                    pcall(function()
                        old:Destroy()
                    end)
                end
                local parent = lighting
                ;
                if target == "Clouds" then
                    parent = terrain
                end
                if parent then
                    if target == "Sky" or target == "Atmosphere" or target == "Clouds" then
                        effects[target] = existing(target) or create(class, parent)
                    else
                        effects[target] = create(class, parent)
                    end
                    if target == "Motion" and effects[target] then
                        put(effects[target], "Enabled", false)
                        ;
                        put(effects[target], "Size", 0)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function suppressGameFX(node)
        if not api.enabled or owned[node] then
            return 
        end
        if node:IsA("PostEffect") then
            force(node, "Enabled", false)
        end
    end
    local function protect(node)
        local c = node
        while c and c ~= world do
            if owned[c] or c:IsA("Tool") or (c:IsA("Model") and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")) then
                return true
            end
            c = c.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function queue(node)
        if node and not reflectSeen[node] then
            reflectSeen[node] = true
            ;
            table.insert(reflectQueue, node)
        end
    end
    local function restartReflect()
        reflectQueue, reflectSeen, reflectHead = {
        }, {
        }, 1
        if api.enabled and values["FX.ReflectEnabled"] then
            queue(world)
        else
            restore(function(object, key)
                return key == "Reflectance" and object:IsA("BasePart")
            end)
        end
    end
    local function pumpReflect()
        if not api.enabled or not values["FX.ReflectEnabled"] then
            return 
        end
        local stop = os.clock() + 0.002
        ;
        local count = 0
        while reflectHead <= #reflectQueue and count < 40 and os.clock() <= stop do
            local node = reflectQueue[reflectHead]
            ;
            reflectHead = reflectHead + 1
            ;
            count = count + 1
            if node and node.Parent and not protect(node) then
                if node:IsA("BasePart") and not node:IsA("Terrain") then
                    force(node, "Reflectance", values["FX.Reflectance"])
                end
                for _, child in ipairs(node:GetChildren()) do
                    queue(child)
                end
            end
        end
        if reflectHead > #reflectQueue then
            reflectQueue, reflectSeen, reflectHead = {
            }, {
            }, 1
        end
    end
    local function clearGI()
        if giFolder then
            owned[giFolder] = nil
            ;
            giFolder:Destroy()
            ;
            giFolder = nil
        end
        for _, pair in ipairs(giLights) do
            owned[pair.part] = nil
            ;
            owned[pair.light] = nil
        end
        giLights = {
        }
        ;
        giIndex = 0
    end
    local function stepGI()
        if not values["FX.GIEnabled"] then
            if giFolder then
                clearGI()
            end
            ;
            return 
        end
        local cam = world.CurrentCamera
        ;
        if not cam then
            return 
        end
        local count = math.floor(values["FX.GICount"])
        if not giFolder then
            giFolder = create("Folder", world)
        end
        while #giLights > count do
            local pair = table.remove(giLights)
            ;
            owned[pair.part], owned[pair.light] = nil, nil
            ;
            pair.part:Destroy()
        end
        if #giLights < count then
            local part = create("Part", giFolder)
            part.Anchored, part.CanCollide, part.CanTouch, part.CanQuery, part.Transparency = true, false, false, false, 1
            part.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
            ;
            part.CastShadow = false
            local light = create("PointLight", part)
            ;
            light.Shadows = false
            ;
            light.Brightness = 0
            table.insert(giLights, {
                part = part,
                light = light,
            })
        end
        if #giLights == 0 then
            return 
        end
        giIndex = giIndex % #giLights + 1
        local pair = giLights[giIndex]
        ;
        local theta = (giIndex / count) * math.pi * 2
        local pos = cam.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(math.cos(theta) * values["FX.GIRadius"], 8, math.sin(theta) * values["FX.GIRadius"])
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        ;
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local ignored = {
            giFolder,
        }
        ;
        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            table.insert(ignored, LocalPlayer.Character)
        end
        ;
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignored
        local hit = world:Raycast(pos, Vector3.new(0, -64, 0), params)
        pair.light.Brightness = 0
        if hit then
            pair.part.Position = hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            pair.light.Color = read(hit.Instance, "Color") or values["Lighting.OutdoorAmbient"]
            pair.light.Range = values["FX.GIRange"]
            ;
            pair.light.Brightness = values["FX.GIBrightness"]
        end
    end
    local function ensureFlare()
        if flareRoot then
            return 
        end
        flareRoot = Instance.new("Frame")
        ;
        flareRoot.Name = "VisualsLensFX"
        ;
        flareRoot.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        ;
        flareRoot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ;
        flareRoot.Active = false
        ;
        flareRoot.ZIndex = 0
        ;
        flareRoot.Parent = MainGui
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            ;
            image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ;
            image.Image = i == 1 and "rbxassetid://277033149" or "rbxassetid://15164863822"
            image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 248, 210)
            ;
            image.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            ;
            image.ImageTransparency = 1
            ;
            image.Visible = false
            ;
            image.Active = false
            ;
            image.ZIndex = 0
            ;
            image.Parent = flareRoot
            table.insert(flareImages, image)
        end
    end
    local function stepFlare()
        if not values["FX.FlareEnabled"] then
            if flareRoot then
                flareRoot.Visible = false
            end
            ;
            return 
        end
        ensureFlare()
        ;
        flareRoot.Visible = true
        local cam = world.CurrentCamera
        ;
        if not cam then
            flareRoot.Visible = false
            ;
            return 
        end
        local sun = lighting:GetSunDirection()
        ;
        local point, front = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(cam.CFrame.Position + sun * 1000)
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        ;
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local ignored = {
        }
        ;
        if giFolder then
            table.insert(ignored, giFolder)
        end
        ;
        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            table.insert(ignored, LocalPlayer.Character)
        end
        ;
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignored
        local blocked = world:Raycast(cam.CFrame.Position, sun * 1000, params)
        local visible = front and sun.Y > 0 and not blocked
        local center = cam.ViewportSize / 2
        ;
        local direction = Vector2.new(point.X, point.Y) - center
        for i, image in ipairs(flareImages) do
            local factor = ({
                1,
                1.7,
                0.3,
                -0.3,
                -0.9,
            })[i]
            ;
            local p = center + direction * factor
            local size = (i == 1 and 220 or 45 + i * 10) * values["FX.FlareSize"]
            image.Position = UDim2.new(0, p.X, 0, p.Y)
            ;
            image.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
            image.ImageTransparency = 1 - values["FX.FlareIntensity"] * (i == 1 and 1 or 0.35)
            image.Visible = not not visible
        end
    end
    function api.supported(d)
        if type(d) == "string" then
            d = VisualSchema.byId[d]
        end
        if not d then
            return false
        end
        if d.target == "FX" then
            return true
        end
        if d.target == "Terrain" or d.target == "Clouds" then
            if not terrain then
                return false
            end
        end
        if d.target == "Quality" then
            return quality ~= nil
        end
        if d.optional then
            return optionalSupport[d.id]
        end
        if d.target == "Lighting" then
            return rawValue(d) ~= nil
        end
        return true
    end
    local function targetObject(d)
        if d.target == "Lighting" then
            return lighting
        end
        if d.target == "Terrain" then
            return terrain
        end
        if d.target == "Quality" then
            return quality
        end
        return effects[d.target]
    end
    local function liveValue(d)
        local value = read(targetObject(d), d.prop)
        if d.target == "Quality" then
            return value and value.Value or nil
        end
        return value
    end
    function api.read(id)
        local d = VisualSchema.byId[id]
        if not d then
            return nil, "Unknown setting"
        end
        if d.target == "FX" then
            return values[id]
        end
        return liveValue(d)
    end
    function api.status(id)
        local d = VisualSchema.byId[id]
        if not d then
            return "UNKNOWN"
        end
        if d.kind == "readonly" then
            return "READ ONLY / ENGINE CONTROLLED"
        end
        if not api.supported(d) then
            return "UNAVAILABLE ON THIS CLIENT"
        end
        if not api.enabled then
            return "STAGED / SHADERS OFF"
        end
        if api.errors[id] then
            return "BLOCKED / " .. api.errors[id]
        end
        local gate = dependency(id)
        if gate and not values[gate] then
            return "INACTIVE / ENABLE " .. (VisualSchema.byId[gate].label or gate)
        end
        if d.target == "Atmosphere" and values["FX.FogEnabled"] then
            return "INACTIVE / CLASSIC FOG MODE"
        end
        if d.target == "FX" then
            return "LIVE / LOCAL"
        end
        local actual = liveValue(d)
        if actual ~= nil and same(actual, values[id]) then
            return "APPLIED / LIVE"
        end
        return "WAITING / GAME OVERRIDE"
    end
    function api.explain(id)
        local d = VisualSchema.byId[id]
        if not d then
            return "Unknown field"
        end
        if d.target == "Terrain" then
            return "Only native Terrain water is affected; mesh/part water is not Terrain water."
        end
        if d.target == "Clouds" then
            return "Terrain clouds need Terrain, a visible sky and nonzero cover/density; indoor scenes can hide them."
        end
        if d.target == "DOF" then
            return "Depth of field depends on depth, focus distance and client graphics support; a flat/uniform scene may show little change."
        end
        if d.target == "Bloom" then
            return "Bloom needs sufficiently bright pixels above its threshold and supported client quality."
        end
        if d.target == "SunRays" then
            return "Sun rays need the sun/bright sky in view and supported client graphics."
        end
        if id == "Lighting.ShadowSoftness" or id == "Lighting.GlobalShadows" then
            return "Shadows need shadow-casting geometry and a compatible renderer/quality. Renderer technology is not client-writable."
        end
        if id == "FX.FlareEnabled" or id == "FX.FlareIntensity" or id == "FX.FlareSize" then
            return "Flare appears only with the sun in view, above the horizon and without raycast obstruction."
        end
        if id == "FX.MotionEnabled" or id == "FX.MotionStrength" or id == "FX.MotionCap" then
            return "Motion blur responds to camera rotation; a stationary camera should remain sharp."
        end
        if id:sub(1, 5) == "FX.GI" then
            return "Local bounded point lights require raycast surface hits. This is not engine GI or ray tracing."
        end
        if d.target == "Sky" then
            return "Roblox asset visibility/moderation and streaming are game-controlled. Texture changes are not guaranteed asset access."
        end
        if d.kind == "readonly" then
            return "Engine-controlled property; intentionally no hidden-property writes."
        end
        return "Applies locally. Other game scripts can override local properties; enable Realtime Shader Lock to reassert supported values."
    end
    local function printable(value)
        if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
            return string.format("RGB(%d,%d,%d)", math.floor(value.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(value.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(value.B * 255 + 0.5))
        end
        if typeof(value) == "Vector3" then
            return string.format("XYZ(%.3f,%.3f,%.3f)", value.X, value.Y, value.Z)
        end
        return tostring(value)
    end
    function api.audit()
        local result = {
            fields = {
            },
            total = 0,
            matched = 0,
            blocked = 0,
            inactive = 0,
        }
        for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
            local status = api.status(d.id)
            local actual = api.read(d.id)
            local entry = {
                id = d.id,
                requested = printable(values[d.id]),
                actual = printable(actual),
                status = status,
                note = api.explain(d.id),
            }
            result.total = result.total + 1
            if status:find("APPLIED", 1, true) or status == "LIVE / LOCAL" then
                result.matched = result.matched + 1
            elseif status:find("BLOCKED", 1, true) or status:find("WAITING", 1, true) then
                result.blocked = result.blocked + 1
            else
                result.inactive = result.inactive + 1
            end
            result.fields[#result.fields + 1] = entry
        end
        return result
    end
    function api.report()
        local audit = api.audit()
        local out = {
            "VISUALS V19 / LOCAL PROPERTY DIAGNOSTICS",
            "Preset: " .. api.baseName,
            "Master: " .. tostring(api.enabled) .. " | Edit count: " .. api.dirtyCount,
            "Matched: " .. audit.matched .. " / " .. audit.total .. " | blocked/overridden: " .. audit.blocked,
            "Property readback is not proof of GPU rendering or universal asset permission.",
            "",
        }
        for _, entry in ipairs(audit.fields) do
            out[#out + 1] = entry.id .. " | " .. entry.status
            out[#out + 1] = "  requested=" .. entry.requested .. " | actual=" .. entry.actual
            out[#out + 1] = "  " .. entry.note
        end
        return table.concat(out, "\n")
    end
    local function fogAtmospheres()
        if values["FX.FogEnabled"] then
            if not atmosphereParking or not atmosphereParking.Parent then
                atmosphereParking = create("Folder", world)
                ;
                atmosphereParking.Name = "DEATH_NOTE_VISUALS_FOG_PARKING"
            end
            for _, node in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
                if node:IsA("Atmosphere") then
                    force(node, "Parent", atmosphereParking)
                end
            end
        else
            restore(function(object, key)
                return key == "Parent" and object:IsA("Atmosphere")
            end)
            if effects.Atmosphere and owned[effects.Atmosphere] and effects.Atmosphere.Parent ~= lighting then
                put(effects.Atmosphere, "Parent", lighting)
            end
        end
    end
    function api.apply()
        if not api.enabled or api.closed or applying then
            return 
        end
        applying = true
        local ok, reason = pcall(function()
            ensureEffects()
            ;
            api.errors = {
            }
            for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
                if d.target ~= "FX" and d.kind ~= "readonly" then
                    local object = targetObject(d)
                    local value = values[d.id]
                    ;
                    local skip = false
                    if d.target == "Quality" then
                        skip = not values["FX.ManageQuality"]
                        value = value == 0 and Enum.SavedQualitySetting.Automatic or Enum.SavedQualitySetting["QualityLevel" .. tostring(math.floor(value))]
                    elseif d.prop == "FogStart" or d.prop == "FogEnd" then
                        if not values["FX.FogEnabled"] then
                            value = 1000000000
                        end
                        if values["FX.FogEnabled"] and d.prop == "FogEnd" then
                            value = math.max(value, values["Lighting.FogStart"] + 1)
                        end
                    end
                    if not skip then
                        if not force(object, d.prop, value) then
                            api.errors[d.id] = "CLIENT REJECTED PROPERTY WRITE"
                        elseif not ((d.prop == "FogStart" or d.prop == "FogEnd") and not values["FX.FogEnabled"]) then
                            local actual = liveValue(d)
                            if actual ~= nil and not same(actual, values[d.id]) then
                                api.errors[d.id] = "WRITE DID NOT STICK / ENGINE OR GAME OVERRIDE"
                                api.overrideCount = api.overrideCount + 1
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if not values["FX.ManageQuality"] then
                restore(function(object, key)
                    return object == quality and key == "SavedQualityLevel"
                end)
            end
            fogAtmospheres()
            for _, node in ipairs(lighting:GetDescendants()) do
                suppressGameFX(node)
            end
            local cam = world.CurrentCamera
            if cam then
                for _, node in ipairs(cam:GetDescendants()) do
                    suppressGameFX(node)
                end
            end
            if bindObjects then
                bindObjects()
            end
        end)
        applying = false
        if not ok then
            api.runtimeWarning = tostring(reason):sub(1, 160)
            ;
            return false, api.runtimeWarning
        end
        return true
    end
    function api.disable()
        api.enabled = false
        ;
        driftPending = false
        ;
        driftPulse = 0
        if unbindObjects then
            unbindObjects()
        end
