        reflectQueue, reflectSeen, reflectHead = {
        }, {
        }, 1
        restore()
        ;
        clearGI()
        for object in pairs(owned) do
            pcall(function()
                object:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        owned = {
        }
        ;
        effects = {
        }
        ;
        atmosphereParking = nil
        if flareRoot then
            flareRoot:Destroy()
            ;
            flareRoot = nil
            ;
            flareImages = {
            }
        end
        lastLook, filteredBlur = nil, 0
        publish("disabled")
    end
    function api.enable()
        if api.closed then
            return false, "Visuals closed"
        end
        if api.enabled then
            return true
        end
        if RedTelemetry.StopQuickGraphics then
            RedTelemetry.StopQuickGraphics()
        end
        api.enabled = true
        local applied, reason = api.apply()
        if applied == false then
            api.disable()
            ;
            return false, reason
        end
        restartReflect()
        ;
        publish("enabled")
        return true
    end
    function api.validate(id, value)
        local d = VisualSchema.byId[id]
        ;
        if not d or d.kind == "readonly" then
            return nil, "Unknown/read-only setting"
        end
        if d.kind == "number" then
            if id == "Lighting.ClockTime" and type(value) == "string" then
                local h, m = value:match("^(%d%d?):(%d%d)$")
                ;
                if h and tonumber(h) < 24 and tonumber(m) < 60 then
                    value = tonumber(h) + tonumber(m) / 60
                end
            end
            local n = tonumber(value)
            ;
            if not finite(n) then
                return nil, "Enter a finite number"
            end
            n = math.clamp(n, d.min, d.max)
            if id == "Lighting.ClockTime" then
                n = n % 24
            end
            if id == "Quality.SavedQualityLevel" or id == "FX.GICount" or id == "Sky.StarCount" then
                n = math.floor(n + 0.5)
            end
            return n
        elseif d.kind == "boolean" then
            if type(value) ~= "boolean" then
                return nil, "Use ON/OFF"
            end
            ;
            return value
        elseif d.kind == "color" then
            local ok, r, g, b = pcall(function()
                return value.R, value.G, value.B
            end)
            if not ok or not finite(r) or not finite(g) or not finite(b) then
                return nil, "Use RGB / HEX"
            end
            return Color3.new(math.clamp(r, 0, 1), math.clamp(g, 0, 1), math.clamp(b, 0, 1))
        elseif d.kind == "vector" then
            local ok, x, y, z = pcall(function()
                return value.X, value.Y, value.Z
            end)
            if not ok or not finite(x) or not finite(y) or not finite(z) then
                return nil, "Enter three finite axes"
            end
            return Vector3.new(math.clamp(x, -360, 360), math.clamp(y, -360, 360), math.clamp(z, -360, 360))
        elseif d.kind == "asset" then
            if type(value) ~= "string" and type(value) ~= "number" then
                return nil, "Enter a Roblox asset ID"
            end
            value = tostring(value):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if value == "" then
                return ""
            end
            local id = value:match("^(%d+)$") or value:match("^rbxassetid://(%d+)$")
            if id and tonumber(id) > 0 then
                return "rbxassetid://" .. id
            end
            if value:match("^rbxasset://[%w_/%-%.]+$") then
                return value
            end
            return nil, "Use a positive Roblox asset ID, not a URL or code"
        end
    end
    function api.set(id, value, editor)
        if api.closed then
            return false, "Visuals closed"
        end
        local v, reason = api.validate(id, value)
        if v == nil then
            return false, reason
        end
        local d = VisualSchema.byId[id]
        if editor and not api.supported(d) then
            return false, "This property is not supported on this client"
        end
        local before = copy(values)
        local changed = {
            [id] = true,
        }
        values[id] = v
        api.lastAutoEnabled = nil
        if editor then
            local gate = dependency(id)
            if gate and not values[gate] then
                values[gate] = true
                ;
                changed[gate] = true
                ;
                api.lastAutoEnabled = gate
            end
            if id == "Lighting.ClockTime" and values["FX.AutoCycle"] then
                values["FX.AutoCycle"] = false
                ;
                changed["FX.AutoCycle"] = true
            end
            if d.target == "Atmosphere" and values["FX.FogEnabled"] then
                values["FX.FogEnabled"] = false
                ;
                changed["FX.FogEnabled"] = true
            end
        end
        if d.target == "Sky" then
            api.skySelected = true
            ;
            api.skyName = "Custom Sky"
        end
        if id == "Lighting.FogStart" then
            values["Lighting.FogEnd"] = math.max(values["Lighting.FogEnd"], v + 1)
            ;
            changed["Lighting.FogEnd"] = true
        elseif id == "Lighting.FogEnd" then
            values[id] = math.max(v, values["Lighting.FogStart"] + 1)
        end
        if api.enabled then
            local applied, applyReason = api.apply()
            if applied == false or api.errors[id] then
                local failed = api.errors[id] or applyReason or "Client rejected this change"
                replaceValues(before)
                ;
                api.apply()
                ;
                updateDirty()
                ;
                publish("rejected", changed)
                return false, failed
            end
            if changed["FX.ReflectEnabled"] or changed["FX.Reflectance"] then
                restartReflect()
            end
            if not values["FX.GIEnabled"] then
                clearGI()
            end
        end
        updateDirty()
        ;
        publish(editor and "edit" or "set", changed)
        return true, api.enabled and "APPLIED" or "STAGED"
    end
    function api.edit(id, value)
        local before = copy(values)
        local wasOff = not api.enabled
        local ok, reason = api.set(id, value, true)
        if not ok then
            return false, reason
        end
        if wasOff and values["FX.AutoPreview"] and id ~= "FX.AutoPreview" then
            local enabled, why = api.enable()
            if not enabled or api.errors[id] then
                local failure = api.errors[id] or why or "Unable to preview this setting"
                api.disable()
                ;
                replaceValues(before)
                ;
                updateDirty()
                ;
                publish("rejected", {
                    [id] = true,
                })
                return false, failure
            end
        end
        return true, api.enabled and "APPLIED" or "STAGED"
    end
    local function captureReset()
        resetState = {
            values = copy(values),
            name = api.baseName,
            id = api.baseId,
            skyName = api.skyName,
        }
    end
    function api.resetShader()
        if api.closed then
            return false, "Visuals closed"
        end
        if not resetState then
            return false, "Select a shader or load a profile first"
        end
        local before = copy(values)
        replaceValues(resetState.values)
        api.baseName = resetState.name
        ;
        api.baseId = resetState.id
        ;
        api.skyName = resetState.skyName
        baseValues = copy(resetState.values)
        api.contextRevision = api.contextRevision + 1
        local ok, reason = api.enable()
        if not ok then
            replaceValues(before)
            ;
            updateDirty()
            ;
            publish("reset-failed")
            ;
            return false, reason
        end
        local applied, why = api.apply()
        restartReflect()
        ;
        if not values["FX.GIEnabled"] then
            clearGI()
        end
        updateDirty()
        ;
        publish("reset-shader")
        if applied == false then
            return false, why
        end
        return true, "FULL SHADER RESTORED"
    end
    function api.resetGroup(group)
        local changed = {
        }
        for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
            if d.group == group and d.kind ~= "readonly" then
                values[d.id] = (resetState and resetState.values or baseValues or initial)[d.id]
                ;
                changed[d.id] = true
            end
        end
        api.contextRevision = api.contextRevision + 1
        if api.enabled then
            api.apply()
            ;
            restartReflect()
            ;
            if not values["FX.GIEnabled"] then
                clearGI()
            end
        end
        updateDirty()
        ;
        publish("reset-section", changed)
    end
    function api.original()
        api.disable()
        if RedTelemetry.StopQuickGraphics then
            RedTelemetry.StopQuickGraphics()
        end
        resetState = nil
        ;
        replaceValues(initial)
        ;
        api.skySelected = false
        ;
        api.skyName = "Game Original"
        newContext("GAME ORIGINAL", nil)
        ;
        publish("original")
    end
    function api.preset(id)
        local selected
        for _, p in ipairs(VisualPresetData.presets) do
            if p.id == id then
                selected = p
                ;
                break
            end
        end
        if not selected then
            return false, "Unknown preset"
        end
        -- A complete baseline prevents old toggles/custom edits leaking into the next look.
        local nextValues = copy(initial)
        if api.skySelected then
            for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
                if d.target == "Sky" then
                    nextValues[d.id] = values[d.id]
                end
            end
        else
            for key, value in pairs(VisualPresetData.skies[#VisualPresetData.skies].values) do
                nextValues[key] = value
            end
        end
        for key, value in pairs(selected.values) do
            local v = api.validate(key, value)
            if v ~= nil then
                nextValues[key] = v
            end
        end
        if not api.skySelected then
            api.skyName = "PShade Default"
        end
        replaceValues(nextValues)
        ;
        api.skySelected = true
        newContext(selected.name, selected.id)
        local ok, reason = api.enable()
        if not ok then
            return false, reason
        end
        api.apply()
        ;
        restartReflect()
        ;
        if not values["FX.GIEnabled"] then
            clearGI()
        end
        baseValues = copy(values)
        ;
        captureReset()
        ;
        updateDirty()
        ;
        publish("preset")
        return true
    end
    function api.sky(name)
        if name == "Game Original" then
            for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
                if d.target == "Sky" then
                    values[d.id] = initial[d.id]
                end
            end
        else
            local found = false
            for _, sky in ipairs(VisualPresetData.skies) do
                if sky.name == name then
                    for key, v in pairs(sky.values) do
                        values[key] = v
                    end
                    ;
                    found = true
                    ;
                    break
                end
            end
            if not found then
                return false, "Unknown sky pack"
            end
        end
        api.skySelected = true
        ;
        api.skyName = name
        if api.baseName == "GAME ORIGINAL" or api.baseName:sub(1, 6) == "SKY / " then
            api.baseName = "SKY / " .. name
        end
        -- A sky selection changes the sky baseline, not the active shader's identity or other edits.
        for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
            if d.target == "Sky" then
                baseValues[d.id] = values[d.id]
            end
        end
        api.contextRevision = api.contextRevision + 1
        api.enable()
        ;
        api.apply()
        ;
        if not resetState then
            captureReset()
        end
        ;
        updateDirty()
        ;
        publish("sky")
        return true
    end
    local function serializeValues()
        local out = {
        }
        for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
            if d.kind ~= "readonly" then
                local v = values[d.id]
                if d.kind == "color" then
                    v = {
                        v.R,
                        v.G,
                        v.B,
                    }
                elseif d.kind == "vector" then
                    v = {
                        v.X,
                        v.Y,
                        v.Z,
                    }
                end
                out[d.id] = v
            end
        end
        return out
    end
    function api.export()
        return HttpService:JSONEncode({
            format = "DEATH_NOTE_VISUALS",
            version = 1,
            name = api.name,
            values = serializeValues(),
        })
    end
    function api.import(raw)
        if type(raw) ~= "string" or #raw > 32000 then
            return false, "Profile must be JSON under 32 KB"
        end
        local ok, data = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
        end)
        if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" or data.format ~= "DEATH_NOTE_VISUALS" or data.version ~= 1 or type(data.values) ~= "table" then
            return false, "Invalid Visuals JSON profile"
        end
        local staged = {
        }
        for id, value in pairs(data.values) do
            local d = VisualSchema.byId[id]
            ;
            if not d or d.kind == "readonly" then
                return false, "Unknown/read-only profile field"
            end
            if d.kind == "color" or d.kind == "vector" then
                if type(value) ~= "table" or #value ~= 3 or not finite(value[1]) or not finite(value[2]) or not finite(value[3]) then
                    return false, "Invalid RGB/XYZ array"
                end
                value = d.kind == "color" and Color3.new(value[1], value[2], value[3]) or Vector3.new(value[1], value[2], value[3])
            end
            local v, reason = api.validate(id, value)
            ;
            if v == nil then
                return false, reason
            end
            ;
            staged[id] = v
        end
        for id, value in pairs(staged) do
            values[id] = value
        end
        api.skySelected = true
        ;
        api.skyName = "Profile Sky"
        ;
        newContext("IMPORTED PROFILE", nil)
        if api.enabled then
            api.apply()
            ;
            restartReflect()
            ;
            if not values["FX.GIEnabled"] then
                clearGI()
            end
        end
        baseValues = copy(values)
        ;
        captureReset()
        ;
        updateDirty()
        ;
        publish("import")
        return true
    end
    function api.saveSlot(id)
        if type(id) ~= "number" or id % 1 ~= 0 or id < 1 or id > 3 then
            return false, "Use a session slot from 1 to 3"
        end
        slots[id] = {
            values = copy(values),
            baseValues = copy(baseValues),
            baseName = api.baseName,
            baseId = api.baseId,
            skyName = api.skyName,
            reset = resetState and {
                values = copy(resetState.values),
                name = resetState.name,
                id = resetState.id,
                skyName = resetState.skyName,
            } or nil,
        }
        return true
    end
    function api.loadSlot(id)
        local slot = slots[id]
        if not slot then
            return false, "Slot is empty / session only"
        end
        resetState = slot.reset and {
            values = copy(slot.reset.values),
            name = slot.reset.name,
            id = slot.reset.id,
            skyName = slot.reset.skyName,
        } or nil
        replaceValues(slot.values)
        ;
        api.skySelected = true
        api.baseName = slot.baseName
        ;
        api.baseId = slot.baseId
        ;
        api.skyName = slot.skyName or "Profile Sky"
        ;
        baseValues = copy(slot.baseValues)
        api.contextRevision = api.contextRevision + 1
        if api.enabled then
            api.apply()
            ;
            restartReflect()
            ;
            if not values["FX.GIEnabled"] then
                clearGI()
            end
        end
        updateDirty()
        ;
        publish("slot")
        return true
    end
    unbindObjects = function()
        for _, watch in pairs(watchTargets) do
            for _, connection in ipairs(watch.connections) do
                connection:Disconnect()
            end
        end
        watchTargets = {
        }
    end
    bindObjects = function()
        local wanted = {
            Lighting = lighting,
            Terrain = terrain,
            Quality = quality,
        }
        for target, object in pairs(effects) do
            wanted[target] = object
        end
        for target, watch in pairs(watchTargets) do
            if wanted[target] ~= watch.object then
                for _, connection in ipairs(watch.connections) do
                    connection:Disconnect()
                end
                watchTargets[target] = nil
            end
        end
        for target, object in pairs(wanted) do
            if object and not watchTargets[target] then
                local watch = {
                    object = object,
                    connections = {
                    },
                }
                watchTargets[target] = watch
                for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
                    if d.target == target then
                        local field = d
                        local ok, connection = pcall(function()
                            return object:GetPropertyChangedSignal(field.prop):Connect(function()
                                if applying or not api.enabled or api.closed then
                                    return 
                                end
                                local actual = liveValue(field)
                                if actual == nil then
                                    return 
                                end
                                if field.kind == "readonly" then
                                    local new = tostring(actual)
                                    if values[field.id] ~= new then
                                        values[field.id] = new
                                        ;
                                        publish("engine", {
                                            [field.id] = true,
                                        })
                                    end
                                    return 
                                end
                                local gate = dependency(field.id)
                                if gate and not values[gate] then
                                    return 
                                end
                                if same(actual, values[field.id]) then
                                    return 
                                end
                                if values["FX.LiveLock"] then
                                    driftPending = true
                                    api.overrideCount = api.overrideCount + 1
                                else
                                    values[field.id] = actual
                                    ;
                                    updateDirty()
                                    ;
                                    publish("game", {
                                        [field.id] = true,
                                    })
                                end
                            end)
                        end)
                        if ok then
                            table.insert(watch.connections, connection)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function step(delta)
        if not api.enabled or api.closed then
            return 
        end
        delta = math.clamp(delta or 0.016, 0.001, 0.1)
        driftPulse = driftPulse + delta
        if driftPending and driftPulse >= 1 / 30 then
            driftPending = false
            ;
            driftPulse = 0
            ;
            api.apply()
        end
        pumpReflect()
        if values["FX.AutoCycle"] then
            values["Lighting.ClockTime"] = (values["Lighting.ClockTime"] + delta * 24 / (values["FX.CycleMinutes"] * 60)) % 24
            ;
            force(lighting, "ClockTime", values["Lighting.ClockTime"])
        end
        local cam = world.CurrentCamera
        if values["FX.MotionEnabled"] and cam then
            local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
            local speed = lastLook and (look - lastLook).Magnitude / delta or 0
            filteredBlur = filteredBlur + (math.min(values["FX.MotionCap"], speed * values["FX.MotionStrength"]) - filteredBlur) * (1 - math.exp(-12 * delta))
            if effects.Motion then
                put(effects.Motion, "Enabled", true)
                ;
                put(effects.Motion, "Size", filteredBlur)
            end
            lastLook = look
        else
            lastLook = nil
            ;
            filteredBlur = 0
            ;
            if effects.Motion then
                put(effects.Motion, "Enabled", false)
                ;
                put(effects.Motion, "Size", 0)
            end
        end
        pulse = pulse + delta
        ;
        giPulse = giPulse + delta
        ;
        flarePulse = flarePulse + delta
        if pulse >= 0.5 then
            pulse = 0
            ;
            if values["FX.LiveLock"] then
                api.apply()
            end
            ;
            updateDirty()
            ;
            publish("tick", {
                ["Lighting.ClockTime"] = true,
            })
        end
        if giPulse >= 0.15 then
            giPulse = 0
            ;
            stepGI()
        end
        if flarePulse >= 0.05 then
            flarePulse = 0
            ;
            stepFlare()
        end
    end
    table.insert(connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
        local ok, reason = pcall(step, delta)
        ;
        if not ok then
            api.runtimeWarning = tostring(reason):sub(1, 110)
        else
            api.runtimeWarning = nil
        end
    end))
    table.insert(connections, lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(suppressGameFX))
    table.insert(connections, world.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(node)
        if not api.enabled then
            return 
        end
        if values["FX.ReflectEnabled"] then
            queue(node)
        end
        if node:IsA("PostEffect") and world.CurrentCamera and node:IsDescendantOf(world.CurrentCamera) then
            suppressGameFX(node)
        end
    end))
    function api.close()
        api.disable()
        ;
        api.closed = true
        ;
        listeners = {
        }
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
    end
    for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
        if not d.help then
            d.help = api.explain(d.id)
        end
    end
    return api
end)()
-- Simple ESP, fixed screen geometry and a readable local body-part expander.
-- No shot interception, auto-aim, remote calls or server-side hit/damage guarantees.
RedTelemetry.VisualOverlay = (function()
    local api = {
        closed = false,
        revision = 0,
        stats = {
            visible = 0,
        },
        runtimeWarning = nil,
    }
    local values = {
        Enabled = false,
        Style = "Outline",
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(95, 225, 255),
        MarkerEnabled = false,
        MarkerShape = "Circle",
        MarkerSize = 220,
        ExpanderEnabled = false,
        ExpanderPart = "Root",
        ExpanderSize = 8,
        TeamCheck = true,
    }
    api.values = values
    api.styles = {
        "Outline",
        "Box",
        "Names",
    }
    api.shapes = {
        "Square",
        "Circle",
        "Triangle",
    }
    local world = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local players = game:GetService("Players")
    local folder, markerGui, markerRoot
    local entries, lines, listeners, connections = {
    }, {
    }, {
    }, {
    }
    local nextListener = 0
    local pulse = 0
    local function finite(n)
        return type(n) == "number" and n == n and math.abs(n) < math.huge
    end
    local function drop(object)
        if object then
            pcall(function()
                object:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end
    local function make(
        class,
        name,
        parent
    )
        local object = Instance.new(class)
        ;
        object.Name = name
        ;
        object.Parent = parent
        ;
        return object
    end
    local function publish()
        api.revision = api.revision + 1
        local batch = {
        }
        ;
        for id, fn in pairs(listeners) do
            batch[#batch + 1] = {
                id,
                fn,
            }
        end
        for _, entry in ipairs(batch) do
            if listeners[entry[1]] == entry[2] then
                pcall(entry[2])
            end
        end
    end
    function api.subscribe(fn)
        if api.closed or type(fn) ~= "function" then
            return {
                Disconnect = function()

                end,
            }
        end
        nextListener = nextListener + 1
        ;
        local id = nextListener
        ;
        listeners[id] = fn
        local handle = {
            Connected = true,
        }
        function handle:Disconnect()
            self.Connected = false
            ;
            listeners[id] = nil
        end
        return handle
    end
    function api.fromHSV(
        h,
        s,
        v
    )
        h = (h % 1) * 6
        ;
        s = math.clamp(
            s,
            0,
            1
        )
        ;
        v = math.clamp(
            v,
            0,
            1
        )
        local c = v * s
        ;
        local x = c * (1 - math.abs(h % 2 - 1))
        ;
        local m = v - c
        local r, g, b = 0, 0, 0
        if h < 1 then
            r, g, b = c, x, 0
        elseif h < 2 then
            r, g, b = x, c, 0
        elseif h < 3 then
            r, g, b = 0, c, x
        elseif h < 4 then
            r, g, b = 0, x, c
        elseif h < 5 then
            r, g, b = x, 0, c
        else
            r, g, b = c, 0, x
        end
        return Color3.new(
            r + m,
            g + m,
            b + m
        )
    end
    function api.toHSV(color)
        local r, g, b = color.R, color.G, color.B
        local hi, lo = math.max(
            r,
            g,
            b), math.min(r,
            g,
            b
        )
        local delta = hi - lo
        ;
        local h = 0
        if delta > 1e-06 then
            if hi == r then
                h = ((g - b) / delta) % 6
            elseif hi == g then
                h = (b - r) / delta + 2
            else
                h = (r - g) / delta + 4
            end
            h = h / 6
        end
        return h, hi > 0 and delta / hi or 0, hi
    end
    local function clearEntry(entry)
        for _, object in pairs(entry) do
            drop(object)
        end
    end
    local function clearESP()
        for _, entry in pairs(entries) do
            clearEntry(entry)
        end
        entries = {
        }
        ;
        drop(folder)
        ;
        folder = nil
        ;
        api.stats.visible = 0
    end
    local function rootOf(model)
        local root = model and (model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model:FindFirstChild("Torso") or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or model:FindFirstChild("Head"))
        return root and root:IsA("BasePart") and root or nil
    end
    local function sameTeam(player)
        local mine = LocalPlayer.Team
        local theirs = player.Team
        return mine ~= nil and theirs ~= nil and mine == theirs and LocalPlayer.Neutral ~= true and player.Neutral ~= true
    end
    local function buildEntry(
        player,
        model,
        root
    )
        if not folder or not folder.Parent then
            folder = make(
                "Folder",
                "DEATH_NOTE_SIMPLE_ESP",
                world
            )
        end
        local entry = {
        }
        if values.Style == "Outline" then
            entry.visual = make(
                "Highlight",
                "SimpleESPOutline",
                folder
            )
            entry.visual.Adornee = model
            ;
            entry.visual.FillTransparency = 1
            ;
            entry.visual.OutlineTransparency = 0
            entry.visual.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        elseif values.Style == "Box" then
            entry.visual = make(
                "SelectionBox",
                "SimpleESPBox",
                folder
            )
            entry.visual.Adornee = model
            ;
            entry.visual.SurfaceTransparency = 1
            ;
            entry.visual.LineThickness = 0.04
            ;
            entry.visual.Transparency = 0
        else
            entry.visual = make(
                "BillboardGui",
                "SimpleESPName",
                folder
            )
            entry.visual.Adornee = model:FindFirstChild("Head") or root
            entry.visual.Size = UDim2.new(
                0,
                210,
                0,
                52
            )
            ;
            entry.visual.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(
                0,
                2.2,
                0
            )
            entry.visual.AlwaysOnTop = true
            ;
            entry.visual.LightInfluence = 0
            ;
            entry.visual.MaxDistance = 1200
            entry.text = make(
                "TextLabel",
                "SimpleESPNameText",
                entry.visual
            )
            entry.text.Size = UDim2.new(
                1,
                0,
                1,
                0
            )
            ;
            entry.text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ;
            entry.text.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            entry.text.TextSize = 12
            ;
            entry.text.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
            ;
            entry.text.TextWrapped = true
        end
        return entry
    end
    local function refreshESP()
        if not values.Enabled then
            clearESP()
            ;
            return 
        end
        if folder and not folder.Parent then
            clearESP()
        end
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            return 
        end
        local localRoot = LocalPlayer.Character and rootOf(LocalPlayer.Character)
        local origin = localRoot and localRoot.Position or camera.CFrame.Position
        local candidates = {
        }
        for _, player in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
            local model = player.Character
            local humanoid = model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = rootOf(model)
            if player ~= LocalPlayer and not (values.TeamCheck and sameTeam(player)) and model and model.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and root then
                local distance = (root.Position - origin).Magnitude
                if distance <= 1200 then
                    candidates[#candidates + 1] = {
                        player = player,
                        model = model,
                        root = root,
                        humanoid = humanoid,
                        distance = distance,
                    }
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
            return a.distance < b.distance
        end)
        local active = {
        }
        for i = 1, math.min(24, #candidates) do
            local contact = candidates[i]
            ;
            local model = contact.model
            active[model] = true
            local entry = entries[model]
            if entry and (not entry.visual or not entry.visual.Parent) then
                clearEntry(entry)
                ;
                entries[model] = nil
                ;
                entry = nil
            end
            if not entry then
                entry = buildEntry(
                    contact.player,
                    model,
                    contact.root
                )
                ;
                entries[model] = entry
            end
            if values.Style == "Outline" then
                entry.visual.OutlineColor = values.Color
            elseif values.Style == "Box" then
                entry.visual.Color3 = values.Color
            else
                entry.visual.Adornee = model:FindFirstChild("Head") or contact.root
                entry.text.Text = (
                    contact.player.DisplayName or contact.player.Name) .. string.format("\nHP %d/%d",
                    math.floor(contact.humanoid.Health),
                    math.max(1, math.floor(contact.humanoid.MaxHealth or 100))
                )
                entry.text.TextColor3 = values.Color
            end
        end
        for model, entry in pairs(entries) do
            if not active[model] then
                clearEntry(entry)
                ;
                entries[model] = nil
            end
        end
        api.stats.visible = math.min(24, #candidates)
    end
    local function clearMarker()
        drop(markerGui)
        ;
        markerGui = nil
        ;
        markerRoot = nil
        ;
        lines = {
        }
    end
    local function markerVertices()
        if values.MarkerShape == "Square" then
            return {
                {
                    -0.5,
                    -0.5,
                },
                {
                    0.5,
                    -0.5,
                },
                {
                    0.5,
                    0.5,
                },
                {
                    -0.5,
                    0.5,
                },
            }
        end
        if values.MarkerShape == "Triangle" then
            return {
                {
                    0,
                    -0.5,
                },
                {
                    0.5,
                    0.5,
                },
                {
                    -0.5,
                    0.5,
                },
            }
        end
        local points = {
        }
        for i = 0, 71 do
            local a = i / 72 * math.pi * 2
            ;
            points[#points + 1] = {
                math.cos(a) * 0.5,
                math.sin(a) * 0.5,
            }
        end
        return points
    end
    local function refreshMarker()
        if not values.MarkerEnabled then
            clearMarker()
            ;
            return 
        end
        if not markerGui or not markerGui.Parent then
            clearMarker()
            markerGui = make(
                "ScreenGui",
                "DEATH_NOTE_FIXED_VISUAL_SHAPE",
                MainGui.Parent
            )
            markerGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            ;
            markerGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            ;
            markerGui.DisplayOrder = 0
            ;
            markerGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            markerRoot = make(
                "Frame",
                "FixedScreenShape",
                markerGui
            )
            markerRoot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            ;
            markerRoot.Position = UDim2.new(
                0.5,
                0,
                0.5,
                0
            )
            markerRoot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ;
            markerRoot.Active = false
        end
        markerRoot.Size = UDim2.new(
            0,
            values.MarkerSize,
            0,
            values.MarkerSize
        )
        local vertices = markerVertices()
        for i = 1, #vertices do
            local a, b = vertices[i], vertices[i % #vertices + 1]
            local dx, dy = (b[1] - a[1]) * values.MarkerSize, (b[2] - a[2]) * values.MarkerSize
            local line = lines[i]
            if not line then
                line = make(
                    "Frame",
                    "ShapeEdge" .. i,
                    markerRoot
                )
                ;
                line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                ;
                line.BorderSizePixel = 0
                ;
                line.Active = false
                ;
                lines[i] = line
            end
            line.Size = UDim2.new(
                0,
                math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy),
                0,
                2
            )
            line.Position = UDim2.new(
                0.5 + (a[1] + b[1]) / 2,
                0,
                0.5 + (a[2] + b[2]) / 2,
                0
            )
            line.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
            ;
            line.BackgroundColor3 = values.Color
        end
        for i = #lines, #vertices + 1, -1 do
            drop(lines[i])
            ;
            lines[i] = nil
        end
    end
    -- Readable local-only replacement, not a decoded copy of the linked obfuscated core.
    -- Ownership records are strong and restore only values still matching our last write.
    local expanded = {
    }
    local expanderFolder
    api.expanderErrors = {
    }
    api.stats.expanded = 0
    api.parts = {
        "Root",
        "Head",
        "Torso",
    }
    local function readPart(part, key)
        local ok, value = pcall(function()
            return part[key]
        end)
        if ok then
            return value
        end
    end
    local function sameSize(a, b)
        if a == nil or b == nil then
            return false
        end
        local ok, same = pcall(function()
            return (a - b).Magnitude < 0.0001
        end)
        return ok and same
    end
    local function restorePart(part, record)
        if sameSize(readPart(part, "Size"), record.forcedSize) then
            pcall(function()
                part.Size = record.size
            end)
        end
        local current = readPart(part, "CanCollide")
        if record.forcedCollision ~= nil and current == record.forcedCollision and record.collision ~= nil then
            pcall(function()
                part.CanCollide = record.collision
            end)
        end
        drop(record.box)
        expanded[part] = nil
    end
    local function restoreExpanded()
        for part, record in pairs(expanded) do
            restorePart(part, record)
        end
        expanded = {
        }
        ;
        drop(expanderFolder)
        ;
        expanderFolder = nil
        api.stats.expanded = 0
        ;
        api.expanderErrors = {
        }
    end
    local function selectedPart(model)
        local part
        if values.ExpanderPart == "Head" then
            part = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
        elseif values.ExpanderPart == "Torso" then
            part = model:FindFirstChild("Torso") or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or model:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
        else
            part = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        return part and part:IsA("BasePart") and part or nil
    end
    local function updateExpander()
        if not values.ExpanderEnabled then
            restoreExpanded()
            ;
            return 
        end
        local active = {
        }
        ;
        local count = 0
        api.expanderErrors = {
        }
        for _, player in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= LocalPlayer and not (values.TeamCheck and sameTeam(player)) then
                local model = player.Character
                local humanoid = model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local part = model and model.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and selectedPart(model)
                if part and part.Parent then
                    local original = readPart(part, "Size")
                    if original then
                        local record = expanded[part]
                        if not record then
                            record = {
                                size = original,
                                collision = readPart(part, "CanCollide"),
                            }
                            expanded[part] = record
                        end
                        active[part] = true
                        local size = values.ExpanderSize
                        local wanted = Vector3.new(
                            math.max(record.size.X, size),
                            math.max(record.size.Y, size),
                            math.max(record.size.Z, size)
                        )
                        local ok, reason = pcall(function()
                            if not sameSize(part.Size, wanted) then
                                part.Size = wanted
                            end
                            if part.CanCollide ~= false then
                                part.CanCollide = false
                            end
                        end)
                        if ok then
                            local actualSize = readPart(part, "Size")
                            local actualCollision = readPart(part, "CanCollide")
                            if sameSize(actualSize, wanted) then
                                record.forcedSize = actualSize
                            end
                            if actualCollision == false then
                                record.forcedCollision = false
                            end
                            if not sameSize(actualSize, wanted) or actualCollision ~= false then
                                api.expanderErrors[tostring(player.UserId)] = "Game overrode the local size/collision write"
                            else
                                count = count + 1
                            end
                            if not expanderFolder or not expanderFolder.Parent then
                                drop(expanderFolder)
                                ;
                                expanderFolder = make(
                                    "Folder",
                                    "DEATH_NOTE_LOCAL_EXPANDER",
                                    world
                                )
                            end
                            if not record.box or not record.box.Parent then
                                record.box = make(
                                    "SelectionBox",
                                    "LocalExpandedPartBounds",
                                    expanderFolder
                                )
                                record.box.Adornee = part
                                ;
                                record.box.SurfaceTransparency = 1
                                ;
                                record.box.LineThickness = 0.035
                            end
                            record.box.Color3 = values.Color
                        else
                            api.expanderErrors[tostring(player.UserId)] = tostring(reason):sub(1, 130)
                            -- Retain ownership of any successful first write so OFF can still undo it.
                            local currentSize = readPart(part, "Size")
                            if sameSize(currentSize, wanted) then
                                record.forcedSize = currentSize
                            end
                            if readPart(part, "CanCollide") == false then
                                record.forcedCollision = false
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for part, record in pairs(expanded) do
            if not active[part] then
                restorePart(part, record)
            end
        end
        api.stats.expanded = count
        if not next(expanded) then
            drop(expanderFolder)
            ;
            expanderFolder = nil
        end
    end
    function api.expanderStatus()
        if not values.ExpanderEnabled then
            return "EXPANDER OFF / ORIGINAL PARTS RESTORED"
        end
        for _, reason in pairs(api.expanderErrors) do
            return "LOCAL WRITE NOTICE: " .. reason
        end
        return tostring(api.stats.expanded or 0) .. " LOCAL PARTS EXPANDED / SERVER HITS NOT GUARANTEED"
    end
    function api.set(id, value)
        if api.closed then
            return false, "Visual overlay closed"
        end
        if id == "Enabled" or id == "MarkerEnabled" or id == "ExpanderEnabled" or id == "TeamCheck" then
            if type(value) ~= "boolean" then
                return false, "Use ON/OFF"
            end
        elseif id == "Style" then
            if value ~= "Outline" and value ~= "Box" and value ~= "Names" then
                return false, "Choose Outline, Box or Names"
            end
        elseif id == "MarkerShape" then
            if value ~= "Square" and value ~= "Circle" and value ~= "Triangle" then
                return false, "Choose Square, Circle or Triangle"
            end
        elseif id == "ExpanderPart" then
            if value ~= "Root" and value ~= "Head" and value ~= "Torso" then
                return false, "Choose Root, Head or Torso"
            end
        elseif id == "ExpanderSize" then
            value = tonumber(value)
            ;
            if not finite(value) then
                return false, "Enter a finite size in studs"
            end
            value = math.clamp(
                value,
                1,
                30
            )
        elseif id == "MarkerSize" then
            value = tonumber(value)
            ;
            if not finite(value) then
                return false, "Enter a finite pixel size"
            end
            value = math.floor(math.clamp(value, 24, 800) + 0.5)
        elseif id == "Color" then
            if typeof(value) ~= "Color3" or not finite(value.R) or not finite(value.G) or not finite(value.B) then
                return false, "Choose a valid color"
            end
            value = Color3.new(
                math.clamp(value.R, 0, 1),
                math.clamp(value.G, 0, 1),
                math.clamp(value.B, 0, 1)
            )
        else
            return false, "Unknown visual option"
        end
        local changed = values[id] ~= value
        if id == "Style" and changed then
            clearESP()
        end
        if id == "ExpanderPart" and changed then
            restoreExpanded()
        end
        values[id] = value
        local ok, reason = pcall(function()
            refreshESP()
            ;
            refreshMarker()
            ;
            updateExpander()
        end)
        api.runtimeWarning = not ok and tostring(reason):sub(1, 160) or nil
        publish()
        return ok, api.runtimeWarning
    end
    connections[#connections + 1] = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(delta)
        if api.closed or not values.Enabled and not values.MarkerEnabled and not values.ExpanderEnabled then
            return 
        end
        pulse = pulse + math.clamp(
            delta or 0.016,
            0,
            0.25
        )
        if pulse < 0.15 then
            return 
        end
        ;
        pulse = 0
        local ok, reason = pcall(function()
            refreshESP()
            ;
            refreshMarker()
            ;
            updateExpander()
        end)
        api.runtimeWarning = not ok and tostring(reason):sub(1, 160) or nil
    end)
    function api.close()
        if api.closed then
            return 
        end
        api.closed = true
        ;
        values.Enabled = false
        ;
        values.MarkerEnabled = false
        ;
        values.ExpanderEnabled = false
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        clearESP()
        ;
        clearMarker()
        ;
        restoreExpanded()
        ;
        connections = {
        }
        ;
        listeners = {
        }
    end
    return api
end)()
-- Local camera assistance only. No firing, shot redirection, damage, remotes or hit-rate guarantees.
RedTelemetry.CameraAim = (function()
    local api = {
        closed = false,
        revision = 0,
        targetPlayer = nil,
        targetPart = nil,
        activeMode = nil,
        state = "OFF",
    }
    local values = {
        Enabled = false,
        FOVShape = "Circle",
        Size = 240,
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 150),
        AimPart = "Head",
        AssistChance = 100,
        TeamCheck = true,
        WallCheck = true,
        AimStrength = 20,
        AimlockEnabled = false,
        AimlockStrength = 80,
    }
    api.values = values
    ;
    api.shapes = {
        "Circle",
        "Square",
        "Rectangle",
    }
    ;
    api.parts = {
        "Head",
        "Torso",
        "Mix",
    }
    api.fromHSV = RedTelemetry.VisualOverlay.fromHSV
    ;
    api.toHSV = RedTelemetry.VisualOverlay.toHSV
    local players = game:GetService("Players")
    local world = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local guiService
    ;
    pcall(function()
        guiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
    end)
    local connections, listeners, guiConnections = {
    }, {
    }, {
    }
    local held, mix = {
    }, {
    }
    local aimGranted, lockGranted = false, false
    local aimTarget, lockTarget
    local gui, shapeRoot, touchButton
    local edges = {
    }
    local drawnShape, drawnSize, drawnColor
    local windowFocused = true
    local scanElapsed = 1
    local nextListener = 0
    local bindName = "DEATH_NOTE_CAMERA_AIM_V22"
    local bound = false
    local function finite(n)
        return type(n) == "number" and n == n and math.abs(n) < math.huge
    end
    local function drop(object)
        if object then
            pcall(function()
                object:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end
    local function connect(
        signal,
        fn,
        list
    )
        local c = signal:Connect(fn)
        ;
        local bucket = list or connections
        ;
        bucket[#bucket + 1] = c
        ;
        return c
    end
    local function publish()
        api.revision = api.revision + 1
        local batch = {
        }
        ;
        for id, fn in pairs(listeners) do
            batch[#batch + 1] = {
                id,
                fn,
            }
        end
        for _, entry in ipairs(batch) do
            if listeners[entry[1]] == entry[2] then
                pcall(entry[2])
            end
        end
    end
    function api.subscribe(fn)
        if api.closed or type(fn) ~= "function" then
            return {
                Disconnect = function()

                end,
            }
        end
        nextListener = nextListener + 1
        ;
        local id = nextListener
        ;
        listeners[id] = fn
        local handle = {
            Connected = true,
        }
        function handle:Disconnect()
            self.Connected = false
            ;
            listeners[id] = nil
        end
        return handle
    end
    local function heldNow()
        return next(held) ~= nil
    end
    local function roll()
        if values.AssistChance <= 0 then
            return false
        end
        if values.AssistChance >= 100 then
            return true
        end
        return math.random() * 100 < values.AssistChance
    end
    local function clearTarget()
        aimTarget = nil
        ;
        lockTarget = nil
        ;
        api.targetPlayer = nil
        ;
        api.targetPart = nil
        ;
        api.activeMode = nil
        ;
        scanElapsed = 1
    end
    local function releaseAll()
        held = {
        }
        ;
        aimGranted = false
        ;
        aimTarget = nil
        ;
        api.targetPlayer = nil
        ;
        api.targetPart = nil
        ;
        api.activeMode = nil
    end
    function api.hold(source, down)
        if api.closed then
            return false
        end
        local was = heldNow()
        if down then
            if not values.Enabled or not windowFocused or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
                return false
            end
            held[source] = true
        else
            held[source] = nil
        end
        local active = heldNow()
        if active and not was then
            mix = {
            }
            ;
            aimTarget = nil
            ;
            aimGranted = roll()
            ;
            scanElapsed = 1
        elseif not active then
            aimGranted = false
            ;
            aimTarget = nil
        end
        publish()
        ;
        return true
    end
    function api.contains(dx, dy)
        local half = values.Size / 2
        if values.FOVShape == "Circle" then
            return dx * dx + dy * dy <= half * half
        end
        if values.FOVShape == "Rectangle" then
            return math.abs(dx) <= half and math.abs(dy) <= half * 0.625
        end
        return math.abs(dx) <= half and math.abs(dy) <= half
    end
    function api.alpha(strength, delta)
        local n = math.clamp(
            strength / 100,
            0,
            1
        )
        return 1 - (1 - n) ^ (math.clamp(delta or 1 / 60, 0, 0.1) * 60)
    end
    local function sameTeam(player)
        return LocalPlayer.Team ~= nil and player.Team ~= nil and LocalPlayer.Team == player.Team and LocalPlayer.Neutral ~= true and player.Neutral ~= true
    end
    local function torsoOf(model)
        return model:FindFirstChild("Torso") or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or model:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
    end
    local function selectedPart(player)
        local model = player.Character
        ;
        if not model then
            return nil
        end
        local head = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
        ;
        local torso = torsoOf(model)
        local part
        if values.AimPart == "Head" then
            part = head
        elseif values.AimPart == "Torso" then
            part = torso
        else
            local choice = mix[player]
            if not choice or choice.model ~= model then
                choice = {
                    model = model,
                    head = math.random(1, 2) == 1,
                }
                ;
                mix[player] = choice
            end
            part = choice.head and (head or torso) or (torso or head)
        end
        return part and part:IsA("BasePart") and part or nil
    end
    local function lineOfSight(
        camera,
        model,
        part
    )
        if not values.WallCheck then
            return true
        end
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        ;
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        ;
        params.IgnoreWater = true
        local ignored = {
        }
        ;
        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            ignored[#ignored + 1] = LocalPlayer.Character
        end
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignored
        local start = camera.CFrame.Position
        ;
        local direction = part.Position - start
        if direction.Magnitude < 0.001 then
            return true
        end
        local ok, hit = pcall(function()
            return world:Raycast(
                start,
                direction,
                params
            )
        end)
        if not ok then
            return false
        end
        return not hit or hit.Instance and hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(model) or false
    end
    local function candidate(
        player,
        mode,
        camera
    )
        if player == LocalPlayer or not player.Parent or values.TeamCheck and sameTeam(player) then
            return nil
        end
        local model = player.Character
        local humanoid = model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not model or not model.Parent or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            return nil
        end
        local part = selectedPart(player)
        ;
        if not part or not part.Parent then
            return nil
        end
        local delta = part.Position - camera.CFrame.Position
        if delta.Magnitude < 0.001 or delta.Magnitude > 5000 then
            return nil
        end
        local score
        if mode == "Aimbot" then
            local point, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            if not onScreen or point.Z <= 0 then
                return nil
            end
            local size = camera.ViewportSize
            local dx, dy = point.X - size.X / 2, point.Y - size.Y / 2
            if not api.contains(dx, dy) then
                return nil
            end
            score = dx * dx + dy * dy
        else
            local mine = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            score = (part.Position - (mine and mine.Position or camera.CFrame.Position)).Magnitude
        end
        return {
            player = player,
            model = model,
            part = part,
            score = score,
        }
    end
    function api.findTarget(mode)
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        ;
        if not camera then
            return nil
        end
        local candidates = {
        }
        for _, player in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
            local item = candidate(
                player,
                mode,
                camera
            )
            ;
            if item then
                candidates[#candidates + 1] = item
            end
        end
        table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
            if a.score == b.score then
                return a.player.UserId < b.player.UserId
            end
            ;
            return a.score < b.score
        end)
        for _, item in ipairs(candidates) do
            if lineOfSight(camera, item.model, item.part) then
                return item
            end
        end
    end
    local function stillValid(
        item,
        mode,
        camera,
        checkWall
    )
        if not item or item.player.Character ~= item.model or not item.part.Parent then
            return false
        end
        local current = candidate(
            item.player,
            mode,
            camera
        )
        if not current or current.part ~= item.part then
            return false
        end
        return not checkWall or lineOfSight(
            camera,
            item.model,
            item.part
        )
    end
    local function clearGui()
        for _, c in ipairs(guiConnections) do
            c:Disconnect()
        end
        ;
        guiConnections = {
        }
        drop(gui)
        ;
        gui = nil
        ;
        shapeRoot = nil
        ;
        touchButton = nil
        ;
        edges = {
        }
        drawnShape, drawnSize, drawnColor = nil, nil, nil
    end
    local function make(
        class,
        name,
        parent
    )
        local object = Instance.new(class)
        ;
        object.Name = name
        ;
        object.Parent = parent
        ;
        return object
    end
    local function drawFOV()
        if not values.Enabled then
            clearGui()
            ;
            return 
        end
        if not gui or not gui.Parent then
            clearGui()
            gui = make(
                "ScreenGui",
                "DEATH_NOTE_AIM_FOV",
                MainGui.Parent
            )
            gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            ;
            gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            ;
            gui.DisplayOrder = 1
            ;
            gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
            shapeRoot = make(
                "Frame",
                "AimFOVShape",
                gui
            )
            shapeRoot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            ;
            shapeRoot.Position = UDim2.new(
                0.5,
                0,
                0.5,
                0
            )
            shapeRoot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ;
            shapeRoot.Active = false
            touchButton = make(
                "TextButton",
                "TouchHoldAim",
                gui
            )
            touchButton.Size = UDim2.new(
                0,
                104,
                0,
                43
            )
            ;
            touchButton.Position = UDim2.new(
                1,
                -124,
                1,
                -117
            )
            touchButton.Text = "HOLD AIM"
            ;
            touchButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            ;
            touchButton.TextSize = 13
            touchButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
                22,
                28,
                41
            )
            ;
            touchButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            ;
            touchButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
            RedUI.Corner(touchButton, 10)
            connect(touchButton.InputBegan, function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    api.hold(input, true)
                end
            end, guiConnections)
        end
        touchButton.Visible = UserInputService.TouchEnabled == true
        touchButton.TextColor3 = values.Color
        if drawnShape == values.FOVShape and drawnSize == values.Size and drawnColor == values.Color then
            return 
        end
        local points = {
        }
        if values.FOVShape == "Circle" then
            for i = 0, 71 do
                local a = i / 72 * math.pi * 2
                ;
                points[#points + 1] = {
                    math.cos(a) * 0.5,
                    math.sin(a) * 0.5,
                }
            end
        else
            local h = values.FOVShape == "Rectangle" and 0.3125 or 0.5
            points = {
                {
                    -0.5,
                    -h,
                },
                {
                    0.5,
                    -h,
                },
                {
                    0.5,
                    h,
                },
                {
                    -0.5,
                    h,
                },
            }
        end
        shapeRoot.Size = UDim2.new(
            0,
            values.Size,
            0,
            values.Size
        )
        for i, a in ipairs(points) do
            local b = points[i % #points + 1]
            ;
            local dx, dy = (b[1] - a[1]) * values.Size, (b[2] - a[2]) * values.Size
            local edge = edges[i]
            if not edge then
                edge = make(
                    "Frame",
                    "AimFOVEdge" .. i,
                    shapeRoot
                )
                ;
                edge.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                ;
                edge.BorderSizePixel = 0
                ;
                edge.Active = false
                ;
                edges[i] = edge
            end
            edge.Size = UDim2.new(
                0,
                math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy),
                0,
                2
            )
            edge.Position = UDim2.new(
                0.5 + (a[1] + b[1]) / 2,
                0,
                0.5 + (a[2] + b[2]) / 2,
                0
            )
            edge.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
            ;
            edge.BackgroundColor3 = values.Color
        end
        for i = #edges, #points + 1, -1 do
            drop(edges[i])
            ;
            edges[i] = nil
        end
        drawnShape, drawnSize, drawnColor = values.FOVShape, values.Size, values.Color
    end
    function api.status()
        return api.state
    end
    function api.set(id, value)
        if api.closed then
            return false, "Camera assist closed"
        end
        if id == "Enabled" or id == "AimlockEnabled" or id == "TeamCheck" or id == "WallCheck" then
            if type(value) ~= "boolean" then
                return false, "Use ON/OFF"
            end
        elseif id == "FOVShape" then
            if value ~= "Circle" and value ~= "Square" and value ~= "Rectangle" then
                return false, "Choose Circle, Square or Rectangle"
            end
        elseif id == "AimPart" then
            if value ~= "Head" and value ~= "Torso" and value ~= "Mix" then
                return false, "Choose Head, Torso or Mix"
            end
        elseif id == "Size" or id == "AssistChance" or id == "AimStrength" or id == "AimlockStrength" then
            value = tonumber(value)
            ;
            if not finite(value) then
                return false, "Enter a finite number"
            end
            local minimum = id == "Size" and 40 or id == "AssistChance" and 0 or 1
            value = math.floor(math.clamp(value, minimum, id == "Size" and 1000 or 100) + 0.5)
        elseif id == "Color" then
            if typeof(value) ~= "Color3" or not finite(value.R) or not finite(value.G) or not finite(value.B) then
                return false, "Choose a valid colour"
            end
            value = Color3.new(
                math.clamp(value.R, 0, 1),
                math.clamp(value.G, 0, 1),
                math.clamp(value.B, 0, 1)
            )
        else
            return false, "Unknown camera-assist setting"
        end
        local changed = values[id] ~= value
        values[id] = value
        if id == "Enabled" and not value then
            releaseAll()
        end
        if id == "AimlockEnabled" and changed then
            lockGranted = value and roll() or false
            ;
            lockTarget = nil
            ;
            scanElapsed = 1
        end
        if changed and (id == "AimPart" or id == "TeamCheck" or id == "WallCheck" or id == "FOVShape" or id == "Size") then
            mix = {
            }
            ;
            clearTarget()
        end
        if id == "Enabled" and value and changed and UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
            api.hold("keyboard", true)
        end
        api.targetPlayer = nil
        ;
        api.targetPart = nil
        ;
        api.activeMode = nil
        if not values.Enabled and not values.AimlockEnabled then
            api.state = "OFF"
        elseif id == "Enabled" and value then
            api.state = "HOLD E / TOUCH TO AIM"
        elseif id == "AimlockEnabled" and value then
            api.state = lockGranted and "AIMLOCK READY" or "AIMLOCK CHANCE SKIPPED"
        end
        local ok, reason = pcall(drawFOV)
        ;
        api.runtimeWarning = not ok and tostring(reason):sub(1, 150) or nil
        publish()
        ;
        return ok, api.runtimeWarning
    end
    local function step(delta)
        if api.closed then
            return 
        end
        drawFOV()
        if not values.Enabled and not values.AimlockEnabled then
            api.state = "OFF"
            ;
            return 
        end
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not camera or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            clearTarget()
            ;
            releaseAll()
            ;
            api.state = "PAUSED / LOCAL CHARACTER"
            ;
            return 
        end
        if not windowFocused or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() or guiService and guiService.MenuIsOpen or camera.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
            releaseAll()
            ;
            clearTarget()
            ;
            api.state = "PAUSED / INPUT OR CAMERA"
            ;
            return 
        end
        local mode
        if values.Enabled and heldNow() then
            if not aimGranted then
                api.state = "ASSIST CHANCE SKIPPED"
                ;
                api.targetPlayer = nil
                ;
                api.targetPart = nil
                ;
                api.activeMode = nil
                ;
                return 
            end
            mode = "Aimbot"
        elseif values.AimlockEnabled then
            if not lockGranted then
                api.state = "AIMLOCK CHANCE SKIPPED"
                ;
                api.targetPlayer = nil
                ;
                api.targetPart = nil
                ;
                api.activeMode = nil
                ;
                return 
            end
            mode = "Aimlock"
        else
            api.state = "HOLD E / TOUCH TO AIM"
            ;
            api.targetPlayer = nil
            ;
            api.targetPart = nil
            ;
            api.activeMode = nil
            ;
            return 
        end
        scanElapsed = scanElapsed + math.clamp(
            delta or 1 / 60,
            0,
            0.1
        )
        local selected
        if mode == "Aimbot" then
            selected = aimTarget
        else
            selected = lockTarget
        end
        local rescan = scanElapsed >= 0.06
        if selected and not stillValid(selected, mode, camera, true) then
            selected = nil
            ;
            rescan = true
        end
        if rescan then
            scanElapsed = 0
            if mode == "Aimbot" or not selected then
                selected = api.findTarget(mode)
            end
            if mode == "Aimbot" then
                aimTarget = selected
            else
                lockTarget = selected
            end
        end
        if not selected then
            api.state = "NO ELIGIBLE TARGET"
            ;
            api.targetPlayer = nil
            ;
            api.targetPart = nil
            ;
            api.activeMode = nil
            ;
            return 
        end
        local current = camera.CFrame
        local direction = selected.part.Position - current.Position
        if direction.Magnitude < 0.001 then
            return 
        end
        local desired = CFrame.lookAt(current.Position, selected.part.Position)
        local strength = mode == "Aimlock" and values.AimlockStrength or values.AimStrength
        local alpha = api.alpha(strength, delta)
        camera.CFrame = alpha >= 0.999999 and desired or current:Lerp(desired, alpha)
        api.targetPlayer = selected.player
        ;
        api.targetPart = selected.part
        ;
        api.activeMode = mode
        api.state = mode:upper() .. " / " .. selected.player.Name .. " / " .. selected.part.Name
    end
    local function protectedStep(delta)
        local ok, reason = pcall(step, delta)
        api.runtimeWarning = not ok and tostring(reason):sub(1, 160) or nil
        if not ok then
            api.state = "CAMERA ASSIST ERROR"
            ;
            api.targetPlayer = nil
            ;
            api.targetPart = nil
            ;
            api.activeMode = nil
        end
    end
    connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, processed)
        if not processed and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E and values.Enabled and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
            api.hold("keyboard", true)
        end
    end)
    connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
            api.hold("keyboard", false)
        end
        if held[input] then
            api.hold(input, false)
        end
    end)
    connect(UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased, function()
        windowFocused = false
        ;
        releaseAll()
        ;
        clearTarget()
    end)
    if UserInputService.WindowFocused then
        connect(UserInputService.WindowFocused, function()
            windowFocused = true
        end)
    end
    connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving, function()
        releaseAll()
        ;
        clearTarget()
    end)
    connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function()
        releaseAll()
        ;
        clearTarget()
    end)
    connect(players.PlayerRemoving, function(player)
        mix[player] = nil
        ;
        if api.targetPlayer == player then
            clearTarget()
        end
    end)
    local ok = pcall(function()
        RunService:BindToRenderStep(
            bindName,
            Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2,
            protectedStep
        )
    end)
    if ok then
        bound = true
    else
        connect(RunService.RenderStepped, protectedStep)
    end
    function api.close()
        if api.closed then
            return 
        end
        api.closed = true
        ;
        values.Enabled = false
        ;
        values.AimlockEnabled = false
        ;
        releaseAll()
        ;
        clearTarget()
        ;
        clearGui()
        if bound then
            pcall(function()
                RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(bindName)
            end)
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(connections) do
            c:Disconnect()
        end
        connections = {
        }
        ;
        listeners = {
        }
        ;
        mix = {
        }
        ;
        api.state = "CLOSED"
    end
    return api
end)()

end

local function frame(
    parent,
    name,
    size,
    pos,
    color
)
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    ;
    f.Name = name
    ;
    f.Size = size
    ;
    f.Position = pos or UDim2.new()
    ;
    f.BackgroundColor3 = color or palette.card
    ;
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ;
    f.Parent = parent
    ;
    return f
end
local function round(f, n)
    RedUI.Corner(f, n or 9)
end
local function text(
    parent,
    name,
    s,
    size,
    pos,
    pt,
    color
)
    local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")

    t.Name = name
    ;
    t.Text = s
    ;
    t.Size = size
    ;
    t.Position = pos
    ;
    t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    ;
    t.TextSize = pt or 11
    ;
    t.TextColor3 = color or palette.white
    ;
    t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    ;
    t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ;
    t.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ;
    t.Parent = parent
    ;
    return t
end
local function button(
    parent,
    name,
    s,
    size,
    pos,
    callback,
    color
)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    ;
    b.Name = name
    ;
    b.Text = s
    ;
    b.Size = size
    ;
    b.Position = pos or UDim2.new()
    ;
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    ;
    b.TextSize = 10
    ;
    b.TextColor3 = palette.white
    ;
    b.BackgroundColor3 = color or palette.card
    ;
    b.AutoButtonColor = true
    ;
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ;
    b.Parent = parent
    ;
    round(b, 7)
    on(b.Activated, callback)
    ;
    return b
end
local function input(

    pos
)

    b.Name = name
    ;
    b.Text = s
    ;
    b.Size = size
    ;
    b.Position = pos
    ;
    b.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    ;
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    ;
    b.TextSize = 11
    ;
    b.TextColor3 = palette.white
    ;
    b.BackgroundColor3 = palette.field
    ;
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ;
    b.Parent = parent
    ;
    round(b, 6)
    ;
    return b
end

    f.ScrollBarImageColor3 = palette.cyan
    ;
    f.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    ;
    f.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
    ;
    f.Parent = parent
    ;
    return f
end
