sectionInfo.Visible = false
search = input(
    content,
    "VisualsSearch",
    "",
    UDim2.new(0, 166, 0, 25),
    UDim2.new(1, -178, 0, 6)
)
;
search.PlaceholderText = "Search this section..."
local list = scroll(
    content,
    "VisualsSectionList",
    UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -40),
    UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 37)
)
local footer = text(
    root,
    "VisualsFooter",
    "PRESETS: PSHADE ULTIMATE / @IM_PATRICK  |  LOCAL VISUALS ONLY  |  GPU COST VARIES",
    UDim2.new(1, -145, 0, 17),
    UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, -18),
    8,
    palette.muted
)
local function cancelDrag()
    if drag then
        drag = nil
        ;
        list.ScrollingEnabled = true
    end
end
local function clearList()
    cancelDrag()
    ;
    refreshers = {
    }
    ;
    presetCards = {
    }
    ;
    cardOrder = 0
    ;
    viewGeneration = viewGeneration + 1
    for _, c in ipairs(viewConnections) do
        c:Disconnect()
    end
    ;
    viewConnections = {
    }
    for _, node in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
        node:Destroy()
    end
    list.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
end
local function matches(s)
    local q = search.Text:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    ;
    return q == "" or s:lower(
        ):find(q,
        1,
        true
    )
end
local function pretty(n)
    return (string.format("%.3f", n):gsub("0+$", ""):gsub("%.$", ""))
end
local function card(name, height)
    cardOrder = cardOrder + 1
    ;
    local f = frame(
        list,
        name,
        UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, height),
        nil,
        palette.card
    )
    ;
    f.LayoutOrder = cardOrder
    ;
    round(f, 9)
    ;
    RedUI.Stroke(
        f,
        Color3.fromRGB(48, 55, 79),
        1
    )
    ;
    return f
end
-- Per-field bindings share the controller's current values; text being typed is never polled away.
local function fieldCard(d)
    local available = visual.supported(d) and d.kind ~= "readonly"
    local color = d.kind == "color" and palette.pink or d.kind == "boolean" and palette.cyan or palette.gold
    local height = d.kind == "color" and 128 or d.kind == "vector" and 112 or 103
    if d.help then
        height = height + 24
    end
    if not available then
        height = 92
    end
    local f = card("Setting_" .. d.id, height)
    local label = text(
        f,
        "Label",
        d.label,
        UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 20),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 6),
        11,
        available and color or palette.muted
    )
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    text(
        f,
        "Property",
        d.id,
        UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 15),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 27),
        8,
        palette.muted
    )
    if not available then
        local value = d.kind == "readonly" and tostring(visual.values[d.id]) or "UNAVAILABLE ON THIS CLIENT / TERRAIN"
        local disabled = text(
            f,
            "Unavailable",
            value,
            UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 23),
            UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 46),
            10,
            palette.muted
        )
        disabled.TextWrapped = true
        text(
            f,
            "ApplyState",
            d.kind == "readonly" and "READ ONLY / ENGINE CONTROLLED" or "NO SUPPORTED PROPERTY TARGET",
            UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14),
            UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -19),
            8,
            palette.muted
        )
        return 
    end
    local status = text(
        f,
        "ApplyState",
        "",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 15),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -19),
        8,
        palette.cyan
    )
    local validationError
    local renderers = {
    }
    local context = visual.contextRevision
    local generation = viewGeneration
    local function alive()
        return not visual.closed and generation == viewGeneration and f.Parent == list
    end
    local function render()
        if not alive() then
            return 
        end
        local contextChanged = context ~= visual.contextRevision
        context = visual.contextRevision
        for _, fn in ipairs(renderers) do
            fn(contextChanged)
        end
        status.Text = validationError or visual.status(d.id)
        status.TextColor3 = validationError and palette.pink or visual.enabled and palette.cyan or palette.gold
    end
    local function commit(value, quiet)
        local ok, reason = visual.edit(d.id, value)
        if not ok then
            validationError = reason or "CHANGE REJECTED BY CLIENT"
            if not quiet then
                message(validationError)
            end
        else
            validationError = nil
        end
        render()
        return ok
    end
    -- Every editable box has a protected draft and a context token. Preset/slot/import changes
    -- invalidate old drafts and delayed edits instead of writing yesterday's value back later.
    local function bindEntry(
        box,
        parse,
        format
    )
        local state = {
            editing = false,
            writing = false,
            dirty = false,
            token = 0,
            ignoreBlur = false,
        }
        local function writeCurrent()
            state.writing = true
            box.Text = format(visual.values[d.id])
            state.writing = false
        end
        table.insert(renderers, function(contextChanged)
            if contextChanged then
                state.token = state.token + 1
                state.dirty = false
                state.ignoreBlur = state.editing or box:IsFocused()
                writeCurrent()
            elseif not state.editing and not state.dirty and not box:IsFocused() then
                writeCurrent()
            end
        end)
        on(box.Focused, function()
            state.editing = true
            state.dirty = false
            state.ignoreBlur = false
            state.token = state.token + 1
            validationError = nil
        end)
        on(box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function()
            if state.writing or not alive() then
                return 
            end
            if not state.editing and not box:IsFocused() then
                return 
            end
            state.editing = true
            state.dirty = true
            state.ignoreBlur = false
            state.token = state.token + 1
            local token = state.token
            local editContext = visual.contextRevision
            validationError = nil
            task.delay(0.12, function()
                if not alive() or state.token ~= token or visual.contextRevision ~= editContext then
                    return 
                end
                if not state.editing or not state.dirty then
                    return 
                end
                local value = parse(box.Text)
                if value ~= nil then
                    commit(value, true)
                end
            end)
        end)
        on(box.FocusLost, function()
            -- Capture the draft before publishing can refresh the text box.
            local raw = box.Text
            local ignore = state.ignoreBlur
            state.token = state.token + 1
            state.editing = false
            state.dirty = false
            state.ignoreBlur = false
            if not alive() then
                return 
            end
            if not ignore then
                local value, reason = parse(raw)
                if value == nil then
                    validationError = reason or "INVALID INPUT / PREVIOUS VALUE KEPT"
                    message(validationError)
                else
                    commit(value, false)
                end
            end
            writeCurrent()
            render()
        end)
        writeCurrent()
    end
    if d.kind == "boolean" then
        local b = button(f, "Toggle_" .. d.id, "", UDim2.new(0, 77, 0, 27), UDim2.new(1, -88, 0, 45), function()
            commit(not visual.values[d.id])
        end)
        text(
            f,
            "StateMeaning",
            "SYNCED WITH CURRENT LOOK",
            UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 24),
            UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 46),
            8,
            palette.muted
        )
        table.insert(renderers, function()
            local value = visual.values[d.id]
            b.Text = value and "ON" or "OFF"
            b.BackgroundColor3 = value and Color3.fromRGB(28, 107, 116) or palette.field
        end)
    elseif d.kind == "number" then
        local box = input(
            f,
            "Value_" .. d.id,
            "",
            UDim2.new(0, 75, 0, 27),
            UDim2.new(1, -86, 0, 45)
        )
        bindEntry(box, function(raw)
            return visual.validate(d.id, raw)
        end, pretty)
        button(f, "Minus_" .. d.id, "-", UDim2.new(0, 25, 0, 27), UDim2.new(1, -144, 0, 45), function()
            commit(visual.values[d.id] - d.step)
        end)
        button(f, "Plus_" .. d.id, "+", UDim2.new(0, 25, 0, 27), UDim2.new(1, -115, 0, 45), function()
            commit(visual.values[d.id] + d.step)
        end)
        local bar = frame(
            f,
            "Slider_" .. d.id,
            UDim2.new(1, -168, 0, 24),
            UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 47),
            palette.field
        )
        round(bar, 7)
        local fill = frame(
            bar,
            "Fill",
            UDim2.new(),
            nil,
            Color3.fromRGB(78, 149, 175)
        )
        round(fill, 7)
        local function slide(x)
            local a = math.clamp(
                (x - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, bar.AbsoluteSize.X),
                0,
                1
            )
            commit(math.floor((d.min + (d.max - d.min) * a) / d.step + 0.5) * d.step, true)
        end
        bar.Active = true
        on(bar.InputBegan, function(inputEvent)
            if inputEvent.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or inputEvent.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                local focused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
                if focused then
                    focused:ReleaseFocus()
                end
                drag = {
                    input = inputEvent,
                    slide = slide,
                }
                list.ScrollingEnabled = false
                slide(inputEvent.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(renderers, function()
            local a = math.clamp(
                (visual.values[d.id] - d.min) / (d.max - d.min),
                0,
                1
            )
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(
                a,
                0,
                1,
                0
            )
        end)
    elseif d.kind == "color" then
        local swatch = frame(
            f,
            "Swatch",
            UDim2.new(0, 29, 0, 29),
            UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 49),
            visual.values[d.id]
        )
        round(swatch, 6)
        local hex = input(
            f,
            "Hex_" .. d.id,
            "",
            UDim2.new(0, 75, 0, 27),
            UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 50)
        )
        bindEntry(hex, function(raw)
            local value = raw:gsub("#", ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if #value ~= 6 or not value:match("^%x+$") then
                return nil, "USE SIX HEX DIGITS, e.g. #A8C9FF"
            end
            return Color3.fromRGB(
                tonumber(value:sub(1, 2), 16),
                tonumber(value:sub(3, 4), 16),
                tonumber(value:sub(5, 6), 16)
            )
        end, function(value)
            return string.format(
                "#%02X%02X%02X",
                math.floor(value.R * 255 + 0.5),
                math.floor(value.G * 255 + 0.5),
                math.floor(value.B * 255 + 0.5)
            )
        end)
        for i, channel in ipairs({
            "R",
            "G",
            "B",
        }) do
            local component = i
            local x = 128 + (i - 1) * 47
            text(
                f,
                channel .. "Label",
                channel,
                UDim2.new(0, 41, 0, 12),
                UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 40),
                8,
                palette.muted
            )
            local box = input(
                f,
                channel .. "_" .. d.id,
                "",
                UDim2.new(0, 41, 0, 27),
                UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 54)
            )
            bindEntry(box, function(raw)
                local number = tonumber(raw)
                if not number or number ~= number or math.abs(number) == math.huge then
                    return nil, "RGB REQUIRES A FINITE NUMBER"
                end
                local value = visual.values[d.id]
                local rgb = {
                    value.R * 255,
                    value.G * 255,
                    value.B * 255,
                }
                rgb[component] = math.clamp(
                    number,
                    0,
                    255
                )
                return Color3.fromRGB(
                    rgb[1],
                    rgb[2],
                    rgb[3]
                )
            end, function(value)
                local rgb = {
                    value.R,
                    value.G,
                    value.B,
                }
                return tostring(math.floor(rgb[component] * 255 + 0.5))
            end)
        end
        table.insert(renderers, function()
            swatch.BackgroundColor3 = visual.values[d.id]
        end)
    elseif d.kind == "vector" then
        for i, axis in ipairs({
            "X",
            "Y",
            "Z",
        }) do
            local component = i
            local box = input(
                f,
                axis .. "_" .. d.id,
                "",
                UDim2.new(0.31, -6, 0, 27),
                UDim2.new((i - 1) * 0.32, 10, 0, 48)
            )
            bindEntry(box, function(raw)
                local number = tonumber(raw)
                if not number or number ~= number or math.abs(number) == math.huge then
                    return nil, "XYZ REQUIRES A FINITE NUMBER"
                end
                local value = visual.values[d.id]
                local xyz = {
                    value.X,
                    value.Y,
                    value.Z,
                }
                xyz[component] = number
                return Vector3.new(
                    xyz[1],
                    xyz[2],
                    xyz[3]
                )
            end, function(value)
                return pretty(({
                    value.X,
                    value.Y,
                    value.Z,
                })[component])
            end)
        end
    elseif d.kind == "asset" then
        local box = input(
            f,
            "Asset_" .. d.id,
            "",
            UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 27),
            UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 46)
        )
        bindEntry(box, function(raw)
            return visual.validate(d.id, raw)
        end, tostring)
    end
    if d.help then
        local hint = text(
            f,
            "Hint",
            d.help,
            UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20),
            UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -43),
            8,
            palette.muted
        )
        hint.TextWrapped = true
    end
    table.insert(refreshers, render)
    render()
end
local function infoCard(
    name,
    title,
    body,
    height
)
    local f = card(name, height or 115)
    text(f, "Title", title, UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 6), 11, palette.cyan).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    local details = text(
        f,
        "Details",
        body,
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -38),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 31),
        9,
        palette.muted
    )
    details.TextWrapped = true
    ;
    details.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    return f
end
local function diagnosticPanel()
    infoCard(
        "VisualDiagnosticsIntro",
        "REAL PROPERTY READBACK / NO FALSE RENDER CLAIMS",
        "This report compares requested values with actual local properties. A matched property does not prove GPU rendering: shadows, depth of field, bloom, native Terrain water, streaming and asset permissions have scene/engine requirements.",
        124
    )
    local f = card("VisualDiagnosticActions", 42)
    local reportBox
    button(f, "RefreshVisualDiagnostics", "REFRESH REPORT", UDim2.new(0.5, -12, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6), function()
        reportBox.Text = visual.report()
    end)
    button(f, "CopyVisualDiagnostics", "COPY REPORT", UDim2.new(0.5, -12, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 6), function()
        if RedEnv.SetClipboard(reportBox.Text) then
            message("Diagnostic report copied")
        else
            message("Clipboard unavailable; copy the text manually")
        end
    end)
    local body = card("VisualDiagnosticReport", 410)
    reportBox = input(
        body,
        "VisualDiagnosticText",
        visual.report(),
        UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16),
        UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
    )
    reportBox.MultiLine = true
    ;
    reportBox.TextWrapped = true
    ;
    reportBox.TextSize = 9
    ;
    reportBox.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    ;
    reportBox.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
end
local function drawESP()
    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 9)
    ;
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    ;
    layout.Parent = list
    local generation = viewGeneration
    local renderers = {
    }
    local function alive()
        return generation == viewGeneration and not esp.closed
    end
    local function commit(id, value)
        local ok, why = esp.set(id, value)
        if not ok then
            message(why or "Visual change unavailable")
        end
        return ok
    end
    local function bindBox(
        box,
        parse,
        format
    )
        local editing, writing, token = false, false, 0
        local function write()
            writing = true
            ;
            box.Text = format()
            ;
            writing = false
        end
        table.insert(renderers, function()
            if not editing and not box:IsFocused() then
                write()
            end
        end)
        on(box.Focused, function()
            editing = true
            ;
            token = token + 1
        end)
        on(box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function()
            if writing or not alive() or not editing and not box:IsFocused() then
                return 
            end
            editing = true
            ;
            token = token + 1
            ;
            local pending = token
            task.delay(0.12, function()
                if not alive() or token ~= pending then
                    return 
                end
                local id, value = parse(box.Text)
                if id then
                    commit(id, value)
                end
            end)
        end)
        on(box.FocusLost, function()
            local raw = box.Text
            ;
            token = token + 1
            ;
            editing = false
            if not alive() then
                return 
            end
            local id, value = parse(raw)
            if id then
                commit(id, value)
            else
                message(value or "Invalid input; previous value kept")
            end
            write()
        end)
        write()
    end
    local switch = card("SimpleESPControl", 65)
    text(switch, "Title", "ESP", UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 24), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8), 15, palette.white).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    text(
        switch,
        "Hint",
        "Turn ON to choose an ESP type",
        UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 17),
        UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 35),
        9,
        palette.muted
    )
    local toggle = button(switch, "SimpleESPToggle", "", UDim2.new(0, 106, 0, 31), UDim2.new(1, -118, 0, 15), function()
        commit("Enabled", not esp.values.Enabled)
    end)
    local typeCard = card("SimpleESPTypes", 81)
    text(
        typeCard,
        "Title",
        "CHOOSE ESP TYPE",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 21),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 7),
        11,
        palette.cyan
    )
    local typeButtons = {
    }
    for i, name in ipairs(esp.styles) do
        local style = name
        local caption = style == "Names" and "NAME + HEALTH" or style:upper()
        typeButtons[style] = button(typeCard, "ESPType_" .. style, caption, UDim2.new(0.32, -6, 0, 29), UDim2.new((i - 1) * 0.33, 8, 0, 36), function()
            commit("Style", style)
        end)
    end
    table.insert(renderers, function()
        toggle.Text = esp.values.Enabled and "ESP ON" or "ESP OFF"
        toggle.BackgroundColor3 = esp.values.Enabled and Color3.fromRGB(35, 107, 116) or palette.field
        typeCard.Visible = esp.values.Enabled
        typeCard.Size = UDim2.new(
            1,
            -4,
            0,
            esp.values.Enabled and 81 or 0
        )
        for name, b in pairs(typeButtons) do
            b.BackgroundColor3 = esp.values.Style == name and Color3.fromRGB(135, 54, 104) or palette.field
        end
    end)
    -- Full HSV square with independent hue rail, plus HEX and RGB inputs.
    local picker = card("SimpleESPColorPicker", 239)
    text(picker, "Title", "ESP COLOUR", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 23), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 7), 13, palette.white).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    local sv = frame(
        picker,
        "ColorSVSquare",
        UDim2.new(0.64, -18, 0, 144),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 42),
        Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    )
    ;
    sv.Active = true
    ;
    sv.ClipsDescendants = true
    local saturation = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    ;
    saturation.Parent = sv
    local shade = frame(
        sv,
        "ValueShade",
        UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        UDim2.new(),
        Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    )
    ;
    shade.Active = true
    local valueGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    valueGradient.Rotation = 90
    valueGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
    })
    valueGradient.Parent = shade
    local cursor = frame(
        sv,
        "ColorSVCursor",
        UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 9),
        UDim2.new(),
        Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    )
    ;
    round(cursor, 9)
    ;
    RedUI.Stroke(
        cursor,
        Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
        1
    )
    cursor.Active = false
    local hue = frame(
        picker,
        "ColorHueRail",
        UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 144),
        UDim2.new(0.64, -2, 0, 42),
        Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    )
    ;
    hue.Active = true
    local rainbow = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    ;
    rainbow.Rotation = 90
    local keys = {
    }
    for i = 0, 6 do
        keys[#keys + 1] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / 6, esp.fromHSV(i / 6, 1, 1))
    end
    rainbow.Color = ColorSequence.new(keys)
    ;
    rainbow.Parent = hue
    local hueCursor = frame(
        hue,
        "ColorHueCursor",
        UDim2.new(1, 4, 0, 4),
        UDim2.new(0, -2, 0, 0),
        Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    )
    ;
    RedUI.Stroke(
        hueCursor,
        Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
        1
    )
    local swatch = frame(
        picker,
        "ColorPreview",
        UDim2.new(0, 27, 0, 27),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 197),
        esp.values.Color
    )
    ;
    round(swatch, 5)
    local readout = text(
        picker,
        "ColorReadout",
        "",
        UDim2.new(1, -61, 0, 26),
        UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 197),
        9,
        palette.muted
    )
    local hex = input(
        picker,
        "SimpleESPColorHex",
        "",
        UDim2.new(0.28, -10, 0, 27),
        UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0, 42)
    )
    local function colorHex(c)
        return string.format(
            "#%02X%02X%02X",
            math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5)
        )
    end
    bindBox(hex, function(raw)
        local s = raw:gsub("#", ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if #s ~= 6 or not s:match("^%x+$") then
            return nil, "Enter six HEX digits, e.g. #55CCFF"
        end
        return "Color", Color3.fromRGB(
            tonumber(s:sub(1, 2), 16),
            tonumber(s:sub(3, 4), 16),
            tonumber(s:sub(5, 6), 16)
        )
    end, function()
        return colorHex(esp.values.Color)
    end)
    for i, channel in ipairs({
        "R",
        "G",
        "B",
    }) do
        local component = i
        text(
            picker,
            channel .. "Label",
            channel,
            UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 24),
            UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0, 72 + (i - 1) * 35),
            9,
            palette.muted
        )
        local box = input(
            picker,
            "SimpleESPColor" .. channel,
            "",
            UDim2.new(0.28, -30, 0, 26),
            UDim2.new(0.72, 20, 0, 72 + (i - 1) * 35)
        )
        bindBox(box, function(raw)
            local n = tonumber(raw)
            if not n or n ~= n or math.abs(n) == math.huge then
                return nil, "Enter an RGB number from 0 to 255"
            end
            local c = esp.values.Color
            ;
            local rgb = {
                c.R * 255,
                c.G * 255,
                c.B * 255,
            }
            ;
            rgb[component] = math.clamp(
                n,
                0,
                255
            )
            return "Color", Color3.fromRGB(
                rgb[1],
                rgb[2],
                rgb[3]
            )
        end, function()
            local c = esp.values.Color
            ;
            return tostring(math.floor(({
                c.R,
                c.G,
                c.B,
            })[component] * 255 + 0.5))
        end)
    end
    local h, s, v = esp.toHSV(esp.values.Color)
    local lastColor
    local function colorRefresh()
        local c = esp.values.Color
        if c ~= lastColor then
            local nh, ns, nv = esp.toHSV(c)
            if nv > 1e-06 then
                s = ns
                ;
                if ns > 1e-06 then
                    h = nh
                end
            end
            v = nv
            ;
            lastColor = c
        end
        saturation.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), esp.fromHSV(h, 1, 1))
        cursor.Position = UDim2.new(
            s,
            -4,
            1 - v,
            -4
        )
        hueCursor.Position = UDim2.new(
            0,
            -2,
            h,
            -2
        )
        swatch.BackgroundColor3 = c
        readout.Text = colorHex(c) .. "  /  ESP / screen shape / expanded-part outline"
    end
    table.insert(renderers, colorRefresh)
    local function pickSV(x, y)
        s = math.clamp(
            (x - sv.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, sv.AbsoluteSize.X),
            0,
            1
        )
        v = 1 - math.clamp(
            (y - sv.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, sv.AbsoluteSize.Y),
            0,
            1
        )
        commit("Color", esp.fromHSV(h, s, v))
        ;
        colorRefresh()
    end
    local function pickHue(_, y)
        h = math.clamp(
            (y - hue.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, hue.AbsoluteSize.Y),
            0,
            1
        )
        commit("Color", esp.fromHSV(h, s, v))
        ;
        colorRefresh()
    end
    local function startPicker(signal, slide)
        on(signal, function(inputEvent)
            if inputEvent.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or inputEvent.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                local focused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
                ;
                if focused then
                    focused:ReleaseFocus()
                end
                drag = {
                    input = inputEvent,
                    slide = slide,
                }
                ;
                list.ScrollingEnabled = false
                slide(inputEvent.Position.X, inputEvent.Position.Y)
            end
        end)
    end
    startPicker(sv.InputBegan, pickSV)
    ;
    startPicker(shade.InputBegan, pickSV)
    ;
    startPicker(cursor.InputBegan, pickSV)
    ;
    startPicker(hue.InputBegan, pickHue)
    ;
    startPicker(hueCursor.InputBegan, pickHue)
    local marker = card("FixedShapeControls", 184)
    text(marker, "Title", "HITBOX / FIXED SCREEN SHAPE", UDim2.new(1, -145, 0, 22), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 8), 12, palette.white).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    local markerToggle = button(marker, "FixedShapeToggle", "", UDim2.new(0, 112, 0, 28), UDim2.new(1, -124, 0, 7), function()
        commit("MarkerEnabled", not esp.values.MarkerEnabled)
    end)
    text(
        marker,
        "Scope",
        "VISUAL ONLY / NO BULLET OR TARGETING EFFECT",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 17),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 37),
        8,
        palette.gold
    )
    local shapeButtons = {
    }
    for i, name in ipairs(esp.shapes) do
        local shape = name
        shapeButtons[shape] = button(marker, "FixedShape_" .. shape, shape:upper(), UDim2.new(0.32, -6, 0, 28), UDim2.new((i - 1) * 0.33, 8, 0, 63), function()
            commit("MarkerShape", shape)
        end)
    end
    text(
        marker,
        "SizeLabel",
        "SIZE / PIXELS",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 18),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 101),
        10,
        palette.cyan
    )
    local sizeBox = input(
        marker,
        "FixedShapeSizeValue",
        "",
        UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 28),
        UDim2.new(1, -85, 0, 123)
    )
    bindBox(sizeBox, function(raw)
        local n = tonumber(raw)
        if not n or n ~= n or math.abs(n) == math.huge then
            return nil, "Enter a size from 24 to 800 pixels"
        end
        return "MarkerSize", n
    end, function()
        return tostring(esp.values.MarkerSize)
    end)
    local bar = frame(
        marker,
        "FixedShapeSizeSlider",
        UDim2.new(1, -113, 0, 24),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 125),
        palette.field
    )
    ;
    round(bar, 7)
    ;
    bar.Active = true
    local fill = frame(
        bar,
        "Fill",
        UDim2.new(),
        nil,
        palette.cyan
    )
    ;
    round(fill, 7)
    local function sizeSlide(x)
        local a = math.clamp(
            (x - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, bar.AbsoluteSize.X),
            0,
            1
        )
        commit("MarkerSize", 24 + math.floor(a * 776 + 0.5))
    end
    startPicker(bar.InputBegan, sizeSlide)
    text(
        marker,
        "SizeHint",
        "Fixed at screen centre. Large values may extend beyond small screens.",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 17),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -23),
        8,
        palette.muted
    )
    table.insert(renderers, function()
        markerToggle.Text = esp.values.MarkerEnabled and "SHAPE ON" or "SHAPE OFF"
        markerToggle.BackgroundColor3 = esp.values.MarkerEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(35, 107, 116) or palette.field
        for name, b in pairs(shapeButtons) do
            b.BackgroundColor3 = esp.values.MarkerShape == name and Color3.fromRGB(135, 54, 104) or palette.field
        end
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(
            (esp.values.MarkerSize - 24) / 776,
            0,
            1,
            0
        )
    end)
    -- World-part expander controls are deliberately separate from pixel-sized screen geometry.
    local expand = card("LocalExpanderControls", 218)
    text(expand, "Title", "LOCAL HITBOX EXPANDER", UDim2.new(1, -145, 0, 22), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 8), 12, palette.white).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    local expandToggle = button(expand, "LocalExpanderToggle", "", UDim2.new(0, 112, 0, 28), UDim2.new(1, -124, 0, 7), function()
        commit("ExpanderEnabled", not esp.values.ExpanderEnabled)
    end)
    text(
        expand,
        "Scope",
        "CHANGES LOCAL PART SIZE / NO SERVER HIT OR DAMAGE GUARANTEE",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 17),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 37),
        8,
        palette.gold
    )
    local partButtons = {
    }
    for i, name in ipairs(esp.parts) do
        local part = name
        partButtons[part] = button(expand, "LocalExpanderPart_" .. part, part:upper(), UDim2.new(0.32, -6, 0, 28), UDim2.new((i - 1) * 0.33, 8, 0, 63), function()
            commit("ExpanderPart", part)
        end)
    end
    text(
        expand,
        "SizeLabel",
        "MINIMUM EDGE / STUDS",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 18),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 101),
        10,
        palette.cyan
    )
    local expanderSize = input(
        expand,
        "LocalExpanderSizeValue",
        "",
        UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 28),
        UDim2.new(1, -85, 0, 123)
    )
    bindBox(expanderSize, function(raw)
        local n = tonumber(raw)
        if not n or n ~= n or math.abs(n) == math.huge then
            return nil, "Enter a size from 1 to 30 studs"
        end
        return "ExpanderSize", n
    end, function()
        return pretty(esp.values.ExpanderSize)
    end)
    local expanderBar = frame(
        expand,
        "LocalExpanderSizeSlider",
        UDim2.new(1, -113, 0, 24),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 125),
        palette.field
    )
    ;
    round(expanderBar, 7)
    ;
    expanderBar.Active = true
    local expanderFill = frame(
        expanderBar,
        "Fill",
        UDim2.new(),
        nil,
        palette.cyan
    )
    ;
    round(expanderFill, 7)
    startPicker(expanderBar.InputBegan, function(x)
        local a = math.clamp(
            (x - expanderBar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, expanderBar.AbsoluteSize.X),
            0,
            1
        )
        commit("ExpanderSize", math.floor((1 + a * 29) * 10 + 0.5) / 10)
    end)
    local expanderStatus = text(
        expand,
        "ApplyStatus",
        "",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 160),
        8,
        palette.cyan
    )
    expanderStatus.TextWrapped = true
    text(expand, "SizeHint", "Never shrinks an original axis. OFF and target changes restore owned size/collision edits.", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 25), UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -30), 8, palette.muted).TextWrapped = true
    local team = card("SimpleTeamCheck", 86)
    text(team, "Title", "TEAM CHECK", UDim2.new(1, -145, 0, 23), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 9), 12, palette.white).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    local teamToggle = button(team, "SimpleTeamCheckToggle", "", UDim2.new(0, 112, 0, 28), UDim2.new(1, -124, 0, 7), function()
        commit("TeamCheck", not esp.values.TeamCheck)
    end)
    local teamHint = text(
        team,
        "Hint",
        "ON skips matching non-neutral Roblox Teams in ESP and expander. OFF includes teammates. Custom factions are not guessed.",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 34),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 41),
        9,
        palette.muted
    )
    teamHint.TextWrapped = true
    table.insert(renderers, function()
        expandToggle.Text = esp.values.ExpanderEnabled and "EXPANDER ON" or "EXPANDER OFF"
        expandToggle.BackgroundColor3 = esp.values.ExpanderEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(35, 107, 116) or palette.field
        for name, b in pairs(partButtons) do
            b.BackgroundColor3 = esp.values.ExpanderPart == name and Color3.fromRGB(135, 54, 104) or palette.field
        end
        expanderFill.Size = UDim2.new(
            (esp.values.ExpanderSize - 1) / 29,
            0,
            1,
            0
        )
        expanderStatus.Text = esp.expanderStatus()
        teamToggle.Text = esp.values.TeamCheck and "CHECK ON" or "CHECK OFF"
        teamToggle.BackgroundColor3 = esp.values.TeamCheck and Color3.fromRGB(35, 107, 116) or palette.field
    end)
    local function refresh()
        if alive() then
            for _, fn in ipairs(renderers) do
                fn()
            end
        end
    end
    table.insert(refreshers, refresh)
    ;
    refresh()
end
local function drawAim()
    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 9)
    ;
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    ;
    layout.Parent = list
    local generation = viewGeneration
    local renderers = {
    }
    local function alive()
        return generation == viewGeneration and not aim.closed
    end
    local function commit(id, value)
        local ok, why = aim.set(id, value)
        if not ok then
            message(why or "Camera assist change unavailable")
        end
        return ok
    end
    local function bindBox(
        box,
        parse,
        format
    )
        local editing, writing, token = false, false, 0
        local function write()
            writing = true
            ;
            box.Text = format()
            ;
            writing = false
        end
        table.insert(renderers, function()
            if not editing and not box:IsFocused() then
                write()
            end
        end)
        on(box.Focused, function()
            editing = true
            ;
            token = token + 1
        end)
        on(box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function()
            if writing or not alive() or not editing and not box:IsFocused() then
                return 
            end
            editing = true
            ;
            token = token + 1
            ;
            local pending = token
            task.delay(0.12, function()
                if not alive() or token ~= pending then
                    return 
                end
                local id, value = parse(box.Text)
                if id then
                    commit(id, value)
                end
            end)
        end)
        on(box.FocusLost, function()
            local raw = box.Text
            ;
            token = token + 1
            ;
            editing = false
            if not alive() then
                return 
            end
            local id, value = parse(raw)
            if id then
                commit(id, value)
            else
                message(value or "Invalid input; previous value kept")
            end
            write()
        end)
        write()
    end
    local main = card("AimbotControl", 82)
    text(main, "Title", "AIMBOT / CAMERA ASSIST", UDim2.new(1, -150, 0, 23), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 8), 13, palette.white).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    local enabled = button(main, "AimbotToggle", "", UDim2.new(0, 118, 0, 29), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 7), function()
        commit("Enabled", not aim.values.Enabled)
    end)
    local activity = text(
        main,
        "Activity",
        "",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 18),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 37),
        9,
        palette.cyan
    )
    text(
        main,
        "Hint",
        "PC: HOLD E  /  MOBILE: HOLD AIM button. No auto-fire or bullet redirection.",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 19),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 59),
        8,
        palette.muted
    )
    local choices = card("AimFOVChoices", 80)
    text(
        choices,
        "Title",
        "CHOOSE FOV SHAPE",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 7),
        11,
        palette.cyan
    )
    local shapeButtons = {
    }
    for i, name in ipairs(aim.shapes) do
        local shape = name
        shapeButtons[shape] = button(choices, "AimShape_" .. shape, shape:upper(), UDim2.new(0.32, -6, 0, 29), UDim2.new((i - 1) * 0.33, 8, 0, 36), function()
            commit("FOVShape", shape)
        end)
    end
    table.insert(renderers, function()
        enabled.Text = aim.values.Enabled and "AIMBOT ON" or "AIMBOT OFF"
        enabled.BackgroundColor3 = aim.values.Enabled and Color3.fromRGB(35, 107, 116) or palette.field
        activity.Text = aim.runtimeWarning and ("NOTICE: " .. aim.runtimeWarning) or aim.status()
        choices.Visible = aim.values.Enabled
        ;
        choices.Size = UDim2.new(
            1,
            -4,
            0,
            aim.values.Enabled and 80 or 0
        )
        for name, b in pairs(shapeButtons) do
            b.BackgroundColor3 = aim.values.FOVShape == name and Color3.fromRGB(135, 54, 104) or palette.field
        end
    end)
    local startPicker
    local function numberControl(
        name,
        label,
        id,
        minimum,
        maximum,
        step,
        help
    )
        local f = card(name, 113)
        text(f, "Title", label, UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 7), 11, palette.cyan).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        local box = input(
            f,
            name .. "Value",
            "",
            UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 28),
            UDim2.new(1, -87, 0, 40)
        )
        bindBox(box, function(raw)
            local n = tonumber(raw)
            if not n or n ~= n or math.abs(n) == math.huge then
                return nil, "Enter a finite number"
            end
            return id, n
        end, function()
            return pretty(aim.values[id])
        end)
        local bar = frame(
            f,
            name .. "Slider",
            UDim2.new(1, -113, 0, 24),
            UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 42),
            palette.field
        )
        ;
        round(bar, 7)
        ;
        bar.Active = true
        local fill = frame(
            bar,
            "Fill",
            UDim2.new(),
            nil,
            palette.cyan
        )
        ;
        round(fill, 7)
        startPicker(bar.InputBegan, function(x)
            local a = math.clamp(
                (x - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, bar.AbsoluteSize.X),
                0,
                1
            )
            commit(id, math.floor((minimum + (maximum - minimum) * a) / step + 0.5) * step)
        end)
        local hint = text(
            f,
            "Hint",
            help,
            UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 30),
            UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 78),
            8,
            palette.muted
        )
        ;
        hint.TextWrapped = true
        table.insert(renderers, function()
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(
                (aim.values[id] - minimum) / (maximum - minimum),
                0,
                1,
                0
            )
        end)
    end
    -- Full HSV square with independent hue rail, plus HEX and RGB inputs.
    local picker = card("AimColourPicker", 239)
    text(picker, "Title", "AIM FOV COLOUR", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 23), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 7), 13, palette.white).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    local sv = frame(
        picker,
        "AimColorSVSquare",
        UDim2.new(0.64, -18, 0, 144),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 42),
        Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    )
    ;
    sv.Active = true
    ;
    sv.ClipsDescendants = true
    local saturation = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    ;
    saturation.Parent = sv
    local shade = frame(
        sv,
        "ValueShade",
        UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        UDim2.new(),
        Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    )
    ;
    shade.Active = true
    local valueGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    valueGradient.Rotation = 90
    valueGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
    })
    valueGradient.Parent = shade
    local cursor = frame(
        sv,
        "AimColorSVCursor",
        UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 9),
        UDim2.new(),
        Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    )
    ;
    round(cursor, 9)
    ;
    RedUI.Stroke(
        cursor,
        Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
        1
    )
    cursor.Active = false
    local hue = frame(
        picker,
        "AimColorHueRail",
        UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 144),
        UDim2.new(0.64, -2, 0, 42),
        Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    )
    ;
    hue.Active = true
    local rainbow = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    ;
    rainbow.Rotation = 90
    local keys = {
    }
    for i = 0, 6 do
        keys[#keys + 1] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / 6, aim.fromHSV(i / 6, 1, 1))
    end
    rainbow.Color = ColorSequence.new(keys)
    ;
    rainbow.Parent = hue
    local hueCursor = frame(
        hue,
        "AimColorHueCursor",
        UDim2.new(1, 4, 0, 4),
        UDim2.new(0, -2, 0, 0),
        Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    )
    ;
    RedUI.Stroke(
        hueCursor,
        Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
        1
    )
    local swatch = frame(
        picker,
        "AimColorPreview",
        UDim2.new(0, 27, 0, 27),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 197),
        aim.values.Color
    )
    ;
    round(swatch, 5)
    local readout = text(
        picker,
        "AimColorReadout",
        "",
        UDim2.new(1, -61, 0, 26),
        UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 197),
        9,
        palette.muted
    )
    local hex = input(
        picker,
        "AimColorHex",
        "",
        UDim2.new(0.28, -10, 0, 27),
        UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0, 42)
    )
    local function colorHex(c)
        return string.format(
            "#%02X%02X%02X",
            math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5)
        )
    end
    bindBox(hex, function(raw)
        local s = raw:gsub("#", ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if #s ~= 6 or not s:match("^%x+$") then
            return nil, "Enter six HEX digits, e.g. #55CCFF"
        end
        return "Color", Color3.fromRGB(
            tonumber(s:sub(1, 2), 16),
            tonumber(s:sub(3, 4), 16),
            tonumber(s:sub(5, 6), 16)
        )
    end, function()
        return colorHex(aim.values.Color)
    end)
    for i, channel in ipairs({
        "R",
        "G",
        "B",
    }) do
        local component = i
        text(
            picker,
            channel .. "Label",
            channel,
            UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 24),
            UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0, 72 + (i - 1) * 35),
            9,
            palette.muted
        )
        local box = input(
            picker,
            "AimColor" .. channel,
            "",
            UDim2.new(0.28, -30, 0, 26),
            UDim2.new(0.72, 20, 0, 72 + (i - 1) * 35)
        )
        bindBox(box, function(raw)
            local n = tonumber(raw)
            if not n or n ~= n or math.abs(n) == math.huge then
                return nil, "Enter an RGB number from 0 to 255"
            end
            local c = aim.values.Color
            ;
            local rgb = {
                c.R * 255,
                c.G * 255,
                c.B * 255,
            }
            ;
            rgb[component] = math.clamp(
                n,
                0,
                255
            )
            return "Color", Color3.fromRGB(
                rgb[1],
                rgb[2],
                rgb[3]
            )
        end, function()
            local c = aim.values.Color
            ;
            return tostring(math.floor(({
                c.R,
                c.G,
                c.B,
            })[component] * 255 + 0.5))
        end)
    end
    local h, s, v = aim.toHSV(aim.values.Color)
    local lastColor
    local function colorRefresh()
        local c = aim.values.Color
        if c ~= lastColor then
            local nh, ns, nv = aim.toHSV(c)
            if nv > 1e-06 then
                s = ns
                ;
                if ns > 1e-06 then
                    h = nh
                end
            end
            v = nv
            ;
            lastColor = c
        end
        saturation.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), aim.fromHSV(h, 1, 1))
        cursor.Position = UDim2.new(
            s,
            -4,
            1 - v,
            -4
        )
        hueCursor.Position = UDim2.new(
            0,
            -2,
            h,
            -2
        )
        swatch.BackgroundColor3 = c
        readout.Text = colorHex(c) .. "  /  FOV outline only / independent of ESP"
    end
    table.insert(renderers, colorRefresh)
    local function pickSV(x, y)
        s = math.clamp(
            (x - sv.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, sv.AbsoluteSize.X),
            0,
            1
        )
        v = 1 - math.clamp(
            (y - sv.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, sv.AbsoluteSize.Y),
            0,
            1
        )
        commit("Color", aim.fromHSV(h, s, v))
        ;
        colorRefresh()
    end
    local function pickHue(_, y)
        h = math.clamp(
            (y - hue.AbsolutePosition.Y) / math.max(1, hue.AbsoluteSize.Y),
            0,
            1
        )
        commit("Color", aim.fromHSV(h, s, v))
        ;
        colorRefresh()
    end
    startPicker = function(signal, slide)
        on(signal, function(inputEvent)
            if inputEvent.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or inputEvent.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                local focused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
                ;
                if focused then
                    focused:ReleaseFocus()
                end
                drag = {
                    input = inputEvent,
                    slide = slide,
                }
                ;
                list.ScrollingEnabled = false
                slide(inputEvent.Position.X, inputEvent.Position.Y)
            end
        end)
    end
    startPicker(sv.InputBegan, pickSV)
    ;
    startPicker(shade.InputBegan, pickSV)
    ;
    startPicker(cursor.InputBegan, pickSV)
    ;
    startPicker(hue.InputBegan, pickHue)
    ;
    startPicker(hueCursor.InputBegan, pickHue)
    numberControl(
        "AimSize",
        "FOV SIZE / PIXELS",
        "Size",
        40,
        1000,
        1,
        "Circle diameter / Square side / Rectangle width. Rectangle height is 62.5% of its width. Selection and outline use the same screen centre."
    )
    local sizeCard = list:FindFirstChild("AimSize")
    sizeCard.LayoutOrder = choices.LayoutOrder + 1
    picker.LayoutOrder = sizeCard.LayoutOrder + 1
    table.insert(renderers, function()
        sizeCard.Visible = aim.values.Enabled
        ;
        sizeCard.Size = UDim2.new(
            1,
            -4,
            0,
            aim.values.Enabled and 113 or 0
        )
        picker.Visible = aim.values.Enabled
        ;
        picker.Size = UDim2.new(
            1,
            -4,
            0,
            aim.values.Enabled and 239 or 0
        )
    end)
    local body = card("AimBodyPart", 83)
    text(
        body,
        "Title",
        "TARGET BODY PART",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 7),
        11,
        palette.cyan
    )
    local partButtons = {
    }
    for i, name in ipairs(aim.parts) do
        local part = name
        partButtons[part] = button(body, "AimPart_" .. part, part:upper(), UDim2.new(0.32, -6, 0, 29), UDim2.new((i - 1) * 0.33, 8, 0, 37), function()
            commit("AimPart", part)
        end)
    end
    table.insert(renderers, function()
        for name, b in pairs(partButtons) do
            b.BackgroundColor3 = aim.values.AimPart == name and Color3.fromRGB(135, 54, 104) or palette.field
        end
    end)
    numberControl(
        "AimChance",
        "AIM-ASSIST CHANCE / %",
        "AssistChance",
        0,
        100,
        1,
        "One chance roll per new E/touch hold or Aimlock ON activation. Changes apply on the next activation. This is NOT bullet-hit percentage."
    )
    local checks = card("AimChecks", 101)
    text(
        checks,
        "Title",
        "TARGET CHECKS / SHARED WITH AIMLOCK",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 7),
        11,
        palette.cyan
    )
    local team = button(checks, "AimTeamCheck", "", UDim2.new(0.5, -14, 0, 29), UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 38), function()
        commit("TeamCheck", not aim.values.TeamCheck)
    end)
    local wall = button(checks, "AimWallCheck", "", UDim2.new(0.5, -14, 0, 29), UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 38), function()
        commit("WallCheck", not aim.values.WallCheck)
    end)
    text(checks, "Hint", "Native Roblox Teams + local line-of-sight raycast. Custom factions and server penetration rules are not inferred.", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 26), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 73), 8, palette.muted).TextWrapped = true
    table.insert(renderers, function()
        team.Text = aim.values.TeamCheck and "TEAM CHECK ON" or "TEAM CHECK OFF"
        wall.Text = aim.values.WallCheck and "WALL CHECK ON" or "WALL CHECK OFF"
        team.BackgroundColor3 = aim.values.TeamCheck and Color3.fromRGB(35, 107, 116) or palette.field
        wall.BackgroundColor3 = aim.values.WallCheck and Color3.fromRGB(35, 107, 116) or palette.field
    end)
    local lock = card("AimlockControl", 99)
    text(lock, "Title", "AIMLOCK", UDim2.new(1, -150, 0, 23), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 9), 14, palette.white).Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    local lockToggle = button(lock, "AimlockToggle", "", UDim2.new(0, 118, 0, 29), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 8), function()
        commit("AimlockEnabled", not aim.values.AimlockEnabled)
    end)
    text(lock, "Hint", "ON acquires the closest eligible opponent in world distance, then tracks it until invalid. Independent of E hold and FOV. Team/Wall Check still apply.", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 47), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 45), 9, palette.muted).TextWrapped = true
    table.insert(renderers, function()
        lockToggle.Text = aim.values.AimlockEnabled and "AIMLOCK ON" or "AIMLOCK OFF"
        ;
        lockToggle.BackgroundColor3 = aim.values.AimlockEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(35, 107, 116) or palette.field
    end)
    numberControl(
        "AimlockStrength",
        "AIMLOCK STRENGTH / %",
        "AimlockStrength",
        1,
        100,
        1,
        "Higher = stronger/faster camera response; 100 = instant orientation lock. Frame-rate-independent smoothing. OFF stops camera writes, without changing CameraType or CameraSubject."
    )
    local function refresh()
        if alive() then
            for _, fn in ipairs(renderers) do
                fn()
            end
        end
    end
    table.insert(refreshers, refresh)
    ;
    refresh()
end
local function drawProfile()
    local f = card("ProfileManager", 330)
    text(
        f,
        "Title",
        "JSON PROFILES / NO CODE EXECUTION",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 21),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 8),
        11,
        palette.cyan
    )
    local info = text(
        f,
        "Info",
        "Export, copy or paste a validated profile. Slots stay in this session only. No files, uploads or external loaders.",
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 38),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 32),
        9,
        palette.muted
    )
    ;
    info.TextWrapped = true
    local box = input(
        f,
        "VisualProfileJSON",
        draft,
        UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 124),
        UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 77)
    )
    ;
    box.MultiLine = true
    ;
    box.TextWrapped = true
    ;
    box.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    ;
    box.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    ;
    box.TextSize = 9
    on(box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function()
        draft = box.Text
    end)
    button(f, "ExportVisualProfile", "EXPORT", UDim2.new(0.31, -4, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 210), function()
        local ok, result = pcall(visual.export)
        ;
        if ok then
            box.Text = result
        else
            message("JSON export unavailable")
        end
    end)
    button(f, "CopyVisualProfile", "COPY", UDim2.new(0.31, -4, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0.33, 4, 0, 210), function()
        if RedEnv.SetClipboard(box.Text) then
            message("Profile JSON copied")
        else
            message("Clipboard unavailable; select/copy JSON manually")
        end
    end)
    button(f, "ImportVisualProfile", "IMPORT", UDim2.new(0.31, -4, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0.66, 0, 0, 210), function()
        local ok, reason = visual.import(box.Text)
        ;
        message(ok and (visual.enabled and "Profile applied" or "Profile staged; turn shaders ON to preview") or reason)
    end)
    for i = 1, 3 do
        local x = (i - 1) * 0.33
        button(f, "SaveVisualSlot" .. i, "SAVE " .. i, UDim2.new(0.31, -4, 0, 25), UDim2.new(x, 8, 0, 253), function()
            visual.saveSlot(i)
            ;
            message("Session slot " .. i .. " saved")
        end)
        button(f, "LoadVisualSlot" .. i, "LOAD " .. i, UDim2.new(0.31, -4, 0, 25), UDim2.new(x, 8, 0, 286), function()
            local ok, reason = visual.loadSlot(i)
            ;
            message(ok and ("Session slot " .. i .. (visual.enabled and " applied" or " staged")) or reason)
        end)
    end
end
redraw = function()
    clearList()
    ;
    bucket = viewConnections
    ;
    heading.Text = mode == "Aim" and "AIMBOT / CAMERA ASSIST" or mode == "ESP" and "ESP & FIXED SHAPE" or category:upper()
    sidebar.Visible = mode ~= "ESP" and mode ~= "Aim"
    search.Visible = mode ~= "ESP" and mode ~= "Aim"
    content.Size = (
        mode == "ESP" or mode == "Aim") and UDim2.new(1,
        0,
        1,
        0) or UDim2.new(1,
        -124,
        1,
        0
    )
    content.Position = (
        mode == "ESP" or mode == "Aim") and UDim2.new() or UDim2.new(0,
        124,
        0,
        0
    )
    heading.Size = (
        mode == "ESP" or mode == "Aim") and UDim2.new(1,
        -24,
        0,
        23) or UDim2.new(1,
        -191,
        0,
        23
    )
    shadersTab.BackgroundColor3 = mode == "Shaders" and Color3.fromRGB(147, 52, 107) or palette.card
    settingsTab.BackgroundColor3 = mode == "Settings" and Color3.fromRGB(48, 95, 135) or palette.card
    espTab.BackgroundColor3 = mode == "ESP" and Color3.fromRGB(48, 110, 108) or palette.card
    aimTab.BackgroundColor3 = mode == "Aim" and Color3.fromRGB(66, 105, 86) or palette.card
    for name, b in pairs(sideButtons) do
        b.BackgroundColor3 = name == category and Color3.fromRGB(52, 66, 100) or palette.card
        ;
        b.TextColor3 = name == category and palette.cyan or palette.muted
    end
    if mode == "Settings" then
        sectionInfo.Text = "PRECISE VALUES / RGB + HEX / TOUCH SLIDERS"
        local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        ;
        layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
        ;
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        ;
        layout.Parent = list
        local actions = card("SectionActions", 34)
        button(actions, "ResetVisualSection", "RESET SHADER", UDim2.new(0.35, -8, 0, 25), UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 4), function()
            local ok, why = visual.resetShader()
            ;
            message(ok and "Full selected shader restored" or why)
            ;
            redraw()
        end)
        button(actions, "ResetOnlyVisualSection", "SECTION", UDim2.new(0.26, -8, 0, 25), UDim2.new(0.35, 2, 0, 4), function()
            visual.resetGroup(category)
            ;
            message("Section restored to look baseline")
            ;
            redraw()
        end)
        button(actions, "RestoreGameVisuals", "RESTORE GAME", UDim2.new(0.39, -8, 0, 25), UDim2.new(0.61, 1, 0, 4), function()
            visual.original()
            ;
            redraw()
        end, Color3.fromRGB(104, 47, 79))
        if category == "Profiles" then
            drawProfile()
        elseif category == "Diagnostics" then
            diagnosticPanel()
        else
            for _, d in ipairs(VisualSchema.fields) do
                if d.group == category and matches(d.label .. " " .. d.id) then
                    fieldCard(d)
                end
            end
        end
    elseif mode == "ESP" then
        drawESP()
    elseif mode == "Aim" then
        drawAim()
    else
        sectionInfo.Text = category == "Weather" and "ATMOSPHERIC LOOKS / NO WEATHER SCRIPTS" or "PSHADE ULTIMATE / INSPECTED PRESET DATA"
        local layout = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
        layout.CellSize = UDim2.new(
            0.5,
            -6,
            0,
            148
        )
        layout.CellPadding = UDim2.new(
            0,
            10,
            0,
            10
        )
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        layout.Parent = list
        -- Original PShade photographs, above their shader names, never gradient-only substitutes.
        -- Image loading is independent of shader activation and never blocks the GUI.
        local function presetCard(
            name,
            sub,
            callback,
            tint,
            clock,
            photoUri,
            selectedName
        )
            if not matches(name .. " " .. sub) then
                return 
            end
            local b = button(
                list,
                "ShaderCard_" .. name,
                "",
                UDim2.new(),
                nil,
                callback
            )
            cardOrder = cardOrder + 1
            b.LayoutOrder = cardOrder
            b.Position = UDim2.new()
            b.BackgroundColor3 = palette.card
            b.ClipsDescendants = true
            round(b, 10)
            local photoFrame = frame(
                b,
                "PreviewFrame",
                UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 88),
                UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6),
                palette.field
            )
            photoFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
            round(photoFrame, 8)
            local fallback = text(
                photoFrame,
                "PreviewStatus",
                "LOADING ORIGINAL PHOTO...",
                UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0),
                UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0),
                9,
                palette.muted
            )
            fallback.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            fallback.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            fallback.TextWrapped = true
            local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            image.Name = "OriginalPreviewImage"
            image.Size = UDim2.new(
                1,
                0,
                1,
                0
            )
            image.Position = UDim2.new()
            image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            image.BorderSizePixel = 0
            image.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            image.ImageTransparency = 0
            image.Active = false
            image.Image = photoUri or VisualPresetData.defaultImage
            image.Parent = photoFrame
            round(image, 8)
            local originalUri = image.Image
            local assetId = originalUri:match("rbxassetid://(%d+)")
            local generation = viewGeneration
            local attempts = 0
            local nextRetry = 0
            local retry
            local function validPreview()
                return not visual.closed and viewGeneration == generation and b.Parent == list and image.Parent == photoFrame
            end
            local function refreshPhoto()
                if not validPreview() then
                    return 
                end
                local loaded = image.IsLoaded == true
                fallback.Visible = not loaded
                if loaded then
                    retry.Visible = false
                end
            end
            local function checkLater()
                task.delay(10, function()
                    if not validPreview() or image.IsLoaded then
                        return 
                    end
                    fallback.Text = "PHOTO NOT READY / RETRY"
                    retry.Visible = true
                end)
            end
            retry = button(photoFrame, "RetryPreview_" .. name, "RETRY", UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 20), UDim2.new(1, -50, 1, -25), function()
                if not validPreview() or os.clock() < nextRetry then
                    return 
                end
                nextRetry = os.clock() + 2
                attempts = attempts + 1
                retry.Visible = false
                fallback.Text = "LOADING ORIGINAL PHOTO..."
                fallback.Visible = true
                image.Image = ""
                task.defer(function()
                    if not validPreview() then
                        return 
                    end
                    -- Both URIs reference the same original asset; no replacement photograph.
                    if assetId and attempts % 2 == 1 then
                        image.Image = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. assetId .. "&w=420&h=420"
                    else
                        image.Image = originalUri
                    end
                    refreshPhoto()
                    checkLater()
                end)
            end, Color3.fromRGB(53, 74, 104))
            retry.TextSize = 8
            retry.Visible = false
            local badge = text(
                photoFrame,
                "PhotoBadge",
                clock and string.format("%02d:%02d", math.floor(clock) % 24, math.floor(clock % 1 * 60)) or "PSHADE",
                UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 18),
                UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 4),
                9,
                palette.white
            )
            badge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
            badge.BackgroundColor3 = palette.field
            badge.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            badge.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            round(badge, 5)
            local label = text(
                b,
                "Name",
                name:upper(),
                UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 23),
                UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 98),
                14,
                palette.white
            )
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            local subtitle = text(
                b,
                "Sub",
                sub,
                UDim2.new(1, -75, 0, 16),
                UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 123),
                8,
                palette.cyan
            )
            subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            subtitle.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            local action = text(
                b,
                "Action",
                "APPLY >",
                UDim2.new(0, 59, 0, 17),
                UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 122),
                9,
                palette.gold
            )
            action.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            action.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
            local stroke = RedUI.Stroke(
                b,
                Color3.fromRGB(73, 84, 115),
                1
            )
            table.insert(presetCards, {
                name = selectedName or name,
                stroke = stroke,
                action = action,
                photo = image,
            })
            on(image:GetPropertyChangedSignal("IsLoaded"), refreshPhoto)
            refreshPhoto()
            checkLater()
        end
        presetCard("Game Original", "RESTORE / SHADERS OFF", function()
            visual.original()
        end, nil, nil, VisualPresetData.defaultImage, "GAME ORIGINAL")
        if category == "Skyboxes" then
            for _, sky in ipairs(VisualPresetData.skies) do
                presetCard(sky.name, "SKY PACK / SAMPLE PHOTO", function()
                    visual.sky(sky.name)
                end, Color3.fromRGB(38, 79, 119), nil, sky.image, "SKY / " .. sky.name)
            end
        else
            for _, preset in ipairs(VisualPresetData.presets) do
                if preset.category == category then
                    presetCard(preset.name, preset.category:upper() .. " / PSHADE", function()
                        visual.preset(preset.id)
                    end, preset.values["Color.TintColor"] or palette.card, preset.values["Lighting.ClockTime"], preset.image, preset.name)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, refresh in ipairs(refreshers) do
        refresh()
    end
    bucket = uiConnections
end
drawSide = function()
    for _, c in ipairs(sideConnections) do
        c:Disconnect()
    end
    ;
    sideConnections = {
    }
    ;
    bucket = sideConnections
    for _, b in pairs(sideButtons) do
        b:Destroy()
    end
    ;
    sideButtons = {
    }
    if mode == "ESP" or mode == "Aim" then
        bucket = uiConnections
        ;
        return 
    end
    for i, name in ipairs(mode == "Shaders" and categories or VisualSchema.groups) do
        local b = button(sidebar, "VisualSection_" .. name, name:upper(), UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 35), UDim2.new(), function()
            category = name
            ;
            search.Text = ""
            ;
            redraw()
        end)
        b.LayoutOrder = i
        ;
        b.TextSize = 9
        ;
        sideButtons[name] = b
    end
    bucket = uiConnections
end
on(search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"), function()
    redraw()
end)
on(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(inputEvent)
    if not drag then
        return 
    end
    local pointer = drag.input
    if (pointer.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and inputEvent == pointer) or (pointer.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and inputEvent.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then
        drag.slide(inputEvent.Position.X, inputEvent.Position.Y)
    end
end)
on(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(inputEvent)
    if drag and (inputEvent == drag.input or inputEvent.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
        cancelDrag()
    end
end)
on(UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased, cancelDrag)
on(page:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), function()
    if not page.Visible then
        cancelDrag()
    end
end)
on(MainWindow:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), function()
    if not MainWindow.Visible then
        cancelDrag()
    end
end)
local function refreshStudio()
    if visual.closed then
        return 
    end
    if mode == "Aim" then
        master.Text = aim.values.Enabled and "AIM ON" or "AIM OFF"
        master.BackgroundColor3 = aim.values.Enabled and Color3.fromRGB(40, 112, 119) or palette.card
        status.Text = aim.values.AimlockEnabled and "AIMLOCK ON" or aim.values.Enabled and "HOLD E / TOUCH" or "AIM OFF"
        status.TextColor3 = (aim.values.Enabled or aim.values.AimlockEnabled) and palette.cyan or palette.gold
        footer.Text = aim.runtimeWarning and ("AIM NOTICE: " .. aim.runtimeWarning) or "CAMERA ONLY / ASSIST CHANCE IS NOT A BULLET HIT RATE"
        for _, refresh in ipairs(refreshers) do
            refresh()
        end
        return 
    end
    if mode == "ESP" then
        master.Text = esp.values.Enabled and "ESP ON" or "ESP OFF"
        master.BackgroundColor3 = esp.values.Enabled and Color3.fromRGB(40, 112, 119) or palette.card
        status.Text = esp.values.Enabled and (tostring(esp.stats.visible or 0) .. " CONTACTS") or esp.values.ExpanderEnabled and (tostring(esp.stats.expanded or 0) .. " EXPANDED") or esp.values.MarkerEnabled and "SHAPE ON / ESP OFF" or "ESP / SHAPE OFF"
        status.TextColor3 = esp.values.Enabled and palette.cyan or palette.gold
        footer.Text = esp.runtimeWarning and ("ESP NOTICE: " .. esp.runtimeWarning) or "LOCAL EXPANDER / SERVER HIT VALIDATION AND DAMAGE ARE NOT GUARANTEED"
        for _, refresh in ipairs(refreshers) do
            refresh()
        end
        return 
    end
    master.Text = visual.enabled and "SHADERS ON" or "SHADERS OFF"
    master.BackgroundColor3 = visual.enabled and Color3.fromRGB(40, 112, 119) or palette.card
    status.Text = visual.enabled and "LIVE SYNC" or "OFF / STAGED"
    status.TextColor3 = visual.enabled and palette.cyan or palette.gold
    local edits = visual.dirtyCount > 0 and (" / " .. visual.dirtyCount .. " EDITS") or ""
    footer.Text = visual.runtimeWarning and ("CLIENT NOTICE: " .. visual.runtimeWarning) or ("PSHADE | " .. visual.baseName:upper() .. edits .. (visual.enabled and " | LIVE SYNC" or " | STAGED / TURN ON"))
    for _, refresh in ipairs(refreshers) do
        refresh()
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(presetCards) do
        local selected = visual.baseName == p.name or p.name:sub(1, 6) == "SKY / " and visual.skyName == p.name:sub(7)
        p.stroke.Color = selected and palette.pink or Color3.fromRGB(
            73,
            84,
            115
        )
        p.stroke.Thickness = selected and 2 or 1
        p.action.Text = selected and (visual.dirtyCount > 0 and "EDITED" or visual.enabled and "LIVE" or "READY") or "APPLY >"
        p.action.TextColor3 = selected and palette.pink or palette.gold
    end
end
table.insert(uiConnections, visual.subscribe(function()
    refreshStudio()
end))
table.insert(uiConnections, esp.subscribe(function()
    refreshStudio()
end))
table.insert(uiConnections, aim.subscribe(function()
    refreshStudio()
end))
local elapsed = 0
on(RunService.Heartbeat, function(delta)
    if not page.Visible or not MainWindow.Visible then
        return 
    end
    elapsed = elapsed + (delta or 0.016)
    if elapsed < 0.15 then
        return 
    end
    elapsed = 0
    refreshStudio()
end)
on(MainGui.Destroying, function()
    visual.close()
    ;
    aim.close()
    ;
    esp.close()
    ;
    cancelDrag()
    ;
    for _, c in ipairs(uiConnections) do
        c:Disconnect()
    end
    ;
    for _, c in ipairs(viewConnections) do
        c:Disconnect()
    end
    ;
    for _, c in ipairs(sideConnections) do
        c:Disconnect()
    end
end)
drawSide()
;
redraw()
;
refreshStudio()
end)()
end

local function MakeAboutBox(title, val, sub, order, clickAction)
    local c = Instance.new(clickAction and "TextButton" or "Frame")
    c.Size = UDim2.new(0.488, 0, 0, 68)
    c.BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("CardBase")
    c.BorderSizePixel = 0
    c.ZIndex = 22
    c.LayoutOrder = order or 1
    if clickAction then
        c.AutoButtonColor = false
        c.Text = ""
    end
    c.Parent = aboutGrid
    RedUI.Corner(c, 14)
    local s = RedUI.Stroke(c, RedTheme.Get("CardStroke"), 1.2)
    RedTheme.Bind(c, "BackgroundColor3", "CardBase")
    RedTheme.Bind(s, "Color", "CardStroke")

    local tl = RedUI.Label(c, string.upper(title), UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 14), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 9, RedTheme.Get("TextMuted"))
    tl.ZIndex = 23
    local vl = RedUI.Label(c, val, UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 20), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 22), Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 13, RedTheme.Get("TextPrimary"))
    vl.ZIndex = 23
    local sl = RedUI.Label(c, "- " .. sub, UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 14), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 46), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 9, RedTheme.Get("TextSecondary"))
    sl.ZIndex = 23

    if clickAction then
        c.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            RedTween.Play(c, 0.15, { BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("CardHover") })
        end)
        c.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            RedTween.Play(c, 0.2, { BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("CardBase") })
        end)
        c.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            RedAudio.Play("Click")
            clickAction(vl, sl)
        end)
    end

    return vl, sl, c
end

local boxAvatarVal, boxAvatarSub = MakeAboutBox("Outfit Value (Estimate)", tostring(RedTelemetry.ExactAvatarValue or 0) .. " R$", "Supplied logic / limited fallback 1000", 1)
local boxAgeVal, boxAgeSub = MakeAboutBox("Account Age", RedTelemetry.AccountAgeText, "Registered: " .. RedTelemetry.AccountCreationDate, 2)
local boxUidVal, boxUidSub = MakeAboutBox("User ID (UID) [Click to Copy]", tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId), "Tap box to copy User ID", 3, function(v, s)
    RedEnv.SetClipboard(tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId))
    s.Text = "- User ID Copied to Clipboard!"
    ShowToast("UID Copied", tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId), "Success")
    task.delay(2, function() s.Text = "- Tap box to copy User ID" end)
end)
local boxMemVal, boxMemSub = MakeAboutBox("Membership Tier", (LocalPlayer.MembershipType == Enum.MembershipType.Premium and "Roblox Premium VIP" or "Standard Member"), "Membership is not age verification", 4)
local boxFriVal, boxFriSub = MakeAboutBox("Mutual Friends Online", tostring(RedTelemetry.TotalFriendsCount) .. " Friends", "Realtime Social Connections", 5)
local boxCoordVal, boxCoordSub = MakeAboutBox("Live World Coordinates", "X: 0 | Y: 0 | Z: 0", "Physics Position Tracker", 6)
local boxVelVal, boxVelSub = MakeAboutBox("Linear Velocity", "0, 0, 0", "Assembly Movement Vector", 7)
local boxSpeedVal, boxSpeedSub = MakeAboutBox("Movement Speed", "0 studs/sec", "Magnitude Velocity", 8)
local boxPlaceVal, boxPlaceSub = MakeAboutBox("Current Place ID", tostring(game.PlaceId), "Job: " .. string.sub(tostring(game.JobId), 1, 14) .. "...", 9)
local boxExecVal, boxExecSub = MakeAboutBox("Executor Engine", RedEnv.ExecutorName, "Luau Death Note v3.2.0", 10)

task.spawn(function()
    while AboutModal and AboutModal.Parent do
        pcall(function()
            local pos = RedTelemetry.CharPosition
            local vel = RedTelemetry.CharVelocity
            local speed = math.floor(vel.Magnitude)

            boxCoordVal.Text = string.format("X: %.1f | Y: %.1f | Z: %.1f", pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z)
            boxVelVal.Text = string.format("X: %.1f | Y: %.1f | Z: %.1f", vel.X, vel.Y, vel.Z)
            boxSpeedVal.Text = string.format("%d studs/sec", speed)

            local valRbx = RedTelemetry.ExactAvatarValue or RedTelemetry.EstimatedAvatarValue or 0
            boxAvatarVal.Text = tostring(valRbx) .. " R$ (estimate)"
            boxAvatarSub.Text = "- " .. (RedTelemetry.AvatarValuationStatus or "Supplied outfit-value logic")

            boxFriVal.Text = tostring(RedTelemetry.TotalFriendsCount) .. " Friends Online"
            boxAgeVal.Text = RedTelemetry.AccountAgeText
            boxAgeSub.Text = "- Joined (UTC): " .. RedTelemetry.AccountCreationDate
        end)
        task.wait(0.25)
    end
end)

-- 3. Telemetry Grid (Performance, Latency, Environment)
local TelemetryGrid = Instance.new("Frame")
TelemetryGrid.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 64)
TelemetryGrid.BackgroundTransparency = 1
TelemetryGrid.Parent = PageDashboard

local tgLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
tgLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
tgLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
tgLayout.Parent = TelemetryGrid

local function MakeMetric(title, val, sub, col)
    local c = Instance.new("Frame")
    c.Size = UDim2.new(0.32, 0, 1, 0)
    c.BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("CardBase")
    c.BorderSizePixel = 0
    c.Parent = TelemetryGrid
    RedUI.Corner(c, 14)
    local s = RedUI.Stroke(c, RedTheme.Get("CardStroke"), 1.2)
    RedTheme.Bind(c, "BackgroundColor3", "CardBase")
    RedTheme.Bind(s, "Color", "CardStroke")

    RedUI.Label(c, string.upper(title), UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 14), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 9, RedTheme.Get("TextMuted"))
    local vl = RedUI.Label(c, val, UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 20), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 22), Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 16, col or RedTheme.Get("TextPrimary"))
    local sl = RedUI.Label(c, "- " .. sub, UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 14), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 44), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 9, RedTheme.Get("TextSecondary"))
    return vl, sl
end

local fpsVal, fpsSub = MakeMetric("Performance", "60 FPS", "144Hz+ Smooth", RedTheme.Get("TextPrimary"))
local pingVal, pingSub = MakeMetric("Latency", "50ms", "Direct Gateway", Color3.fromRGB(255, 183, 3))
local envVal, envSub = MakeMetric("Environment", RedEnv.ExecutorName, "Active Engine", RedTheme.Get("Accent"))

task.spawn(function()
    while TelemetryGrid and TelemetryGrid.Parent do
        local fps = RedTelemetry.CurrentFPS
        fpsVal.Text = fps .. " FPS"
        fpsSub.Text = fps >= 55 and "- 144Hz+ Smooth" or (fps >= 30 and "- Moderate" or "- Throttled")

        local ping = RedTelemetry.CurrentPing
        pingVal.Text = ping .. "ms"
        pingSub.Text = ping < 100 and "- Direct Gateway" or (ping < 200 and "- Normal" or "- High Latency")
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)
