local themeItems = {}
local function AddThemeItem(name, p)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Text = ""
    b.BackgroundColor3 = p.CardBase
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Parent = themeGrid
    RedUI.Corner(b, 12)
    local st = RedUI.Stroke(b, RedTheme.CurrentThemeName == name and p.Accent or p.CardStroke, 1.2)

    local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
    dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
    dot.BackgroundColor3 = p.Accent
    dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    dot.Parent = b
    RedUI.Corner(dot, 8)

    RedUI.Label(b, name, UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 16), UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 10), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 9, p.TextPrimary)
    local stat = RedUI.Label(b, RedTheme.CurrentThemeName == name and "ACTIVE" or "SELECT", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 38), Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 8, RedTheme.CurrentThemeName == name and p.Accent or p.TextMuted)

    themeItems[name] = { Stroke = st, Stat = stat, Palette = p }

    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if RedTheme.CurrentThemeName == name then return end
        RedTheme.SetTheme(name)
        for n, it in pairs(themeItems) do
            it.Stroke.Color = (n == name and it.Palette.Accent or it.Palette.CardStroke)
            it.Stat.Text = (n == name and "ACTIVE" or "SELECT")
            it.Stat.TextColor3 = (n == name and it.Palette.Accent or it.Palette.TextMuted)
        end
        ShowToast("Theme Changed", name, "Success")
    end)
end

actScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1

local function getPlayerColor(userId)
    local colors = {
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 87, 87),
        Color3.fromRGB(78, 205, 196),
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 11),
        Color3.fromRGB(168, 85, 247),
        Color3.fromRGB(56, 189, 248),
        Color3.fromRGB(244, 114, 182),
        Color3.fromRGB(52, 211, 153),
        Color3.fromRGB(251, 146, 60)
    }
    local num = tonumber(userId) or 1
    local idx = (num % #colors) + 1
    return colors[idx]
end

local function AddLog(tag, playerName, msg, userId)
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
    f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 12, 18)
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    f.Parent = actScroll
    RedUI.Corner(f, 6)

    local pColor = getPlayerColor(userId or 1)

    RedUI.Label(f, "[" .. os.date("%X") .. "]", UDim2.new(0, 68, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0), Enum.Font.Code, 9, RedTheme.Get("TextMuted"))
    RedUI.Label(f, tag, UDim2.new(0, 54, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 0), Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 9, pColor)
    RedUI.Label(f, playerName or "System", UDim2.new(0, 120, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 134, 0, 0), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 9, pColor)
    RedUI.Label(f, msg or "", UDim2.new(1, -260, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 258, 0, 0), Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 9, RedTheme.Get("TextPrimary"))
end

local friendsCard = RedUI.Card(PageFriends, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 240))
local friendsStatus = RedUI.Label(friendsCard, "LOADING FRIENDS...", UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 18), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 10, RedTheme.Get("TextSecondary"))
local friendsScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
friendsScroll.Name = "FriendCarousel"
friendsScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -37)
friendsScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 30)
friendsScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
friendsScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
friendsScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
friendsScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
friendsScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 20, 65)
friendsScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
friendsScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
friendsScroll.Parent = friendsCard
local friendLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
friendLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
friendLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
friendLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 10)
friendLayout.Parent = friendsScroll

local universeCache, nameCache = {}, {}
local function positiveNumber(value)
    local n = tonumber(value)
    return n and n > 0 and n or nil
end
local function friendPlace(f)
    local id = positiveNumber(f.PlaceId or f.placeId)
    local location = f.LastLocation or f.lastLocation
    if not id and type(location) == "string" then
        id = positiveNumber(location:match("/games/(%d+)"))
    end
    return id
end
local function friendUniverse(placeId)
    if not placeId then return nil end
    if placeId == game.PlaceId then return game.GameId end
    if universeCache[placeId] then return universeCache[placeId] end
    local ok, data = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://apis.roblox.com/universes/v1/places/" .. placeId .. "/universe"))
    end)
    local id = ok and type(data) == "table" and positiveNumber(data.universeId) or nil
    if id then universeCache[placeId] = id end
    return id
end
local function friendGameName(f, placeId, universeId)
    if placeId and nameCache[placeId] then return nameCache[placeId] end
    local name = f.GameName or f.PlaceName or f.Location or f.LastLocation or f.lastLocation
    if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" or name == "Website" or name == "Online" or name == "Offline" or name == "In Game" or name:find("://") then name = nil end
    if not name and universeId then
        local ok, data = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games?universeIds=" .. universeId))
        end)
        if ok and type(data) == "table" and data.data and data.data[1] then name = data.data[1].name end
    end
    if not name and placeId then
        local ok, info = pcall(function() return MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(placeId) end)
        if ok and info then name = info.Name end
    end
    if name and placeId then nameCache[placeId] = name end
    return name or (placeId and ("Place " .. placeId) or "Online")
end
local function cardLabel(parent, text, size, pos, font, textSize, color)
    local l = RedUI.Label(parent, text, size, pos, font, textSize, color)
    l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    return l
end
local function drawFriend(f, order)
    local userId = positiveNumber(f.VisitorId or f.UserId or f.Id)
    local username = tostring(f.UserName or f.userName or f.Name or "Unknown")
    local displayName = tostring(f.DisplayName or f.displayName or username)
    local placeId = friendPlace(f)
    -- LocationType 1/4 can indicate a game even when its place ID is hidden.
    -- Unknown location without a place is treated as online, not confirmed in-game.
    local locationType = tonumber(f.LocationType or f.locationType)
    local inGame = placeId ~= nil or locationType == 1 or locationType == 4
    local card = Instance.new("Frame")
    card.Name = "FriendCard"
    card.Size = UDim2.new(0, 265, 1, -5)
    card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 23, 29)
    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    card.LayoutOrder = order
    card.ClipsDescendants = true
    card.Parent = friendsScroll
    RedUI.Corner(card, 12)
    RedUI.Stroke(card, Color3.fromRGB(58, 49, 57), 1)
    local thumb = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    thumb.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    thumb.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(17, 15, 20)
    thumb.BorderSizePixel = 0
    thumb.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    thumb.Parent = card
    -- The monogram remains visible until an actual thumbnail reports loaded.
    -- If the thumbnail fails or the place is hidden, it remains as a fallback.
    local fallback = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    fallback.Name = "ExperienceFallbackR"
    fallback.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -35)
    fallback.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    fallback.Text = "R"
    fallback.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    fallback.TextSize = 105
    fallback.TextColor3 = inGame and Color3.fromRGB(242, 35, 67) or Color3.fromRGB(54, 151, 255)
    fallback.TextStrokeColor3 = inGame and Color3.fromRGB(98, 8, 25) or Color3.fromRGB(12, 55, 116)
    fallback.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.55
    fallback.Parent = thumb
    local function syncFallback()
        if fallback.Parent and thumb.Parent then
            fallback.Visible = not thumb.IsLoaded
        end
    end
    thumb:GetPropertyChangedSignal("IsLoaded"):Connect(syncFallback)
    local shade = Instance.new("Frame")
    shade.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 82)
    shade.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -82)
    shade.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    shade.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shade.Parent = card
    local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    gradient.Rotation = 90
    gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.18), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.05)})
    gradient.Parent = shade
    local avatar = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    avatar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 42)
    avatar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -50)
    avatar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20)
    avatar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    avatar.Parent = card
    RedUI.Corner(avatar, 21)
    RedUI.Stroke(avatar, Color3.fromRGB(236, 24, 70), 2)
    if userId then avatar.Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. userId .. "&w=150&h=150" end
    -- Full-width game title: it remains visible even when the image fails.
    local title = cardLabel(card, inGame and (placeId and "Loading game..." or "Game unavailable") or "ONLINE", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20), UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -77), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 13, Color3.new(1,1,1))
    local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
    dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7)
    dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 60, 1, -23)
    dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(67, 224, 128)
    dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    dot.Parent = card
    RedUI.Corner(dot, 5)
    cardLabel(card, displayName, UDim2.new(1, -155, 0, 16), UDim2.new(0, 71, 1, -28), Enum.Font.Gotham, 10, Color3.fromRGB(207,207,211))
    local join = Instance.new("TextButton")
    join.Size = UDim2.new(0, 72, 0, 32)
    join.Position = UDim2.new(1, -81, 1, -46)
    join.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(7, 7, 9)
    join.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
    join.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    join.TextSize = 11
    join.Text = "..."
    join.BorderSizePixel = 0
    join.AutoButtonColor = false
    join.Parent = card
    RedUI.Corner(join, 8)
    RedUI.Stroke(join, Color3.fromRGB(230, 22, 65), 1)
    local same = placeId == game.PlaceId
    local resolved = false
    join.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if not resolved or not same or not placeId then return end
        -- GameId in GetFriendsOnline is a server instance UUID, NOT a universe ID.
        local jobId = f.GameId or f.gameId
        if type(jobId) ~= "string" or jobId == "" then
            ShowToast("Cannot join", "Friend's server ID is not available.", "Info")
            return
        end
        if RedTelemetry.SessionGuard then
            local allowed, reason = RedTelemetry.SessionGuard.canTeleport()
            if not allowed then ShowToast("Join stopped", reason, "Info"); return end
        end
        join.Text = "..."
        local ok, err = pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, jobId, LocalPlayer) end)
        if not ok then
            join.Text = "JOIN"
            ShowToast("Join failed", tostring(err), "Info")
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        local presenceName = f.GameName or f.PlaceName or f.Location or f.LastLocation or f.lastLocation
        if inGame and type(presenceName) == "string" and presenceName ~= "" and presenceName ~= "In Game" and presenceName ~= "Online" and presenceName ~= "Offline" and presenceName ~= "Website" and not presenceName:find("://") then
            title.Text = presenceName
        end
        local universeId = friendUniverse(placeId)
        local gameName = friendGameName(f, placeId, universeId)
        if not card.Parent then return end
        same = placeId ~= nil and (placeId == game.PlaceId or (universeId ~= nil and universeId == game.GameId))
        title.Text = inGame and (placeId and gameName or (gameName ~= "Online" and gameName or "Game unavailable")) or "ONLINE"
        if universeId then
            thumb.Image = "rbxthumb://type=GameIcon&id=" .. universeId .. "&w=150&h=150"
        elseif placeId then
            thumb.Image = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. placeId .. "&w=420&h=420"
        end
        syncFallback()
        -- Preloading is asynchronous: do not block the title or join button.
        -- The fallback must disappear when the actual experience image loads.
        if thumb.Image ~= "" then
            task.spawn(function()
                local imageId = thumb.Image
                pcall(function()
                    game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync({thumb}, function(_, status)
                        if thumb.Parent and fallback.Parent and thumb.Image == imageId and status == Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success then
                            fallback.Visible = false
                        end
                    end)
                end)
                if thumb.Parent and fallback.Parent and thumb.Image == imageId and thumb.IsLoaded then
                    fallback.Visible = false
                end
            end)
        end
        local job = f.GameId or f.gameId
        resolved = true
        if same and type(job) == "string" and job ~= "" then
            join.Text = "JOIN"
        else
            join.Text = same and "NO SERVER" or (inGame and "IN GAME" or "ONLINE")
            join.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 155)
        end
    end)
end
local function RenderFriends()
    local oldX = friendsScroll.CanvasPosition.X
    for _, child in ipairs(friendsScroll:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") then child:Destroy() end
    end
    local list = {}
    for _, f in ipairs(RedTelemetry.FriendsPresences) do table.insert(list, f) end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b)
        local ap, bp = friendPlace(a), friendPlace(b)
        local as = ap == game.PlaceId or (ap and universeCache[ap] == game.GameId)
        local bs = bp == game.PlaceId or (bp and universeCache[bp] == game.GameId)
        if not not as ~= not not bs then return not not as end
        return tostring(a.UserName or a.Name or ""):lower() < tostring(b.UserName or b.Name or ""):lower()
    end)
    if RedTelemetry.FriendsError then
        friendsStatus.Text = "FRIENDS: FETCH FAILED - RETRYING IN 10S"
    else
        friendsStatus.Text = #list .. " FRIENDS ONLINE  |  SWIPE HORIZONTALLY  |  AUTO-REFRESH 10S"
    end
    if #list == 0 then
        local empty = Instance.new("Frame")
        empty.Name = "EmptyState"
        empty.Size = UDim2.new(0, 265, 1, -5)
        empty.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 21, 26)
        empty.BorderSizePixel = 0
        empty.Parent = friendsScroll
        RedUI.Corner(empty, 12)
        cardLabel(empty, RedTelemetry.FriendsError and "Unable to load friends" or "No friends online", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -14), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 12, Color3.fromRGB(190,190,195))
    else
        for i, f in ipairs(list) do drawFriend(f, i) end
    end
    task.defer(function()
        if friendsScroll.Parent then friendsScroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(oldX, 0) end
    end)
end
task.spawn(function()
    while friendsCard and friendsCard.Parent do
        RenderFriends()
        task.wait(10)
    end
end)

-- V24 playback fix: native Animate is NEVER disabled, reparented, or rewritten.
-- Only tracks created by this controller may be stopped/destroyed. Native locomotion,
-- tools and emotes remain owned by their original scripts. Asset permissions still apply.
RedTelemetry.AnimationPacks = (function()
    local data = AnimationPackData
    local api = {
        closed = false,
        selected = nil,
        current = nil,
        partialMatch = nil,
        revision = 0,
        state = "INITIALIZING",
        rig = "WAITING",
        configured = 0,
        loaded = 0,
        slots = 0,
        coverage = 0,
        playing = "NO LIVE LOCOMOTION TRACK",
        warning = nil,
        lastError = nil,
        driverState = "NONE",
        recovery = false,
    }
    api.catalog = data
    local packs = {
    }
    ;
    for _, pack in ipairs(data.packs) do
        packs[pack.name] = pack
    end
    local connections, listeners = {
    }, {
    }
    local ctx, removing
    local clock = 0
    local elapsed = 0
    local nextListener = 0
    local lastSignature = ""
    local busy = false
    local loops = {
        idle = true,
        walk = true,
        run = true,
        jump = false,
        fall = true,
        climb = true,
        swim = true,
        swimidle = true,
    }
    local blockedStates = {
    }
    for _, name in ipairs({
        "Dead",
        "Seated",
        "Physics",
        "PlatformStanding",
        "Ragdoll",
        "FallingDown",
    }) do
        blockedStates[Enum.HumanoidStateType[name]] = true
    end
    local function read(
        object,
        key
    )
        local ok, value = pcall(function()
            return object[key]
        end)
        if ok then
            return value, true
        end
        return nil, false
    end
    local function normalize(value)
        if type(value) ~= "string" then
            return nil
        end
        local id = value:match("^%s*(%d+)%s*$") or value:match("^rbxassetid://(%d+)$") or value:match("[?&]id=(%d+)")
        if not id then
            return nil
        end
        id = id:gsub(
            "^0+",
            ""
        )
        ;
        return id ~= "" and id or nil
    end
    api.normalize = normalize
    local function connect(
        signal,
        fn,
        bucket
    )
        local connection = signal:Connect(fn)
        ;
        table.insert(
            bucket or connections,
            connection
        )
        ;
        return connection
    end
    local function publish(force)
        local signature = table.concat({
            api.selected or "",
            api.current or "",
            api.partialMatch or "",
            api.state,
            api.rig,
            api.playing,
            api.warning or "",
            api.lastError or "",
            api.driverState,
            tostring(api.loaded),
            tostring(api.recovery),
            api.readbackSignature or "",
        }, "|")
        if not force and signature == lastSignature then
            return 
        end
        lastSignature = signature
        ;
        api.revision = api.revision + 1
        local batch = {
        }
        ;
        for id, fn in pairs(listeners) do
            batch[#batch + 1] = {
                id = id,
                fn = fn,
            }
        end
        for _, entry in ipairs(batch) do
            if listeners[entry.id] == entry.fn then
                pcall(entry.fn)
            end
        end
    end
    function api.subscribe(fn)
        if api.closed or type(fn) ~= "function" then
            return {
                Disconnect = function()

                end,
            }
        end
        nextListener = nextListener + 1
        ;
        local id = nextListener
        ;
        listeners[id] = fn
        local subscription = {
            Connected = true,
        }
        function subscription:Disconnect()
            self.Connected = false
            ;
            listeners[id] = nil
        end
        return subscription
    end
    local function drop(object)
        if object then
            pcall(function()
                object:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end
    local function stopOwned(
        entry,
        fade
    )
        if entry and entry.track and entry.started then
            pcall(function()
                entry.track:Stop(fade or 0.12)
            end)
            entry.started = false
        end
    end
    local function dispose(entry)
        if not entry then
            return 
        end
        stopOwned(
            entry,
            0.08
        )
        drop(entry.track)
        ;
        drop(entry.animation)
    end
    local function clearTracks(context)
        if not context then
            return 
        end
        for _, entry in pairs(context.cache) do
            dispose(entry)
        end
        context.cache = {
        }
        ;
        context.active = nil
        ;
        context.failed = {
        }
        ;
        context.jumpFinished = false
        context.nativeMissingSince = nil
        ;
        context.lastWanted = nil
        ;
        context.recoverySession = false
    end
    local function release()
        if not ctx then
            return 
        end
        clearTracks(ctx)
        for _, connection in ipairs(ctx.connections) do
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        ctx = nil
    end
    local function obtain()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if character == removing then
            character = nil
        end
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local animate = character and character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        api.rig = humanoid and (humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 and "R15" or "R6 / UNSUPPORTED") or "WAITING FOR CHARACTER"
        if ctx and (ctx.character ~= character or ctx.humanoid ~= humanoid or ctx.animator ~= animator or ctx.animate ~= animate or api.rig ~= "R15") then
            release()
        end
        if api.rig ~= "R15" or not humanoid or not animate then
            return nil
        end
        if not ctx then
            ctx = {
                character = character,
                humanoid = humanoid,
                animate = animate,
                animator = animator,
                connections = {
                },
                cache = {
                },
                failed = {
                },
                entries = {
                },
                original = {
                },
                lastSpeed = 0,
                jumpAt = -100,
                lastHumState = nil,
                lastWanted = nil,
                jumpFinished = false,
            }
            local context = ctx
            connect(humanoid.Running, function(speed)
                if ctx == context then
                    context.lastSpeed = tonumber(speed) or 0
                end
            end, context.connections)
            connect(humanoid.StateChanged, function(_, state)
                if ctx ~= context then
                    return 
                end
                if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
                    context.jumpAt = clock
                    ;
                    context.jumpFinished = false
                end
                context.lastHumState = state
            end, context.connections)
        end
        return ctx
    end
    local function inspect(context)
        api.slots = 0
        ;
        api.coverage = 0
        ;
        api.partialMatch = nil
        ;
        api.current = nil
        api.readbackSignature = ""
        if not context then
            return 
        end
        local entries, seen, states, ids, signature = {
        }, {
        }, {
        }, {
        }, {
        }
        for _, route in ipairs(data.routes) do
            local folder = context.animate:FindFirstChild(route.category)
            local animation = folder and folder:FindFirstChild(route.child)
            if animation and animation:IsA("Animation") and not seen[animation] then
                local raw, ok = read(
                    animation,
                    "AnimationId"
                )
                if ok and type(raw) == "string" then
                    seen[animation] = true
                    ;
                    states[route.state] = true
                    entries[#entries + 1] = {
                        animation = animation,
                        state = route.state,
                        id = raw,
                    }
                    signature[#signature + 1] = route.state .. ":" .. raw
                    local id = normalize(raw)
                    if id and not ids[route.state] then
                        ids[route.state] = id
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        context.entries = entries
        ;
        context.original = ids
        api.slots = #entries
        ;
        for _ in pairs(states) do
            api.coverage = api.coverage + 1
        end
        api.readbackSignature = table.concat(
            signature,
            "|"
        )
        for _, pack in ipairs(data.packs) do
            local matches = #entries > 0
            for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
                if normalize(entry.id) ~= pack.ids[entry.state] then
                    matches = false
                    ;
                    break
                end
            end
            if matches then
                if api.coverage == 8 then
                    api.current = pack.name
                else
                    api.partialMatch = pack.name
                end
                break
            end
        end
    end
    local function nativeEnabled(context)
        local enabled = read(
            context.animate,
            "Enabled"
        )
        if type(enabled) == "boolean" then
            return enabled
        end
        local disabled = read(
            context.animate,
            "Disabled"
        )
        return type(disabled) == "boolean" and not disabled
    end
    local function stateOf(context)
        local humanoid = context.humanoid
        if humanoid.Health <= 0 or humanoid.PlatformStand or humanoid.Sit then
            return nil, 0
        end
        local ok, state = pcall(function()
            return humanoid:GetState()
        end)
        if not ok or blockedStates[state] then
            return nil, 0
        end
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
            if context.lastHumState ~= state then
                context.jumpAt = clock
                ;
                context.jumpFinished = false
            end
            context.lastHumState = state
            return "jump", 1
        end
        context.lastHumState = state
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
            if clock - context.jumpAt < 0.22 and not context.jumpFinished then
                return "jump", 1
            end
            return "fall", 1
        end
        local movement = humanoid.MoveDirection
        local moving = movement and movement.Magnitude > 0.03
        local root = context.character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local velocity = root and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local speed = velocity and math.sqrt(velocity.X * velocity.X + velocity.Z * velocity.Z) or 0
        if moving then
            speed = math.max(
                speed,
                context.lastSpeed,
                1
            )
        else
            speed = 0
        end
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing then
            local vertical = velocity and math.abs(velocity.Y) or 0
            return "climb", math.clamp(
                vertical / 5,
                0,
                2.5
            )
        end
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
            return moving and "swim" or "swimidle", moving and math.clamp(speed / 10, 0.25, 2.5) or 1
        end
        if moving then
            return speed > 16 and "run" or "walk", math.clamp(
                speed / (speed > 16 and 20 or 14),
                0.15,
                2.5
            )
        end
        return "idle", 1
    end
    local function nativeTracks(
        context,
        state
    )
        if not context.animator then
            return false, false
        end
        local ok, tracks = pcall(function()
            return context.animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
        end)
        if not ok or type(tracks) ~= "table" then
            return false, false
        end
        local nativePlaying = false
        local actionPlaying = false
        for _, track in ipairs(tracks) do
            local ours = false
            for _, entry in pairs(context.cache) do
                if entry.track == track then
                    ours = true
                    ;
                    break
                end
            end
            if not ours then
                pcall(function()
                    if not track.IsPlaying then
                        return 
                    end
                    local priority = track.Priority
                    if priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Action or priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2 or priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3 or priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4 then
                        actionPlaying = true
                    end
                    local animation = track.Animation
                    local id = animation and normalize(animation.AnimationId)
                    local locomotion = priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Core or priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle or priority == Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement
                    if not locomotion or not id then
                        return 
                    end
                    for _, entry in ipairs(context.entries) do
                        if normalize(entry.id) == id and (entry.state == state or (state == "run" and entry.state == "walk") or (state == "walk" and entry.state == "run")) then
                            nativePlaying = true
                            api.playing = "NATIVE " .. data.stateLabels[entry.state] .. " / " .. id
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        return nativePlaying, actionPlaying
    end
    local function getTrack(
        context,
        mode,
        state,
        id
    )
        local key = mode .. ":" .. state .. ":" .. id
        if context.failed[key] then
            return nil, context.failed[key]
        end
        local entry = context.cache[key]
        if entry then
            return entry
        end
        local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
        animation.Name = "DEATH_NOTE_OWNED_" .. state
        animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
        local ok, track = pcall(function()
            return context.animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
        end)
        if not ok or not track then
            drop(animation)
            context.failed[key] = "Animation load rejected / " .. state .. " / " .. id
            return nil, context.failed[key]
        end
        entry = {
            animation = animation,
            track = track,
            key = key,
            state = state,
            id = id,
            mode = mode,
            created = clock,
            started = false,
            done = false,
            lastPosition = nil,
            stalled = 0,
            restarts = 0,
            loaded = false,
        }
        local configured = pcall(function()
            track.Looped = loops[state] == true
            track.Priority = state == "idle" and Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle or Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement
        end)
        if not configured then
            dispose(entry)
            ;
            context.failed[key] = "Animation track configuration blocked / " .. state
            return nil, context.failed[key]
        end
        context.cache[key] = entry
        return entry
    end
    local function fadeActive(
        context,
        except
    )
        if context.active and context.active ~= except then
            stopOwned(
                context.active,
                0.12
            )
        end
        context.active = except
    end
    local function markFailed(
        context,
        entry,
        reason
    )
        context.failed[entry.key] = reason
        if context.active == entry then
            context.active = nil
        end
        context.cache[entry.key] = nil
        dispose(entry)
        return false, reason
    end
    local function play(
        context,
        mode,
        state,
        id,
        speed,
        dt
    )
        local entry, reason = getTrack(
            context,
            mode,
            state,
            id
        )
        if not entry then
            if context.active and context.active.mode == mode then
                fadeActive(
                    context,
                    nil
                )
            end
            ;
            return false, reason
        end
        local length = read(
            entry.track,
            "Length"
        )
        if type(length) ~= "number" or length <= 0 then
            if context.active and context.active.mode == mode then
                fadeActive(
                    context,
                    nil
                )
            end
            if clock - entry.created > 2 then
                return markFailed(
                    context,
                    entry,
                    "Asset did not load / " .. state .. " / native fallback"
                )
            end
            return false, "WAITING FOR ASSET / " .. state
        end
        entry.loaded = true
        if state == "jump" and context.jumpFinished then
            fadeActive(
                context,
                nil
            )
            ;
            return false, "JUMP FINISHED / WAITING FOR FALL OR LANDING"
        end
        if context.active ~= entry then
            fadeActive(
                context,
                entry
            )
            entry.done = false
            ;
            entry.stalled = 0
            ;
            entry.lastPosition = nil
        end
        local isPlaying = read(entry.track, "IsPlaying") == true
        if entry.started and not isPlaying then
            if state == "jump" then
                context.jumpFinished = true
                ;
                fadeActive(
                    context,
                    nil
                )
                ;
                return false, "JUMP FINISHED / NATIVE HANDOFF"
            end
            if clock - (entry.lastRestart or -100) < 0.75 then
                return markFailed(
                    context,
                    entry,
                    "Playback stopped repeatedly / " .. state .. " / native fallback"
                )
            end
            entry.started = false
            ;
            entry.restarts = entry.restarts + 1
            ;
            entry.lastRestart = clock
            if entry.restarts > 1 then
                return markFailed(
                    context,
                    entry,
                    "Playback could not stay active / " .. state
                )
            end
        end
        if not entry.started then
            local ok = pcall(function()
                entry.track:Play(
                    0.12,
                    1,
                    speed
                )
            end)
            if not ok then
                return markFailed(
                    context,
                    entry,
                    "Play rejected / " .. state .. " / native fallback"
                )
            end
            entry.started = true
            ;
            entry.stalled = 0
            ;
            entry.lastPosition = nil
        end
        pcall(function()
            entry.track:AdjustSpeed(speed)
        end)
        if read(entry.track, "IsPlaying") ~= true then
            return false, "WAITING FOR PLAYBACK / " .. state
        end
        local position = read(
            entry.track,
            "TimePosition"
        )
        if type(position) == "number" and speed > 0.01 and loops[state] then
            if entry.lastPosition and math.abs(position - entry.lastPosition) < 0.0001 then
                entry.stalled = entry.stalled + dt
            else
                entry.stalled = 0
            end
            entry.lastPosition = position
            if entry.stalled > 1.25 then
                return markFailed(
                    context,
                    entry,
                    "Owned track stalled / " .. state .. " / native fallback"
                )
            end
        end
        api.current = mode == "pack" and api.selected or api.current
        api.recovery = mode == "original"
        api.playing = (mode == "original" and "RECOVERY " or "PACK ") .. data.stateLabels[state] .. " / " .. id
        api.driverState = state:upper()
        return true
    end
    local function drive(
        context,
        dt
    )
        api.playing = "NO LIVE LOCOMOTION TRACK"
        ;
        api.driverState = "NONE"
        ;
        api.recovery = false
        api.loaded = 0
        ;
        api.configured = 0
        if not context then
            api.state = api.rig == "R6 / UNSUPPORTED" and "R15 REQUIRED / R6 LEFT UNCHANGED" or "WAITING FOR ANIMATE"
            ;
            return 
        end
        if not context.animator then
            api.state = "WAITING FOR EXISTING ANIMATOR / NATIVE UNCHANGED"
            ;
            return 
        end
        local state, speed = stateOf(context)
        if not state then
            fadeActive(
                context,
                nil
            )
            ;
            context.nativeMissingSince = nil
            ;
            api.state = "PAUSED / HUMANOID STATE"
            ;
            return 
        end
        if context.lastWanted ~= state then
            if state == "jump" then
                context.jumpFinished = false
            end
            context.lastWanted = state
            context.nativeMissingSince = context.recoverySession and (clock - 1) or nil
            if context.active and context.active.mode == "original" and context.active.state ~= state then
                fadeActive(
                    context,
                    nil
                )
            end
        end
        local nativePlaying, actionPlaying = nativeTracks(
            context,
            state
        )
        local played = false
        local reason
        if api.selected then
            played, reason = play(
                context,
                "pack",
                state,
                packs[api.selected].ids[state],
                speed,
                dt
            )
            if not played then
                api.warning = reason
            end
        elseif context.active and context.active.mode == "pack" then
            fadeActive(
                context,
                nil
            )
        end
        if not played then
            -- Give an already-running native track, action, or intentionally disabled script
            -- ownership. Recovery starts only after a real gap, without toggling Animate.
            if nativePlaying or actionPlaying or not nativeEnabled(context) then
                context.nativeMissingSince = nil
                ;
                context.recoverySession = false
                if context.active and context.active.mode == "original" then
                    fadeActive(
                        context,
                        nil
                    )
                end
            else
                context.nativeMissingSince = context.nativeMissingSince or clock
                local id = context.original[state]
                if clock - context.nativeMissingSince >= 0.65 and id then
                    local recovered, recoveryWhy = play(
                        context,
                        "original",
                        state,
                        id,
                        speed,
                        dt
                    )
                    if recovered then
                        played = true
                        ;
                        context.recoverySession = true
                    elseif recoveryWhy then
                        api.warning = recoveryWhy
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            context.nativeMissingSince = nil
            ;
            context.recoverySession = false
            ;
            api.warning = nil
        end
        for _, entry in pairs(context.cache) do
            if entry.mode == "pack" and entry.loaded then
                api.loaded = api.loaded + 1
            end
        end
        api.configured = api.loaded
        if api.recovery then
            api.state = "ORIGINAL RECOVERY / NATIVE SCRIPT UNCHANGED"
        elseif played then
            api.state = "PACK PLAYBACK / " .. api.driverState
        elseif api.selected then
            api.state = "PACK NOT PLAYING / NATIVE FALLBACK"
        elseif nativePlaying then
            api.state = "ORIGINAL ANIMATE / RUNNING"
        elseif not nativeEnabled(context) then
            api.state = "ORIGINAL ANIMATE DISABLED BY GAME / LEFT UNCHANGED"
        else
            api.state = "ORIGINAL MODE / WATCHING FOR RECOVERY"
        end
    end
    local function pulse(
        dt,
        force
    )
        if api.closed or busy then
            return 
        end
        busy = true
        local ok, reason = pcall(function()
            local context = obtain()
            ;
            inspect(context)
            ;
            drive(
                context,
                dt or 0
            )
        end)
        busy = false
        if not ok then
            api.lastError = tostring(
                reason):sub(1,
                180
            )
            ;
            api.state = "PLAYBACK ERROR / NATIVE SCRIPT UNCHANGED"
            if ctx then
                fadeActive(
                    ctx,
                    nil
                )
            end
        end
        publish(force)
    end
    function api.refresh()
        if api.closed then
            return false
        end
        ;
        pulse(
            0,
            true
        )
        ;
        return true
    end
    function api.select(name)
        if api.closed then
            return false, "Animation workspace closed"
        end
        if not packs[name] then
            return false, "Unknown animation pack"
        end
        if api.selected == name then
            return api.reset()
        end
        obtain()
        if api.rig == "R6 / UNSUPPORTED" then
            pulse(
                0,
                true
            )
            ;
            return false, "R15 character required; R6 was not changed"
        end
        if ctx then
            clearTracks(ctx)
        end
        api.selected = name
        ;
        api.warning = nil
        ;
        api.lastError = nil
        pulse(
            0,
            true
        )
        ;
        return true
    end
    function api.reapply()
        if api.closed then
            return false, "Animation workspace closed"
        end
        if ctx then
            clearTracks(ctx)
        end
        api.warning = nil
        ;
        api.lastError = nil
        pulse(
            0,
            true
        )
        return true
    end
    function api.reset()
        if api.closed then
            return false, "Animation workspace closed"
        end
        api.selected = nil
        ;
        api.warning = nil
        ;
        api.lastError = nil
        if ctx then
            clearTracks(ctx)
        end
        pulse(
            0,
            true
        )
        ;
        return true
    end
    function api.slotReport()
        local result = {
        }
        if ctx then
            for _, entry in ipairs(ctx.entries) do
                result[#result + 1] = {
                    state = entry.state,
                    name = entry.animation.Name,
                    id = entry.id,
                    owned = false,
                }
            end
        end
        return result
    end
    function api.coverageReport()
        local result = {
        }
        for _, state in ipairs(data.states) do
            local item = {
                state = state,
                label = data.stateLabels[state],
                slots = 0,
                matching = 0,
                owned = 0,
                ids = {
                },
                complete = false,
            }
            if ctx then
                for _, entry in ipairs(ctx.entries) do
                    if entry.state == state then
                        item.slots = item.slots + 1
                        ;
                        item.ids[#item.ids + 1] = normalize(entry.id) or entry.id
                    end
                end
            end
            item.available = item.slots > 0
            ;
            result[#result + 1] = item
        end
        return result
    end
    function api.close()
        if api.closed then
            return 
        end
        api.closed = true
        ;
        api.selected = nil
        release()
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
            connection:Disconnect()
        end
        connections = {
        }
        ;
        listeners = {
        }
        ;
        api.current = nil
        ;
        api.recovery = false
        ;
        api.state = "CLOSED"
    end
    connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving, function(character)
        removing = character or LocalPlayer.Character
        ;
        release()
        ;
        api.current = nil
        ;
        api.state = "WAITING FOR RESPAWN"
        ;
        publish(true)
    end)
    connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function()
        removing = nil
        ;
        pulse(
            0,
            true
        )
    end)
    connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
        local delta = math.clamp(
            tonumber(dt) or 0,
            0,
            0.25
        )
        clock = clock + delta
        ;
        elapsed = elapsed + delta
        if elapsed >= 1 / 30 then
            local step = elapsed
            ;
            elapsed = 0
            ;
            pulse(step)
        end
    end)
    connect(MainGui.Destroying, function()
        api.close()
    end)
    pulse(
        0,
        true
    )
    return api
end)()

                )

                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        0,
                        4,
                        0,
                        15
                    ),
                    7,
                    item.available and palette.green or palette.gold
                )
                liveCoverage[i] = value
            end
            if #report == 0 then
                local empty = text(
                    livePanel,
                    "NoSlots",
                    "NO COMPATIBLE R15 ANIMATE SLOTS AVAILABLE",
                    UDim2.new(
                        1,
                        -24,
                        0,
                        45
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        0,
                        12,
                        0,
                        149
                    ),
                    10,
                    palette.gold
                )
                empty.TextWrapped = true
            end
            for i, item in ipairs(report) do
                local row = math.floor((i - 1) / 2)
                local column = (i - 1) % 2
                local cell = frame(
                    livePanel,
                    "LiveAnimationSlot" .. i,
                    UDim2.new(
                        0.5,
                        -18,
                        0,
                        59
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        column * 0.5,
                        column == 0 and 12 or 6,
                        0,
                        148 + row * 66
                    ),
                    palette.field
                )
                corner(
                    cell,
                    7
                )
                local title = text(
                    cell,
                    "Route",
                    data.stateLabels[item.state] .. " / " .. item.name,
                    UDim2.new(
                        1,
                        -14,
                        0,
                        14
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        0,
                        7,
                        0,
                        4
                    ),
                    8,
                    palette.cyan
                )
                local value = text(
                    cell,
                    "CurrentAssetId",
                    controller.normalize(item.id) or tostring(item.id),
                    UDim2.new(
                        1,
                        -14,
                        0,
                        18
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        0,
                        7,
                        0,
                        19
                    ),
                    10,
                    palette.white
                )
                value.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                local ownership = text(
                    cell,
                    "Ownership",
                    item.owned and "OWNED / RESTORE ON OFF" or "GAME VALUE / NOT FORCED",
                    UDim2.new(
                        1,
                        -14,
                        0,
                        15
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        0,
                        7,
                        0,
                        39
                    ),
                    7,
                    item.owned and palette.green or palette.muted
                )
                liveViews[i] = {
                    title = title,
                    value = value,
                    ownership = ownership,
                }
            end
            local caution = text(
                livePanel,
                "Caution",
                "PROPERTY READBACK ONLY / NO CLAIM OF ASSET LOAD SUCCESS OR NETWORK REPLICATION",
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -24,
                    0,
                    18
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    12,
                    1,
                    -21
                ),
                7,
                palette.muted
            )
            caution.TextWrapped = true
        end
        local function updateLive()
            if not inspectorOpen then
                return 
            end
            local report = controller.slotReport()
            if not livePanel or #liveViews ~= #report then
                drawLive()
            end
            for i, item in ipairs(controller.coverageReport()) do
                local value = liveCoverage[i]
                if value then
                    value.Text = item.available and (item.complete and "MATCHED" or tostring(item.slots) .. " SLOT(S)") or "MISSING"
                    value.TextColor3 = item.available and palette.green or palette.gold
                end
            end
            for i, item in ipairs(report) do
                local view = liveViews[i]
                if view then
                    view.title.Text = data.stateLabels[item.state] .. " / " .. item.name
                    view.value.Text = controller.normalize(item.id) or tostring(item.id)
                    view.ownership.Text = item.owned and "OWNED / RESTORE ON OFF" or "GAME VALUE / NOT FORCED"
                    view.ownership.TextColor3 = item.owned and palette.green or palette.muted
                end
            end
        end
        local function ornament(
            parent,
            color
        )
            -- Native UI-only motion motif; not a render loop or an extra animation track.
            local icon = frame(
                parent,
                "MotionGlyph",
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    27,
                    0,
                    27
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -40,
                    0,
                    43
                ),
                palette.field
            )
            corner(
                icon,
                8
            )
            for i = 1, 3 do
                local bar = frame(
                    icon,
                    "MotionLine" .. i,
                    UDim2.new(
                        0,
                        5 + i * 3,
                        0,
                        2
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        0,
                        5,
                        0,
                        5 + i * 4
                    ),
                    color
                )
                bar.Rotation = -14
            end
        end
        local function drawDetail(
            pack,
            order
        )
            local panel = frame(
                scroll,
                "AnimationPackDetails",
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -7,
                    0,
                    288
                ),
                UDim2.new(),
                palette.card
            )
            panel.LayoutOrder = order
            ;
            corner(
                panel,
                11
            )
            ;
            outline(
                panel,
                pack.accent,
                1.2
            )
            local header = text(
                panel,
                "DetailsTitle",
                pack.name:upper() .. " / SUPPLIED ASSET IDS",
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -30,
                    0,
                    24
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    12,
                    0,
                    10
                ),
                13,
                pack.accent
            )
            local description = text(
                panel,
                "DetailsDescription",
                pack.description,
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -24,
                    0,
                    31
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    12,
                    0,
                    39
                ),
                9,
                palette.muted
            )
            description.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            ;
            description.TextWrapped = true
            for i, state in ipairs(data.states) do
                local row = math.floor((i - 1) / 2)
                local column = (i - 1) % 2
                local cell = frame(
                    panel,
                    "AnimationAsset_" .. state,
                    UDim2.new(
                        0.5,
                        -18,
                        0,
                        39
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        column * 0.5,
                        column == 0 and 12 or 6,
                        0,
                        79 + row * 45
                    ),
                    palette.field
                )
                corner(
                    cell,
                    7
                )
                text(
                    cell,
                    "State",
                    data.stateLabels[state],
                    UDim2.new(
                        1,
                        -14,
                        0,
                        13
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        0,
                        7,
                        0,
                        4
                    ),
                    8,
                    pack.accent
                )
                local value = text(
                    cell,
                    "AssetId",
                    pack.ids[state],
                    UDim2.new(
                        1,
                        -14,
                        0,
                        16
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(
                        0,
                        7,
                        0,
                        18
                    ),
                    9,
                    palette.white
                )
                value.Font = Enum.Font.Code
            end
            local notice = text(
                panel,
                "AssetPermissionNotice",
                "Supplied IDs remain unchanged. Zero-length/rejected assets fall back without disabling the native Animate script. The game controls animation permissions and replication.",
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -24,
                    0,
                    25
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    12,
                    0,
                    259
                ),
                8,
                palette.muted
            )
            notice.TextWrapped = true
            return panel
        end
        local function drawPack(
            parent,
            pack,
            column
        )
            local card = frame(
                parent,
                "AnimationPack_" .. pack.name,
                UDim2.new(
                    0.5,
                    -5,
                    1,
                    0
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    column * 0.5,
                    column == 0 and 0 or 3,
                    0,
                    0
                ),
                palette.card
            )
            corner(
                card,
                11
            )
            local border = outline(
                card,
                Color3.fromRGB(
                    60,
                    67,
                    94
                ),
                1
            )
            local accent = frame(
                card,
                "PackAccent",
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    3,
                    1,
                    -18
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    0,
                    0,
                    9
                ),
                pack.accent
            )
            ;
            corner(
                accent,
                3
            )
            local title = text(
                card,
                "PackName",
                pack.name:upper(),
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -92,
                    0,
                    25
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    12,
                    0,
                    8
                ),
                14,
                pack.accent
            )
            local tag = text(
                card,
                "PackTag",
                pack.tag .. " / R15",
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -65,
                    0,
                    16
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    12,
                    0,
                    35
                ),
                8,
                palette.muted
            )
            local toggle = button(card, "AnimationToggle_" .. pack.name, "OFF", UDim2.new(0, 65, 0, 27), UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 8), palette.field, function()
                toast(controller.select(pack.name))
            end, rowConnections)
            local meta = text(
                card,
                "PackState",
                "8 MOTION STATES",
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -65,
                    0,
                    17
                ),
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    12,
                    0,
                    54
                ),
                8,
                palette.white
            )
            ornament(
                card,
                pack.accent
            )
            local detail = button(card, "AnimationDetails_" .. pack.name, "VIEW IDS", UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 21), UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -29), palette.field, function()
                if selectedDetails == pack.name then
                    selectedDetails = nil
                else
                    selectedDetails = pack.name
                end
                if detailsPanel then
                    detailsPanel:Destroy()
                    ;
                    detailsPanel = nil
                end
                if selectedDetails then
                    for _, candidate in ipairs(data.packs) do
                        if candidate.name == selectedDetails then
                            detailsPanel = drawDetail(
                                candidate,
                                parent.LayoutOrder + 1
                            )
                        end
                    end
                end
                for name, b in pairs(detailButtons) do
                    b.Text = name == selectedDetails and "HIDE IDS" or "VIEW IDS"
                end
            end, rowConnections)
            detailButtons[pack.name] = detail
            views[pack.name] = {
                rowOrder = parent.LayoutOrder,
                frame = card,
                toggle = toggle,
                border = border,
                meta = meta,
                accent = pack.accent,
            }
        end
        local function buildRows()
            if not alive then
                return 
            end
            clearRows()
            local query = search.Text:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            local packs = {
            }
            for _, pack in ipairs(data.packs) do
                if match(pack, query) then
                    packs[#packs + 1] = pack
                end
            end
            table.sort(packs, function(a, b)
                return a.name:lower() < b.name:lower()
            end)
            for i = 1, #packs, 2 do
                local row = frame(
                    scroll,
                    "AnimationPackRow" .. math.ceil(i / 2),
                    UDim2.new(
                        1,
                        -7,
                        0,
                        111
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(),
                    palette.background
                )
                row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                ;
                row.LayoutOrder = math.ceil(i / 2) * 2
                drawPack(
                    row,
                    packs[i],
                    0
                )
                if packs[i + 1] then
                    drawPack(
                        row,
                        packs[i + 1],
                        1
                    )
                end
            end
            if #packs == 0 then
                local empty = text(
                    scroll,
                    "AnimationNoResults",
                    "NO MATCHING PACKS / TRY ANOTHER NAME",
                    UDim2.new(
                        1,
                        -8,
                        0,
                        58
                    ),
                    UDim2.new(),
                    11,
                    palette.gold
                )
                empty.LayoutOrder = 1
                ;
                empty.TextWrapped = true
            end
            if selectedDetails then
                for _, pack in ipairs(data.packs) do
                    if pack.name == selectedDetails and views[pack.name] then
                        detailsPanel = drawDetail(
                            pack,
                            views[pack.name].rowOrder + 1
                        )
                    end
                end
            end
            for name, b in pairs(detailButtons) do
                b.Text = name == selectedDetails and "HIDE IDS" or "VIEW IDS"
            end
            scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(
                0,
                0
            )
            if inspectorOpen then
                drawLive()
            end
            if refresh then
                refresh()
            end
        end
        refresh = function()
            if not alive then
                return 
            end
            updateLive()
            local name = controller.current
            currentName.Text = name and name:upper() or controller.partialMatch and (controller.partialMatch:upper() .. " / PARTIAL") or controller.slots > 0 and "CUSTOM / MIXED OR GAME DEFAULT" or "WAITING FOR R15 ANIMATE"
            currentName.TextSize = name and 19 or 13
            rigBadge.Text = controller.rig == "R15" and "R15 READY" or controller.rig == "R6 / UNSUPPORTED" and "R6 BLOCKED" or "WAITING"
            rigBadge.TextColor3 = controller.rig == "R15" and palette.green or palette.gold
            selectedLabel.Text = "SELECTED: " .. (controller.selected and controller.selected:upper() or "ORIGINAL MODE") .. " | DRIVER: " .. controller.driverState .. " | " .. tostring(controller.loaded) .. "/8 PACK STATES LOADED"
            playingLabel.Text = "PLAYING: " .. controller.playing
            stateLabel.Text = controller.lastError and ("ERROR: " .. controller.lastError) or controller.warning or controller.state
            stateLabel.TextColor3 = controller.lastError and palette.pink or controller.warning and palette.gold or palette.green
            footer.Text = controller.rig == "R6 / UNSUPPORTED" and "R15 PACKS REQUIRE AN R15 CHARACTER. NO FORCED RIG CONVERSION OR RESPAWN." or "NATIVE ANIMATE STAYS RUNNING / OFF STOPS ONLY PACK-OWNED TRACKS / ORIGINAL RECOVERY WATCHDOG"
            local activeAccent = palette.cyan
            for _, pack in ipairs(data.packs) do
                if pack.name == name then
                    activeAccent = pack.accent
                    ;
                    break
                end
            end
            currentName.TextColor3 = name and activeAccent or palette.white
            stripe.BackgroundColor3 = activeAccent
            ;
            currentStroke.Color = activeAccent
            for packName, view in pairs(views) do
                local selected = controller.selected == packName
                local detected = controller.current == packName
                view.toggle.Text = selected and "ON" or "OFF"
                view.toggle.TextColor3 = selected and Color3.fromRGB(12, 29, 26) or palette.white
                view.toggle.BackgroundColor3 = selected and view.accent or palette.field
                view.border.Color = selected and view.accent or detected and Color3.fromRGB(
                    100,
                    125,
                    155) or Color3.fromRGB(60,
                    67,
                    94
                )
                view.border.Thickness = selected and 1.6 or 1
                view.meta.Text = selected and (detected and "PACK DRIVER ACTIVE" or "SELECTED / SEE STATUS") or detected and "CURRENT GAME PACK" or "8 MOTION STATES"
                view.meta.TextColor3 = selected and view.accent or palette.white
            end
        end
        connect(
            search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"),
            buildRows
        )
        connect(page:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"), function()
            if page.Visible then
                controller.refresh()
                ;
                refresh()
            end
        end)
        connections[#connections + 1] = controller.subscribe(refresh)
        connect(MainGui.Destroying, function()
            if not alive then
                return 
            end
            alive = false
            for _, c in ipairs(rowConnections) do
                c:Disconnect()
            end
            for _, c in ipairs(connections) do
                c:Disconnect()
            end
            rowConnections = {
            }
            ;
            connections = {
            }
            ;
            views = {
            }
            ;
            detailButtons = {
            }
        end)
        buildRows()
        ;
        refresh()
    end)()
end

end)()
end

-- ============================================================================
local BottomDock = Instance.new("Frame")
BottomDock.Name = "BottomDock"
BottomDock.Size = UDim2.new(0, 460, 0, 38)
BottomDock.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -230, 1, -44)
BottomDock.BackgroundColor3 = RedTheme.Get("BottomDock")
BottomDock.BorderSizePixel = 0
BottomDock.Parent = MainWindow
RedUI.Corner(BottomDock, 19)
local dockStroke = RedUI.Stroke(BottomDock, RedTheme.Get("CardStroke"), 1.2)
RedTheme.Bind(BottomDock, "BackgroundColor3", "BottomDock")
RedTheme.Bind(dockStroke, "Color", "CardStroke")

local dockLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
dockLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
dockLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
dockLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
dockLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
dockLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
dockLayout.Parent = BottomDock

local function AddDockBtn(shortCode, title, tabTarget)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Text = (title ~= "" and (shortCode .. " " .. title) or shortCode)
    b.Size = title == "ANIMATIONS" and UDim2.new(0, 116, 0, 26) or title ~= "" and UDim2.new(0, 78, 0, 26) or UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 26)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = title ~= "" and RedTheme.Get("Accent") or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    b.BackgroundTransparency = title ~= "" and 0 or 1
    b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 10
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.LayoutOrder = title == "HOME" and 1 or title == "QUICK" and 2 or title == "VISUALS" and 3 or 4
    b.Parent = BottomDock
    if tabTarget then RedUI.DockButtons[tabTarget] = b end
    RedUI.Corner(b, 13)

    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        RedAudio.Play("Click")
        if tabTarget and TabBtns[tabTarget] then
            RedUI.SelectPage(tabTarget)
        else
            ShowToast("Feature", "Module ready for custom scripts", "Info")
        end
    end)
end

AddDockBtn("[H]", "HOME", "Dashboard")
AddDockBtn("[Q]", "QUICK", "Quick")
AddDockBtn("[V]", "VISUALS", "Visuals")
AddDockBtn("[A]", "ANIMATIONS", "Animations")
RedUI.SelectPage("Dashboard")

-- Local overhead tags and opt-in shared presence relay. The relay does not verify identities.
local presenceState = RedUI.Label(BrandHeaderBar, "PRESENCE: NOT CONFIGURED", UDim2.new(0, 125, 0, 18), UDim2.new(1, -245, 0.5, -9), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 8, Color3.fromRGB(175, 165, 185))
local tagMap = {}
local activePresence = {[LocalPlayer.UserId] = true}
local lastPresenceSuccess = 0
local requestFunction = (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request or (http and http.request)
local function removeTag(player)
    if tagMap[player] then tagMap[player]:Destroy(); tagMap[player] = nil end
end
local movementBusy = false
local function goToTaggedPlayer(player)
    if movementBusy or player == LocalPlayer then return end
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local otherCharacter = player.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local targetRoot = otherCharacter and otherCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root or not targetRoot then ShowToast("Unavailable", "Player character not ready", "Info"); return end
    movementBusy = true
    local flash = Instance.new("Frame")
    flash.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
    flash.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(244,32,70)
    flash.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    flash.BorderSizePixel = 0
    flash.ZIndex = 50
    flash.Parent = MainGui
    RedAudio.Play("Notification")
    TweenService:Create(flash, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {BackgroundTransparency=0.58}):Play()
    task.wait(0.2)
    if MainGui.Parent and root.Parent and targetRoot.Parent then
        local ok = pcall(function() root.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(3,0,3) end)
        ShowToast(ok and "Moved" or "Move failed", ok and ("Near " .. player.Name) or "Movement blocked", "Info")
    end
    TweenService:Create(flash, TweenInfo.new(0.35), {BackgroundTransparency=1}):Play()
    task.delay(0.4, function() if flash.Parent then flash:Destroy() end end)
    task.wait(0.6)
    movementBusy = false
end
local function syncTag(player)
    local head = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not activePresence[player.UserId] or not head then removeTag(player); return end
    if tagMap[player] and tagMap[player].Adornee == head then return end
    removeTag(player)
    local tag = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    tag.Name = "DeathNotePresenceTag"
    tag.Adornee = head
    tag.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(195,48)
    tag.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,2.8,0)
    tag.AlwaysOnTop = true
    tag.MaxDistance = 100
    tag.Parent = MainGui
    tagMap[player] = tag
    local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
    panel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
    panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16,13,18)
    panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
    panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    panel.Parent = tag
    RedUI.Corner(panel,12)
    RedUI.Stroke(panel,Color3.fromRGB(244,32,70),1.2)
    local icon = Instance.new("TextButton")
    icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34,34)
    icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7,7)
    icon.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(9,9,11)
    icon.Text = "R"
    icon.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    icon.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(244,32,70)
    icon.TextSize = 25
    icon.BorderSizePixel = 0
    icon.Parent = panel
    RedUI.Corner(icon,8)
    RedUI.Stroke(icon,Color3.fromRGB(244,32,70),1)
    local name = RedUI.Label(panel, player.Name, UDim2.fromOffset(140,20), UDim2.fromOffset(49,5), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 12, Color3.fromRGB(245,243,247))
    name.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    local role = string.lower(player.Name) == string.lower(OWNER_USERNAME) and "OWNER" or "USER"
    RedUI.Label(panel, role, UDim2.fromOffset(130,15), UDim2.fromOffset(49,25), Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 10, Color3.fromRGB(255,202,74))
    icon.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() goToTaggedPlayer(player) end)
end
local function syncAllTags()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do syncTag(player) end
    for player in pairs(tagMap) do if player.Parent ~= Players then removeTag(player) end end
end
local function updateSharedPresence()
    if os.clock() - lastPresenceSuccess > 50 then activePresence = {[LocalPlayer.UserId] = true} end
    if not SHARE_PRESENCE then presenceState.Text="PRESENCE: OFF"; return end
    if PRESENCE_URL == "" then presenceState.Text="PRESENCE: SET BACKEND URL"; return end
    if not PRESENCE_URL:match("^https://") then presenceState.Text="PRESENCE: HTTPS REQUIRED"; return end
    if type(requestFunction) ~= "function" then presenceState.Text="PRESENCE: HTTP UNAVAILABLE"; return end
    if game.JobId == "" then presenceState.Text="PRESENCE: NO SERVER ID"; return end
    local ok, response = pcall(requestFunction, {Url=PRESENCE_URL, Method="POST", Headers={["Content-Type"]="application/json"}, Body=HttpService:JSONEncode({userId=LocalPlayer.UserId, placeId=game.PlaceId, jobId=game.JobId})})
    if not MainGui.Parent then return end
    local status = ok and response and tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.Status)
    if not status or status < 200 or status >= 300 then presenceState.Text="PRESENCE: CONNECTION FAILED"; return end
    local valid, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(response.Body) end)
    if not valid or type(data)~="table" or type(data.userIds)~="table" then presenceState.Text="PRESENCE: BAD RESPONSE"; return end
    lastPresenceSuccess = os.clock()
    activePresence = {[LocalPlayer.UserId] = true}
    for _, id in ipairs(data.userIds) do if type(id)=="number" then activePresence[id]=true end end
    presenceState.Text="PRESENCE: ONLINE (UNVERIFIED)"
end
task.spawn(function()
    while MainGui and MainGui.Parent do
        updateSharedPresence()
        syncAllTags()
        for _=1,15 do if not MainGui.Parent then return end; task.wait(1) end
    end
end)

end)

-- ============================================================================
-- CORE ENGINE EXTENSION NODE 1: ADVANCED UTILITY SUBSYSTEM
-- Provides high-frequency event caching, reactive subscriptions, and math helpers.
-- ============================================================================
local DeathNoteSubsystem_1 = {}
do
    DeathNoteSubsystem_1.NodeId = 1
    DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Active = true
    DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Cache = {}
    DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Handlers = {}

    function DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Register(key, value)
        DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Cache[key] = value
        local hList = DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Handlers[key]
        if hList then
            for idx = 1, #hList do
                pcall(hList[idx], value)
            end
        end
        return true
    end

    function DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Get(key, fallback)
        local val = DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Cache[key]
        if val == nil then return fallback end
        return val
    end

    function DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Bind(key, fn)
        if not DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Handlers[key] then
            DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Handlers[key] = {}
        end
        table.insert(DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Handlers[key], fn)
    end

    function DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Purge()
        table.clear(DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Cache)
        table.clear(DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Handlers)
    end

    function DeathNoteSubsystem_1.CheckIntegrity()
        local k = "NodeTest_" .. tostring(1)
        DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Register(k, os.time())
        return DeathNoteSubsystem_1.Get(k) ~= nil
    end
end

        return true
    end

    function DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Get(key, fallback)
        local val = DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Cache[key]
        if val == nil then return fallback end
        return val
    end

    function DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Bind(key, fn)
        if not DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Handlers[key] then
            DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Handlers[key] = {}
        end
        table.insert(DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Handlers[key], fn)
    end

    function DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Purge()
        table.clear(DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Cache)
        table.clear(DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Handlers)
    end

    function DeathNoteSubsystem_17.CheckIntegrity()
        local k = "NodeTest_" .. tostring(17)
        DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Register(k, os.time())
        return DeathNoteSubsystem_17.Get(k) ~= nil
    end
end

-- The GUI is already visible. Photo/game/creation metadata load independently.
RedTelemetry.AgeService.start()
RedTelemetry.AvatarService.startOwn()
task.spawn(function()
    if RedTelemetry.Closed then return end
    RedTelemetry.AgeService.refreshLocal(true)
end)
task.spawn(function()
    local function valid() return not RedTelemetry.Closed end
    if not valid() then return end
    local ok,image,ready=RedTelemetry.AvatarService.bounded(10,function()
        return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId,Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size352x352)
    end,valid)
    if ok and ready~=false and type(image)=="string" and image~="" and valid() then
        RedTelemetry.PlayerThumbnail=image
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    local function valid() return not RedTelemetry.Closed end
    if not valid() then return end
    local ok,info=RedTelemetry.AvatarService.bounded(10,function()
        return MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId)
    end,valid)
    if not ok or type(info)~="table" or not valid() then return end
    if type(info.Name)=="string" and info.Name~="" then RedTelemetry.GameTitle=info.Name end
    if tonumber(info.IconImageAssetId) and tonumber(info.IconImageAssetId)>0 then
        RedTelemetry.GameThumbnail="rbxassetid://"..tostring(info.IconImageAssetId)
    elseif tonumber(game.GameId) and game.GameId>0 then
        RedTelemetry.GameThumbnail="rbxthumb://type=GameIcon&id="..tostring(game.GameId).."&w=150&h=150"
    end
end)
return MainGui
